oṃ namaḥ śrīgaṇeśāya |
parāśarasaṃhitā
mādhavācāryakṛtavyākhyāsahitā
atha dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
vāgīśādyās sumanasaḥ sarvārthānām upakrame |
yaṃ natvā kṛtakṛtyāḥ syus taṃ namāmi gajānanam ||
prathame ‘dhyāye vyāsena pṛṣṭayor varṇacatuṣṭayasādhāranāsādhāraṇadharmayor madhye sādhāraṇdharmaṃ saṃkṣipyāsādhāraṇadharmaḥ prapañcitaḥ | athedānīṃ saṃkṣiptaḥ sādhāraṇadharmo dvitīye ‘dhyāye prapañcyate | atha vā pūrvādhyāya āmuṣmikadharmaprādhānyena pravṛttaḥ | ayaṃ tu aihikajīvanahetudharmaprādhānyena pravartate | tatrādāv adhyāyapratipādyam arthaṃ pratijānīte |
ataḥ paraṃ gṛhasthasya karmācāraṃ kalau yuge |
dharmaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ śaktyā cāturvarṇyāśramāgatam || 1 ||
taṃ pravakṣyāmy ahaṃ pūrvaṃ parāśaravaco yathā |
ataḥ paraṃ cāturvarṇyasādhāraṇadharmasaṃkṣepakathanād uttarasmin kāle sa eva vistarakathanasyocito ‘vasaraḥ | bahugranthapāṭhe śaktirahitān mandaprajñān prati saṃkṣepeṇābhihite sati samarthānām [2] uttamaprajñānāṃ buddhisthatvāt tān prati vistareṇa kathayitum ucitatvāt | ata evāhur ācāryāḥ |
saṃkṣepavistarābhyāṃ hi mandottamadhiyāṃ nṛṇām |
vastūcyamānam ety (?) antaḥkaraṇaṃ tena varṇyate || iti |
yad vā ataḥ paraṃ āmuṣmikapradhānadharmakathanād anantaram | “ṣaṭkarmābhirataḥ,” “saṃdhyāsnānam” ityādinā hy āmuṣmikaphale dharme ‘bhihite sati aihikaphalasya kṛṣyādidharmasya buddhisthatvā tadabhidhānasya yukto ‘vasaraḥ |
vakṣyamāṇasya kṛṣyādidharmasya brahmacārivanasthayatiṣv asaṃbhavam abhipretya tadyogyam āśramiṇaṃ darśayati gṛhasthasya – iti kṛtatretādvāpareṣu vaiśyasyaiva kṛṣyādāv adhikāro na tu gṛhasthamātrasya viprādeḥ ato viśinaṣṭi kalau yuge iti | karmaśabdo loke vyāpāramātre prayujyate | ācāraśabdaś ca dharmarūpe śāstrīye vyāpāre kṛṣyādes tu yugāntareṣu karmatvam | kalāv ācāratvam ity ubhayarūpatvam asti | tathaivāśramāntareṣu karmatvaṃ gārhasthyasya coktācāratvam ity ubhayarūpatā | tadvivakṣayā karmācāram ity uktam | kalau gṛhasthasya yājanādīnāṃ durlabhatvāj jīvanahetutayā kṛṣyādividhānād ācāratvam upapannam |
kṛṣyādeḥ sādhāraṇadharmatvam upapādayati – cāturvarṇyāśramāgatam [3] iti | caturvarṇā eva cāturvarṇyam | tac ca sarvatra prasiddham | āśramo gārhasthyarūpaḥ | santi hi śūdrasyāpi vivāhapañcamahāyajñādayo gṛhasthadharmāḥ | etac ca sarvam asmābhiḥ uttaratra āśramanirūpaṇe vispaṣṭam abhidhāsyate | tasminn āśrame vidhānāt sādhāraṇyam | parāśaraśabdenātrātītakalpotpanno vivakṣitaḥ | etad evābhivyañjayituṃ pūrvam ity uktam | pūrvakalpasiddhaṃ parāśaravākyaṃ kalidharme kṛṣyādau yathāvṛttaṃ tathaivāhaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi | ataḥ saṃpradāyāgatatvāt kṛṣyāder ācāratāyāṃ na vivādaḥ kartavyaḥ ity āśayaḥ | “gurutaraviṣaye vinayaḥ kartavyaḥ” iti śiṣṭācāraṃ śikṣayituṃ śaktyā saṃpravakṣyāmi iti uktam | na tu kasmiṃścid dharme svasya aśaktiṃ dyotayitum | kalidharmapravīṇasya parāśarasya tatrāśaktyasaṃbhavāt || 1 ||
pratijñātaṃ dharmaṃ darśayati |
ṣaṭkarmasahito vipraḥ kṛṣikarma ca kārayet || 2 ||
ṣaṭkarmāṇi pūrvoktāni yajanādīni saṃdhyādīni ca | taiḥ sahito vipraḥ śuśrūṣakaiḥ śūdraiḥ kṛṣiṃ kārayet | na ca [4] yājanādīnāṃ jīvanahetutvāt kim anayā kṛṣyā iti vācyam | kalau jīvanaparyāptatayā yājanādīnāṃ durlabhatvāt | yat tu manunoktam,
vaiśyavṛttyāpi jīvaṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo ‘pi vā |
hiṃsāprāyaṃ parādhīnāṃ kṛṣiṃ yatnena varjayet ||
kṛṣiṃ sādhv iti manyante sā vṛttiḥ sadvigarhitā |
bhūmiṃ bhūmiśayāṃś hanti kāṣṭham ayomukham || iti | (MDh 10.83–84)
tat svayaṃkṛtābhiprāyam, anyathā manoḥ svavacanavirodhāt | yataḥ tenaiva kṛṣir abhyupagatā |
ubhābhyām apy ajīvaṃs tu kathaṃ syād iti ced bhavet |
kṛṣigorakṣam āsthāya jīved vaiśyasya jīvikām || iti | (MDh 10.82)
nanu – kūmapurāṇe svayaṃkṛtā kṛṣir abhyupagatā |
svayaṃ vā karṣaṇaṃ kuryād vāṇijyaṃ vā kusīdakam |
kaṣṭā pāpīyasī vṛttiḥ kusīdaṃ tad vivarjayet || (KūPu 1.2.15.4)
[5] iti |
tan na, asya vacanasya kusīdanindāpartvāt | ata eva nāradaḥ |
āpatsv api ca kaṣṭāsu brāhmaṇasya na vārdhuṣam | iti | (NSm 4.111)
nanu – bṛhaspatiḥ svayaṃkartṛkāṃ kṛṣim aṅgīcakāra |
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijyaṃ prakurvītāsvayaṃkṛtam |
āpatkāle svayaṃ kurvann ainasā (?) yujyate dvijaḥ || iti |
bāḍham | kārayitum apy aśaktasya tatkartṛtvam, “āpatkāle” iti viśeṣitatvā |
nanu – kārayitṛtvam apy āpadviṣayam eva, kṛṣer vaiśyadharmatvāt viprasya yājanādīnām eva mukhyajīvanahetutvāt | evaṃ tarhy āpattāratamyena vyavasthāstu | alpāpadi kārayitṛtvaṃ atyantāpadi kartṛtvam iti | atha vā yugabhedena vyavasthāpyatām | yugāntareṣu kārayitṛtvam āpaddharmaḥ kalau mukhyadharmaḥ, prādhānyenopakramya pratipādanāt |
tatra kṛṣau halasya ekasya balīvardasaṃkhyāniyamam āha hārītaḥ |
aṣṭāgavaṃ dharmyahalaṃ ṣaḍgavaṃ jīvanārthinām |
caturgavaṃ nṛśaṃsānāṃ dvigavaṃ brahmaghātinām || iti | (HārSm 5.13)
tatrāntyau pakṣau heyau itarāv api krameṇa mukhyānukalpau draṣṭavyau || 2 ||
kṛṣau varjyān balīvardān āha |
[6]
kṣudhitaṃ tṛṣitaṃ śrāntaṃ balīvardaṃ na yojayet |
hīnāṅgaṃ vyādhitaṃ klībaṃ vṛṣaṃ vipro na vāhayet || iti || 3 ||
kṣudhādayas tattalliṅgair avagantavyāḥ | hīnāṅgaḥ pādavikalaḥ | klībaḥ puṃstvarahitaḥ || 3 ||
kīdṛśas tarhi valīvardaḥ kṛṣau yojya ity ata āha |
sthirāṅgaṃ nīrujaṃ tṛptaṃ sunardaṃ ṣaṇḍhavarjitam |
vāhayed divasasyārdhaṃ paścāt snānaṃ samācaret || iti || 4 ||
sthirāṅgaḥ pādādivaikalyarahitaḥ | nīrujo vyādhirahitaḥ | tṛptaḥ kṣuttṛṣṇābhyām apīḍitaḥ | sunardo hisako (?) dṛptaḥ śramarahitaḥ – iti yāvat | ṣaṇḍheti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ | ṣaṇḍhatvavarjitaḥ puṃstvopetaḥ śaktaḥ – iti yāvat | divasasyārdhaṃ yāmadvayam | paścāt snānaṃ samācaret iti balīvardān snāpayed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ: “snāpayitvānaḍuho ‘laṃkṛtya brāhmaṇān bhojayet” iti (Hārśn 4.10) | vāhane viśeṣa āśvamedhike darśitaḥ |
[7]        vāhayed dhuṃkṛtenaiva śākhayā vā sapatrayā |
na daṇḍena na yaṣṭyā vā na pāśena ca vā punaḥ ||
na kṣuttṛṣṇāśramaśrāntaṃ vāhayed vikalendriyam |
sutṛpteṣu ca bhuñjīyāt pibet pīteṣ codakam ||
ahnaḥ pūrvaṃ dviyāmaṃ vā dhuryāṇāṃ vāhanaṃ smṛtam |
viśrāmyen madhyame bhāge bhāge cāntye yathāsukham ||
yatra vā tvarayā kṛtyaṃ saṃśayo yatra cādhvani |
vāhayet tatra duryāṃs tu na sa pāpena lipyate ||
anyathā vāhayan rājan niyataṃ yāti rauravam |
rudhiraṃ vāhayet teṣāṃ yas tu mohān narādhamaḥ ||
bhrūṇahatyāsamaṃ pāpaṃ tasya syāt pāṇḍunandana || iti || 4 ||
balīvardasnāpanānantaraṃ kartavyam āha |
[8]
japyaṃ devārcanaṃ homaṃ svādhyāyaṃ caivam abhyaset |
ekadvitricaturviprān bhojayet snātakān dvijaḥ || 5 ||
yathā balīvardaśrāntikṛtadoṣāpanayanāya snāpanam evaṃ prānyupaghātadoṣāpanayanāya yathāśakti japyādīnām anyatamam anutiṣṭhet | ekaṃ dvau trīṃś caturo vā viprān snātakān yathāśakti bhojayet | snātakā navavidhabhikṣukāḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam adhvagaṃ sarvavedasam |
gurvarthaṃ pitṛmātrarthaṃ svādhyāyārthyupatāpinam || iti | (MDh 11.1)
sāntānikaḥ santānāya vivāhopayuktadravyārthī | sarvavedasaḥ sarvasvadakṣiṇaṃ yāgaṃ kṛtvā niḥsvatvam āpanno dravyārthī tam ity arthaḥ | pitṛmātrarthaṃ pitṛmātṛśuśrūṣārthinam | svādhyāyārthī svādhyāyapravacananirvāhāya dravyārthī | svādhyāyārthisahitaḥ upatāpī svādhyāyārthyupatāpī tam iti madhyamapadalopī samāsaḥ || 5 ||
kṛṣau phalitasya dhānyasya viniyogam āha |
[9]        svayaṃkṛṣṭe tathā kṣetre dhānyaiś ca svayamarjitauḥ |
nirvapet pañcayajñāṃś ca kratudīkṣāṃ ca kārayet || iti || 6 ||
yāni svayaṃkṛṣṭe kṣetre phalitāni dhānyāni yāni dāsaiḥ karṣite kṣetre svayamarjitāni dhānyāni taiḥ sarvaiḥ smārtān pañcamahāyajñān śrautīm agniṣṭomādikratudīkṣāṃ ca kuryāt | kārayed iti svārthiko ṇic | atha vā svayaṃ mahāyajñān kurvīta | yiyakṣubhyo dhānyaṃ datvā taiḥ kratudīkṣāṃ ca kārayet | kūrmapurāṇe ‘py evaṃ viniyogo darśitaḥ |
labdhalābhaḥ pitṝn devān brāhmaṇāṃś cāpi pūjayet |
te tṛptās tasya tad doṣaṃ śamayanti na saṃśayaḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.25.7)
|| 6 ||
kṛṣīvalasya tilādidhānyasaṃpannasya dhanalobhena prasaktas tilādivikrayas taṃ nivārayati |
tilā rasā na vikreyā vikreyā dhānyatatsamāḥ |
viprasyaivaṃvidhā vṛttis tṛṇakāṣṭhādivikrayaḥ || iti || 7 ||
rasāḥ dadhimadhughṛtādayaḥ | yadi dhānyāntararahitasya tilavikrayam antareṇa jīvanaṃ vā dharmo vā na siddhyet [10] tadā tilā dhānyāntarair vinimātavyāḥ ity abhipretya “vikreyā dhānyatatsamāḥ” ity uktam | yāvadbhiḥ prasthais tilā dattās tāvadbhir eva dhānyāntaram upādeyaṃ nādhikam ity arthaḥ | tad uktaṃ nāradena |
aśaktau bheṣajasyārthe yajñahetos tathaiva ca |
yady avaśyaṃ tu vikreyās tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ || iti | (NSm 4.66)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
dharmārthaṃ vikrayaṃ neyās tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ | iti | (YDh 3.39)
tilanyāyo rase ‘pi yojanīyaḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
rasā rasair nimātavyā na tv eva lavaṇaṃ rasaiḥ |
kṛtānnaṃ cākṛtānnena tilā dhānyena tatsamāḥ || iti | (MDh 10.94)
āpastambo ‘pi: “annena cānnasya manuṣyāṇāṃ ca manuṣyaiḥ rasānāṃ ca rasaiḥ gandhānāṃ ca gandhair vidyayā ca vidyānām” iti (ĀpDh 1.7.20.15) | rasavinimaye viśeṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: [11] “rasā rasair mahato hīnato vā vimātavyāḥ” iti (VaDh 2.37) | annavinimaye viśeṣam āha gautamaḥ: “samenāsamena tu pakvasya” iti (GDh 7.3) |
nanu – tilavikrayo ‘bhyanugato manunā |
kāmam utpādya kṛṣyāṃ tu svayam eva kṛṣīvalaḥ |
vikrīṇīta tilān śuddhān dharmārtham acirasthitān || iti | (MDh 10.90)
atra kecid āhuḥ: tad etad aviniyamābhiprāyam iti | apare tu manyante: ṛṇāpākaraṇādyāvaśyakadharmārthe tilavikrayo na viruddhaḥ | ayam eva pakṣo yuktaḥ, vasiṣṭhavacanasaṃvādāt: “kāmaṃ vā svayaṃ kṛṣyotpādya tilān vikrīṇīran” iti (VaDh 2.31) |         vinimayābhiprāye tu vacanāntareṇa saha paunaruktyam aparihāryaṃ syāt | yato manunaiva vacanāntareṇa “tilā dānyena tatsamāḥ” it niyamo darśitaḥ | yat tv anyasmin vacane [12] arthāt tilavikrayaniṣedhaḥ pratibhāti | tathā hi manu-yamābhyām upadarśitam:
bhojanābhyañjanād dānād yad anyat kurute tilaiḥ |
kṛmibhūtaḥ śvaviṣṭhāyāṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha majjati || iti | (MDh 10.91)
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, āvaśyakadharmavyatiriktaviṣayatvāt | yo ‘yaṃ tilānāṃ dhānyasamatvena vinimayaḥ yaś ca tṛṇādivikrayaḥ seyam evaṃvidhā viprasya jīvanārthā vṛttiḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya tu vikreyaṃ śuṣkadārutṛṇādi ca | iti | (NSm 4.64)
|| 7 ||
idānīṃ kṛṣāv ānuṣaṅgikasya pāpmanaḥ pratīkāraṃ vaktuṃ prathamatas taṃ pāpmānaṃ darśayati |
brāhmaṇaś cet kṛṣiṃ kuryāt tan mahādoṣam āpnuyāt | iti |
kṛṣau hiṃsāyā avarjanīyatvāt sāvadhānasyāpi kṛṣīvalasya doṣo ‘nuṣajjyate iti | atra “hiṃsāprāyām” iti manuvacanaṃ pūrvam evodāhṛtam |
[13]
uktasya doṣasya mahattvaṃ viśadayati |
saṃvatsareṇa yat pāpaṃ matsyaghātī samāpnuyāt || 8 ||
ayomukhena kāṣṭhena tad ekāhena lāṅgalī | iti |
lohasahitena lāṅgalamukhena prāṇināṃ citravadho bhavatīti matsyavadhāt pāpādhikyam
uktam || 8 ||
uktarītyā karṣakamātrasya pāpaprasaktau tad vārayituṃ viśinaṣṭi |
pāśako matsyaghātī ca vyādhaḥ śākunikas tathā || 9 ||
adātā karṣakaś caiva sarve te samabhāginaḥ | iti |
vāgurāṃ prasārya mṛgādigrāhī pāśakaḥ | vyādho mṛgavadhājīvaḥ | śākunikaḥ pakṣighātī | adātā khale rāśimūlam upāgatebhyo ‘pradātā | teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ pratyavāyaḥ samānaḥ | tataś ca dṛṣṭāntatvena pāśakādaya iha varṇyante | yathā pāśakādīnāṃ pāpaṃ mahat evam evādātuḥ karṣakasyety arthaḥ || 9 ||
yadarthaṃ kṛṣīvalasya pāpmā darśitaḥ tam idānīṃ pāpaparihāraprakāram āha |
vṛkṣaṃ chittvā mahīṃ bhittvā hattvā ca kṛmi kīṭakān || 10 ||
karṣakaḥ khalayajñena sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | iti |
[14]
chedanabhedanahananair yāvanti pāpāni niṣpadyante teṣāṃ sarveṣāṃ khale dhānyadānaṃ pratīkāraḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
bhūmiṃ bhittvauṣadhīś chittvā hattvā kīṭapipīlikāḥ |
punanti khalayajñena karṣakā nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
yūpo ‘yaṃ nihito madhye meḍhir nāma hi karṣakaiḥ |
tasmād atantritodadyāt tatra dhānyārthadakṣiṇām || iti || (HārSm 5.14–15)
|| 10||
khalayajñākaraṇe pratyavāyam āha |
yo na dadyād dvijātibhyo rāśimūlam upāgataḥ || 11 ||
sa cauraḥ sa ca pāpiṣṭho brahmaghnaṃ taṃ vinirdiśet | iti |
karṣakasyāyaṃ khalayajño nityaḥ kāmyaś ca – iti vacanadvayabalād avagamyate | akaraṇe pratyavāyāt nityatvaṃ chedanādipāpanivartakatvāt kāmyatvaṃ ca | khalayajñasya nityatvaṃ śaivapurāṇe darśitam |
adatvā karṣako devi yas tu dhānyaṃ praveśayet |
tasya tṛṣṇābhibhūtasya devi pāpaṃ bravīmy aham ||
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ tu durātmā kṛṣikārakaḥ |
marudeśe bhaved vṛkṣaḥ sa puṣpaphalavarjitaḥ ||
tasyānte mānuṣo bhūtvā kadācit kālaparyayāt |
daridro vyādhito mūrkhaḥ kulahīnaś ca jāyate || iti || 11 ||
[15]
dātavyasya dhānyasya parimāṇam āha |
rājñe datvā tu ṣaḍbhāgaṃ devānāṃ caikaviṃśakam || 12 ||
viprāṇāṃ triṃśakaṃ bhāgaṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
ṣaṭsu bhāgeṣv eko bhāga ṣaḍbhāgaḥ | ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyākeṣu bhāgeṣv anyatamo bhāga ekaviṃśakaḥ | tadvat triṃśakeṣu bhāgeṣv anyatamo bhāgas triṃśaḥ  iti jñeyam | devavat pitṛbhyo ‘pi deyam | tad uktaṃ kūrmapurāṇe |
devebhyaś ca pitṛbhyaś ca dadyād bhāgaṃ tu viṃśakam |
triṃśadbhāgaṃ tu viprāṇāṃ kṛṣiṃ kurvan na doṣabhāk || iti | (KūPu 1.2.25.8)
|| 12 ||
viprasya setikartavyāṃ kṛṣim uktvā varṇāntarāṇām api tām āha |
kṣatriyo ‘pi kṛṣiṃ kṛtvā devān viprāṃś ca pūjayet || 13 ||
vaiśyaḥ śūdras tathā kuryāt kṛṣivāṇijyaśilpakam |
[16] iti | yady api vaiśyasya kṛṣiḥ pūrvādhyāye vihitā tathāpy atra itikartavyatāvidhānāya punar upanyāsaḥ | tathā kuryāt ity atideśena brāhmaṇasya kṛṣau vihitetikartavyatā sarvāpy atra vihitā bhavati | kṛṣivat vāṇijyaśilpayor api kalau varṇacatuṣṭayasādhāraṇyaṃ darśayituṃ vāṇijyaśilpakam ity uktam iti || 13 ||
yadi śūdrasyāpi kṛṣyādikam abhyupagamyeta tarhi tenaiva jīvanasiddheḥ kalau dvijaśuśrūṣā parityājyety āśaṅkyāha |
vikarma kurvate śūdrā dvijaśuśrūṣayojjhitāḥ || 14 ||
bhavanty alpāyuṣas te vai nirayaṃ yānty asaṃśayam | iti |
lābhādhikyena viśiṣṭajīvanahetutvāt kṛṣyādikaṃ vikarmety ucyate | dvijaśuśrūṣayā tu jīrṇavastrādikam eva labhyate iti na lābhādhikyam | ato ‘dhikalipsayā kṛṣyādikam eva kurvanto yadi dvijaśuśrūṣāṃ parityajeyus tadā teṣām aihikam āmuṣmikaṃ ca hīyeta || 14 ||
itthaṃ varṇacatuṣṭayasādhāraṇaṃ jīvanahetuṃ dharmaṃ pratipādya nigamayati |
caturṇām api varṇānām eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || 15 ||
atīteṣv api kaliyugeṣu viprādīnāṃ kṛṣyādikam astīti sūcayitum “sanātanaḥ” ity uktam |
[17]        yady api smṛtyantareṣv iva vātrāpi varṇadharmānantaram āśramadharmā vaktum ucitāḥ tathāpi vyāsenāpṛṣṭatvād ācāryeṇopekṣitāḥ | asmābhis tu śrotṛhitārthāya te ‘pi varṇyante | na ca – pūrvādhyāya eva kṛto na varṇitā – iti mantavyam, tatra prasaṅgābhāvāt | atra tu “cāturvarṇyāśramāgatam” ity āśramaśabdena teṣāṃ buddhisthatvād asti prasaṅgaḥ | te cāśramāś caturvidhāḥ | tad uktaṃ skānde |
brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho ‘tha bhikṣukaḥ |
ete krameṇa viprāṇāṃ catvāraḥ pṛthag āśramāḥ || iti |
manur api |
brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho yatis tathā |
ete gṛhasthaprabhavāś catvāraḥ pṛthag āśramāḥ || iti | (MDh 6.87)
gṛhasthaprabhavāḥ gṛhasthopayogina ity arthaḥ | tatropanayanena saṃskṛto brahmacārī, “brahmacaryam āgām upa mā nayasva” iti mantravarṇāt | brahmacaryam uddiśyāgāṃ mām upanayasva ity arthaḥ | upanayanaṃ ca garbhādhānādiṣu paṭhitatvāt brāhmaḥ saṃskāraḥ | tad āha hārītaḥ: “dvividho hi saṃskāro bhavati brāhmo daivaś ca | garbhādhānādismārtho brāhmaḥ | pākayajñā [18] haviryajñāḥ saumāś ca daivaḥ | brāhmeṇa saṃskṛta ṛṣīṇāṃ samānatāṃ sāyujyaṃ gacchati | daivenottareṇa saṃskṛto devānāṃ samānatāṃ sāyujyaṃ gacchati” iti (HārSm 11.1–5) | garbhādānādayo gautamenānukrāntāḥ |
garbhādhānapuṃsavanānavalobhanasīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇānna-prāśanacaulopanayanam | catvari vedavratāni | snānaṃ sahadharmacāriṇīsaṃyogaḥ | pañcānāṃ yajñānām anuṣṭhānaṃ devapitṛmanuṣyabhūtabrahmaṇām eteṣāṃ ca | aṣṭakā pārvaṇaśrāddhaṃ śrāvaṇyāgrahāyaṇī caitry āśvayujī cati sapta pākayajñasaṃsthāḥ | agnyādheyo ‘gnihotraṃ darśapūrṇamāsāgrayaṇacāturmāsyāni nirūḍhapaśubandhāḥ sautrāmaṇīti sapta haviryajñasaṃsthāḥ | agniṣṭomo ‘tyagniṣṭoma ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśī vājapeyo ‘tirātrāptoryāma iti sapta somasaṃsthāḥ | ity ete catvāriṃśat saṃskārāḥ |
iti (GDh 8.13–20) | tatra garbhādhānādayaś cūḍāntāḥ saṃskārā bījagarbhajanitadoṣanivṛttyarthāḥ | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ cūḍāntān saṃskārān nirūpyāha,
[19]        evam enaḥ śamaṃ yāti bījagarbhasamudbhavam | iti | (YDh 1.13)
ato brahmacaryāśramāt prāk te varṇyante | tatra dvijānāṃ garbhādhānādayaḥ samantrakāḥ kāryāḥ | tad āya yājñavalkyaḥ |
brahmakṣatriyaviṭśūdrā varṇās tv ādyās trayo dvijāḥ |
niṣekādiśmaśānāntās teṣāṃ vai mantrataḥ kriyāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.10)
garbhādhānādīnāṃ kālaviśeṣam āha sa eva |
garbhādhānādīnāṃ puṃsaḥ savanaṃ spandanāt purā |
ṣaṣṭhe ‘ṣṭame vā sīmanto māsy ete jātakarma ca ||
ahany ekādaśe nāma caturthe māsi niṣkramaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhe ‘nnaprāśanaṃ māsi cūḍā kāryā yathākulam || iti | (YDh 1.11–12)
rajodarśanadivasam ārabhya ṣoḍaśa divasā ṛtuḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
ṛtuḥ svābhāvikaḥ strīṇāṃ rātrayaḥ ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ |
caturbhir itare sārdham ahobhiḥ sadvigarhitaiḥ ||
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu ninditaikādaśī ca yā |
yugmāsu ca śeṣāḥ syuḥ praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ ||
yugmāsu putrā jāyante striyo ‘yugmāsu rātriṣu |
tasmād yugmāsu putrārthī saṃviśed ārtave striyam || (MDh 3.46–48)
[20] iti | rajodarśanam ārabhya catvāry ahāni sadvigarhitāni | yugmāsu samāsu | saṃviśet gacchet |
iti garbhādhānam
atha puṃsavanam | evam ṛtau garbhādhānaṃ kṛtvā garbhacalanāt purā puṃsavanaṃ kāryam | tad calanaṃ dvitīye vā tṛtīye vā bhavati | tad āha baijavāpaḥ: “māsi dvitīye vā tṛtīye vā purā spandane” iti | pāraskaro ‘pi: “māse dvitīye tṛtīye vā | tad ahaḥ puṃsā nakṣatreṇa candramā yujyeta” iti (PārGṛ 1.5.14.2–3) | puṃnakṣatrāṇi ca hastādīni jyotiḥśāstre prasiddhāni | anavalobhanam āśvalāyanagṛhyapariśiṣṭe ‘vagantavyam |
iti puṃsavanam
[21]
atha sīmantonnayanam | sīmantonnayanasya yājñavalkyoktakālād anye ‘pi kālā munibhiḥ darśitāḥ | tatra laugākṣiḥ: “tṛtīye garbhamāse sīmantonnayanaṃ kāryam” iti | āpastambo ‘pi: “prathame garbhe caturthe māsi” iti | baijavāpo ‘pi: “atha sīmantonnayanaṃ caturthe pañcame ṣaṣṭhe vāpi” iti | sāṅkhyāyanagṛhye ‘pi: “saptame māsi prathame garbhe sīmantonnayanam” iti | śaṅkho ‘pi: “garbhaspandane sīmantonnayanaṃ yāvad vā na prasavaḥ” iti | viśeṣāśravaṇāt sakṛd eva kartavyaṃ na pratigarbham | tathā ca hārītaḥ: “sakṛt saṃskṛtasaṃskārā sīmantena dvijastriyaḥ” iti | devalo ‘pi |
yā sakṛt saṃskṛtā nārī sarvagarbheṣu saṃskṛtā | iti |
garbhasaṃskārapakṣe tu pratigarbham āvartanīyam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
[22]        sīmantonnayanaṃ karma na strīsaṃskāra iṣyate |
kecid garbhasya saṃskārād garbhaṃ garbhaṃ prayuñjate ||
anayoḥ pakṣayor yathāgṛhyaṃ vyavasthā | akṛtasīmantāyāḥ prasave satyavrata āha |
strī yady akṛtasīmantā prasūyeta kathaṃcana |
gṛhītaputrā vidhivat punaḥ saṃskāram arhati || iti |
iti sīmantonnayanam
atha jātakarma | tatra jātakarmaṇaḥ kālo yājñavalkyena darśitaḥ: “māsy ete jātakarma ca” iti (YDh 1.11) | ete āgate jāta iti yāvat | viṣṇur api |
jātakarma tataḥ kuryāt putre jāte yathoditam | iti |
svagṛhye iti śeṣaḥ | tac ca snānānantaraṃ kāryam | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
jāte putre pituḥ snānaṃ sacailaṃ tu vidhīyate | iti |
jātakarma ca nābhivardhanāt prāg eva kāryam | tathā ca manuḥ |
prāṅ nābhivardhanāt puṃso jātakarma vidhīyate |
mantravat prāśanaṃ cāsya hiraṇyamadhusarpiṣām || (MDh 2.21)
[23] iti | vardhanaṃ chedanam | na cāśaucaśaṅkayā karmānadhikāraḥ iti vācyam, nābhicchedāt prāg āśaucābhāvāt | tad āha jaiminiḥ |
yāvan na chidyate nālaṃ tāvan nāpnoti sūtakam |
chinne nāle tataḥ paścāt sūtakaṃ tu vidhīyate || iti |
viṣṇudharmottare |
acchinnanābhyāṃ kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ vai putrajanmani |
āśaucoparame kāryam atha vā niyatātmabhiḥ || iti |
tac ca śrāddhaṃ hemadravyeṇa kāryam | tad āha vyāsaḥ |
dravyābhāve dvijābhāve pravāse putrajanmani |
hemaśrāddhaṃ prakurvīta yasya bhāryā rajasvalā || iti |
ādityapurāṇe pakvānnaniṣedho darśitaḥ |
jātaśrāddhe na dadyāt tu pakvānnaṃ brāhmaṇeṣv api |
yasmāc cāndrāyaṇāt śuddhis teṣāṃ bhavati nānyathā || iti |
tasmin dine yathāśakti dānaṃ kartavyam | tad uktaṃ ādipurāṇe |
devāś ca pitaraś caiva putre jāte dvijanmanām |
āyānti tasmāt tad ahaḥ puṇyaṃ pūjyaṃ ca sarvadā |
tatra dadyāt suvarṇaṃ tu bhūmiṃ gāṃ turagaṃ ratham || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi: [24] “sarveṣāṃ sakulyānāṃ dvipadacatuṣpadadhānyahiraṇyādi dadyāt” iti | etac cāśaucamadhye ‘pi kāryam |
āśauce tu samutpanne putrajanma yadā bhavet |
kartus tātkālikī śuddhiḥ pūrvāśaucena śuddhyati || iti |
iti jātakarma
atha nāmakaraṇam | tasya ca yājñavalkyoktakālād anye ‘pi kālā manunā darśitā |
nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ tu dvādaśyāṃ vāsya kārayet |
puṇye tithau muhūrte vā nakṣatre vā guṇānvite || iti | (MDh 2.30)
kārayed iti svārthiko ṇic,
tatas tu nāma kurvīta pitaiva daśame ‘hani | (ViPu 3.10.8)
iti viṣṇupurāṇavacanāt | yad vā pitur abhāve ayogyatve vānyena kārayet | tad āha śaṅkhaḥ: “kuladevatānakṣatrābhisaṃbandhaṃ pitā kuryād anyo vā kulavṛddhaḥ” iti | bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
[25]        nāmadheyaṃ daśamyāṃ ca kecid icchanti pārthiva |
dvādaśyām atha vā rātrau māse pūrṇe tathāpare ||
aṣṭādaśe ‘hani tathā vadanty anye manīṣiṇaḥ || iti |
gṛhyapariśiṣṭe ‘pi: “jananād daśarātre vyuṣṭe saṃvatsare ca nāmakaraṇam” iti | tatra svagṛhyānusāreṇa vyavasthā | nāmadheyasvarūpaṃ ca varṇabhedena darśayati manuḥ |
maṅgalyaṃ brāhmaṇasya syāt kṣatriyasya balānvitam |
vaiśyasya dhanasaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya tu jugupsitam ||
śarmavad brāhmaṇasya syād rājño rakṣāsamanvitam |
vaiśyasya puṣṭisaṃyuktaṃ śūdrasya preṣyasaṃyutam ||
strīṇāṃ sukhodyam akrūraṃ vispaṣṭārthaṃ manoramam |
maṅgalyaṃ dīrghavarṇāntam āśīrvādābhidhānavat || iti | (MDh 2.31–33)
maṅgalyādīni pūrvapadāni | śarmādīny uttarapadāni | tathā ca nāmāny evaṃvidhāni saṃpadyante – śrīśarmā, vikramapālaḥ, māṇikyaśreṣṭhī, hīnadāsaḥ ityādi | strīṇāṃ tu śrīdāsītyādi | sukhodyaṃ vadituṃ śakyam ity arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha baijavāpaḥ: “pitā nāma karoty ekākṣaraṃ dvyakṣaraṃ tryakṣaraṃ caturakṣaram aparimitaṃ vā ghoṣavad ādyantarantasthaṃ dīrghābhiniṣṭhānāntam” [26] iti | ayam arthaḥ – ghoṣavanti yāny akṣarāṇi vargatṛtīyacaturthāni tāny ādau kāryāṇi | antarasthā ya-ra-la-vā madhye kartavyāḥ | ante ‘bhiniṣṭhāno visarjanīyaḥ | tathā ca – bhadrapālo jātavedā ityādi nāma bhavati | yathoktanāmakaraṇasya phalam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “evaṃ kṛte nāmni śuci tatkulaṃ bhavati” iti |
iti nāmakaraṇam
atha niṣkramaṇam | tatra manuḥ |
caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt | iti | (MDh 2.34)
tatra kartavyam āha yamaḥ |
tatas tṛtīya kartavyaṃ māsi sūryasya darśanam |
caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ śuśoś candrasya darśanam || iti |
laugākṣir api: [27] “tṛtīye ‘rdhamāse darśanam ādityasya” iti | purāṇe ‘pi |
dvādaśe ‘hani rājendra śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ gṛhāt |
caturthe māsi kartavyaṃ tathānyeṣāṃ ca saṃmatam || iti |
atrāpi yathāsvaśākhaṃ vyavasthā |
iti niṣkramaṇam
athānnaprāśanam | tatra yamaḥ |
tato ‘nnaprāśanaṃ māsi ṣaṣṭhe kāryaṃ yathāvidhi |
aṣṭame vāpi kartavyaṃ yac ceṣṭaṃ maṅgalaṃ kula || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi: “saṃvatsare ‘nnaprāśanam ardhasaṃvartara ity eke” iti | laugākṣiḥ: “ṣaṣṭhe māsy annaprāśanaṃ jāteṣu danteṣu vā” iti | tatra viśeṣam āha mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
devatāpuratas tasya dhātryutsaṅgatasya ca |
alaṃkṛtasya dātavyam annaṃ pātre ca kāñcane ||
madhvājyakanakopetaṃ prāśayet pāyasaṃ tu tam |
kṛtaprāśam athotsaṅgād dhātrī bālaṃ samutsṛjet || iti |
prāśanānantaraṃ jīvikāparikṣā mārkaṇḍeyena darśitā |
[28]        agrato ‘tha pravinyasya śilpabhāṇḍāni sarvaśaḥ |
śastrāṇi caiva vastrāṇi tataḥ paśyet tu lakṣaṇam ||
prathamaṃ yat spṛśed bālas tato bhāṇḍaṃ svayaṃ tathā |
jīvikā tasya bālasya tenaiva tu bhaviṣyati || iti |
ity annaprāśanam
atha cūḍākaraṇam | tatra yamaḥ |
tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe cūḍākarma vidhīyate |
dvitīye vā tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śrutidarśanāt || iti |
baijavāpaḥ: “tṛtīye varṣe cūḍākaraṇam” iti | śaṅkho ‘pi: “tṛtīye varṣe cūḍākarma | pañcame vā” iti | laugākṣir api: “tṛtīyasya varṣasya bhūyiṣṭhe gate cūḍākaraṇam” iti | śaunako ‘pi: “tṛtīye varṣe caulaṃ yathākuladharmaṃ vā” iti | tatra ṛṣibhedena cūḍāniyamam āha laugākṣiḥ: “dakṣiṇataḥ kamuṃjā vāsiṣṭhānām | ubhayato ‘trikāśyapānām | [29] muṇḍā bhṛgavaḥ | pañcacūḍā aṅgirasaḥ | maṇḍanārthaṃ śikhino ‘nye | yathākuladharmaṃ vā” iti | kamuṃjā keśapaṅktiḥ | atra yathāsvaśākhaṃ vyavasthā | atra yathoktāḥ cūḍākaraṇāntā anupanītaviṣayāḥ atas tatpratisaṅgād anye ‘pi kecanānupanītadharmāḥ kathyante | tatra gautamaḥ: “prāg upanayanāt kāmacārakāmavādabhakṣaḥ” iti (GDh 2.1) | kāmacāra icchāgatiḥ | kāmavādo ‘ślīlādibhāṣaṇam | kāmabhakṣaḥ paryuṣitādibhakṣaṇam | viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
bhakṣyābhakṣye tathā peye vācyāvācye tathānṛte |
asmin kāle na doṣaḥ syāt sa yāvan nopanīyate || iti | (ViPu 3.10.9)
etac cābhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ mahāpātakahetudravyavyatiriktaviṣayam | ata eva smṛtyantaram |
syāt kāmacārabhakṣyoktir mahataḥ pātakād ṛte | iti |
yathā bhakṣyābhakṣyādiniyamo nāsti evam ācamanādikartavyānantaram api nāsti | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ:
[30]        na hy asya vidyate karma kiṃcid ā mauñjibandhanāt |
vṛttyā śūdrasamas tāvad yāvad vede na jāyate || iti | (VaDha 2.6)
gautamo ‘pi: “yathopapādmūtrapurīṣo bhavati | nāsyācamanakalpo vidyate na brahmābhivyāhared anyatra svadhāninayanāt” iti (GDh 2.4–9) |
iti cūḍākaraṇam
athākṣarābhyāsas tu kartavyaḥ | tad āha mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
prāpte tu pañcame varṣe hy aprasupte janārdane |
ṣaṣṭhīṃ pratipadaṃ caiva varjayitvā tathāṣṭamīm ||
riktāṃ pañcadaśīṃ caiva sauribhaumadinaṃ tathā |
evaṃ suniścite kāle vidyārambhaṃ tu kārayet ||
pūjayitvā hariṃ lakṣmīṃ devīṃ caiva sarasvatīm |
svavidyāsūtrakārāṃś ca svāṃ vidyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||
eteṣām eva devānāṃ nāmnā tu juhuyāt ghṛtam |
dakṣiṇābhir dvijendrāṇāṃ kartavyaṃ cāpi pūjanam ||
[31]        prāṅmukho gurur āsīno varuṇābhimukhaṃ śiśum |
adhyāpayet tu prathamaṃ dvijātībhiḥ supūjitaiḥ ||
tataḥ prabhṛty anadhyāyān varjanīyān vivarjayet |
aṣṭamīdvitayaṃ caiva pakṣānte ca dinatrayam || iti |
iti garbhādhānādicūḍāntasaṃskāraprakaraṇam
athopanayanam | tatra manuḥ |
garbhāṣṭame ‘bde kurvīta brāhmaṇasyopanāyanam |
garbhād ekādaśe rājño garbhāt tu dvādaśe viśaḥ ||
brahmavarcasakāmasya kāryaṃ viprasya pañcame |
rājño balārthinaḥ ṣaṣṭhe vaiśyasyārthārthino ‘ṣṭame ||
ā ṣoḍaśād brāhmaṇasya sāvitrī nātivartate |
ā dvāviṃśāt kṣatrabandhor ā caturviṃśater viśaḥ ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ trayo ‘py ete yathākālam asaṃskṛtāḥ |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavanty āryavigarhitāḥ ||
naitair apūtair vidhivad āpay api hi karhicit |
brāhmān yaunāṃś ca saṃbandhān ācaret brāhmaṇaḥ saha || iti | (MDh 2.36–40)
āpastambo ‘pi: [32] “saptame brahmavarcasakāmam aṣṭama āyuṣkāmaṃ navame tejaskāmaṃ daśame ‘nnādyakāmaṃ ekādaśa indriyakāmaṃ dvādaśe paśukāmam” iti (ĀpDh 1.1.1.20) | etac ca varṇatrayasya sādhāraṇam | varṇavyavasthayā kālaniyamam āha sa eva: “vasante brāhmaṇam upanayīta grīṣme rājanyaṃ śaradi vaiśyam” iti (ĀpDh 1.1.1.19) | varṇānupūrvyeṇopanayanasyetikartavyatām āha manuḥ |
kārṣṇarauravabāstāni carmāni brahmacāriṇḥ |
vasīrann ānupūrvyeṇa śāṇakṣaumāvikāni ca || iti |
kārṣṇādīni carmāṇi uttarīyāṇi | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ: “kṛṣṇarurubastājināny uttarīyāṇi” iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “kṛṣṇājinam uttarīyaṃ brāhmaṇasya | rauravaṃ rājanyasya | gavyaṃ bastājinaṃ vā vaiśyasya” iti (VaDh 11.61–63) | tathā ca pāraskaraḥ: “aiṇeyam ajinam uttarīyaṃ brāhmaṇasya | [33] rauravaṃ rājanyasya | ājaṃ gavyaṃ vā vaiśyasya | sarveṣāṃ vā gavyam” iti (PārGṛ 2.5.17–20) | śāṇādīny uttarīyāṇi | tatra āpastambaḥ: “vāsaḥ | śāṇakṣaumājināni | kāṣāyaṃ caike vastram upadiśanti brāhmaṇasya | māñjiṣṭhaṃ rājanyasya | hāridraṃ vaiśyasya” iti (ĀpDh 1.1.2.39–41, 3.1–2) | vasiṣṭhaḥ: “śuklam ahataṃ vāso brāhmaṇasya | kārpāsaṃ māñjiṣṭhaṃ kṣaumaṃ vā kṣatriyasya | hāridraṃ kauśeyaṃ vā vaiśyasya" iti (VaDh 11.64–66) | mekhalām āha manuḥ |
[34]        mauñjī trivṛt samā ślakṣṇā kāryā viprasya mekhalā |
kṣatriyasya tu maurvī jyā vaiśyasya śaṇatāntavī || iti | (MDh 2.42)
trivṛt triguṇā | yamo ‘pi |
viprasya mekhalā mauñjī jyā maurvī kṣatriyasya tu |
śāṇasūtrī tu vaiśyasya mekhalā dharmataḥ smṛtāḥ ||
etāsām apy abhāve tu kuśāśmantakabalvajaiḥ |
mekhalā trivṛtā kāryā granthinaikena vā trivṛt || iti |
manur api |
muñjābhāve tu kartavyāḥ kuśāśmantakabalvajaiḥ |
trivṛtā granthinaikena tribhiḥ pañcabhir eva vā || iti | (MDh 2.43)
daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇo bailvapālāśau kṣatriyo vāṭakhādirau |
pailavodumbarau vaiśyo daṇḍān arhanti dharmataḥ || iti | (MDh 2.45)
anukalpam āha yamaḥ |
eteṣām apy abhāve tu sarveṣāṃ sarvayajñiyāḥ | iti |
manur daṇḍaparimāṇam āha |
[35]        keśāntiko brāhmaṇasya daṇḍaḥ kāryaḥ pramāṇataḥ |
lalāṭasaṃmito rājñaḥ syat tu nāsāntiko viśaḥ || iti | (MDh 2.46)
gautamo ‘pi: “mūrdhalalāṭanāsāgrapramāṇāḥ” iti (GDh 1.28) | daṇḍalakṣaṇam āha manuḥ |
ṛjavas te sarve syur avraṇāḥ saumyadarśanāḥ |
anudvegakarā nṝṇāṃ satvaco nāgnidūṣitāḥ || iti | (MDh 2.47)
gautamo ‘pi: “apīḍitā yūpavaktrāḥ saśalkāḥ” iti (GDh 1.27) | yajñopavītam āha manuḥ |
kārpāsam upavītaṃ syād viprasyordhvavṛtaṃ trivṛt |
śaṇasūtramayaṃ rājño vaiśyasyāvikasūtrakam || iti | (MDh 2.44)
paiṭhīnasir api: “karpāsam upavītaṃ brāhmaṇasya | kṣaumeyaṃ rājanyasya | āvikaṃ vaiśyasya” [36] iti | uktopavītālābhe yathāsaṃbhavaṃ govālādikaṃ grāhyam | tad āha devalaḥ |
kārpāsakṣaumagovālaśaṇavalkatṛṇādikam |
sadā saṃbhavataḥ kāryam upavītaṃ dvijātibhiḥ || iti |
ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ: “api vā vāsasī yajñopavītārthaṃ kuryāt tadabhāve trivṛtā sūtreṇa” iti | tac ca navatantukaṃ kāryam | tad āha devalaḥ |
yajñopavītaṃ kurvīta sūtreṇa navatantukam | iti |
kātyāyanaḥ |
trivṛdūrdhvavṛtaṃ kāryaṃ tantutrayam adhovṛtam | iti | (KSm 1.1.2)
ūrdhvavṛtasya lakṣaṇam āha saṃgrahakāraḥ |
kareṇa dakṣiṇenordhvaṃ gatena triguṇīkṛtam |
valitaṃ mānavaiḥ sūtraṃ śāstra ūrdhvavṛtaṃ smṛtam || iti |
ūrdhvaṃ gatena dakṣiṇena kareṇa yad valitaṃ tad ūrdhvavṛtam ity arthaḥ | yajñopavītaprayogam āha devalaḥ |
grāmān niṣkramya saṃkhyāy ṣaṣṇavatyaṅgulīṣu tat |
tāvattriguṇitaṃ sūtraṃ prakṣālyābliṅgakais tribhiḥ ||
devāgāre ‘tha vā goṣṭhe nadyāṃ vānyatra vā śucau |
sāvitryā trivṛtaṃ kuryān navasūtraṃ tu tad bhavet ||
[37]        bilvāśvatthādiyajñiyavṛkṣasyānyatamasya tu |
badhnīyāt tat sajīvaṃ tu pitṛbhyo nama ity atha ||
vyāmaṃ nāvekṣitavyaṃ syāt pitṝṇāṃ tṛptidaṃ hi tat |
tris tāḍayet karatalaṃ devānāṃ tṛptidaṃ hi tat ||
savye mṛdaṃ gṛhītvāsmin sthāpayed bhūr iti bruvan |
patraṃ puṣpaṃ phalaṃ vāpi vyāhṛtibhiḥ pratikṣipet ||
abhimantryātha bhūr agniś ceti vṛttatraṃ tribhiḥ |
haribrahmeśvarebhyaś ca praṇamyaivadhāreyet || iti |
avadhāraṇamantras tu “yajñopavītam” ityādiḥ | granthiniyamam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
trivṛtaṃ copavītaṃ syāt tasyaiko granthir iṣyate || iti | (KSm 1.1.2)
yajñopavītaparimāṇam āha sa eva |
pṛṣṭhavaṃśe ca nābhyāṃ ca dhṛtaṃ yad vindate kaṭim |
tad dhāryam upavītaṃ syān nātilambaṃ na cocchritam || iti | (KSm 1.1.3)
devalo ‘pi |
[38]        stanād ūrdhvam adho nābhet kartavyaṃ kadācana | iti |
upavītasaṃkhyām āha bhṛguḥ |
upavītaṃ baṭor ekaṃ dve tathetarayoḥ smṛte |
ekam eva yatīnāṃ syād iti śāstrasya niścayaḥ || iti |
etac ca nityābhiprāyam, bahuṣu kāmaśravaṇāt | tad āha devalaḥ: “bahūni cāyuḥkāmasya” iti | etad upavītaṃ sadā dhāryam | tad āha bhṛguḥ |
sadopavītinā bhāvyaṃ sadā baddhaśikhena ca |
viśikho vyupavītaś ca yat karoti na tat kṛtam ||
mantrapūtaṃ sthitaṃ kāye yasya yajñopavītakam |
noddharec ca tataḥ prājño yadīcchec chreya ātmanaḥ ||
sakṛc coddharaṇāt tasya prāyaścittī bhaved dvijaḥ || iti |
upavīte viśeṣam āha devalaḥ |
sūtraṃ salomakaṃ cet syāt tataḥ kṛtvā vilomakam |
sāvitryā daśakṛtvo ‘dbhir mantritābhis tad ukṣayet ||
vicchinnaṃ vāpy adho yātaṃ bhuktvā nirmitam utsṛjet | iti |
yajñopavītādīnāṃ troṭanādau pratipattim āha manuḥ |
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
apsu prāsya vinaṣṭāni gṛhṇītānyāni mantravat || (MDh 2.64)
[39] iti | atha daṇḍādidhāraṇānantaram ādityopasthānaṃ kāryam | tathāha manuḥ |
pratigṛhyepsitaṃ daṇḍam upasthāya ca bhāskaram |
pradakṣiṇaṃ parītyāgniṃ cared bhaikṣyaṃ yathāvidhi || iti | (MDh 2.48)
daṇḍagrahaṇāntetikartavyatāyuktam upanayanaṃ prāpya gāyatrīmahāvākyārthabhūtaṃ bhāskaram upasthāya so ‘ham ity evaṃ jñātvā agniṃ paricarya bhaikṣyaṃ cared ity arthaḥ | agniparicaryā manunā darśitā |
dūrād āhṛtya samidhaḥ saṃnidadhyād vihāyasi |
sāyaṃ prātaś ca juhuyāt tābhir agnim atandritaḥ || iti | (MDh 2.186)
vihāyasi antarikṣe sthāpayen na tu bhūmāv ity arthaḥ | samidāharaṇe viśeṣam āha baijavāpaḥ: “purāstamayāt prāg udīcīṃ diśaṃ gatvā ahiṃsann araṇyāt samidham āharet | śuṣkā brahmavarcasakāma ārdrās tv annādyakāma ubhayor ubhayakāmaḥ” iti | samillakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nāṅguṣṭhād adhikā kāryā samit sthūlatayā kvacit |
na viyuktā tvacā caiva na sakīṭā na pāṭitā ||
prādeśān nādhikā nyūnā tathā na syād viśālikā |
na saparṇā nātiyāmā homeṣu tu vijānatā ||
[40]        viśīrṇā vimalā hrasvā vakrā susuṣirā kṛśā |
dīrghā sthūlā guṇair duṣṭā karmasiddhivināśikā || iti | (KSm 1.8.17–19)
saminniyama ukto vāyupurāṇe |
pālāśyaḥ samidhaḥ kāryāḥ khādiryas tadalābhataḥ |
śamīrohitakāśvatthās tadalābhe ‘rkavetasau || iti |
agnikāryākaraṇe pratyavāyam āha hārītaḥ |
purā jagrāha vai mṛtyur hiṃsayan brahmacāriṇam |
agnis taṃ mocayāmāsa tasmāt paricarec ca tam || iti | (HārSm 2.27)
bhikṣācaryāprakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
brāhmaṇeṣu cared bhaikṣyam anindyeṣv ātmavṛttaye |
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu bhavacchabdopalakṣitā |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ bhaikṣyacaryā yathākramam || iti | (YDh 1.29–30)
brāhmaṇeṣv iti svasvajātīyopalakṣaṇam | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśaś careyur bhaikṣyam anvaham |
sajātīyagṛheṣv eva sārvavarṇikam eva vā || iti |
sārvavarṇikatvam āpadviṣayam | ata eva bhaviṣyatpurāṇe darśitam |
[41]        caturvarṇaṃ cared bhaikṣyam alābhe kurunandana | iti |
āpady api na śūdrāt pakvaṃ gṛhṇīyāt | tad āha aṅgirāḥ |
āmam evādadītāntyād avṛttāv aikarātrikam |
āmaṃ pūyati saṃsāre dharmyaṃ tebhyaḥ pratiṣṭhitam ||
tasmād āmaṃ grahītavyaṃ śūdrād apy aṅgirābravīt | iti |
anāpadi svajātīyeṣv api praśasteṣv eva bhaikṣyam ācaret | tad āha manuḥ |
vedayajñair ahīnānāṃ praśastānāṃ svakarmasu |
brahmacāry āhared bhaikṣyaṃ gṛhebhyaḥ prayato ‘nvaham || iti | (MDh 2.183)
“ādimadhyāvasāneṣu” iti – ayam arthaḥ: bhikṣāpravartanāvākye varṇakrameṇa ādimadhyāvasāneṣu bhavacchabdaḥ prayojyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
bhavatpūrvaṃ cared bhaikṣyam upanīto dvijottamaḥ |
bhavanmadhyaṃ tu rājanyo vaiśyas tu bhavaduttaram || iti | (MDh 2.49)
ukteṣu keṣucid apavādam āha sa eva |
guroḥ kule na bhikṣeta na jñātikulabandhuṣu |
alābhe tv anyagehānāṃ pūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ parityajet ||
[42]        sarvaṃ vāpi cared grāmaṃ pūrvoktānām asaṃbhave |
niyamya prayato vācam abhiśastāṃs tu varjayet || iti | (MDh 2.184–85)
yat tu,
mātaraṃ vā svasāraṃ vā mātur vā bhaginīṃ nijām |
bhikṣeta bhikṣāṃ prathamaṃ yā cainaṃ na vimānayet || (MDh 2.50)
iti tad upanayanāṅgabhikṣāviṣayam, anyathā doṣaśravaṇāt | tad āha yamaḥ |
āhāramātrād adhikaṃ na kvacid bhaikṣyam āharet |
yujyate steyadoṣeṇa kāmato ‘dhikam āharan || iti |
tac ca bhaikṣyaṃ gurvanujñāpuraḥsaraṃ bhoktavyam | tad āhatur manu-yamau |
samāhṛtya tu tad bhaikṣyaṃ yāvad artham amāyayā |
nivedya gurave ‘śnīyād ācamya prāṅmukhaḥ śuciḥ || iti | (MDh 2.51)
gurvasaṃnidhau tadbhāryādibhyo nivadayet | tad āha gautamaḥ: [43] “nivedya gurave ‘nujñāto bhuñjīta | asaṃnidhau tadbhāryāputrasabrahmacāribhyaḥ” iti (GDh 2.35–36) | gurvanujñātaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ satkṛtya bhuñjīta | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṛtāgnikāryo bhuñjīta vāgyato gurvanujñayā |
āpośanakriyāpūrvaṃ satkṛtyānnam akutsayan || iti | (YDh 1.31)
satkāraś ca hārītena darśitaḥ: “bhaikṣyam avekṣitaṃ paryagnikṛtam ādityadarśitaṃ gurave niveditam anujñātam amṛtasaṃmitaṃ prāhuḥ | yad aśnāti brahmacārī brahmasiddhim avāpnoti” iti (HārSm 3.6–7) | gautamo ‘pi: “sāyaṃprātas tv annam abhipūjitam anindan bhuñjīta” iti (GDh 9.30) | ekānnaniṣesham āha manuḥ |
[44]        bhaikṣyeṇa vartayen nityaṃ naikānnādī bhaved vratī |
bhaikṣyeṇa vratino vṛttir upavāsasamā smṛtā ||
vratavad devadaivatye pitrye karmaṇy atharṣivat |
kāmam abhyarthito ‘śnīyāt vratam asya na lupyate || iti | (MDh 2.188–89)
akaraṇe pratyavāyam āha sa eva |
akṛtvā bhaikṣacaraṇam asamiddhya ca pāvakam |
anāturaḥ saptarātram avakīrṇivrataṃ caret || iti | (MDh 2.187)
upanītasya niyamam āha yamaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ vedaṃ mauñjīṃ ca raśanāṃ tathā |
dhārayed brahmacaryaṃ ca bhikṣānnāśī gurau vasan || iti |
vedo darbhamuṣṭiḥ | gurau gurugṛhe ity arthaḥ | yamaḥ |
mekhalām ajinaṃ daṇḍam upavītaṃ ca nityaśaḥ |
kaupīnaṃ kaṭisūtraṃ ca brahmacārī ca dhārayet || iti |
manuḥ |
agnīndhanaṃ bhaikṣyacaryām adhaḥśayyāṃ guror hitam |
ā samāvartanāt kuryāt kṛtopanayano dvijaḥ || iti | (MDh 2.108)
sumantur api |
[45]        brahmacaryaṃ tapo bhaikṣyaṃ saṃdhyayor agnikarma ca |
svādhyāyaṃ guruvṛttiś ca caryeyaṃ brahmacāriṇaḥ || iti |
guruvṛttiprakāram āha vyāsaḥ |
jaghanyaśāyī pūrvaṃ syād utthāyī guruveśmani |
yac ca śiṣyeṇa kartavyaṃ yac ca dāsena vā punaḥ ||
kṛtam ity eva tat sarvaṃ kṛtvā tiṣṭhet tu pārśvataḥ |
kiṃkaraḥ sarvakārī ca sarvakarmasu kovidaḥ ||
abhuktavati nāśnīyād apītavati no pibet |
na tiṣṭhati tathāsīta nāsupte prasvapet tathā || iti |
viśvāmitraḥ |
tadbhāryāputrayoś caiva vṛddhānāṃ dharmaśālinām |
śuśruṣā sarvadā kāryā praṇāmādibhir eva ca || iti |
varjyān āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
madhumāṃsāñjanocchiṣṭaśuktastrīprāṇihiṃsanam |
bhāskarālokanāślīlaparīvādādi varjayet || iti | (YDh 1.33)
manur api |
varjayen madhu māṃsaṃ ca gandhaṃ mālyaṃ rasān striyaḥ |
śuktāni yāni sarvāṇi prāṇināṃ caiva hiṃsanam ||
[46]        abhyaṅgam añjanaṃ cākṣṇor upānacchatradhāraṇam |
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ ca lobhaṃ ca nartanaṃ gītavādanam ||
dyūtaṃ ca janavādaṃ ca parīvādaṃ tathānṛtam |
strīṇāṃ ca prekṣaṇālambham upaghātaṃ parasya ca ||
ekaḥ śayīta sarvatra na retaḥ skandayet kvacit || iti | (MDh2.177–80)
yamaḥ |
khaṭvāsanaṃ ca śayanaṃ varjayed dantadhāvanam |
svaped ekaḥ kuśeṣv eva na retaḥ skandayet śuciḥ || iti |
kūrmapurāṇe |
nādarśaṃ vai samīkṣeta nācared dantadhāvanam |
gurūcchiṣṭaṃ bheṣajārthaṃ prayuñjīta na kāmataḥ || iti | KūPu 1.2.14.20–21)
āpastambo ‘pi: “pitur jyeṣṭhasya ca bhrātur ucchiṣṭaṃ bhoktavyam” iti (ĀpDh 1.1.4.11) | guruputrādyucchiṣṭaṃ tu na bhoktavyam | tad āha manuḥ |
[47]        utsādanaṃ ca gātrāṇāṃ snāpanocchiṣṭabhojane |
na kuryād guruputrasya pādayoś cāvanejanam ||
abhyañjanaṃ snāpanaṃ ca gātrotsādanam eva ca |
gurupatnyā na kāryāṇi keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam || iti | (MDh 2.209, 211)
brahmacaryakālāvadhim āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
prativedaṃ brahmacaryaṃ dvādaśābdāni pañca vā |
grahaṇāntikam ity eke keśāntaś caiva ṣoḍaśe || iti | (YDh 1.36)
keśāntaḥ godānākhyaṃ karma | tac ca ṣaḍaśe varṣe kāryam | tad āha manuḥ |
keśāntaḥ ṣaḍaśe varṣe brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
rājanyabandhor dvāviṃśe vaiśyasya dvyadhike tataḥ || iti | (MDh 2.65)
yamaḥ |
vased dvādaśa varṣāṇi caturviṃśatim eva vā |
ṣaṭtriṃśataṃ vā varṣāṇi prativedaṃ vrataṃ caret || iti |
etat trivedagrahaṇābhiprāyam | ata eva manuḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśadābdikaṃ caryaṃ gurau traivedikaṃ vratam |
tadardhikaṃ pādikaṃ vā grahaṇāntikam eva vā || iti | (MDh 3.1)
evamuktalakṣaṇo brahmacārī dvividhaḥ | upakurvāṇako [48] naiṣṭhikaś ca | upakurvānakasyoktā dharmāḥ | naiṣṭhikasyocyante | tatrāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
naiṣṭhiko brahmacārī tu vased ācāryasaṃnidhau |
tadabhāve ‘sya tanaye patnyāṃ vaiśvānare ‘pi vā || iti | (YDh 1.49)
manur api |
yadi tv ātyantiko vāso rocetāsya guroḥ kule |
yuktaḥ paricared enam ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt ||
ācārye tu khalu prete guruputre guṇānvite |
gurudāre sapiṇḍe vā guruvad vṛttim ācaret ||
eteṣv avidyamāneṣu sthānāsanavihāravān |
prayuñjāno ‘gniśuśrūṣāṃ sādhayed deham ātmanaḥ || iti | (MDh 2.243–48)
etac ca sadvṛttabrāhmaṇagurvādiviṣayam | anyathā doṣaḥ | tad uktaṃ tenaiva |
nābrāhaṇe gurau śiṣyo vāsam ātyantikaṃ vaset |
brāhmaṇe vānanūcāne kāṅkṣan gatim uttamām || (MDh 2.242)
[49] iti | vasiṣṭho ‘pi:
brahmacāry ācāryaṃ paricared ā śarīravimokṣaṇāt | ācārye ca prete ‘gniṃ paricaret | saṃyatavāk | caturthaṣaṣṭhāṣtamakālabhojī | bhaikṣyam ācaret | gurvadhīnaḥ | jaṭilaḥ śikhājaṭo vā | guruṃ gacchantam anugacchann āsīnaṃ cet tiṣṭhan śayānaṃ ced āsīna upāsīta | āhūtādhyāyī | sarvaṃ labdhaṃ nivedya tadanujñayā buñjīta | khaṭvāśayanadantadhāvanaprakṣālanāñjanābhyañjanopānacchatravarjī | sthānāsanaśīlas trir ahno ‘bhyupeyād apaḥ — iti (VaDh 7.4–17) |
ahani triṣavaṇasvnāyī syād ity arthaḥ | agniparicaryā hārīta-śaṅkhalikhita-yamair nirūpitā:
yajñiyāḥ samidha āhṛtya saṃmārjanopalepanodvbodhanasamūhanasamindhana-paryagnikaraṇaparikramaṇopasthāna[50]homastotranamaskārādibhir agniṃ paricaret | nāgnim adhitiṣṭhet | na padbhyāṃ karṣet | na mukhenopadhamet | nāpaś cāgniṃ ca yugapad dhārayet | nājīrṇabhukto nocchiṣṭo vābhyādadhyāt | vividhair havirviśeṣair āgneyair ahar ahar agniṃ samindhet | āmantrya gacchet | āhṛtya nivedayet tanmanāḥ | śarīroparamānte brahmaṇaḥ sāyujyaṃ gacchati – iti (HārSm 3.5–14)
evaṃ kurvataḥ phalam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
anena vidhinā dehaṃ sādayan vijitendriyaḥ |
brahmalokam avāpnoti na ceha jāyate punaḥ || iti | (YDh 1.50)
manur api |
ā samāpteḥ śarīrasya yas tu śuśrūṣate gurum |
sa gacchaty añjasā vipro brahmaṇaḥ sadma śāśvatam || iti | (MDh 2.244)
nanu – naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryāṅgīkāre gārhasthyaṃ nirviṣayaṃ syāt |
tan na, gārhasthyasya rāgiviṣayatvāt | tad āha jābāliḥ: [51] “yadi gṛham eva kāmayet tadā yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhuyāt” iti |
atra kecit naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryaṃ kubjādiviṣayaṃ manvānā gārhasthyasya taditaraviṣayatām āhuḥ | udāharanti ca tatra viṣṇuvacanam |
kubjavāmanajātyandhaklībapaṅgvārttarogiṇām |
vratacaryā bhavet teṣāṃ yāvajjīvam asaṃśayam || iti |
tan na, naiṣṭhikabrahmacaryasya kubjādiṣv eva niyatatve samarthaṃ pratyaicchikatvam ucyamānaṃ virudhyeta | aicchikatvaṃ ca vasiṣṭhena darśitam: “catvāra āśramāḥ brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivrājakāḥ | teṣāṃ vedam adhītya vedau vedān vāviśīrṇabrahmacaryo yam icchet tam āvaset” iti (VaDh 7.1–3) | bhaviṣyatpurāṇe ‘pi |
gārhasthyam icchan bhūpāla kuryād dāraparigraham |
brahmacaryeṇa vā kālaṃ nayet saṃkalpapūrvakam ||
vaikhānaso vāpi bhavet parivrāḍ atha vecchayā |
[52] iti | tasmāt rāgiviṣayatvenaiva gārhasthyaṃ vyavasthāpanīyam iti |
iti brahmacāriprakaraṇam
atha gṛhasthāśramaṃ nirūpayituṃ tadadhikārahetu snānam ādau nirūpyate | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
gurave tu varaṃ datvā snāyīta tadanujñayā |
vedaṃ vratāni vā pāraṃ nītvā hy ubhayam eva vā || iti | (YDh 1.51)
tatra dātavyo varo manunā darśitaḥ |
na pūrvaṃ gurave kiṃcid upakurvīta dharmavit |
snāsyaṃs tu guruṇājñaptaḥ śaktyā gurvartham āharet ||
kṣetraṃ hiraṇyaṃ gām aśvaṃ chatropanaham āsanam |
dhānyaṃ vāsāṃsi śākaṃ vā gurave prītim āvahet || iti | (MDh 2.245–46)
ayaṃ ca varo guruprītyartho na tu vidyāniṣkrayārthaḥ, vedavidyārhasya mūlyasyāsaṃbhavāt | tathā ca chadogaśrutiḥ: “yady apy asmā imām adbhiḥ parigṛhītāṃ dhanasya pūrṇāṃ dadyād etad eva tato bhūyaḥ” iti (ChU 3.11.6) | tāpanīyaśrutir api [53]: “saptadvīpavatī bhūmir dakṣiṇārthaṃ na kalpate” iti (NṛUTā 5.2) | hārītasmṛtir api |
ekam apy akṣaraṃ yas tu guruḥ śiṣye nivedayet |
pṛthivyāṃ nāsti tad dravyaṃ yad datvā tv anṛṇī bhavet || iti | (HārSm 2.21)
“vā vedaṃ vratāni” ity anena snātakatraividhyaṃ darśitam | tatra vedamātraparisamāpaka ekaḥ | vratamātraparisamāpako dvitīyaḥ | ubhayaparisamāpakas tṛtīyaḥ | vrataśabdenātra gṛhyaprasiddhāny upanayanavratasāvitrīvratavedavratāni vivakṣitāni | snātakatraividhyaṃ hārītenoktam: “trayaḥ snātakā bhavanti | vidyāsnātako vratasnātako vidyāvratasnātakaḥ” iti (HśrSm 4.1–2) | vedaṃ pāraṃ nītvety atrārthāvagatir api vivakṣitā | ata eva kūrmapurāṇe |
[54]        vedaṃ vedau tathā vedān vedān vā caturo dvijaḥ |
adhītya cādhigamyārthaṃ tataḥ snāyād dvijottamāḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.15.1)
snānaprakāraś ca gṛhye prasiddhaḥ | snātakadharmāḥ kūrmapurāṇe darśitāḥ |
yajñopavītadvitayaṃ sodakaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |
chatraṃ coṣṇīṣam amalaṃ pāduke cāpy upānahau |
raukme ca kuṇḍale vedaṃ kṛttakeśanakhaḥ śuciḥ ||
svādhyāye nityayuktaḥ syād bahirmālyaṃ ca dhārayet ||
śuklāmbaradharo nityaṃ sugandhaḥ priyadarśanaḥ |
na jīrṇamalavadvāsā bhavet tu vibhave sati ||
na raktam ulbaṇaṃ cānyadhṛtaṃ vāso na kanthikām || iti | (KūPu 1.2.15.3–7)
iti snātakaprakaraṇam
atha vivāhaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
guruṇānumataḥ snātvā samāvṛtto yathāvidhi |
udvaheta dvijo bhāryāṃ savarṇāṃ lakṣaṇānvitām || iti | (MDh 3.4)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
[55]        aviplutabrahmacaryo lakṣaṇyāṃ striyam udvahet |
ananyapūrvikāṃ kāntām asapiṇḍāṃ yavīyasīm ||
arogiṇīṃ bhrātṛmatīm asamānārṣagotrajām || iti | (YDh 1.52–53)
lakṣaṇyāṃ bāhyābhyantaralakṣaṇayuktām | bāhyāni lakṣaṇāni manunā darśitāni |
avyaṅgāṅgīṃ saumyanāmnīṃ haṃsavāraṇagāminīm |
tanulomakeśadaśanāṃ mṛdvaṅgīm udvahet striyam || iti | (MDh 3.10)
varjyām āha sa eva |
nodvahet kapilāṃ kanyāṃ nādhikāṅgīṃ na rogiṇīm |
nālomikāṃ nātilomāṃ na vācālāṃ na piṅgalām ||
narkṣavṛkṣanadīnāmnīṃ nāntyaparvatanāmikām |
na pakṣyahipreṣyanāmnīṃ na ca bhīṣaṇanāmikām || iti | (MDh 3.8–9)
kapilā raktataṇḍulavarṇā | piṅgalā agnivarṇā | antyeti mlecchanāmnī | viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
na śmaśruvyañjanavatī na caiva puruṣākṛtim |
na ghargharasvarāṃ kṣāmāṃ tathā kākasvarāṃ na ca ||
nānibaddhekṣaṇāṃ tadvad vṛttākṣīṃ nodvahed budhaḥ |
yasyāś ca romaśe jaṅghe gulphau yasyās tathonnatau
[56]        gaṇḍayoḥ kūpakau yasyā hasantyās tāṃ ca nodvahet |
nātirūkṣacchaviṃ pāṇḍukarajām aruṇekṣaṇām ||
āpīnahastapādāṃ ca na kanyām udvahed budhaḥ |
na vāmanāṃ nātidīrghāṃ nodvahet saṅgatabhruvam ||
na cāticchidradaśanāṃ na karālamukhīṃ naraḥ || iti | (ViPu 3.10.18–22)
āntarāṇi tu lakṣaṇāny āśvalāyanagṛhye ‘bhihitāni |
durvijñeyāni lakṣaṇānīti | aṣṭau piṇḍān kṛtvā – ṛtam agre prathamaṃ jajña ṛte satyaṃ pratiṣṭhitaṃ yad iyaṃ kumāry abhijātā tad iyam iha pratipadyatāṃ yat satyaṃ tad dṛśyatām – iti piṇḍān abhimantrya kumārīṃ brūyād eṣām ekaṃ gṛhāṇeti | kṣetrāc ced ubhayataḥ sasyād gṛhṇīyād annavaty asyāḥ prajā bhaviṣyatīti vidyād | goṣṭhāt paśumatī | vedipurīṣād brahmavarcasvinī | avidāsino hradāt [57] sarvasaṃpannā | devanāt kitavī | catuṣpathād vipravrājinī | īraṇād adhanyā | śmaśānāt patighnī” iti (ĀśGṛ 1.5.4–6)
vipravrājinī vividhaṃ prakarṣeṇa vrajatīti vipravrājinī svairiṇīty arthaḥ | ananyapūrvikām iti dānenopabhogena vā puruṣāntarāgṛhītām | anena punarbhūr vyāvartyate | ata eva kāśyapaḥ |
sapta paunarbhavāḥ kanyā varjanīyāḥ kulādhamāḥ |
vācā dattā manodattā kṛtakautukamaṅgalāḥ ||
udakasparśatā yā ca yā ca pāṇigṛhītikā |
agniṃ parigatā yā ca punarbhūprasavā ca yā ||
ity etāḥ kāśyapenoktā dahanti kulam agnivat || iti |
baudhāyanaḥ: “vāgdattā manodattāgniṃ parigatā saptamaṃ padaṃ nītā bhuktā gṛhītagarbhā prasūtā ceti saptavidhā punarbhūḥ tāṃ gṛhītvā na prajāṃ na dharmaṃ vindet” iti | nārado ‘pi |
kanyaivākṣatayonir yā pāṇigrahaṇadūṣitā |
punarbhūḥ prathamā proktā punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi || iti | NSm 12.46)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
[58]        akṣatā ca kṣatā caiva punarbhūḥ saṃskṛtā punaḥ | iti | (YDh 1.67)
kāntāṃ kamanīyāṃ udvoḍhur manonayanānandakāriṇīm | ata eva āpastambaḥ: “yasyāṃ manaś cakṣuṣor nirbandhas tasyām ṛddhiḥ” iti (ĀpDh 1.3.20) | asapiṇḍām iti – samāna ekaḥ piṇḍo yasyāḥ sā sapiṇḍā, na sapiṇḍā asapiṇḍā, tām | sapiṇḍatā ca saptamapuruṣaparyavasāyinī | tatraikaḥ piṇḍadātā trayaḥ piṇḍabhājaḥ pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahāḥ | trayo lepabhājaḥ vṛddhaprapitāmahādayaḥ | tathā ca matsyapurāṇe |
lepabhājaś caturthādyāḥ pitrādyāḥ piṇḍabhāginaḥ |
piṇḍadaḥ saptamas teṣāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapauruṣam || iti | (MatPu 18.29)
mārkaṇḍeyo ‘pi |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
piṇḍasaṃbandhino hy ete vijñeyāḥ puruṣās trayaḥ ||
lepasaṃbandhinaś cānye pitāmahapitāmahāt |
prabhṛtyuktās trayas teṣāṃ yajamānas tu saptamaḥ |
ity eṣa munibhiḥ proktaḥ saṃbadhaḥ sāptapauruṣaḥ || iti | (MārPu 31.3–5)
etad uktaṃ bhavati – saptānāṃ puruṣāṇām ekapiṇḍakriyānupraveśaḥ sāpiṇḍyahetuḥ | tathā ca devadattasya svakīyaiḥ pitrādibhiḥ [59] ṣaḍbhiḥ saha sāpiṇḍyam | tathā putrādibhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ saha sāpiṇḍyam – iti |
nanu – evaṃ sati bhrātṛpitṛvyādibhiḥ saha sāpiṇḍyaṃ na syāt, parigaṇiteṣv anantarbhāvāt |
maivam, uddeśyadevataikyena kriyaikyasyātra vivakṣitatvāt | devadattakartṛkakriyāyāṃ ye devatātvenānupraviśanti teṣāṃ madhye yaḥ ko ‘pi bhrātṛpitṛvyakartṛkriyāyām apy anupraviśatīty asti taiḥ saha sāpiṇḍyam | evaṃ bhāryāṇām api bhartṛkartṛkapiṇḍadānakriyāyāṃ sahakartṛkatvāt sāpiṇḍyam iti | tad idaṃ nirvāpyasāpiṇḍyam |
apare punar anyathā sāpiṇḍyam āhuḥ | tathā hi – samāna ekaḥ piṇḍo dehāvayavo yeṣāṃ te sapiṇḍāḥ | tatra putrasya sākṣāt pitṛdehāvayavānvayena pitrā saha sāpiṇḍyam | tathā pitāmahādibhir api pitṛdvāreṇa taccharīrāvayavānvayāt | sākṣānmātṛśarīrāvayavānvayena mātrā | mātāmahādibhir api mātṛdvāreṇa taccharīrāvayavānvayāt | tathā pitṛvyapitṛṣvasrādibhir api pitāmahadehāvayavānvayāt | tathā mātṛṣvasṛmātulādibhiḥ saha mātāmahadehānvayāt | patnyā saha ekaśarīrārambhakatayā patyuḥ | evaṃ bhrātṛbhāryāṇām apy ekaśarīrārambhakaiḥ svasvapatibhiḥ sahaikaśarīrārambhakatvena | evaṃ tatra tatra sākṣāt paramparayā vā ekaśarīrāvayavānvayena sāpiṇḍyaṃ yojanīyam |
uktaṃ dvividhaṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ yasyā nāsti seyam asapiṇḍā tām udvahet |
nanu – evaṃ sati na kvāpy udvāhaḥ saṃbhavet, sarvatra sāpiṇḍyasya kathaṃcid yojayituṃ śakyatvāt | vidhātṛśarīrānuvṛtter duṣpariharatvāt, [60] “bahu syāṃ prajāyeya” (ChU 6.2.3) iti śruteḥ |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, aviśeṣeṇa prāptasya sāpiṇḍyasya saptasu pañcasu ca puruṣeṣu saṅkucitatvena tadūrdhvaṃ sāpiṇḍyanivṛtteḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “piṇḍanivṛttiḥ saptame pañcame vā” iti (GDh 14.13) | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
pañcamāt saptamād ūrdhvaṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛtas tathā | iti | (YDh 1.53)
mātṛpakṣe pañcamāt pitṛpakṣe saptamāt puruṣād ūrdhvaṃ “sāpiṇḍyaṃ nivarate” ity adhyāhṛtya yojanīyam,
sapiṇḍatā to puruṣe saptame vinivartate | (MDh 5.60)
iti manusmaraṇāt | etad uktaṃ bhavati | pitṛpakṣe kūṭastham ārabhya tatputrādigaṇanāyāṃ saptamād ūrdhvaṃ varavadhvor vivāho na duṣyati | mātṛpakṣe ca kūṭastham ārabhya tatputrādiparigaṇanāyāṃ varavadhvor mātā cet pañcamī bhavati tadā tayoḥ sāpiṇḍyanivṛtter vivāho na doṣāyeti | yat tu viṣṇupurāṇavacanam,
[61]        pañcamīṃ mātṛpakṣāt tu pitṛpakṣāt tu saptamīm |
gṛhastha udvahet kanyāṃ nyāyyena vidhinā nṛpa || iti | (ViPu 3.11.23)
tatra saptamīṃ pañcamīm atītyety adhyāhāryam, anyathā “pañcamāt saptamād ūrdhvam” (YDh 1.53) iti vacanavirodhāt,
pañcame saptame caiva yeṣāṃ vaivāhikī kriyā |
kriyāparā api hi te sarvataḥ śūdratāṃ gatāḥ ||
iti marīcivacanavirodhāc ca | yady api paiṭhīnasinā kalpadvayam uktam: “pañcamīṃ mātṛtaḥ pariharet saptamīṃ pitṛtaḥ | trīn mātṛtaḥ pañca pitṛto vā” iti | śaṅkha āha |
yady ekajātā bahavaḥ pṛthakkṣetrāḥ pṛthagdhanāḥ |
ekapiṇḍāḥ pṛthakśaucāḥ piṇḍas tv āvartate triṣu || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – yeṣām ekaḥ pitā mātaro bhinnajātīyās te mātṛbhedād asamānajātīyāḥ tathāpi pitraikyād asti teṣāṃ sāpiṇḍyam | tac ca triṣu puruṣeṣv atīteṣu nivartate iti |
nanu – evaṃ sati pitṛpakṣe ‘pi tribhiḥ puruṣaiḥ sāpiṇḍyanivṛtteḥ “pañca pitṛto vā” iti vacanaṃ virudhyeta | evaṃ tarhi [62] “trīn mātṛtaḥ pañca pitṛto vā” iti paiṭhīnasivacanaṃ sajātīyeṣv evārvāṅniṣedhaparam | anukalpo vāstu | “mātṛtaḥ pitṛtas tathā” (YDh 1.53) iti pitṛśabdena bījino ‘pi saṃgrahaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “ūrdhvaṃ saptamāt pitṛbandhubhyaḥ | bījinaś ca | mātṛbandhubhyaḥ pañcamāt” iti (GDh 4.3–5) | yo hi niyogāt putram utpādayati sa bījī | pitṛmātṛbāndhavāḥ smṛtyantare |
pituḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrāḥ pitur mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
pitur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyāḥ pitṛbāndhavāḥ ||
mātuḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrā mātur mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
mātur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā mātṛbāndhavāḥ || iti |
nanu – asapiṇḍām iti na vaktavyam | vakṣyamāṇena “asamānārṣagotrajām” ity anenaiva sapiṇḍāyā vivāhaniṣedhasiddheḥ |
satyam, tathāpi mātur aspiṇḍā bhavati saivodvāhakarmaṇi praśasteti vaktum | tathā ca manuḥ |
asapiṇḍā ca yā mātur asagotrā ca yā pituḥ |
sā praśastā dvijātīnāṃ dārakarmaṇi maithune || (MDh 3.5)
[63] iti | yā mātur asapiṇḍā asagotrā ca, yā pitur asagotrā cakārād asapiṇḍā ca sā maithune mithunasādhye dārakarmaṇi dvijātīnāṃ praśastā pariṇeyety arthaḥ |
nanu – atra mātṛgrahaṇam anarthakam | pitṛgotrasāpiṇḍyaniṣedhenaiva mātṛgotrasāpiṇḍyaniṣedhasiddheḥ | tasyāḥ pṛthak piṇḍagotrayor abhāvāt,
ekatvaṃ sā gatā bhartuḥ piṇḍe gotre ca sūtake |
svagotrād bhraśyate nārī vivāhāt saptame pade ||
iti vacanāt |
maivam, gāndharvādivivāheṣu kanyāpradānābhāvena pitṛgotrasāpiṇḍyayor anivṛtteḥ | tathā ca mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇam |
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu yā tūḍhā kanyakā bhavet |
bhartṛgotreṇa kartavyā tasyāḥ piṇḍodakakriyā ||
gāndharvādivivāheṣu pitṛgotreṇa dharmavat | iti | (GaPu 2.26.21–22)
etena mātulasutāvivāhaviṣaye vivādo ‘pi parāstaḥ | tathā hi – tanniṣedhavacanāni gāndharvādivivāhoḍhaviṣayāṇi | tatra sāpiṇḍyanivṛtter abhāvāt | tadanugrāhakaśrutismṛti-sadācārāt na brāhmādivivāhoḍhajaviṣayāṇi | tatra sāpiṇḍyanivṛtteḥ | tāni ca niṣedhavacanāni yathā | tatra śātātapaḥ |
mātulasya sutām ūḍhvā mātṛgotrāṃ tathaiva ca |
samānapravarāṃ caiva dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
paiṭhīnasir api: [64] “pitṛmātṛṣvasṛduhitaro mātulasutāś ca dharmatas tā bhaginyaḥ | tā varjayed iti vijñāyate” iti | sumantur api: “pitṛpatnyaḥ sarvā mātaraḥ | tadbhrātaro mātulāḥ | tadbhaginyo mātṛṣvasāraḥ | tadduhitaraś ca bhaginyaḥ | tadapatyāni bhāgineyāni | anyathā saṅkarakāriṇyaḥ” iti | vyāsaḥ |
mātuḥ sapiṇḍā yatnena varjanīyā dvijātibhiḥ | iti |
nanu – aviśeṣeṇa pravṛttānām eṣāṃ vacanānāṃ kathaṃ viśeṣaviṣayatā |
viśeṣavacanabalād iti brūmaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
paitṛṣavaseyīṃ bhaginīṃ svasrīyāṃ mātur eva ca |
mātuś ca bhrātur āptasya gatvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
etās tisras tu bhāryārthe nopayacchetu tu buddhimān || iti | (MDh 11.172–72)
bhaginīpadaṃ paitṛṣvaseyādiviśeṣaṇam | āptasyeti mātur bhrātṛviśeṣaṇam | tatra sutām ity adhyāhāraḥ | āptasya saṃnikṛṣṭasya sapiṇḍasya gāndharvādivivāhoḍhāyāḥ mātur bhrātur ity arthaḥ | paitṛṣvasreyīm ity atrāpy anivṛttasāpiṇḍyā gāndharvādinoḍhā pitṛṣvasā vivakṣitā | tathā ca sati tadduhitur bhaginīti viśeṣaṇaṃ sārthakam | brāhmādivivāheṣu sāpiṇḍyanivṛtteḥ [65] bhaginīpadaṃ nānviyāt | ayam eva nyāyo mātṛṣvsrīyāyām api yojanīyaḥ | tasmād bhaginyāptapadopetamanuvacanabalād aviśeṣe niṣedho viśeṣaviṣaya evopasaṃhriyate |
nanu – brāhmādivivāhaviṣaye mātulasutāyā iva mātṛṣvasṛsutāyā api vivāhaḥ prāpnuyāt |
tan na, śiṣṭagarhitatvena tatra niṣedhasmṛtikalpanāt | śiṣṭagarhitasyānupādeyatvaṃ yājñavalkya āha |
asvargyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ dharmam apy ācaren na tu | iti | (YDh 1.156)
yady api mātulasutāpariṇayanam udīcyaśiṣṭagarhitaṃ tathāpi dākṣiṇātyaśiṣṭair ācaritatvena nāvigīto ‘yam udīcyānām ācāraḥ | na ca dākṣiṇātyānāṃ rāgamūlatvaṃ śaṅkanīyam | vidhiniṣedhaparīkṣakair eva tadvivāhakaraṇāt | mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāvivāhas tu avigītena śiṣṭācāreṇa garhitaḥ | mātulasutāvivāhasyānugrāhakāḥ śrutyādayaḥ | tatra mantravargaḥ |
āyāhīndra pathibhir īḷitebhir yajñam imaṃ no bhāgadheyaṃ juṣasva |
tṛptāṃ jahur mātulasyeva yoṣā bhāgas te paitṛṣvaseyī vapām iva || iti | (RV 7.4.3.22.6)
ayam arthaḥ – he indra pathibhir īḍitebhiḥ stutaiḥ saha [66] no ‘smākam imaṃ yajñam āyāhi | āgatya ca asmādibhir dīyamānaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ juṣasva | tṛptām ājyādinā saṃskṛtāṃ vapāṃ tvām uddiśya jahuḥ tyaktavantaḥ | tatra dṛṣṭāntadvayam | yathā mātulasya yoṣā duhitā bhāgineyasya bhāgaḥ bhajanīyā bhāgineyena pariṇetuṃ yogyā, yathā ca paitṛṣvaseyī pautrasya bhāgaḥ tathāsya te tava bhāgo vapākhyaḥ iti | vājasaneyake ‘pi: “tasmād vā samānād eva puruṣās attā cādyaś ca jāyate uta tṛtīye saṃgacchāvahai caturthe saṃgacchavahai” iti | samānād ekasmāt puruṣāt attā bhoktā adyaś ca bhogyaḥ dvāv apy utpadyete | tau na mithaḥ saṃkalpayataḥ kūṭastham ārabhya tṛtīye caturthe vā puruṣe saṃgacchāvahai vivahāvahai ity arthaḥ |
yady apy ayam arthavādaḥ tathāpi mānāntaravirodhābhāvāt svārthe pramāṇam, virodhivacanānām abhāvāt, mātṛsapiṇḍāviṣayatvasya varṇitatvāt ca | tasmād aviruddhārthavādenānūditatvād uparidhāraṇavad vidhiḥ kalpayituṃ śakyate | tathā hi pretāgnihotre (PMS Vārt. 3.4.15) śrūyate: “adhastāt samidhaṃ dhārayann anudravet upari hi devebhyo yajñaṃ dhārayati” iti | tatra paitṛkasya haviṣo ‘dhastāt samantrakaṃ samiddhāraṇaṃ vidhāya tadvākyaśeṣeṇa samidho haviruparidhāraṇṃ daive karmaṇi śrutaṃ tat kim arthavādaḥ, uta vidhir ayam, ity saṃśayaḥ | [67] tatrādhodhāraṇavidhis tāvakatvena tadekavākyatālābhād arthavāda iti – pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | prasiddhaṃ hy artham anūdya tena stutir yuktā | uparidhāraṇaṃ tu na kvāpi prasiddham | atas tāvakatvāyogād vākyabhedam abhyupagamyāpy apūrvārthatvād vidhiḥ kalpitaḥ |
evaṃ “tṛtīye puruṣe saṃgacchāvahai” ityādāv api apūrvārthatvena mātulasutāṃ vivahet – iti vidhiḥ kalpyate | tasmāc chrutāv anugrahīto ‘yam vivāhaḥ | smṛtayas tu brāhmaḍiṣu sāpiṇḍyanirākaraṇena mātulasutāvivāhaprāpakatayā darśitāḥ | śiṣṭācāraś ca dākṣiṇyātyānām avigīta udāhṛtaḥ |
kecit tu – āsurādiṣv api deśaviśeṣeṇa mātulasutāvivāho dharmya – iti manyante | udāharanti ca vacanāni | tatra baudhāyanaḥ |
pañcadhā vipratipattir dakṣiṇataḥ | anupanītena bhāryayā ca saha bhojanaṃ paryuṣitabhojanaṃ mātuladuhitṛpitṛṣvasṛduhitṛpariṇayanam iti | tathottarata ūrṇāvikrayaḥ sīdhupānam ubhayatodadbhir vyavahāraḥ āyudhīyakaṃ samudrayānam iti | itarad itarasmin kurvan duṣyati itara itarasmin | tatra tatra deśaprāmāṇyam eva syāt | (BDh 1.1.2.1–6)
[68] iti | itaro dākṣiṇātya itarasmin uttaradeśe mātulasaṃbandhaṃ kurvan duṣyati | na svadeśe | tathetara udīcya itarasmin dakṣiṇadeśe sīdhupānādikaṃ kurvan duṣyati | na svadeśe | kutaḥ | deśaprāmāṇyāt | deśanibandhanatvād ācāraprāmāṇyasyety arthaḥ | tathā ca devalaḥ |
yasmin deśe ya ācāro nyāyadṛṣṭaḥ sukalpitaḥ |
sa tasminn eva kartavyo na tu deśāntare smṛtaḥ || iti |
bhṛguḥ |
yasmin deśe pure grāme traividye nagare ‘pi vā |
yo yatra vihito dharmas taṃ dharmaṃ na vicālayet || iti |
nanu – śiṣṭācāraprāmāṇye svaduhitṛvivāho ‘pi prasajyeta, prajāpatinā tathācaraṇāt | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “prajāpatir vai svāṃ duhitaram abhyadhyāyat” iti |
maivam, “na devacaritaṃ caret” iti nyāyāt | ata eva baudhāyanaḥ |
anuṣiṣṭhaṃ tu yad devair munibhir yad anuṣṭhitam |
nānuṣṭheyaṃ manuṣyais tad uktaṃ karma samācaret || (BDh 1.1.2.12)
[69] iti | tad evaṃ pūrvoktabrāhmādivivāhavyavasthayā deśabhedaviṣayavyavasthayā ca mātulasutāvivāho “asapiṇḍām” ityādiśāstrād eva siddhaḥ | tatra vayonyūnatāyā iyattām āha manuḥ |
triṃśadvarṣodvahet kanyāṃ hṛdyāṃ dvādaśavārṣikīm |
tryaṣṭavarṣo ‘ṣṭavarṣāṃ vā dharme sīdati satvaraḥ || iti | (MDh 9.94)
bṛhaspatir api |
triṃśadvarṣo daśābdāṃ tu bhāryāṃ vindeta nagnikām |
ekaviṃśativarṣo vā saptavarṣām avāpnuyāt || iti |
viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
varṣair ekaguṇāṃ bhāryām udvahet triguṇaḥ svayam | iti | (ViPu 3.10.16)
arogiṇīm acikitsyarājayakṣmādirogarahitām | bhrātṛmatīṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā bhrātā yasyāḥ sā bhrātṛmatī | anena putrikāśaṅkā vyudasyate | ata eva manuḥ |
yasyās tu na bhaved bhrātā na vijñāyeta vā pitā |
nopayaccheta tāṃ prājñaḥ putrikādharmaśaṅkayā || iti | (MDh 3.11)
yasyāḥ pitā putrikākaraṇābhiprāyavān vā na vā iti na vijñāyate tāṃ nopayacchet | yatra tu naiṣā śaṅkā tām [70] abhrātṛkām apy upayacched ity abhiprāyaḥ | “na vijñāyeta vā pitā” ity ukteḥ vareṇa saha saṃpratipattiṃ vināpi pituḥ saṃkalpamātreṇa kanyā putrikā bhavatīti draṣṭavyam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “abhisaṃdhimātrāt putrikety ekeṣām | tatsaṃśayān nopayacched abhrātṛkām” iti (GDh 29.19.20) | manur api |
aputro ‘nena vidhinā sutāṃ kurvīta putrikām |
yad apatyaṃ bhaved asyāṃ tan mama syāt svadhākaram || iti | (MDh 9.127)
vareṇa saha saṃpratipattikaraṇaṃ tu putrikākaraṇe spaṣṭam eva vijñāyate | sā ca saṃpratipattir vasiṣṭhena darśitā |
abhrātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyām alaṃkṛtām |
asyāṃ yo jāyate putraḥ sa me putro bhaved iti || iti | (VaDh 17.17)
“sa nau putro bhaved” iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ | asyāś ca putrikāyā gāndharvādāv iva svapitrādibhiḥ saha na sāpiṇḍyasagotratvanivṛttiḥ | ata eva laugākṣiḥ |
mātāmahasya gotreṇa mātuḥ piṇḍodakakriyām |
kurvīta putrikāputra evam āha prajāpatiḥ || iti |
tad evam abhihitāṃ putrikāṃ śaṅkamānaḥ putrārthī bhrātṛmatīm evodvahet | “asamānārṣagotrajām” ṛṣer idam ārṣaṃ pravaraḥ gotrapravartakasya muner vyāvartakamunigaṇa ity arthaḥ | tad yathā – [71] gotrapravartakasya bharadvājasya vyāvartakāv aṅgiro-bṛhaspatī | ata eva āṅgirasa-bārhaspatya-bhāradvājagotro ‘ham iti prayuñjate | evam anyad apy udāhāryam | gotraṃ tu vaṃśaparamparāprasiddham | yasyā vadhvā vareṇa saha pravaraikyaṃ gotraikyaṃ vā nāsti sā vadhūr vivāham arhati | kvacid gotrabhede ‘pi pravaraikyam asti | tad yathā – yājñavalkya-vādhūla-mauna-maukānāṃ bhinnagotrāṇāṃ bhārgava-vītahavya-sāvetaseti pravarasyaikyāt | atas tatra vivāhaprasaktau tadvyavacchedāya asamānārṣajām – ity uktam | kvacit pravarabhede ‘pi gotraikyam | tad yathā – āṅgirasāmbarīṣa-yauvanāśva-māndhātrambalīṣa-yauvanā-śveti-atrāṅgirasa-māndhātṛpravarabhede ‘pi yauvanāśvagotram ekam | atas tatra vivāho mā bhūd ity asamānagotragrahaṇam | gotrapravartakāś ca prādhānyenāṣṭhau munayaḥ | te cāgastyāṣṭamāḥ saptarṣayaḥ | tathā ca baudhāyanaḥ |
viśvāmitro jamadagnir bharadvājo ‘tha gautamaḥ |
saptarṣīṇām agastyāṣṭamānāṃ yad apatyaṃ tad gotram ity ācakṣate || iti |
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
jamadagnir bharadvājo viśvāmitro ‘trigautamau |
[72]        vasiṣṭhakaśyapāgastyā munayo gotrakāriṇaḥ |
eteṣāṃ yāny apatyāni tāni gotrāṇi manvate || iti |
eteṣāṃ ca gotrāṇām avāntarabhedāḥ sahasrasaṃkhyākās teṣāṃ gaṇās tv ekonapañcāśat | tathā ca baudhāyanaḥ |
gotrāṇāṃ ca sahasrāṇi prayutāny arbudāni ca |
ūnapañcāśad eteṣāṃ pravarā ṛṣidarśanāt || iti |
pravaragotrayoḥ samānatvāsamānatve baudhāyana-kātyāyana-viśvāmitra-gargādipraṇīteṣu pravaragrantheṣu prasiddhe | na cātra militayor gotrapravarayoḥ paryudāsanimittatvaṃ śaṅkanīyam, pratyekaṃ doṣābhidhānāt | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ: “sagotrāṃ ced amatyopagacchen mātṛvad enāṃ bibhṛyāt” iti | śātātapo ‘pi |
pariṇīya sagotrāṃ tu samānapravarāṃ tathā |
kṛtvā tasyāḥ samutsargaṃ taptakṛcchraṃ viśodhanam || iti |
āpastambaḥ |
samānagotrapravarāṃ kanyām ūḍhvopagamya ca |
tasyām utpādya caṇḍālaṃ brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate || iti |
itthaṃ kanyālakṣaṇaṃ parīkṣya kulam api parīkṣaṇīyam | ata eva manuḥ |
mahānty api samṛddhāni go’jāvidhanadhānyataḥ |
strīsaṃbandhe daśaitāni kulāni parivarjayet ||
[73]        hīnakriyaṃ niṣpuruṣaṃ niśchando romaśārśasam |
kṣayyāmayāvyapasmāriśvitrikuṣṭhikulāni ca || iti | (MDh 3.6–7)
hīnakriyaṃ yāgādikriyārahitam | niṣpuruṣaṃ strīmātraśeṣam | niśchando ‘dhyayanavarjitam | yamo ‘pi |
caturdaśa kulānīmāny avivāhyāni nirdiśet |
anārṣeyaṃ brāhmaṇānām ṛtvijāṃ caiva varjayet ||
atyuccam atihrasvaṃ ca ativarṇaṃ ca varjayet |
hīnāṅgam atirikāṅgam āmayāvikulāni ca ||
śvitrikuṣṭhikulādīnāṃ kuryād viparivarjanam |
sadākāmikulaṃ varjyaṃ romaśānāṃ ca yat kulam ||
apasmārikulaṃ yac ca yac ca pāṇḍukulaṃ bhavet | iti |
anārṣeyaṃ avijñātapravaram | etac ca hīnakriyādivarjanaṃ tathāvidhāpatyaparihārārtham, “kulānurūpāḥ prajāḥ saṃbhavanti” (HārSm 2.13) iti hārītavacanāt | purāṇe ‘pi |
mātulān bhajate putraḥ kanyakā bhajate pitṝn |
yathāśīlā bhaven mātā tathāśīlo bhavet sutaḥ || iti |
manur api |
pitur vā bhajate śīlaṃ mātur vobhayam eva vā |
na kathaṃcana duryoniḥ prakṛtiṃ svāṃ vimuñcati ||
[74] iti | heyakulam uktam | upādeyaṃ tu yājñavalkya āha |
daśapūruṣavikhyātāt śrotriyāṇāṃ mahākulāt | iti | (YDh 1.54)
mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaḥ pañca pañca puruṣā vikhyātā yasmin kule tad daśapuruṣavikhyātaṃ tasmāt mahākulāt putrapaśvādisamṛddhāt kanyām udvahed ity arthaḥ | manur api |
uttamair uttamair nityaṃ saṃbandhān ācaret saha |
ninīṣuḥ kulam utkarṣam adhamān adhamāṃs tyajet || iti | (MDh 4.244)
atrottamān sa evāha |
viśuddhāḥ karmabhiś caiva śrutismṛtinidarśitaiḥ |
aviplutabrahmacaryā mahākulasamanvitāḥ ||
mahākulaiś ca saṃbandhā mahatsv eva vyavasthitāḥ |
saṃtuṣṭāḥ sajjanahitāḥ sādhavaḥ samadarśinaḥ ||
lobharāgadveṣāmarṣamānamohādivarjitāḥ |
akrodhanāḥ suprasādāḥ kāryāḥ saṃbandhinaḥ sadā || iti |
adhamān āha sa eva |
te stenāḥ piśunāḥ klībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
vikarmaṇā ca jīvanto vikṛtākṛtayas tathā ||
[75]        prabaddhavairāḥ śūrair ye rājakilbiṣiṇas tathā |
brahmsvādananityāś ca kadaryāś ca viharhitāḥ ||
aprajā yeṣu vaṃśeṣu strīprajāprasavas tathā |
patighnyaś ca suvāsinyaḥ tāś ca yatnena varjayet || iti |
kanyādāne varaniyamo gautamena darśitaḥ: “vidyācāritrabandhulakṣaṇaśīlasaṃpannāya dadyāt” iti (GDh 4.5) | yamo ‘pi |
kulaṃ ca śīlaṃ ca vapur vayaś ca vidyāṃ ca vittaṃ ca sanāthatāṃ ca |
etān guṇān sapta parīkṣya deyā kanyā budhaiḥ śeṣam avintanīyam || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
etair eva guṇair yuktaḥ savarṇaḥ śrotriyo varaḥ |
yatnāt parīkṣitaḥ puṃstve yuvā dhīmān janapriyaḥ || iti | (YDh 1.55)
etaiḥ kanyakāyām uktair guṇair yuktaḥ | puṃstvaparīkṣopāyas tu nāradena darśitaḥ |
yasyāpsu plavate vīryaṃ hrādi mūtraṃ ca phenilam |
pumān syāl lakṣaṇair etair viparītais tu ṣaṇḍhakaḥ ||
[76]        caturdaśavidhaḥ śāstre ṣaṇḍho dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
cikitsyaś cācikitsyaś ca teṣām ukto vidhiḥ kramāt ||
nisargaṣaṇḍho vadhriś ca pakṣaṣaṇḍhas tathaiva ca |
abhiśāpād guro rogād daivakrodhāt tathaiva ca ||
īrṣyāṣaṇḍhaś. ca sevyaś ca vātaretā mukhebhagaḥ |
ākṣipto moghabījaś ca śālīno ‘nyāpatis tathā || iti | (NSm 12.10–13)
nisargaṣaṇḍhaḥ svabhāvato liṅgavṛṣaṇahīnaḥ | vadhriḥ chinnamuṣkaḥ | pañcadaśa dināni striyam āsevyamānaḥ san sakṛdghogaṣamaḥ pakṣaṣaṇḍhaḥ | guruśāpaṣaṇḍhādayas trayaḥ spaṣṭāḥ | īrṣyayā puṃstvam utpādyate yasya sa īrṣyāṣaṇḍhaḥ | stryupacāraviśeṣeṇa puṃstvaśaktir yasya sa sevyaṣāṇḍhaḥ | vātopahataretasko vātaretāḥ | yasya mukha eva puṃstvaśaktiḥ na yonau sa mukhebhagaḥ | retonirodhāt ṣaṇḍhībhūta ākṣiptaṣaṇḍhaḥ | garbhādhānāsamarthabījaḥ moghabījaḥ | apragalbhatayā kṣobhād vā naṣṭapuṃstvaḥ śālīnaḥ | yasya bhāryāvyatirekeṇānyāsu puruṣabhāvaḥ so ‘nyāpatiḥ | etac ca parīkṣya jñeyam | atra kāraṇam āha sa eva |
apatyārthaṃ striyaḥ sṛṣṭāḥ strī kṣetraṃ bījino narāḥ |
kṣetraṃ bījavate deyaṃ nābījī kṣetram arhati || (NSm 12.19 )
[77] iti | ṣaṇḍhavad anyān api varjanīyān narān āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dūrasthānām avidyānāṃ mokṣamārgānusāriṇām |
śūrāṇāṃ nirdhanānāṃ ca na deyā kanyakā budhaiḥ || iti |
kīdṛśāya tarhi deyā ity ata āha manuḥ |
utkṛṣṭāyābhirūpāya varāya sadṛśāya ca |
aprāptām api tāṃ tasmai kanyāṃ dadyād yathāvidhi | iti | (MDh 9.88)
aprāptām apīti aprāptavivāhasamayāṃ bālikām apīty arthaḥ | prāśastyam āha marīciḥ |
janmato garbhādhānād vā pañcamābdāt paraṃ śubham |
kumārīvaraṇaṃ dānaṃ mekhalābandhanaṃ tathā || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
dadyād guṇavate kanyāṃ nagnikāṃ brahmacāriṇe |
api vā guṇahīnāya noparundhyāt rajasvalām || iti | (BDh 4.1.11)
yat tu yamenoktam,
kāmam ā maraṇāt tiṣṭhed gṛhī kanyartumaty api |
na tv evaināṃ prayaccheta guṇahīnāya karhicit || (MDh 9.89)
[78] iti, tad guṇavati saṃbhavati guṇahīnāya kanyāṃ na dadyād ity evaṃparam | na tu sarvathā guṇahīnaniṣedhaparam | no cet “api vā guṇahīnāya” (BDh 4.1.11) iti baudhāyanokto ‘nukalpo nirviṣayaḥ syāt | “ṛtumaty api tiṣṭhet” (MDh 9.89) iti vacanam uktarītyā na svārthe tātparyavat | yataḥ “noparundhyād rajasvalām” (BDh 4.1.11) ity anena virudhyeta | ata eva vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
prayacchen nagnikāṃ kanyāṃ ṛtukālabhayāt pitā |
ṛtumatyāṃ hi tiṣṭhantyāṃ doṣaḥ pitaram ṛcchati || iti |
saṃvarto ‘pi |
romakāle tu saṃprāpte somo bhuṅkte tu kanyakām |
rajaḥkāle tu gandharvā vahnis tu kucadarśane ||
tasmād udvāhayet kanyāṃ yavan nartumatī bhavet || iti | (SaṃSm 1.65, 68)
kanyāśabdena lajjādyabhijñānarahitavayoyuktā vivakṣitā | tathā ca vāyupurāṇam |
[79]        yāvan na lajjayāṅgāni kanyā puruṣasaṃnidhau |
yonyādīny avagūheta tāvad bhavati kanyakā || iti |
saṃgarhakāro ‘pi |
yāvac cailaṃ na gṛhṇāti yāvat krīḍati pāṃsubhiḥ|
yāvad doṣaṃ na jānāti tāvad bhavati kanyakā || iti |
vayoviśeṣeṇa dātuḥ phalaviśeṣam āha marīciḥ |
gaurīṃ dadan nākapṛṣṭhaṃ vaikuṇṭhaṃ rohiṇīṃ dadan |
kanyāṃ dadad brahmalokaṃ rauravaṃ tu rajasvalām || iti |
gauryādiśabdārhaḥ saṃvartena darśitaḥ |
aṣṭavarṣā bhaved gautī navavarṣā tu rohiṇī |
daśadarṣā bhavet kanyā ata ūrdhvaṃ rajasvalā || iti | (SaṃSm 1.66)
yamo ‘pi |
aṣṭavarṣā bhaved gaurī navame nagnikā bhavet |
daśame kanyakā proktā dvādaśe vṛṣalī smṛtā || iti |
mukhyānukalpabhedena dātṛviśeṣān āha nāradaḥ |
pitā dadyāt svayaṃ kanyāṃ bhrātā vānumate pituḥ |
mātāmaho mātulaś ca sakulyo bāndhavas tathā ||
[80]        mātā tv abhāve sarveṣāṃ prakṛtau yadi vartate |
tasyām aprakṛtisthāyāṃ kanyāṃ dadyuḥ sanābhayaḥ ||
yadā tu naiva kaścit syāt kanyā rājānam āvrajet || iti | (NSm 12.20–21)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā sakulyo jananī tathā |
kanyāpradaḥ pūrvanāśe prakṛtisthaḥ paraḥ paraḥ ||
aprayacchan samāpnoti bhrūṇahatyām ṛtāv ṛtau |
gamyaṃ tv abhāve dātṝṇāṃ kanyā kuryāṭ svayaṃvaram || iti | (YDh 1.63–64)
gamyaṃ gamanārhaṃ sāvarṇyādiguṇayuktam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
savarṇam anurūpaṃ ca kulaśīlavayaḥśrutaiḥ |
sahadharmaṃ caret tena putrṃś cotpādayet tataḥ || iti | (NSm 12.23)
“savarṇaṃ varaṃ prāpya” ity adhyāhṛtya yojanīyam | etac cāsati rajodarśane draṣṭavyam | dṛṣṭe tu rajasi satsv api pitrādiṣu kaṃcit kālaṃ pituḥ śāsanaṃ parīkṣya tadupekṣaṇena savyam eva varaṃ varayet | tad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
[81]        trīṇi varṣāṇy ṛtumatī kāṅkṣeta pitṛśāsanam |
tataś caturthe varṣe tu vindeta sadṛśaṃ patim ||
avidyamāne sadṛśē guṇahīnam api śrayet || iti | (BDh 4.1.14)
manur api |
trīṇi varṣāṇy udīkṣeta kumāry ṛtumatī satī |
ūrdhvaṃ tu kālād etasmāt vindeta sadṛśaṃ patim ||
adīyamānā bhartāram adhigacched yadi svayam |
nainaḥ kiṃcid avāpnoti na ca yaṃ sādhigacchati || iti | (MDh 9.90–91)
sā yaṃ bhartāram adhigacchati so ‘pi naino ‘dhigacchatīty arthaḥ | yat tu viṣṇunoktam,
ṛtutrayam upāsyaiva kanyā kuryāt svayaṃvaram |
iti, tad guṇavadvaralābhe sati draṣṭavyam |
nanu – ṛtumatyāṃ kanyāśabdaḥ kathaṃ prayuktaḥ, yato yamena “daśavarṣā bhavet kanyā” ity uktam | na ca daśame varṣe ṛtuḥ saṃbhavati |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, gauryādiśabdavat kanyāśabdasyāpi yamena paribhāṣitatvāt | sā ca paribhāṣā phalakathanādāv upayuktā | tac ca pūrvam evodāhṛta: “kanyāṃ dadad brahmalokam” iti | lokaprasiddhas tu kanyāśabdo vivāharahitastrīmātram ācaṣṭe | evaṃ ca sati śāstreṣu bahavaḥ kanyāśabdā anugṛhītā bhavanti | tathā cānuśasinike ‘ṣṭāvakroṣākhyāne vṛddhastriyāṃ prayuktaḥ |
[82]        kaumāraṃ brahmacaryaṃme kanyaivāsmi na saṃśayaḥ | it | (MBh ānu. 20.22)
śalyaparvaṇy api vṛddhastryāṃ nāradena prayuktaḥ |
asaṃskṛtāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ kuto lokās tavānaghe | iti | (MBh śalya 51.10)
umāmaheśvarasaṃvāde ‘pi |
ṛtusnātā tu yā śuddhā sā kanyety abhidhīyate | iti |
nanu – “asaṃskṛtāyāḥ” iti vacane vivāharahitāyā uttamalokābhāva uktaḥ sao ‘nupapannaḥ | vivāharahitānām api brahmavādinīnām upanayanādhyayanādibhiḥ uttamalokasaṃbhavāt | ata eva hārītenoktam: “dvividhāḥ striyaḥ | brahmavādinyaḥ sadyovadhvaś ca | tatra brahmavādinīnām upanayanam agnīndhanaṃ vedādhyayanaṃ svagṛhe ca bhikṣācaryā iti | sadyovadhūnāṃ tūpasthite vivāhe kathaṃcid upanayanamātraṃ kṛtvā vivāhaḥ kāryaḥ" (HārSm 21.23) [83] iti |
maivam, tasya kalpāntaraviṣayatvāt | tathā ca yamaḥ |
purā kalpe kumārīṇāṃ mauñjībandhanam iṣyate |
adhyāpanaṃ ca vedānāṃ sāvitrīvācanaṃ tathā ||
[84]        pitā pitṛvyo bhrātā vā nainām adhyāpayet paraḥ |
svagṛhe caiva kanyāyā bhaikṣyacaryā vidhīyate ||
varjayed ajinaṃ cīraṃ jaṭādhāraṇam eva ca || iti |
“aṣṭavarṣā bhaved gaurī” ityādinā vivāhakāla uktaḥ | atha vivāhabhedā ucyante | tatra manuḥ |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ pretya ceha hitāhitān |
aṣṭāv imān samāsena strīvivāhān nibodhata ||
brāhmo daivas tathaivārṣaḥ prājāpatyas tathāsuraḥ |
gāndharvo rākṣasaś caiva paiśācaś cāṣṭamo ‘dhamaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.20–21)
eṣāṃ krameṇa lakṣaṇam āha sa eva |
ācchādya cārcayitvā ca śrutaśīlavate svayam |
āhūya dānaṃ kanyāyā brāhmo dharmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
yajñe tu vitate samyag ṛtvije karma kurvate |
alaṃkṛtya sutādānaṃ daivaṃ dharmaṃ pracakṣate ||
ekaṃ gomithunaṃ dve vā varād ādāya dharmataḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ vidhivad ārṣo dharmaḥ sa ucyate ||
sahobhau caratāṃ dharmam iti vācānubhāṣya tu |
kanyāpradānam abhyarcya prājāpatyo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
jñātibhyo draviṇaṃ datvā kanyāyai ca svaśaktitaḥ |
kanyāpradānaṃ svācchandyād āsuro dharma ucyate ||
[85]        icchayānyonyasaṃyogaḥ kanyāyāś ca varasya ca |
gāndharvaḥ sa ca vijñeyo maithunyaḥ kāmasaṃbhavaḥ ||
hatvā chittvā ca bhittvā ca krośantīṃ rudatīṃ gṛhāt |
prasahya kanyāharaṇaṃ rākṣaso vidhir ucyate ||
suptāṃ mattāṃ pramattāṃ vā raho yatropagacchati |
sa pāpiṣṭho vivāhānāṃ paiśācaḥ kathito ‘ṣṭamaḥ || iti| (MDh 3.27–34)
nārado ‘pi |
brāhmas tu prathamas teṣāṃ prājāpatyas tathāparaḥ |
ārṣaś caivātha daivaś ca gāndharvaś cāsuras tathā ||
rākṣaso ‘nantaras tasmāt paiśācaś cāṣṭamo mataḥ || iti | (NSm 12.38–39)
varṇānupūrvyeṇa vivāhaniyamam āha manuḥ |
ṣaḍānupūrvyā viprasy kṣatrasya caturo ‘varān |
viṭśūdrayos tu tān eva vidyād dharmyān arākṣasān | iti | (MDh 3.23)
āditaḥ ṣaḍvivāhā viprasya dharmyāḥ | āsurādayaś [86] catvāraḥ pāścatyāḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ dharmyāḥ | rākṣasavarjaṃ ta eva vaiśyaśūdrayor api | eteṣāṃ brāhmādīnāṃ madhye praśastān āha sa eva |
caturo brāhmaṇasyādyān praśastān kavayo viduḥ |
rākṣasaṃ kṣatriyasaikam āsuraṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || iti | (MDh 3.24)
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
catvāro brāhmaṇasyādyāḥ śastā gāndharvarākṣasau |
rājñas tathāsuro vaiśye śūdre cāntyas tu garhitaḥ || iti |
garhito na kasyāpi praśasta ity arthaḥ | anyavivāhālābhe brāhmaṇādīnāṃ paiśācam apy anujānāti vatsaḥ |
sarvopāyair asādhyā syāt sukanyā puruṣasya yā |
cauryeṇāpi vivāhena sā vivāhyā rahaḥsthitā || iti |
brāhmādīnāṃ phalam āha manuḥ |
daśa pūrvān parān vaṃśyān ātmānaṃ caikaviṃśakam |
brāhmīputraḥ sukṛtakṛn mocayaty enasaḥ pitṝn ||
daivoḍhājaḥ sutaś caiva sapta sapta parāvarān |
ārṣoḍhājaḥ sutas trīṃs trīn ṣaṭ ṣaṭ kāyoḍhajaḥ sutaḥ ||
brāhamādiṣu vivāheṣu caturṣv evānupūrvaśaḥ |
brahmavarcasvinaḥ putrā jāyante śiṣṭasaṃmatāḥ ||
[87]        rūpasattvaguṇopetā dhanavantaḥ yaśasvinaḥ |
paryāptabhogā dharmiṣṭhā jīvanti ca śataṃ samāḥ ||
itareṣv avaśiṣṭeṣu nṛśaṃsānṛtavādinaḥ |
jāyante durvivāhe tu brahmadharmadviṣaḥ sutāḥ || iti | (MDh 3.37–41)
praśasteṣv api caturṣu vivāheṣu pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ praśastataraḥ | tatra baudhāyanaḥ |
teṣāṃ catvāraḥ pūrve brāhmaṇasya teṣv api pūrvaḥ pūrvaḥ śreyān |
uttareṣām uttarottaraḥ pāpīyān | iti |(BDh 1.11.20.10–11)
nanu – āsurava ārṣo ‘pi pāpīyān | krayaprāptatvāviśeṣāt | ata eva kaśyapaḥ |
krayakrītā tu yā nārī na sā patny abhidhīyate |
na sā daive na sā pitrye dāsīṃ tāṃ kaśyapo ‘bravīt || iti | (KāśSm 49)
brāhmādīnām āsurāntānāṃ madhye brāhmadaivaprājāpatyāḥ [88] trayo dharmyāḥ, krayābhāvāt | ārṣāsurau dvāv adharmyau, krayakrītatvāt | tayor apy āsuraḥ paiśācavad āpady api na kartavyaḥ – iti |
tan na, pañcānām iti vacanasya matāntaropanyāsaparatvāt | kuta etat | yataḥ svayam evottaratra gomithunasya śulkatvaṃ matāntaratvenānūdya niṣedhati,
ārṣe gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ kecid āhur mṛṣaiva tat |
alpo vāpi mahān vāpi vikrayas tāvad eva saḥ || iti | (MDh 3,53)
gomithunaṃ śulkam iti yat kecid āhuḥ
tan mṛṣaiva, na hi tasya śulkatvaṃ saṃbhavati, tallakṣaṇābhāvāt | krayasādhanaṃ hi mūlyaṃ deśakālādyapekṣayā alpaṃ vā mahad vā bhavati | prakṛte tu parimāṇaṃ niyatam | yataḥ ārṣas tāvataiva gomithunenaiva saṃpadyate na tv anyathā | ataḥ krayakrītatvābhāvād dharmya evārṣaḥ | ata eva devalaḥ |
pūrve vivāhāś catvāro dharmyās toyapradānikāḥ |
aśulkā brāhmaṇārhāś ca tārayanti dvayoḥ kulam || iti |
na ca – gāndharvādivivāheṣu saptapadābhikramaṇādyabhāvāt patitvabhāryātvābhāvaḥ – iti śaṅkanīyam | svīkārāt prāk tadabhāve ‘pi paścāt tatsadbhāvāt | tad āha devalaḥ |
[89]        gāndhavādivivāheṣu punar vaivāhiko vidhiḥ |
kartavyaś ca tribhir varṇaiḥ samarthenāgnisākṣikam || iti |
gṛhyapariśiṣṭe ‘pi |
gāndharvāsurapaiśācā vivāho rākṣasaś ca yaḥ |
pūrvaḥ parigrahas teṣu paścād dhomo vidhīyate || iti |
homākaraṇe tua na bhāryātvam | ata eva vasiṣṭha-baudhāyanau |
balād apahṛtā kanyā mantrair yadi na saṃskṛtā |
anyasmai vidhivad deyā yathā kanyā tathaiva sā || iti | (VaDh 17.73; BDh 4.1.15)
tasmād gāndharvādiṣv api saptapadābhikramaṇasaṃbhavād asti bhāryātvam | brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu yad dānam uktaṃ tat sakṛd eva | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
sakṛt pradīyate kanyā haraṃs tāṃ coradaṇḍabhāk | iti | (YDh 1.65)
manur api |
sakṛd aṃśo nipatati sakṛt kanyā pradīyate |
sakṛd āha dadānīti trīṇy etāni satāṃ sakṛt || iti |
etac cāduṣṭavarābhiprāyam | yad āha nāradaḥ |
[90]        datvā nyāyena yaḥ kanyāṃ varāya na dadāti tām |
aduṣṭaś ced varo rājñā sa daṇḍyas tatra coravat || iti | (NSm 12.32)
kim ayam utsargaḥ | nety āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dattām api haret pūrvāc chreyāṃś ced vara āvrajet | iti | (YDh 1.65)
etad vāgdānābhiprāyam | yasmai vācā dattā tato ‘nyaś cet praśastataro labhyate tatas tasmai deyā na tu duṣṭāya pūrvasmai | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “pratiśrutyāpy adharmasaṃyuktāya na dhadyāt” iti (GDh 4.5) | varadoṣās tu kātyāyenenoktāḥ |
unmattaḥ patitaḥ kuṣṭhī tathā ṣaṇḍhaḥ sagotrajaḥ |
cakṣuḥśrotravihīnaś ca tathāpasmāradūṣitaḥ ||
vadoṣās tathaivaite kanyādoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
yas tu ūḍhāyāḥ punarudvāho yama-śātātapābhyāṃ darśitaḥ |
varaś cet kulaśīlābhyāṃ na yujyeta kathaṃcana |
na mantrāḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na ca kanyānṛtaṃ bhavet ||
samācchidya tu tāṃ kanyāṃ balād akṣatayonikām |
punar guṇavate dadyād iti śātātapo ‘bravīt ||
[91]        hīnasya kulaśīlābhyāṃ haran kanyāṃ na doṣabhāk |
na mantrāḥ kāraṇaṃ tatra na ca kanyānṛtaṃ bhavet || iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
sa tu yady anyajātīyaḥ patitaḥ klība eva vā |
vikarmasthaḥ sagotro vā dāso dīrghāmayo ‘pi vā ||
ūḍhāpi deyā sānyasmai saprāvaraṇabhūṣaṇā || iti |
manur api |
naṣṭe mṛte pravrajite klībe ca patite tathā |
pañcasv āpatsu nārīṇāṃ patir anyo vidhīyate || iti | (NSm 12.97)
so ‘yaṃ punar udvāho yugāntaraviṣayaḥ | tathā ca ādityapurāṇam |
ūḍhāyāḥ punar udvāhaṃ jyeṣṭhāṃśaṃ govadhaṃ tathā |
kalau pañca na kurvīta bhrātṛjāyāṃ kamaṇḍalum || iti |
yas tu kanyādoṣam anabhidhāya prayacchati sa rājñā daṇḍayitavya ity āha nāradaḥ |
yas tu doṣavatīṃ kanyām anākhyāya prayacchati |
tasya kuryān nṛpo daṇḍaṃ pūrvasāhasacoditam || iti | (NSm 12.33)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
anākhyāya dadad doṣaṃ daṇḍya uttamasāhasam | (YDh 1.66)
[92] iti | kanyādoṣās tu nāradena darśitāḥ |
dīrthakutsitarogārttā vyaṅgā saṃspṛṣṭamaithunā |
dṛṣṭānyagatabhāvā ca kanyādoṣāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti | (NSm 12.36)
na kevalaṃ doṣam anākhyāya dadato daṇḍaḥ api tu sāpi parityājyety āha manuḥ |
vidhivat pratigṛhyāpi tyajet kanyāṃ vigarhitām |
vyādhitāṃ vipraduṣṭāṃ vā chadmanā copapāditām || iti | (MDh 9.72)
nārado ‘pi |
nāduṣṭāṃ dūṣayet kanyāṃ nāduṣṭaṃ dūṣayed varam |
doṣe sati na doṣaḥ syād anyonyaṃ tyajatos tayoḥ || iti | (NSm 12.31)
etat saptamapadābhikramaṇād arvāg veditavyam, tatraiva bhāryātvasyotpatteḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇikā mantrā niyataṃ dāralakṣaṇam |
teṣāṃ niṣṭhā tu vijñeyā vidvadbhiḥ saptame pade || iti | (MDh 8.227)
yamo ‘pi |
nodakena na vā vācā kanyāyāḥ patir iṣyate |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārāt patitvaṃ saptame pade ||
[93] iti | pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskārāt pūrvaṃ pariṇetur maraṇe ‘pi na kanyātvaṃ hīyate | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
adbhir vācā ca dattāyāṃ mriyetādau varo yadi |
na ca mantropanītā syāt kumārī pitur eva sā || iti | (VaDh 17.72)
varaṇānantaraṃ deśāntaragamane viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
varayitvā tu yaḥ kaścit praṇaśyet puruṣo yadā |
ṛtvāgamāṃs trīn atītya kanyānyaṃ varayed varam || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
pratigṛhya tu yaḥ kanyāṃ varo deśāntaraṃ vrajet |
trīn ṛtūn samatikramya kanyānyaṃ varayed varam || iti | (NSm 12.24)
śulkaṃ datvā yadi varo mriyate tadā kiṃ kartavyam ity ata āha manuḥ |
kanyāyāṃ dattaśulkāyāṃ mriyate yadi śulkadaḥ |
devarāya pradātavyā yadi kanyānumanyate || iti | (MDh 9.97)
deśāntaragamane tu viśeṣaḥ kātyāyanenoktaḥ |
pradāya śulkaṃ gacched yaḥ kanyāyāḥ strīdhanaṃ tathā |
dhāryā vā varṣam ekaṃ tu deyānyasmai vidhānataḥ || iti |
evaṃ ca vāgdānād ārabhya saptamapadābhikramaṇāt prāk [94] doṣadarśane maraṇādau vā kanyām anyasmai dadyād ity uktaṃ bhavati | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
anekebhyo hi dattāyām anūḍhāyāṃ tu tatra vai |
putrāgataś ca sarveṣāṃ labheta tad imāṃ sutām ||
athāgaccheta voḍhāyāṃ dattaṃ pūrvavaro haret | iti |
anūḍhāyāṃ yasmai pūrvaṃ pratiśrutā sa eva kanyāṃ labhate | anyenoḍhāyāṃ tu svadattaṃ śulkamātraṃ haret na kanyām ity arthaḥ | “lakṣaṇyāṃ striyam udvahet” (YDh 1.52) iti yad uktaṃ tatrodvahanīyā kanyā dvividhā | savarṇā cāsvarṇā ca | tayor ādyā praśastā | tad āha manuḥ |
savarṇāgre dvijātīnāṃ praśastā dārakamaṇi |
kāmatas tu pravṛttānām imāḥ syuḥ kramaśo ‘varāḥ || iti | (MDh 3.12)
agre snātakasya prathamavivāhe | dārakarmaṇi agnihotrādau dharme | savarṇā vareṇa samāno varṇo brāhmaṇādir yasyāḥ sā | yathā brāhmaṇasya brāhmaṇī kṣatriyasya kṣatriyā vaiśyasya vaiśyā praśastā | dharmārtham ādau savarṇām ūḍhvā paścād riraṃsavaś cet tadā teṣām avarāḥ hīnavarṇāḥ imāḥ kṣatriyādyāḥ krameṇa bhāryāḥ smṛtāḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
[95]        tisro varṇānupūrvyeṇa dve tathaikā yathākramam |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviṣāṃ bhāryā svā śūdrajanmanaḥ || iti | (Ydh 1.57)
manur api |
śūdraiva bhāryā śūdrasya sā ca svā ca viśaḥ smṛte |
te ca sā caiva rājñaḥ syuḥ tāś ca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.13)
nārado ‘pi |
brāhmaṇasyānulomyena striyo ‘nyās tisra eva tu |
śūdrāyāḥ prātilomyena tathānye patayas trayaḥ ||
dve bhārye kṣatriyasyānye vaiśyasyaikā prakīrtitā |
vaiśyāyā dvau patī jñeyāv eko ‘nyaḥ kṣatriyāpatiḥ || iti | (NSm 12.5–6)
vasiṣṭha-pāraskarāv api: “tisro brāhmaṇasya varṇānupūrvyeṇa | dve rājanyasya | ekā vaiśyasya | sarveṣāṃ vā śūdrām apy eke mantravarjam” iti (VaDh 1.24–25; PārGṛ 1.4.8–11) | patiṭhīnasiḥ: [96] “alābhe kanyāyāḥ snātakavrataṃ caret | api vā kṣatriyāyāṃ putram utpādayīta | śūdrāyāṃ vety eke” iti | viṣṇur api |
dvijasya bhāryā śūdrā tu dharmārthaṃ na bhavet kvacit |
ratyartham eva sā tasya rāgāndhasya prakīrtitā || iti | (ViDh 26.5)
evaṃ tāvad eteṣāṃ matena dvijānām āpadi śūdrāsaṃgrahaṇaṃ ratimātraphalam api doṣamāndyād (?) anujñātam | idānīm apareṣāṃ matena brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ tāvac chūdrāvarjanam eva yuktataraṃ nodvāhaḥ ity ucyate | tatra manuḥ |
na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor āpay api hi tiṣṭhatoḥ |
kasmiṃścid api vṛttānte śūdrā bhāryopadiśyate ||
hīnajātistriyaṃ mohād udvahanto dvijātayaḥ |
kulāny eva nayanty āśu sasantānāni śūdratām ||
śūdrāvedī pataty atrer utathyatanayasya ca |
śaunakasya sutotpattyā tadapatyatayā bhṛgoḥ ||
śūdrāṃ śayanam āropya brāhmaṇo yāty adhogatim |
janayitvā sutaṃ tasyāṃ brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate ||
vṛṣalīphenapītasya niḥśvāsopahatasya ca |
tasyāṃ caiva prasūtasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || iti | (MDh 3.14–19)
āśvamedhike ‘pi |
[97]        śūdrāyonau patad bījaṃ hāhāśabdaṃ dvijanmanaḥ |
