kṛtvā purīṣagarteṣu patito ‘smīti duḥkhitam ||
mām adhaḥ pātayann eṣa pāpātmā kāmamohitaḥ |
adhogatiṃ vrajet kṣipram iti śaptvā patat tu tat || iti |
nanu – “tāś ca svā cāgrajanmanaḥ” (MDh 3.13) iti manunā śūdrāvivāho viprasyābhyanujñātaḥ | punaś ca tenaiva “na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ” (MDh 3.14) iti sa niṣiddhaḥ ato vyāhatiḥ, iti cet,
maivam, matabhedena yugabhedena vā vyavasthopapatteḥ | ata eva yājñavalkyena mata bhedaḥ spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ |
tad ucyate dvijātīnāṃ śūdrād dāropasaṃgrahaḥ |
naitan mama mataṃ yasmāt tatrāyaṃ jāyate svayam || iti | (YDh 1.56)
ānuśāsanike ‘pi |
apatyajanma śūdrāyāṃ na praśaṃsanti sādhavaḥ |
ratyartham api śūdrā syān netyāhur apare janāḥ || iti |
yugabhedena vyavasthā ca smṛtyantare spaṣṭīkṛtā |
[98]        asavarṇāsu kanyāsu vivāhaś ca dvijātibhiḥ |
ityādim anukramya,
kulau yuge tv imān dharmān varjyān āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
ity upasaṃhārāt | vivāhavidhis tu manunābhihitaḥ |
pāṇigrahaṇasaṃskāraḥ savarṇāsūpadiśyate |
savarṇāsv ayaṃ jñeyo vidhir udvāhakarmaṇi ||
śaraḥ kṣatriyayā grāhyaḥ pratodo vaiśyakanyayā |
vāsodaśā śūdrayā tu varṇotkṛṣṭasya vedane || iti | (MSm 3.43–44)
śaṅkhalikhitāv api: “iṣuṃ gṛhṇāti rājanyā | pratodaṃ vaiśyā | daśāntaṃ śūdrā | brāhmaṇas tu savarṇāyāḥ pāṇiṃ gṛhṇīyāt” iti | paiṭhīnasiḥ: “sāṅguṣṭhaṃ brāhmaṇyāḥ pāṇi gṛhṇīyāt | kṣatriyā śaram | vaiśyā pratodam | śūdrā vastradaśām | parikramo brāhmaṇasyokto rājanyavaiśyayor ācāryaparikramaḥ” iti | kṛtodvāhasyopagamaniyamam āha manuḥ |
[99]        ṛtukālābhigāmi syāt svadāranirataḥ sadā |
parvavarjaṃ vrajec caināṃ tadvrato ratikāmyayā || iti | (MDh 3.45)
rajodarśanaprabhṛti ṣoḍaśāhorātrātmakaḥ kālo garbhagrahaṇasamartho hi ṛtuḥ | tasmin ṛtāv apatyārthī sadā striyam upagacchet | tac cābhigamanaṃ svadāreṣv eva | ṛtāv api parvatithiṃ varjayet | anṛtāv api tayā striyā prārthito vināpy apatyoddeśaṃ tām abhigacchet | yathānirdiṣṭam ṛtuṃ yājñavalkyo darśayat |
ṣoḍaśartur niśāḥ strīṇāṃ tasmin yugmāsu saṃviśet |
brahmacāry eva parvāṇi ādyāś catasraś c varjayet || iti | (YDh 1.79)
tasmin ṛtau parvāṇi ādyāś catasro rātrīś ca varjayitvā yugmāsu samāsu ṣaṣṭhīprabhṛtiṣu gacchet putrārtham | ayugmāsu strījanmabhayād agamanam, na tu pratiṣiddhatvāt | yugmāsv api rātrāv evopagamanaṃ nāhani, pratiṣiddhatvāt | tathā ca atharvaṇī śrutiḥ: “prāṇaṃ vā ete praskandanti | ye divā ratyā saṃyujyante brahmacaryam eva tad yad rātrau ratyā saṃyujyante” iti (PraśUp 1.13) | śaṅkhalikhitāv api: [100] “nārtave divā maithunaṃ vrajet” iti | “ṛtukālābhigāmī syāt” ity atra niyamadvayaṃ veditavyam | ṛtau gacched eva na tu varjayet – ity eko niyamaḥ | ṛtāv eva gacchen nānṛtau ity aparaḥ | ata eva devalaḥ |
svayaṃ dārān ṛtusnātān svasthaś cen nopagacchati |
bhrūṇahatyām avāpnoti garbhaṃ prāptaṃ vināśya saḥ || iti |
baudhānayo ‘pi |
trīṇi varṣāṇy ṛtumatīṃ yo bhāryāṃ nopagacchati |
sa tulyaṃ bhrūṇahatyāyā doṣam ṛcchaty asaṃśayam ||
ṛtau nopaiti yo bhāryām anṛtau yaś ca gacchati |
tulyam āhus tayoḥ pāpam ayonau yaś ca siñcati || iti | (BDh 4.1.17, 19)
parvavarjam ity anena niṣiddhatithinakṣatrāṇy upalakṣyante | niṣiddhaparva tv amāvāsyā paurṇamāsī ca | tatra strīgamanaṃ śrutyā niṣiddham: “nāmāvāsyāyāṃ ca paurṇamāsyāṃ ca striyam upeyāt yady upeyān nirindriyaḥ syāt” iti (TS 2.5.6.4) | anyāś ca niṣiddhās tithayo ‘ṣṭamyādayaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
amāvāsyām aṣṭamīṃ ca paurṇamāsīṃ caturdaśīm |
brahmacārī bhaven nityam apy ṛtau snātako dvijaḥ || (MDh 4.128)
[101] iti | amāvāsyādayo yās tithayaḥ tāsu “strīsaṅgatyāgena” ity adhyāhṛtya yojanīyam | ṛtukāle ‘pi dinaṣaṭkaṃ varjyam ity sa evāha |
tāsām ādyāś catasras tu ninditaikādaśī ca yā |
trayodaśī ca śeṣās tu praśastā daśa rātrayaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.47)
niṣiddhaṃ nakṣatraṃ yājñavalkyo darśayati |
evaṃ gacchan striyaṃ kṣāmāṃ maghāṃ mūlaṃ ca varjayet | iti | (YDh 1.80)
kṣāmāṃ laghvāhārādinā kṛśām ity arthaḥ | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ strīpuṃsayor āhāraviśeṣaṃ sanimittam āha |
striyā śukre ‘dhke strī syāt pumān puṃso ‘dhike bhavet |
tasmāt śukravivṛddhyarthaṃ snigdhaṃ vṛṣyaṃ ca bhakṣayet ||
laghvāhārāṃ kuryād evaṃ saṃjanayet sutam || iti |
manur api |
pumān puṃsao ‘dhke śukre strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ |
same ‘pumān puṃstriyor vā kṣīṇe ‘lpe ca viparyayaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.49)
apumān it chedaḥ | saṃkrāntiś ca pañcaparvāntaḥpātitvād varjanīyā | tad uktaṃ viṣṇupurāṇe |
[102]        caturdaśy aṣṭamī caiva amāvāsyā ca pūrṇimā |
parvāṇy etāni rājendra ravisaṃkrāntir eva ca ||
tailastrīmāṃsabhogī ca parvasv eteṣu vai pumān |
viṇmūtrabhojanaṃ nāma prayāti narakaṃ nṛpa || iti | (ViPu 3.11.114–15)
deśāś ca varjyās tatraiva darśitāḥ |
caityacatvarasaudheṣu naiva devacatuṣpathe |
naiva śmaśānopavanasalileṣu mahīpate ||
gacched vyavāyaṃ matimān mūtroccāraprapīḍitaḥ || iti | (ViPu 3.11.118–19)
svadāranirata ity anena manasāpi paradāragamanaṃ niṣiddhatayā vivakṣitam | etad api tatraiva darśitam |
paradānān na gaccheta manasāpi kathaṃcana |
paradāraratiḥ puṃsām ubhayatrāpi bhītidā ||
iti matvā svadāreṣu ṛtumatsu vrajed budhaḥ || iti | (ViPu 3.11.121–23)
anyad api varjyaṃ tatraiva darśitam |
nāsnātāṃ tu striyaṃ gacchen nāturāṃ na rajasvalām |
nāniṣṭāṃ na prakupitāṃ nāpraśastāṃ na rogiṇīm ||
[103]        nādakṣiṇāṃ nānyakāmāṃ nākāmāṃ nānyayoṣitam |
kṣutkṣāmāṃ nātibhuktāṃ vā svayaṃ caitair guṇair yutaḥ ||
snātaḥ sraggandhadhṛk prīto vyavāyaṃ puruṣo vrajet || iti | (ViPu 3.11.111–13)
tadvrataḥ ity anena śrautaṃ varaṃ smārayati | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “sa strīṣaṃ sādam upāsīdadad asyai brahmahatyāyai tṛtīyaṃ pratigṛhṇīteti tā abruvan varaṃ vṛṇāmahā ṛtviyāt prajāṃ vindāmahai kāmam ā vijanatoḥ saṃbhavāmeti tasmād ṛtviyāt striyaḥ prajāṃ vindante kāmam ā vijanatoḥ saṃbhavanti vāre vṛtaṃ hy āsāṃ tṛtīyaṃ brahmahatyāyai pratyagṛhṇant sā malavadvāsābhavat” iti (TS 2.5.1.5) | ayam arthaḥ – indraḥ kila viśvarūpanāmnaḥ purohitasya vadhāt brahmahatyām upāgatām añjalinā svīkṛtya saṃvatsaraṃ ghṛtvā lokāpavādād bhītas tāṃ tredhā vibhajya prathamam bhāgaṃ pṛthivyau dvitīyabhāgaṃ [104] vanaspatibhyo varapūrvakaṃ datvā tṛtīyabhāgadānāya strīsamūham upāgamat | tāś ca varam ayācanta | ṛtukālagamanāt prajāṃ labhemahi ā prasavam anṛtāv api yathecchaṃ saṃbhavāmeti | varaṃ labdhvā tṛtīyaṃ bhāgaṃ pratyagṛhṇan | sa ca bhāgo rajorūpeṇa pariṇataḥ | tataḥ prabhṛti māsi māsi yoṣin malavadvāsāḥ saṃpanneti | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
yathākāmī bhaved vāpi strīṇāṃ varam anusmaran |
svadāranirataś caiva striyo rakṣyā yataḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | (YDh 1.81)
bṛhaspatir api |
ṛtukālābhigamanaṃ puṃsā kāryaṃ prayatnataḥ |
sadaiva vā parvavarjaṃ strīṇām abhimataṃ hi tat || iti |
ṛtukālābhigamanam ity atra kecid evam ācakṣate – ajātaputrasyaivaiṣa niyamo na tu jātaputrasya iti | upapādayanti ca | tatra kūrmapurāṇam |
ṛtukālābhigāmī syād yāvat putro ‘bhijāyate | iti | (KūPu 1.2.15.11)
ṛṇāpākaraṇārthaṃ hi putrotpādanam | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇas tribhir ṛṇavā jāyate brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhyo yajñena devebhyaḥ [105] prajayā pitṛbhya eṣa vā anṛṇo yaḥ putrī yajvā brahmacārivāsī” iti (TS 6.3.10.5) | mantravarṇo ‘pi |
ṛṇam asmin saṃnyayaty amṛtatvaṃ ca gacchati |
pitā putrasya jātasya paśyec cej jīvato mukham || iti | (AitĀ 7.3.4)
tad etad ṛṇāpākaraṇam ekaputrotpādanena saṃpadyate, tāvatāpi putratvasiddheḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
jyeṣṭhena jātamātreṇa putrī bhavati mānavaḥ |
pitṝṇām anṛṇaś caiva sa tasmāt sarvam arhati ||
yasmin ṛṇaṃ saṃnyayati yena cānantyam aśnute |
sa eva dharmajaḥ putraḥ kāmajān itarān viduḥ || iti | (MDh 9.106––07)
nanu – bahuputratvam api kvacit śrūyate,
imāṃ tvam indra mīḍhvaḥ suputrāṃ subhagāṃ kṛṇu |
daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi patim ekādaśaṃ kṛdhi || iti | (RV 8.3.28.5)
krīḍantau putraiḥ | iti | (RV 8.3.28.2)
[106]        rayiṃ ca putrāṃ cādāt | iti ca | (RV 8.3.28.1)
satyam | nāyaṃ vidhiḥ | kiṃ tu bahvapatyapraśaṃsā | tasmād ajātaputrasyaivāyaṃ ṛtugamananiyamaḥ iti | etad apare na kṣamante | yady api putritvam ānṛṇyaṃ caikenaiva putreṇa saṃpadyate tathāpy asti bahuputratvavidhiḥ: “etam u evāham abhyagāsiṣaṃ tasmān mama tvam eko ‘sītīha kauṣītakiḥ putram ucāva | raśmīṃs tvaṃ paryāvartayād bahavo vai te bhavaiṣyanti” (ChU 1.5.2) iti chandogair āmnānāt | tasya cāyam evārthaḥ kauṣītakir nāma muniḥ svayam ādityamaṇḍalam ekam evopāsīna udgānaṃ kṛtvā tatphalatvena putram ekaṃ pratilabhya tam uvāca aham etam ekam evādityaṃ dhyāyan gānam akārṣaṃ tasmād doṣān mama tvam eka eva putro ‘si | ekaputratvaṃ ca na praśastam | atas tvaṃ bahuputratāyai raśmīn bahūn upāsitavelāyām āvartaya iti |
mahābhārate ‘pi |
apatyaṃ nas tvam evaikaḥ kule mahati bhārata ||
anapatyaṃ tv ekaputram ity āhur dharmavādinaḥ ||
cakṣur ekaṃ ca putraś ca asti nāstīti bhārata |
cakṣurnāśe tanor nāśaḥ putranāśe kulakṣayaḥ ||
[107]        anityatāṃ ca martyānāṃ matvā śocāmi putraka |
santānasyāvināśaṃ tu kāmaye bharad astu te || || iti | (MDh ādi 100.61–64)
nanu – jyeṣṭhenaiva putreṇānṛṇyasiddher nirarthakaṃ putrāntarotpādanam |
tan na, sarveṣāṃ putrāṇām ānṛṇyahetutvāt | na hi putrajananamātreṇa pitur ānṛṇyam | kiṃ tarhi samyag anuśiṣṭhena putreṇa śāstrīyeṣu karmasv anuṣṭhiteṣu paścād ānṛṇyaṃ saṃpadyate | ata eva vājasaneyibrāhmaṇe putrānuśāsanavidhiḥ samāmnātaḥ: “tasmāt putram anuśiṣṭaṃ lokyam āhus tasmād enam anuśāsati | sa yady anena kiṃcid akṣṇayākṛtaṃ bhavati tasmād enaṃ sarvasmāt putro muñcati | tasmāt putro nāma sa putreṇaivāsmiṃl loke pratitiṣṭhati” iti (BāU 1.5.17) | asyāyam arthaḥ – asti kiṃcit saṃpratipattināmakaṃ karma | yadā pitur maraṇāvasaro bhavati tadā putram āhūya vedādhyayane yajñe laukikavyāpāre ca yad yat kartavyajātaṃ tasya sarvasya putre saṃpradānaṃ kartavyam | seyaṃ saṃpratipattiḥ | tasyāṃ ca [108] saṃpratipattau yasmād anuśiṣṭa eva putro ‘dhikārī tasmād anuśiṣṭaṃ putraṃ paralokahitam āhuḥ śāstrajñāḥ | ata eva putrānuśāsanaṃ kuryuḥ pitaraḥ | sa cānuśiṣṭaḥ putraḥ yat kiṃcit svapitrā śāstrīyaṃ karma akṣṇayā vakratvena śāstravaiparītyena kṛtaṃ bhavati tasmāt sarvasmāt pāpād enaṃ(?) svapitaraṃ svayaṃ śāstrīyaṃ  karma samyag anutiṣṭhan mocayati | tasmāt punnāmno narakāt trāyate – iti vyutpattyā putro nāma | sa ca pitā svayaṃ mṛtaḥ putraśarīreṇaivāsmin loke yathāśāstraṃ karma kurvann anutiṣṭhati – iti |
evaṃ sati bahūnāṃ madhye yathāvadanuśāsanaṃ prajñāmāṃdyādipratibandhabāhulyāt kasyacid eva saṃpadyate | anuśiṣṭeṣv api bahuṣu yathāvadanuṣṭhānaṃ kasyavid eva | ato jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā yas tādṛśaḥ sa evānṛṇyahetuḥ | ata eva purāṇe ‘bhihitam |
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet | iti | (ViDh 85.67)
“daśāsyāṃ putrān ādhehi” (RV 8.3.28.5) [109] ityādimantrāś caivaṃ sati bahuputratvavidhim upodvalayanti | yat tu “kāmajān itarān” iti manunodāhṛtaṃ tadananuśiṣṭaviṣayam | tasmāt jātaputro ‘py ṛtāv upeyād eva | bahūnāṃ patnīnām ṛtuyaugapadye kramam āha devalaḥ |
yaugapadye tu tīrthānāṃ viprādikramaśo vrajet |
rakṣaṇārtham aputrāṃ vā grahaṇakramaśo ‘pi vā || iti |
tīrtham ṛtuḥ | tadyaugapadye saty asavarṇāsu varṇakrameṇa savarṇāsu vivāhakrameṇa gacchet | yadā tu kācid aputrā putravatya itarāḥ tadā aputrāṃ agrato gacchet | ṛtāv api jātibhedenopagamanakālasaṅkocam āha devalaḥ |
brāhmaṇyāṃ dvādaśāhaṃ syād vedoktam ṛtudhāraṇam |
daśāṣṭau ṣaṭ ca śeṣāṇāṃ vidhīyante ‘nupūrvaśaḥ || iti |
pañcamadivasam ārabhya dvādaśāhādisaṃkhyāvagantavyā | caturthe tu divase gamanaṃ vaikalpikam, vihitapratiṣiddhatvāt | tathā ca hārīto vidadhāti: “caturthe ‘hani snātāyāṃ yugmāsu vā garbhādhānam” iti (HārSm 2.9) | vyāso niṣedhati |
[110]        caturthe sā na gamyāhni gatālpāyuṃ prasūyate | iti |
vyavasthitavikalpaś cāyam uditānuditahomavat | rajonivṛttau caturthyāṃ vidhiḥ tadanuvṛttau pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
rajasy uparate sādhvī snānena strī rajasvalā | iti | (MDh 5.66)
sādhvī garbhādhānādivihitakarmayogyety arthaḥ | dinaviśeṣeṇopagamane phalaviśeṣo ‘bhihito liṅgapurāṇe |
caturthe sā na gamyāhni gatālpāyuṃ prasūyate |
vidyāhīnaṃ vratabhraṣṭaṃ patitaṃ pāradārikam ||
dāridryārṇavamagnaṃ ca tanayaṃ saṃprasūyate |
kanyārthinaiva gantavyā pañcamyāṃ vidhivat punaḥ ||
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ gamyā mahābhāgā satputrajananī bhavet |
saptamyāṃ caiva kanyārthī gacchet saiva prasūyate ||
aṣṭamyāṃ sarvasaṃpannaṃ tanayaṃ saṃprasūyate ||
navamyāṃ dārikārthaṃ syād daśamyāṃ paṇḍitaṃ tathā |
ekādaśyāṃ tathā nārīṃ janayaty eva pūrvavat ||
dvadaśyāṃ dharmatattvajñaṃ śrautasmārtapravartakam |
[111]        trayodaśyāṃ jaḍāṃ nārīṃ varṇasaṅkarakāriṇīm ||
janayaty aṅganā tasmān na gacchet sarvayatnataḥ |
caturdaśyāṃ yadā gacchet suputrajananī bhavet ||
pañcadaśyāṃ ca dharmiṣṭhāṃ ṣoḍaśyāṃ jñānapāragam || iti | LiPu 1.89.109–18)
ṛtukālānabhigamane yo doṣo ‘bhihitaḥ tasyāpavādam āha vyāsaḥ |
vyādhito bandhanastho vā pravāseṣv atha parvasu |
ṛtukāle ‘pi nārīṇāṃ bhrūṇahatyā pramucyate ||
vṛddhāṃ vandhyām avṛttāṃ ca mṛtāpatyām apuṣpiṇīm |
kanyāṃ ca bahuputrāṃ ca varjayan mucyate bhayāt || iti |
bhrūṇahatyā bhrūṇahananam | uktarītyā yasyāṃ vṛddhatvādidoṣarahitāyām ṛtukālopagamanam avaśyaṃbhāvi yasyāṃ ca vṛddhādau nāvaśyaṃbhāvi sā sarvāpi samyak pālanīyā | tathā ca manuḥ |
pitṛbhir bhrātrbhiś caitāḥ patibhir devatais tathā |
pūjyā bhūṣayitavyāś ca bahukalyāṇam īpsubhiḥ ||
yatra nāryas tu pūjyante ramante tatra devatāḥ |
yatraitās tu na pūjyante sarvās tatrāphalāḥ kriyāḥ ||
[112]        śocanti jāmayo yatra vinaśyaty āśu tatkulam |
na śocanti tu yatraitā vardhante tad dhi sarvadā ||
jāmayo yāni gehāni śapanty apratipūjitāḥ |
tāni kṛtyāhatānīva vinaśyanti samantataḥ ||
tasmād etāḥ sadābhyarcyā bhūṣaṇācchādanāśanaiḥ |
bhūtikāmair narair nityaṃ satkāreṣūtsaveṣu ca ||
saṃtuṣṭo bhāryayā bhartā bhartrā bhāryā tathaiva ca |
yasminn eva kule nityaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tatra vai dhruvam || iti | (MDh 3.55–60)
pūjyatvaṃ cāvṛttāvyatiriktāsu draṣṭavyam | avṛttāyās tu prāṇayātrāmātrabhojanam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
hṛtādhikārāṃ malināṃ piṇḍamātropajīvinīm |
paribhūtām adhaḥśayyāṃ vāsayed vyabhicāriṇīm || iti | (YDh 1.70)
yathā vinnā sādhvī bhartavyā tathaivādhivinnāpi | tad āha sa eva |
adhivinnāpi bhartavyā mahad eno ‘nyathā bhavet | iti | (YDh 1.74)
adhivedanaṃ bhāryāntaraparigrahaḥ | adhivedananimittāny api sa evāha |
[113]        surāpī vyādhitā dhūrtā vandhyārthaghny apriyaṃvadā |
strīprasūś cādhivettavyā puruṣadveṣiṇī tathā || iti | (YDh 1.73)
surāpī madyapīty arthaḥ | surāpāne tu nādhivedanamātram | api tu tyāga eva, “tathā mahati pātake” (YDh 1.72) iti tyāgahetutvenābhidhānāt,
pataty ardhaṃ śarīrasya yasya bhāryā surāṃ pibet | (VaDh 21.15)
iti vacanāc ca | ata eva manuḥ |
madyapāsādhuvṛttā ca pratikūlā ca yā bhavet |
vyādhitā vādhivettavyā hiṃsrārthaghnī ca sarvadā || iti | (MDh 9.80)
vyādhitā dīrgharogiṇī | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
dharmavighnakarīṃ bhāryām asatīṃ cātikopinīm |
tyajed dharmasya rakṣārthaṃ tathaivāpriyavādinīm || iti |
tyajed adhivindeta | na tu bhogaṃ parityajet |
[114] adhivedanakālaviśeṣo manunā darśitaḥ |
vandhyāṣṭame ‘dhivedyābde daśame tu mṛtaprajā |
ekādaśe strījananī sadas tv apriyavādinī || iti | (MDh 9.81)
vyādhitāyāṃ viśeṣam āha sa eva |
yā rogiṇī syāt tu hitā saṃpannā caiva śīlataḥ |
sānujñāpyādhivettavyā nāvamānyā tu karhicit || iti | (MDh 8.82)
adhivedanaṃ dvividham | dharmārthaṃ kāmārthaṃ ca | tatra putrotpattyādidharmārthe pūrvoktāni madyapetyādīn nimittāni | kāmārthe tu na sāny apekṣaṇīyāni | kiṃ tu pūrvoḍhā toṣaṇīyā | tathā ca smṛtyantare |
ekām utkramya kāmārtham anyāṃ labdhuṃ ya icchati |
samarthas toṣayitvārthaiḥ pūrvoḍhām aparām vahet || iti |
yady asau svayaṃ na toṣayet tadā tattoṣaṇāya rājā dravyaṃ dāpayet | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ājñāsaṃpādinīṃ dakṣāṃ vīrasūṃ priyavādinīm |
tyajan dāpyas tṛtīyāṃśam adravyo bharaṇaṃ striyāḥ || (YDh 1.76)
[115] iti | sadhanasya tṛtīyāṃśadānaṃ nirdhanasyāśanācchādanādinā poṣaṇam iti | yā tūktadravyāparitoṣaṇāt prakārāntareṇa vā nirgacchet tāṃ praty āha manuḥ |
adhivinnā tu yā nārī nirgacched ruṣitā gṛhāt |
sā sadyaḥ saṃniroddhavyā tyājyā vā kulasaṃnidhau || iti | (MDh 9.83)
tyāgo nāma tadīyajanakakulapreṣaṇam | “pūjyā bhūṣayitavyāś ca” (MDh 3.55) iti yad uktaṃ tatra bahupatnīkasya pūjākramam āha manuḥ |
yadi svāś cāvarāś caiva vinderan yoṣito dvijāḥ |
tāsāṃ varṇakrameṇa syāj jyaiṣṭhyaṃ pūjā ca viśmani ||
bhartuḥ śarīraśuśrūṣāṃ dharmakāryaṃ ca naityakam |
svā svaiva kuryāt sarveṣāṃ nāsajātiḥ kathaṃcana || iti | (MDh 9.85–86)
bahvīṣu patnīṣu sahadharmacāriṇīṃ nirdhārayati yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyām anyāṃ savarṇāyāṃ dharmkāryaṃ na kārayet |
savarṇāsu vidhau dharmye jyeṣṭhayā na vinetarāḥ || (YDh 1.88)
[116] iti | savarṇayaiva saha dharmaṃ caret nāsavarṇayā | alābhe tu savarṇāyā itarayāpi saha dharmaṃ cared ity arthāl labhyate | na ca – evaṃ sati śūdrayāpi sahadharmacaraṇaṃ prasajyeta – iti vācyam, vasiṣṭhavacanena tanniṣedhāt: “kṛṣṇavarṇā yā rāmā ramaṇāyaiva sā na dharmāya” iti (VaDh 18.18) | savarṇānekatve tu dharmānuṣṭhāne jyeṣṭhayā vinā madhyamā kaniṣṭhā ca na yoktavye | kiṃ tu jyeṣṭhayā kanīyasyo viniyoktavyāḥ | tathā ca baudhāyanaḥ: “ekaikām eva sānnāhyed ekaikāṃ gārhapatyam īkṣet ekaikām ājyam avekṣayet” ityādi | kātyānanaḥ |
naikayāpi vinā kāryam ādhānaṃ bhāryayā dvijaiḥ |
akṛtaṃ tad vijānīyāt sarvān anvārabhanti yat || iti | (KSm 1.8.5)
yad yasmāt sarvān anvārabhante tasmād ekayā kṛtam apy akṛtam eva |
kecid atra “jyeṣṭhayā na vinetarāḥ” (YDh 1.88) [117] iti vacanam anyathā vyācakṣate | jyeṣṭhaiva sahadharmacāriṇī netarāḥ – iti | udāharanti ca tatra viṣṇuvacanam:
agnihotrādiyajñeṣu na dvitīyā sahācaret |
anyathā niṣphalaṃ tasya sviṣṭaiḥ kratuśatair api || iti
etad vyākhyānaṃ baudhāyana-kātyāyanavacanavirodhān nādaraṇīyam | viṣṇuvacanaṃ tv asavarṇadvitīyāviṣayam | satsv agniṣu yā pariṇītā tadviṣāyaṃ vā | naṣṭeṣv agniṣu punarādhāne tasyā api kartṛtvād agnihotrādiṣu sahādhikāraḥ | tatraiva viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
agnihotrādiśuśrūṣāṃ bahubhāryaḥ savarṇayā |
kārayet tadbahutve ca jyeṣṭhayā garhitā na cet ||
yā vā syāt vīrasūr āsām ājñāsaṃpādinī ca yā |
dakṣā priyaṃvadā śuddhā tām atra viniyojayet ||
dinakrameṇa vā karma yathājyaiṣṭhyaṃ svaśaktitaḥ |
vibhajya saha vā kuryuḥ yathājñānaṃ ca śāstravat || iti | (KSm 2.19.3–5)
yadi jyeṣṭhā na garhitā tadā tayā kārayet | garhitā cet kaniṣṭhayā vīrasuvā kārayet | vīrasuvo ‘pi bahvyaś cet tāsām api madhye ājñāsaṃpādanādiguṇayuktāṃ [118] viniyojayet | pratidinam ekā kartum aśaktā cet tadā dinakrameṇa yathājyaiṣṭhyaṃ kārayet | ekasminn api dine yady ekā kṛtsnaṃ kartum aśaktā tadā sarvās tat karma yathājñānaṃ vibhajya kuryuḥ | yat tu kātyāyanenaivoktam,
prathamā dharmapatnī syād dvitīyā rativardhinī |
dṛṣṭam eva phalaṃ tatra nādṛṣṭam upapadyate ||
iti, tad viṣṇuvacanena samānārtham |
itthaṃ saviśeṣo vivāho nirūpitaḥ
atha savarṇāsavarṇāvivāhaprasaṅgena buddhyārūḍhānām anulomapratilomajātānāṃ vyavahāropayogisaṃjñāpratipattyarthaṃ jātibhedo nirūpyate | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
savarṇebhyaḥ savarṇāsu jāyante hi sajātayaḥ |
anindyeṣu vivāheṣu putrāḥ santānavardhanāḥ || iti | (YDh1.90)
sajātayo mātāpitṛsamānajātīyāḥ | manur api |
sarvavarṇeṣu tulyāsu patnīṣv akṣatayoniṣu |
ānulomyena saṃbhūtā jātyā jñeyās ta eva te || iti | (MDh 10.5)
brāhmaṇadampatībhyām utpanno jātyā brāhmaṇo bhavet | evaṃ kṣatriyādiṣv api | devalo ‘pi |
brāhmaṇyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ saṃskṛto brāhmaṇo bhavet |
evaṃ kṣatriyaviṭśūdrā jñeyāḥ svebhyaḥ svayonijāḥ || iti |
asavarṇāsv anulomajān āha manuḥ |
[119]        strīṣv anantajātāsu dvijair utpāditān sutān |
sadṛśān eva tān āhur mātṛdoṣavigarhitān || iti | (MDh 10.6)
ūḍhāyāṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ brāhmaṇād utpanno brāhmaṇasadṛśo na tu mukhyabrāhmaṇaḥ, hīnajātīyamātṛsaṃbandhāt | evam anyatrāpi | te cānulomajā mūrdhāvasiktādijātibhedena ṣaḍvidhāḥ | te ca yājñavalkyena darśitāḥ |
viprān mūrdhāvasikto hi kṣatriyāyāṃ viśaḥ striyām |
abmaṣṭhaḥ śūdryāṃ niṣādo jātaḥ pārasavo ‘pi vā ||
vaiśyāśudryos tu rājanyān māhiṣyograu sutau smṛtau |
vaiśyāt tu karaṇaḥ śūdryāṃ vinnāsv eṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || iti | (YDh 1.91–92)
tatra brāhmaṇāj jātās trayaḥ | kṣatriyāt dvau | vaiśyād ekaḥ | tad uktaṃ manunā |
viprasya triṣu varṇeṣu nṛpater varṇayor dvayoḥ |
vaiśyasya varṇa ekasmina ṣaḍ ete ‘pasadāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | (MDh 10.10)
mukhyapitṛjātyabhāvāt apasadāḥ |
nanu – mūrdhāvasiktatvādīni na jātyantarāṇi anulomajānāṃ mātṛjātīyatvāt | tad āha viṣṇuḥ: [120] “samānavarṇāsu putrāḥ samānavarṇā bhavanti | anulomāsu mātṛsavarṇāḥ | pratilomāsv āryavigarhitāḥ” iti (ViDh 16.1–3) | śaṅkho ‘pi: “kṣatriyāyāṃ brāhaṇenotpannaḥ kṣatriya eva bhavati | kṣatriyād vaiśyāyāṃ vaiśya eva bhavati | vaiśyena śūdryāṃ śūdra eva bhavati” iti |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, anayor vacanayor mātṛjātyuditadharmaprāptyarthatvāt, anyathā bījotkarṣavaiyarthyāpatteḥ | yathā kṣetrāpakarṣa utkṛṣṭajātinivārakaḥ evaṃ bījotkarṣo ‘pi nikṛṣṭajātiṃ kuto na nivārayet | tasmāt jātyantarāṇy eva mūrdhāvasiktatvādīni |
nanu – develenānulomajātayo ‘nyathā varṇitāḥ:
brāhmaṇāt kṣatriyāyāṃ tu savarṇo nāma jāyate |
kṣatriyāc caiva vaiśyāyāṃ jāto ‘mbaṣṭha iti smṛtaḥ || iti |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, ekasyām eva jātau mūrdhāvasikta-savarṇasaṃjñayor vikalpena pravṛttatvāt | evam ambaṣṭādiṣv api | na caikatra saṃjñāvikalpe dṛṣṭāntābhāvaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ | ekatra niṣādapārasavasaṃjñāvikalpasya manu-devala-yājñavalkyair udāhṛtatvāt | pratilomajās tu manunā darśitāḥ |
[121]        kṣatryād viprakanyāyāṃ sūto bhavati jātitaḥ |
vaiśyān māgadhavaidehau rājaviprāṅgnāsutau ||
śūdrād āyagavaḥ kṣttā caṇḍālaś cādhamo nṛṇām |
vaiśyarājanyaviprāsu jāyante varṇasaṃkarāḥ ||
āyogavaś ca kṣattā ca caṇḍālaś cādhamo nṛṇām |
prātilomyena jāyante śūdrād apasadās trayaḥ ||
vaiśyān māgadhavaidehau kṣatriyāt sūta eva tu || iti | (MDh 10.11–17)
devalo ‘pi |
śūdrād āyogavaḥ kṣattā caṇḍālaḥ pratilomajāḥ \
vaiśyāyāṃ kṣatriyāyāṃ ca brāhmaṇyāṃ ca yathākramam ||
tathaiva māgadho vaiśyāj jāto vaidehakas tathā |
brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyāj jātaḥ sūto jātyā ca karmaṇā || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyāt sūto vaiśyād vaidehikas tathā |
śūdrāj jātas tu caṇḍālaḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaḥ ||
kṣatriyā māgadhaṃ vaiśyāt śūdrāt kṣattāram eva ca |
śūdrād āyogavaṃ vaiśyā janayāmāsa vai sutam || iti | (YDh 1.93–94)
varṇānām anulomajānāṃ pratilomajānāṃ ca parasparasāṃkaryeṇotpannāḥ śvapākapulkasakukkuṭādayo jātiviśeṣās tv [122] anekavidhāḥ | te ca tadvṛttayaś ca purāṇasāre prapañcitāḥ | teṣu ca jātiviśeṣeṣūttamādhamabhāvaṃ vivinakti devalaḥ |
teṣāṃ savarṇajāḥ śreṣṭhās tebhyo ‘nvag anulomajāḥ |
antarālā bahirvarṇāḥ patitāḥ pratilomajāḥ || iti |
vijātīyān mithunād utpannā antarālāḥ | te ca dvividhāḥ anulomajāḥ pratilomajāś ca | tatrānulomajāḥ savarṇajebhyo hīnā api na varṇabāhyāḥ, mātṛsamānavarṇatvāt | pratilomajās tu varṇabāhyatvāt patitā adhamāḥ | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
asatsantas tu vijñeyāḥ pratilomānulomajāḥ | iti | (YDh 1.95)
kvacid adhamajāter apy uttamajātiprāptir bhavati | tad āha sa eva |
jātyutkarṣo yuge jñeyaḥ saptame pañcame ‘pi vā | iti | (YDh 1.95)
kūṭasthastrīpuṃsayugam ārabhya parigaṇanāyāṃ pañcame ṣaṣṭhe saptame vānulomena yugme jātir utkṛṣyate | tad yathā | pumān vipraḥ vadhūḥ śūdrā tayor yugmaṃ kūṭastham | tasmād utpannā viṣādī sāpi vipreṇoḍhā tayor yugmaṃ dvitīyam | evaṃ tadutpannāyāṃ vadhvāṃ vipreṇoḍhāyāṃ tṛtīyādiyugmaparamparā bhavati | tatra saptame yugme jātam apatyaṃ brāhmaṇyopetaṃ bhavati | evaṃ vaiśyāviprayugulaṃ kūṭastham | tasmād utpannāmbaṣṭhā tasyāś ca viprasya ca yugmaṃ dvitīyam | evaṃ tadutpannāyā vipreṇoḍhāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ yad yugmaṃ tasmād utpannasya brāhmaṇyaṃ bhavati | tathā kṣatriyāviprayor yugmaṃ kūṭastham | [123] tadutpannā mūrdhāvasiktā | tasyāś ca viprasya ca yugmaṃ dvitīyam | tatparamparāyāṃ pañcamād yugmād utpannasya brāhmaṇyaṃ bhavati | etad uktaṃ bhavati – pañcame ṣaṣṭhe saptame veti vyavasthitavikalpāḥ | kūṭasthayor dampatyoḥ samanantaraikāntaradvyantarajātiyoge pañcamādayo ‘vagantavyāḥ  iti | tathā ca sti śūdrāvaiśyayor vaiśyākṣatriyayoś ca samantaratvāt pañcame, śūdrākṣtriyayos tu ṣaṣṭhe jātir utkṛṣyate | kvacid uttamajāter apy adhamajātiprāptir bhavati | tad āha sa eva |
vyatyaye karmaṇāṃ sāmyaṃ pūrvavac cādharottaram | iti | (YDh 1.96)
viprādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ mukhyavṛttitayā vihitāni yājanapālanapāsupālyadvijaśuśrūṣādīni yāni karmāṇi teṣām āpadi vyatyaye viparyāse sati yadi nivṛttāyām apy āpadi tām adhamavṛttiṃ na parityajet tathā putrapautrādayo ‘pi tāṃ na parityajeyuḥ tadānīṃ pūrvavat pañcamaṣaṣṭhasaptameṣu yugmeṣu jātam apatyaṃ tadvṛttyucitajātisāmyaṃ pratipadyate – iti | tad yathā – brāhmaṇaḥ śūdravṛttyā jīvan yadi putram utpādayati so ‘pi tathaiva ity evaṃ paramparāyāṃ saptamād utpannasya śūdratvaṃ bhavati | evaṃ kṣatriyaḥ śūdravṛttyā jīvan ṣaṣṭhe yugme śūdraṃ janayati | vaiśyas tu pañcame yugme – iti draṣṭavyam | ‘pūrvavat’ – iti ārabhyety arthaḥ | adharottaram iti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ | yathā varṇasāṃkarye prātilomyam adhamam ānulomyam uttamaṃ tathā vṛttisāṃkarye ‘pi | tad yathā | kṣatriyasyāpady api yājanādibrāhmaṇavṛttyupajīvanam adhamam | [124] “na tu kadācij jyāyasīm” (VaDh 2.23) iti vasiṣṭhena niṣiddhatvāt pāśupālyādivaiśyavṛttyupajīvanam uttamam, “ajīvantaḥ svadharmeṇānantarāṃ yavīyasīṃ vṛttim ātiṣṭheran” (VaDh 2.22) iti vasiṣṭhenāpi vidhānād iti | pūrvavac cādharottaram ity asyāparā vyākhyā – vividho hi saṃkaraḥ | varṇasaṃkaraḥ saṃkīrṇasaṃkaro varṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃkaraś ceti | tad yathā – uttamādhamavarṇayor dāmpatyaṃ varṇasaṃkaraḥ | tajjanyayor māhiṣyakaraṇyor dāmpatyaṃ saṃkīrṇasaṃkaraḥ | varṇasaṃkīrṇayor dāmpatyaṃ varṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃkaraḥ | tatra ‘viprān mūrdhāvasiktaḥ’ ityānulomyena varṇasaṃkarajā darśitāḥ | ‘brāhmaṇyāṃ kṣatriyāt sūtaḥ’ iti prātilomyena varṇasaṃkarajā darśitāḥ |
mahiṣyeṇa karaṇyāṃ tu rathakāraḥ prajāyate | (YDh 1.95)
[125] iti saṃkīrṇasaṃkarajā darśitāḥ | idānīm adharottaram ity anena varṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃkarajāḥ pradarśyante | adhare pratilomajāḥ | uttare anulomajāḥ | tad yathā | mūrdhāvasiktāyāṃ saṃkīrṇāyām uttamāyāṃ kṣatriyavaiśyaśūdrair varṇair adhamair utpāditāḥ adhare | niṣādyāṃ saṃkīrṇāyām adhamāyāṃ brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyair uttamair varṇair utpāditā uttare | adhare cottare ca adharottaram | pūrvavad iti padena “asatsantas tu vijñeyāḥ” (YDh 1.95) iti vacanārtho ‘tidiśyate | yathā pūrvavarṇair utpāditā varṇasaṃkarajāḥ saṃkīrṇasaṃkarajāś ca pratilomajā asantaḥ anulomajāś ca santaḥ tathā varṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃkarajā api anulomajāḥ santaḥ pratilomajās tv asanta – iti draṣṭavyam | uktatraividhye varṇasaṃkaraṃ varṇasaṃkīrṇasaṃkaraṃ vāśrityotpadyamānā varṇābhāsāḥ ṣaṣtir bhavanti | saṃkīrṇasaṃkaram āśrityotpadyamānā jātyābhāsā anantāḥ | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
prātilomyānulomyena varṇais tajjaiś ca varṇataḥ |
ṣaṣṭir vānye prajāyante tatprsūtes tv anantatā || iti |
prātilomyānulomyābhyāṃ varṇair utpāditā dvādaśa | ṣaḍ anulomavarṇajāḥ sūta-vaideha-caṇḍāla-māgadha-kṣattra-āyogavāḥ pratilomavarṇajāḥ | itthaṃ varṇajais tair dvādaśabhir varṇasaṃbandhād utpāditā aṣṭacatvāriṃśat | evaṃ ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyayaivopalakṣitā anye [126] varṇābhāsā jāyante | tad yathā | mūrdhāvasiktāmbaṣṭhaniṣādamāhiṣyograkaraṇāḥ ṣaḍ anulomavarṇajāḥ | itthaṃ dvādaśa | tatra mūrdhāvasiktenānulomena kṣatriyāvaiśyāśūdrāsūtpāditās trayaḥ | prātilomyena brāhmaṇyām ekaḥ | ambaṣṭhasyānulomyena dvau, prātilomyena dvau | niṣādasyānulomyenaikaḥ, prātilomyena trayaḥ | māhiṣyasyānulomyena dvau, prātilomyena dvau | ugrasyānulomenaikaḥ, prātilomyena trayaḥ | karaṇasyānulomyenaikaḥ, prātilomyena trayaḥ – iti pūrvaṣaṭkotpāditāś caturviṃśatiḥ | evaṃ sūtrādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ varṇānām ekaikasya catasṛṣu varṇeṣv ekaikaḥ – iti te ‘pi caturviṃśatiḥ | evaṃ militvā ṣaṣṭiḥ saṃpadyate | tebhyaḥ ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyākebhya utpāditair apatyair ābhāsānāṃ saṃkhyāyā ānantyaṃ bhavatīti samāptā prāsaṅgikī jātibhedakathā ||
iti jātibhedanirūpaṇam
vivāhānantarabhāvinaḥ prakṛtāḥ pañcamahāyajñādayaḥ somasaṃsthāntāḥ saṃskārā āhnikavacane ṣaṭkarmavacane ca nirūpitāḥ – iti nātra punar ucyante | athāvaśiṣṭā gṛhasthadharmā nirūpyante | tatropākarmavidhim āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
adhyāyānām upākarma śrāvaṇyāṃ śravaṇena vā |
hastenauṣadhibhāve vā pañcamyāṃ śravaṇasya tu || iti | (YDh 1.142)
adhīyante ity adhyāyāḥ vedāḥ | teṣām upākarma prārambhaḥ | [127] sa ca śrāvaṇamāsasya paurṇamāsyām anyasyāṃ vā śravaṇanakṣatrayuktāyāṃ tithau hastanakṣatrayuktāyāṃ śrāvaṇamāsasya pañcamyāṃ vā kartavyaḥ | yadā tu śrāvaṇamāse oṣadhayo na prādur bhavanti tadā bhādrapade māse proktatithiṣu kuryāt | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “athātaḥ svyādhyāyopākarma śrāvaṇyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā” iti (VaDh 12.9) | manur api |
śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vāpy upākṛtya yathāvidhi |
yuktaś chandāṃsy adhīyīta māsān vipro ‘rdhapañcamān || iti | (MDh 4.95)
ardhaṃ pañcamaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathā, sārdhān caturo māsān ity arthaḥ | yadā punaḥ śrāvaṇyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā śukrāstamayādipratibandhaḥ tadānīm āṣāḍhyāṃ kartavyam | tad uktaṃ kūrmapurāṇe |
śrāvaṇasya tu māsasya paurṇamāsyāṃ dvijottamāḥ |
āṣāḍhyāṃ prauṣṭhapadyāṃ vā vedopākaraṇaṃ smṛtam || iti | (KūPu 1.2.14.62)
baudhāyano ‘pi: “śrāvaṇapaurṇamāsyām āṣāḍhyāṃ vopākṛtya taiṣyāṃ māghyāṃ votsṛjet” (BDh 1.5.12.16) [128] iti | teṣu triṣv api māseṣu pūrṇimā-śraṇa-hastāḥ śākhābhedena vyavasthitāḥ | tad āha gobhilaḥ |
parvaṇy audayike kuryuḥ śrāvaṇyāṃ taittirīyakāḥ |
bahvṛcāḥ śravaṇarkṣe tu hastarkṣe sāmavedinaḥ || iti |
gārgyo ‘pi |
parvaṇy audayike kuryuḥ śrāvaṇyāṃ taittirīyakāḥ |
bahvṛcāḥ śravaṇe kuryur grahasaṃkrāntivarjite || iti |
atra audayike iti parvādiṣu sarvatra saṃbadhyate | parvaṇa audayikatve viśeṣam āha sa eva |
śrāvaṇī paurṇamāsī tu saṅgavāt parato yadi |
tadā tv audayikī grāhyā nānyathaidayikī bhavet || iti |
śravaṇasya tv audayiktavam anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ vyāsena darśitam |
śravaṇena tu yat karma hy uttarāṣāḍhasaṃyute |
saṃvatsarakṛte ‘dhyāyas tatkṣaṇād eva naśyati ||
[129]        dhaniṣṭhāsaṃyute kuryāc chrāvaṇaṃ karma yad bhavet |
tat karma saphalaṃ jñeyam upākaraṇasaṃjñitam || iti |
śraveṇe yat karma vihitaṃ tad uttarāṣāḍhasaṃyute na kuryāt | yadi kuryāt tadā naśyatīti yojanīyam | “grahasaṃkrāntivarjite” ity yad uktaṃ tatra viśeṣam āha gārgyaḥ |
ardharātrād adhastāc cet saṃkrāntir grahaṇaṃ tathā |
upākarma na kurvīta parataś cen na doṣakṛt ||
yatrārdharātrād arvāk ced grahaḥ saṃkrama eva vā |
nopākarma tadā kuryāt śrāvaṇyāṃ śravaṇe ‘pi ca || iti |
yat tu — karkaṭamāse nopākarma kartavyam | tathā ca smṛtyantare |
vedopākaraṇe prāpte kulīre saṃsthite ravau |
upākarma na kartavyaṃ kartavyaṃ siṃhayuktake || iti |
— tad etad deśāntaraviṣayam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
narmadottarabhāge tu kartavyaṃ siṃhasaṃyute |
karkaṭe saṃsthite bhānāv upākuryāt tu dakṣiṇe ||
[130] iti |
nanu – upākaraṇaṃ brahmacāridharmaḥ, “upākṛtyādhīyīta” iti tasya grahaṇādhyayanāṅgatvapratīteḥ | grahaṇādhyayanaṃ ca brahmacāriṇa eva, “vedam adhītya snāyāt” iti snānāt prācīnatvāvagamanāt |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, gṛhasthasyāpi grahaṇādhyayane ‘dhikārasaṃbhavāt | ata eva “adhīyīta” ity anuvṛttau śaunaka āha: “samāvṛtto brahmacārikalpena | yathānyāyam itare | jāyopeto ‘py evam” iti | asyārthaḥ – yena niyamaviśeṣeṇa yukto brahmacārī adhīte tenaiva niyamena samāvṛtto ‘py adhīyīta | samāvṛttād itare brahmacāriṇas tu yathānyāyaṃ svavidhyuktaprakāreṇādhīyīran | tathā jāyopeto gṛhastho ‘pi brahmacārivan niyamopeto ‘dhīyīteti | na ca samāvṛttagṛhasthayor grahaṇādhyayanādhikāre “vedam adhītya snāyāt” iti virudhyeta – iti śaṅkanīyam | tasya vacanasya vidyāsnātakaviṣayatvāt | ata eva,
[131]        vedaṃ vratāni vā pāraṃ nītvā hy ubhayam eva vā | (YDh 1.51)
iti pakṣadvayopanyāso darśitaḥ | upākaraṇasya gṛhasthadharmatvābhyupagame manu-yājñavalkyasmṛtyor gṛhasthadharmaprakaraṇapāṭho ‘py anugṛhīto bhavati | upākaraṇasyetikartavyatā kārṣṇājininā darśitā |
upākarmaṇi cotsarge yathākālaṃ sametya ca |
ṛṣīn darbhamayān kṛtvā pūjayet tarpayet tataḥ || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
gautamādīn ṛṣīn sapta kṛtvā dharmamayān punaḥ |
pūjayitvā yathāśakti tarpayed vaṃśam uddharan || iti |
ity upākarma
athotsarjanam | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pauṣamāsasya rohiṇyām aṣṭakāyām athāpi vā |
jalānte chandasāṃ kuryād utsargaṃ vidhivad bahiḥ || iti | (YDh 1.143)
manur api |
puṣye tu chandasāṃ kuryād bahir utsarjanaṃ dvijaḥ |
māghaśuklasya vā prāpte pūrvāhṇe prathame ‘hani || (MDh 4.96)
[132] iti | yadā śrāvaṇyām upākarma tadā puṣyamāsasya śuklapratipadi pūrvāhṇe, yadi prauṣṭhapadyām upākarma tadā māghasyeti vyavasthito ‘yaṃ vikalpaḥ | utsṛṣṭasyāpipunar adhyayanaṃ prāg upākaraṇāt kālaviśeṣe vidadhāti manuḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ tu chandāṃsi śukleṣu niyataḥ paṭhet |
vedāṅgāni rahasyaṃ ca kṛṣṇapakṣeṣu saṃpaṭhet || iti | (MDh 4.98)
kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
chandāṃsy ūrdhvam ato ‘bhyaset śuklapakṣeṣu vai dvijaḥ |
vedāṅgāni purāṇāni kṛṣṇapakṣeṣu mānavaḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.14.65)
yadi bhāvivighnabhayāt sahasādhyetavyam iti buddhiḥ tadā saṃvatsarāne prāg upākaraṇād utsṛjet, “yat svādhyāyam adhīte ‘bdam” iti śruteḥ | upākaraṇotsarjane praśaṃsati kātyāyanaḥ |
pratyabdaṃ yad upākarma sotsargaṃ vidhivad dvijaiḥ |
kriyate chandasāṃ tena punar āpyāyanaṃ bhavet ||
ayātayāmaiś chandobhir yat karma kriyate dvijaiḥ |
krīḍamānair api sadā tat teṣāṃ siddhikārakam || (KSm 3.27.17–18)
[133] anye ‘pi dharmāḥ kūrmapurāṇe darśitāḥ |
nādhārmikair vṛte grāme na vyādhibahule bhṛśam |
na śūdrarājye nivasen na pāṣaṇḍijanair vṛte ||
himavadvindhyayor madhyaṃ pūrvapaścimayoḥ śubham |
muktvā samudrayor deśaṃ nānyatra nivaset dvijaḥ ||
kṛṣṇo vā yatra carati mṛgo nityaṃ svabhāvataḥ |
puṇyāś ca viśruto nadyas tatra vā nivaset dvijaḥ ||
parastriyaṃ na bhāṣeta nāyājyaṃ yājayed budhaḥ |
na devāyatanaṃ gacchet kadācic cāpradakṣiṇam ||
na vījayed vā vastreṇa samavāyaṃ ca varjayet |
naiko ‘dhvānaṃ prapadyeta nādhārmikajanaiḥ saha ||
na ninded yoginaḥ siddhān vratino vā yatīṃs tathā || iti | (KūPu 1.2.16.23–94)
manur api |
devatānāṃ guro rājñaḥ snātakācāryayos tathā |
nākrāmet kāmataś chāyāṃ bubhruṇo dīkṣitasya ca ||
kṣatriyaṃ caiva sarpaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ ca bahuśrutam |
nāvamanyeta vai bhūṣṇuḥ kṛśān ap kadācana ||
ā mṛtyoḥ śriyam anvicchen naināṃ manyeta durlabhām ||
[134]        satyam brūyāt priyaṃ brūyān na brūyāt satyam apriyam |
priyaṃ ca nānṛtaṃ brūyād eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ ||
nātikalyaṃ nātisāyaṃ nātimadhyaṃdine sthite |
nājñātena samaṃ gacchen naiko na vṛṣalaiḥ saha ||
hīnāṅgān atiriktāṅgān vidyāhīnān vayo’dhikān |
rūpadraviṇahīnāṃś ca jātihīnāṃś ca nākṣipet ||
vairiṇaṃ nopaseveta sahāyaṃ caiva vairiṇaḥ |
adhārmikaṃ taskaraṃ ca parasyaiva ca yoṣitam || iti | (MDh 4.130–41)
mārkeṇḍeyo ‘pi |
asadālāpam anṛtaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ vivarjayet |
asacchāstram asadvādam asatsevāṃ ca putraka ||
na mlecchabhāṣāṃ śikṣeta na paśyed ātmanaḥ śakṛt |
nādhitiṣṭhec chakṛnmūtrakeśabhasmakapālikāḥ ||
tuṣāṅgārāsthiśīrṇāni rajjuvastrādikāni ca |
varjeyen mārjanīreṇuṃ nāpeyaṃ ca pibed dvijaḥ || iti |
sāmānyena ca dharmaṃ saṃkṣpyāha manuḥ |
yenāsya pitaro yātā yena yātāḥ pitāmahāḥ |
tena yāyāt satāṃ mārgaṃ tena gacchan na riṣyati ||
[135]        yat karma kurvato ‘sya syāt paritoṣo ‘ntarātmanaḥ |
tat prayatnena kurvīta viparītaṃ tu varjayet || iti | (MDh 4.161–78)
itthaṃ brahmacārigṛhasthāśramadharmau nirūpitau
atha vānaprasthāśramo nirūpyate |
nanu – kecana gārhasthyavyatiriktam āśramāntaraṃ necchanti udāharanti ca tatra gautamasmṛtim: “aikāśramyaṃ tv ācāryāḥ pratyakṣavidhānād gārhasthyasya” iti (GDh 3.36) | ācāryās tu gārhasthyam evāśramo nānyaḥ kaścid astīti manyante | hetuṃ cācakṣate gārhasthyasya pratyakṣaśrutiṣu vidhānād itarasya tadabhāvāt – iti | yathā hi – bahvṛcāḥ “agnim īḷe” (RV 1.1.1.1) ity ārabhya mantrabrāhmaṇātmake kṛtsne ‘pi vede hotṛkartavyam evāmananti | yajurvedinaś ca “iṣe tvā” (TS 1.1.1.1) ityādinā adhvaryukartavyam | sāmagā api “agna āyāhi” (SV 1.1.1.1) ityādinodgātṛkartavyam | hotrādayaś ca gṛhasthā eva | tathā cādhīyamāneṣu pratyakṣavedeṣu gṛhasthakartavyābhidhānena tadāśramavidhiḥ [136) parikalpyate na tv evam itarāśramavidhikalpakaṃ kiṃcid paśyāmaḥ | ata eva “yāvajjīvam agnihotraṃ juhoti” iti śrutiḥ kṛtsnaṃ puruṣāyuṣaṃ gṛhikarmasv eva viniyuṅkte | śrutyantaraṃ ca: “etad vai jarāmaryaṃ sattraṃ yad agnihotram | jarayā vā hy evāsmān mucyate mṛtyunā vā” iti | na caivaṃ sati kathaṃ brahmacaryāśramāṅgīkāraḥ śaṅkanīyam, naiṣṭhikasya pakṣakoṭiniḥkṣiptatvād upakurvāṇakasya vratitvena āśramitvābhāvāt | yadā karmitvenābhimatayor brahmacārivānaprasthayor īdṛśī gatiḥ tadā kaiva kathā kṛtsnakarmatyāgino yateḥ | tasmāt gārhasthyam eka evāśramaḥ — ity ācāryāṇāṃ pakṣaḥ |
atrocyate | asti hi caturṇām āśramāṇāṃ pratyakṣaśrutividhānam | tathā ca jābālā āmananti: “brahmacaryaṃ samāpya gṛhī bhavet | gṛhī bhūtvā vanī bhavet | vanī bhūtvā pravrajet” (JābUp 4) [137] iti | ācāryās tu rāgiṇam abhipretya tasyordhvaretaḥsu naiṣṭhikabrahmacāryādiṣu triṣv āśrameṣv anadhikāraṃ manyamānā gārhasthyam eva varṇayāmāsuḥ | yat tu kṛtsne ‘pi vede gṛhasthadharmasyaivāmnānam ity uktam, tad ayuktam, vānaprasthasyāpi sadārasyāgnihotrādisadbhāvāt | naiṣṭhikabrahmacāridharmas tu chandogaiḥ paṭhyate: “brahmacāry ācāryakulavāsī tṛtīyo ‘tyantam ātmānam ācāryakule ‘vasādayan” (ChU 2.23.2) iti | upakurvāṇakadharmāḥ sarvaśākhāsūpanayanaprakaraṇeṣu prasiddhāḥ | yatidharmāś copaniṣadbhāge | ato yāvajjīvādiśruteḥ kāmiviṣayatvenāśramāntarāṇi na cāpalapituṃ śaykante | sādhitāś cottaramīmāṃsāyāṃ catvāra āśramāḥ |
tasmāt kramaprāpto vānaprasthāśramaḥ prastūyate | tatra yājñavalkyas taṃ vidhatte |
sutavinyastapatnīkas tayā vānugato vanam |
vānaprastho brahmacārī sāgniḥ sopāsano vrajet || iti | (YDh 3.45)
vānaprastho bubhūṣuḥ svasya brahmacaryaniyamena patnyā anupayogāt tāṃ rakṣaṇīyatvena putreṣu niḥkṣipya vanaṃ vrajet | yadā sāpi niyatā satī patiśuśrūṣāṃ kāmayate tadā tayā saha vanaṃ vrajet | tasmin pakṣe vaitānikagṛhyābhyām agnibhyāṃ saha [137] gacchet | sutanikṣepapakṣe tv ātmani agnīn samāropya pravrajet | tad āha chāgaleyaḥ: “apatnīkaḥ samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt” iti | tādṛśo ‘raṇyaṃ gatvā vaikhanasasūtroktamārgeṇāgnim ādadhyāt | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “vānaprastho jaṭilaś cīrājinavāsāḥ | na phālakṛṣṭam adhitiṣṭhet | akṛṣṭaṃ mūlaphalaṃ saṃcinvīta | ūrdhvaretāḥ | kṣmāśayaḥ | dadyād eva na prtigṛhṇīyāt | ūrdhvaṃ pañcabhyo māsebhyaḥ | śrāmaṇakenāgnim ādhāyāhitāgniḥ syāt | vṛkṣamūlaniketana ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍbhyo māsebhyo ‘nagnir aniketaḥ | [139] dadyād devarṣipitṛmanuṣyebhyaḥ sa gacchet svargam ānantyam” iti (VaDh 9.1–13) | śrāmaṇakaṃ tapasvidharmapratipādakaṃ vaikhānasasūtram | akṛṣṭamūlam āhāratvena bruvan grāmyāhāraparityāgaṃ sūcayati | ata eva manuḥ |
saṃtyajya grāmyam āhāraṃ sarvaṃ caiva paricchadam |
putreṣu bhāyāṃ nikṣipya vanaṃ gacchet sahaiva vā || iti | (MDh 6.3)
gṛhasthasya vanapraveśāvasaram āha yamaḥ |
dvitīyam āyuṣo bhāgam uṣitvā tu gṛhe dvijaḥ |
tṛtīyam āyuṣo bhāgaṃ gṛhamedhī vane vaset ||
utpādya dharmataḥ putrān iṣṭvā yajñaiś ca śaktitaḥ |
dṛṣṭvāpatyasya cāpatyaṃ brāhmaṇo ‘raṇyam āviśet || iti |
atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ traivarṇikopalakṣaṇārtham, “uṣitvā tu gṛha dvijaḥ” ity upakramānusārāt | manur api |
gṛhasthas tu yadā paśyed vilīpalitam ātmanaḥ |
apatyasyaiva cāpatyaṃ tadāraṇyaṃ samāśrayet || iti | (MDh 6.2)
śaṅkhalikhitāv api: [140] “putrān utpādya saṃskṛtya vedam adhyāpya vṛttiṃ vidhāya dāraiḥ saṃyojya guṇavati putre kuṭumbam āveśya kṛtaprasthānaliṅgo vṛttiviśeṣān anukrāmet | kramaśo yāyāvarāṇāṃ vṛttim upāsya vanam āśrayed uttarāyaṇe pūrvapakṣe” iti | etac cāśramasamuccayapakṣe draṣṭavyam | asamuccayapakṣe tv akṛtagārhasthyo ‘pi vānaprasthe ‘dhikriyate | tad āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “catvāra āśramāḥ | brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivājakāḥ | teṣāṃ vedam adhītya vedau vedān vāviśīrṇabrahmacaryo yam icchet tam āvaset” iti (VaDh 7.1–3) | āpastambo ‘pi: “catvāra āśramā gārhasthyaṃ ācāryakulaṃ maunaṃ vānaprastham” ity upakramya, “yat kāmayeta tad ārabheta” (ĀpDh 2.9.21.1) ity upasaṃharati | vanapratiṣṭhasya kartavyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aphālakṛṣṭenāgnīṃś ca pitṝn devātithīn api |
bhṛtyāṃś ca tarpayec chaśvaj jaṭālomabhṛd ātmavān || iti | (YDh 3.46)
aphālakṛṣṭaṃ śākamūlanīvārādi | tathā ca manuḥ |
[141]        munyannair vividhair medhyaiḥ śākamūlaphalena ca |
etair eva mahāyajñān nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || iti | (MDh 6.5)
na ca – brahmacārividhurayor anagnikayor vanasthayoḥ katham agnīnāṃ tarpaṇam – iti vācyam, vaikhānasaśāstroktasyāgneḥ sadbhāvāt | na ca – aphālakṛṣṭanīvārādinā puroḍāśakaraṇe “vrīhibhir yajeta” iti śrutir bādhyeta – iti śaṅkanīyam, vrīhīṇām apy aphālakṛṣṭānāṃ saṃbhavāt | tasmād akṛṣṭapacyair vrīhyādibhir vaitānikaṃ karma kuryāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
vaitānikaṃ ca juhuyād agnihotraṃ yathāvidhi |
darśam askandayan parva paurṇamāsaṃ viśeṣataḥ ||
ṛkṣeṣṭyāgrayaṇaṃ caiva cāturmāsyāni cāharet |
turāyaṇaṃ ca kramaśo dakṣasyāyanam eva ca ||
vāsantaśāradair medhyair munyannaiḥ svayam āhṛtaiḥ |
puroḍāśaṃ caruṃ caiva nirvaped vidhipūrvakam || iti | (MDh 6.9–11)
medhyair yajñārhair munyannair akṛṣṭapacyair ity arthaḥ | saṃgrāhyadravyasyeyattām āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[142]        ahno māsasya ṣaṇṇāṃ vā tathā saṃvatsarasya vā |
arthasya saṃcayaṃ kuryāt kṛtam āśvayuje tyajet || iti | (YDh 3.47)
ekadinasādhyasya karmaṇo yāvat paryāptaṃ tāvato ‘rthasya saṃcayaṃ kuryāt | evam ekamāsasaṃvatsarapakṣe ‘pi yojanīyam | tatra yadi kiṃcit saṃcitam avaśiṣyet tat sarvam āśvayujyāṃ tyajet | yad āha viṣṇuḥ: “māsanicayaḥ saṃvatsaranicayo vā | saṃvatsaranicayī pūrvaṃ nicayam āśvayujyāṃ jahyāt” iti | pūrvaṃ saṃtyajya tato nūtanaṃ saṃcinuyāt |
manur api |
tyajed āśvayuje māse munyannaṃ pūrvasaṃcitam |
jīrṇāni caiva vāsāṃsi śākamūlaphalāni ca ||
sadyaḥprakṣālako vā syān māsasaṃcayiko ‘pi vā |
ṣaṇmāsanicayo vāpi samānicaya eva vā || iti | (MDh 6.15–18)
tatra varjyān āha sa eva |
varjayen madhumāṃsaṃ ca bhaumāni kavakāni ca |
bhūstṛṇaṃ śigrukaṃ caiva śleṣmātakaphalāni ca ||
[143]        na phālakṛṣṭam aśnīyād utsṛṣṭam api kenacit |
na grāmajājāny ārto ‘pi puṣpāṇi ca phalāni ca || iti | (MDh 6.14, 16)
kavakāni chatrākāṇi | taponiyamam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dāntas triṣavaṇasnāyī nivṛttaś ca pratigrahāt |
svādhyāyavān dānaśīlaḥ sarvasattvahite rataḥ ||
dantolūkhalikaḥ kālapakvāśī vāśmakuṭṭakaḥ |
śrautaṃ smārthaṃ phalasnehaiḥ karma kuryāt kriyās tathā ||
cāndrāyaṇair nayet kālaṃ kṛcchrair vā vartayet sadā |
pakṣe gate cāpy aśnīyān māse vāhani vā gate ||
svapyād bhūmau śucī rātrau divā saṃprapadair nayet |
sthānāsanavihārair vā yogābhyāsena vā tathā ||
grīṣme pañcāgnimadhyastho varṣāsu sthaṇḍileśayaḥ |
ārdravāsās tu hemante śaktyā vāpi tapaś caret || iti | (YDh 3.48–52)
dantā evolūkhalaṃ nistuṣīkaraṇasādhanaṃ tas yasyāsti sa dantolūkhalikaḥ | bāhyolūkhalādisādhananirapekṣa ity arthaḥ | [144] kālapakvaṃ badareṅgudapanasaphalādi | aśmanā kuṭṭanam avahananaṃ yasya so ‘śmakuṭṭakaḥ | phalasenho likucamadhukādimedhyataruphalajanmāni tailāni | kriyāḥ bhojanābhyañjanādyāḥ | viṣṇur api: “puṣpāśī | phalāśī | mūlāśī | śākāśī | parṇāśī | yavānnaṃ pakṣāntayor vā sakṛd aśnīyāt | (ViDh 95.7–12) iti | kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
ekapādena tiṣṭheta marīcīn vā pibet sadā |
pañcāgnidhūmapo vā syād ūṣmapaḥ somapo ‘tha vā ||
payaḥ pibet śuklapakṣe kṛṣṇapakṣe c gomayam |
śīrṇaparṇāśano vā syāt kṛcchrair vā vartayet sadā ||
atharvaśiraso ‘dhyetā vedāntābhyāsatatparaḥ |
yamān seveta satataṃ niyamāṃś cāpy atandritaḥ ||
[145]        jitendriyo jitakrodhas tattvajñānavicintakaḥ |
brahmacārī bhaven nityaṃ na patnīṃ pratisaṃśrayet ||
yas tu patnyā vanaṃ gatvā maithunaṃ kāmataś caret |
tad vrataṃ tasya lupyeta prāyaścittīyate dvijaḥ ||
naktaṃ cānnaṃ samaśnīyād divā cāhṛtya śaktitaḥ |
caturthakāliko vā syāt syād vā cāṣṭamakālikaḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇavidhānair vā śukle kṛṣṇe ca vartayet |
pakṣe pakṣe samaśnīyād yavāgūṃ kvathitāṃ sakṛt ||
puṣpamūlaphalair vāpi kevalair vartayet sadā |
svābhāvikaiḥ svayaṃ śīrṇair vaikhānasamate sthitaḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.27.16–34)
agniparicaryāyām akṣamaṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
agnīn vāpy ātmasāt kṛtvā vṛkṣāvāso mitāśanaḥ |
vānaprasthagṛheṣv eva yātrārthaṃ bhaikṣyam ācaret ||
grāmād āhṛtya vā grāsān aṣṭau bhuñjīta vāgyataḥ || iti | (Ydh 3.54–55)
manur api |
agnīn ātmani vaitānān samāropya yathāvidhi |
anagnir aniketaḥ syān munir mūlaphalāśanaḥ ||
aprayatnaḥ sukhārtheṣu brahmacārī dharāśayaḥ |
gṛhamedhiṣu cānyeṣu dvijeṣu vanavāsiṣu ||
[146]        grāmād āhṛtya vāśnīyād aṣṭau grāsān vane vasan |
pratigṛhya puṭenaiva pāṇinā śakalena vā ||
etāś cānyāś ca seveta dīkṣā vipro vane vasan |
āsāṃ maharṣicaryāṇāṃ tyaktvānyatamayā tanum |
vītaśokabhayo vipro brahmaloke mahīyate || iti | (MDh 6.25–32)
nanu – aṣṭagrāsavidhāne “ṣoḍaśāraṇyavāsinaḥ’ (Āp/dh 2.4.9.13) iti vacanaṃ virudhyeta |
tan na, śaktāśaktaviṣayatvena vyavasthopapatteḥ | sarvānuṣṭhānāsamarthaṃ praty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vāyubhakṣaḥ prāgudīcīṃ gacched ā varṣmasaṃkṣayāt | iti | (YDh 3.55)
kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
mahāprasthānikaṃ vāsau kuryād anaśanaṃ tu vā |
agnipraveśam anyad vā brahmāparṇavidhau sthitaḥ ||
yas tu samyag imam āśramaṃ śivaṃ saṃśrayaty aśivapuñjanāśanam |
tāpahantṛpadam aiśvaraṃ paraṃ yāti yatra jagato ‘sya saṃsthitiḥ ||
iti (KūPu 1.2.27.38–39)
itthaṃ vānaprasthāśramo nirūpitaḥ
[147]
atha caturthāśramo nirūpyate | tatra manuḥ |
vaneṣu tu vihṛtyaivaṃ tṛtīyaṃ bhāgam āyuṣaḥ |
caturtham āyuṣo bhāgaṃ tyaktvā saṅgān parivrajet || iti | (MDh 6.33)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
vanād gṛhād vā kṛtveṣṭiṃ sārvavedasadakṣiṇām |
prājāpatyāṃ tadante tān agnīn āropya cātmani ||
adhītavedo japakṛt putravān annado ‘gnimān |
śaktyā ca yajjakṛn mokṣe manaḥ kuryāt tu nānyathā || iti | (YDh 3.56–57)
āśramacatuṣṭayasamuccayam abhipretya vanān mokṣe manaḥ kuryād ity uktam | āśramatrayasamuccayābhiprāyeṇa gṛhad veti pakṣāntaropanyāsaḥ |
nanu – atrāpi catuṣṭayasamuccaya evābhipreyatām | parivrājyānantaraṃ vānaprasthyānuṣṭhātuṃ śakyatvāt |
maivam, brahmacaryādīnāṃ caturṇām āśramāṇām ārohasya pratiniyatatvāt | tathā ca jābālā āśramāṇām āhoham āmananti: “brahmacaryaṃ samāpya gṛhī bhavet | gṛhī bhūtvā vanī bhavet | vanī bhūtvā pravrajet” (JābUp 4) [148] iti | na tv evam avarohaḥ kvacid apy āmnātaḥ | pratyutāvarohaṃ dakṣo niṣedhati |
trayāṇām ānulomyaṃ syāt prātilomyaṃ na vidyate |
prātilomyena yo yāti na tasmāt pāpakṛttamaḥ ||
yo gṛhāśramam āsthāya brahmacārī bhavet punaḥ |
na yatir na vanasthaś ca sa sarvāśramavarjitaḥ || iti | (DaSm 1.9–13)
yadi gṛhī kathaṃcit pratyavaruhya brahmacārī bhavet tadāsau sarvāśramabahiṣkṛtaḥ, ārūḍhapatitatvāt | ato na vanasthādibhir āśramavācibhiḥ śabdair abhilapyo bhavati | ayaṃ cāvarohābhāva uttaramīmāṃsāyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāye varṇitaḥ | “vanād gṛhād vā” ity atra “brahmacaryād vā” ity api draṣṭavyam | yadā janmāntarānuṣṭhitasukṛtaparipākavaśāt bālya eva vairāgyam upajāyate tadānīm akṛtodvāho brahmacaryād eva pravrajet | tathā ca jābālaśrutiḥ: [149] “yadi vetarathā brahmacaryād eva pravrajed gṛhād vā vanād vā” iti (JābUp 4) | pūrvam aviraktaṃ jābālaṃ praty āśramacatuṣṭayasamuccayam āyurvibhāgenopanyasya, viraktaṃ uddiśya yadi veti pakṣāntaropanyāsaḥ | itaratheti bālya eva sati vairāgya ity arthaḥ | akṛtodvāhasya saṃnyāso nṛsiṃhapurāṇe darśitaḥ |
yasyaitāni suguptāni jihvopasthodaraṃ śiraḥ |
saṃnyased akṛtodvāho brāhmaṇo brahmacaryavān || iti | (NṛPu 58.36)
aṅgirā apy āha |
saṃsāram eva niḥsāraṃ dṛṣṭvā sāradidṛkṣayā |
pravrajaty akṛtodvāhaḥ paraṃ vairāgyam āśritaḥ ||
pravrajed brahmacaryeṇa pravrajec ca gṛhād api |
vanād vā pravrajed vidvān āturo vātha duḥkhitaḥ || iti |
duḥkhito vyādhicoravyāghrādyupadrutaḥ | āturo mumūrṣuḥ | tatra mahābhāratam |
utpanne saṅkaṭe ghore coravyāghrādisaṅkaṭe |
bhayabhītasya saṃnyāsam aṅgirā munir abravīt ||
āturāṇāṃ ca saṃnyāse na vidhir naiva ca kriyā |
preṣamātraṃ samuccārya saṃnyāsaṃ tatra kīrtayet ||
[150] iti |
nanu – brahmacaryād eva pravrajyāṅgīkāre manuvacanāni virudhyeran |
ṛṇāni trīṇy apākṛtya mano mokṣe niveśayet |
anapākṛtya mokṣaṃ tu sevamāno vrajaty adhaḥ ||
adhītya vidhivad vedān putrān utpādya dharmataḥ |
iṣṭvā ca śaktito yajñair mano mokṣe niveśayet ||
anadhītya guror vedān anutpādya tathātmajān |
aniṣṭvā caiva yajñaiś ca mokṣaṃ icchan vrajaty adhaḥ || iti | (MDh 6.35–37)
ṛaṇatrayaṃ śrutyā darśitam: “jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇas tribhir ṛṇavān jāyate | brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhyo yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ | eṣa vā anṛṇo yaḥ putrī yajvā brahmacārī vāsi” iti (BDh 2.9.26.7) | yadā svargaprāpakaḥ pitṛyāṇamārgo ‘py ṛṇāpākaraṇam antareṇa na saṃbhavati tadā kaiva kathā mokṣamārge | ata eva mantravarṇaḥ:
anṛṇā atho ‘sminn anṛṇāḥ parasmin tṛtīye loke anṛṇā syām |
ye devayānā uta pitṛyāṇāḥ sarvān patho anṛṇā akṣiyema ||
[151] iti | brāhmaṇam api: “sarvān lokān anṛṇo ‘nusaṃcarati” iti |
maivam – aviraktaviṣayatvād eteṣāṃ vacanānām | ata eva viraktasya pravrajyāyāṃ kālavilambaṃ niṣedhati jābālaśrutiḥ: “yad ahar eva virajet tad ahar eva pravrajet” iti (JābUp 4) |
nanu – uktarītyā brahmacaryādiṣu āśrameṣu yathāvad dharmānuṣṭhāyināṃ tat tad āśramāt pravrajet – iti pratīyate | tathā sati snātakavidhurādīnām anāśramiṇām āśramiṇāṃ ca keṣāṃcit kenacit pratibandhena vihitadharmānanuṣṭhāyināṃ saty api vairāgye saṃnyāso na prāpnuyāt |
maivam – teṣāṃ pratyakṣaśrutyaiva tadvidhānāt: “atha punar vratī vāvratī vā snātako vāsnātako vā utsannāgnir anagniko vā yad ahar eva virajet tad ahar eva pravrajet” iti (JābUp 4) | yamo ‘pi |
punardārakriyābhāve mṛtabhāryaḥ parivrajet |
vanastho dhūtapāpo vā paraṃ panthānam āśrayet || iti |
snātakavidhurādīnām antarālavartinām āśramanirapekṣair [152] japopavāsatīrthayātrādikarmabhiś cittaśuddhisaṃbhavena mokṣāśrame ‘dhikāro ‘stīti tṛtīye ‘dhyāye mīmāṃsitam | dharmalope ‘py anutāpavatas tatprāyaścittatvena saṃnyāsaḥ saṃbhavati | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
ye ca santānajā doṣā ye ca syuḥ karmasaṃbhavāḥ |
saṃnyāsas tān dahet sarvān mūṣāgnir iva kāñcanam || iti |
manur api |
mṛttoyaiḥ śudhyate śodhyaṃ nadī vegena śudhyati |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭāḥ saṃnyāsena dvijottamaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.108)
atra kecit – brāhmaṇasyaiva saṃnyāsādhikāro na kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ ity āhuḥ | udāharanti ca śrutismṛtī | tatra vājasaneyakabrāhmaṇam: “etaṃ vai tam ātmānaṃ viditvā brāhmaṇāḥ putraiṣaṇāyāś ca vittaiṣaṇāyāś ca lokaiṣaṇāyāś ca vyutthāyātha bhikṣācaryaṃ caranti” iti (BāUp 3.5.3) | manur apy upakramopasaṃhārayor brāhmaṇaśabdaṃ prayuṅkte:
ātmany agnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhād || (MDh 6.38)
ity upakramaḥ,
[153]        eṣa vo ‘bhihito dharmo brāhmaṇasya caturvidhaḥ | (MDh 6.97)
ity upasaṃhāraḥ | nārado ‘pi |
prathamād āśramād vāpi virakto bhavasāgarāt |
brāhmaṇo mokṣam anvicchaṃs tyaktvā saṅgān parivrajet ||
iti | yogiyājñavalkyo ‘pi |
catvāro brāhmaṇasyoktā āśramāḥ śruticoditāḥ |
kṣatriyasya trayaḥ proktā dvāv eko vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ || (YogY 1.28)
iti | vāmanapurāṇe ‘pi |
catvāra āśramāś caite brāhmaṇasya prakīrtitāḥ |
gārhasthyaṃ brahmacaryaṃ ca vānaprasthaṃ trayo ‘śramāḥ ||
kṣatriyasyāpi kathitā ya ācārā dvijasya hi |
brahmacaryaṃ ca gārhasthyam āśramadvitayaṃ viśaḥ ||
gārhasthyam ucitaṃ tv ekaṃ śūdrasya kṣaṇadācara || (VāyPu 14.16–18)
iti | nanu – śūdrasyāśrama eva nāsti,
catvāra āśramās tāta teṣu śūdras tu nārhati |
iti niṣedhāt | tataḥ kathaṃ tasya gārhasthyāṅgīkāraḥ | ucyate – samantraka eva vivāho niṣidhyate, na tv amantrakaḥ | anyathā vivāhaprakaraṇodāhṛtāni śūdrāviṣayāṇi vacanāni pañcamahāyajñādigṛhasthadharmeṣu śūdrādhikāravacanāni ca virudhyeran | tasmā [154] asti śūdrasya gārhasthyam | saṃnyāsad tūktarītyā viprasyaiva | ata eva smṛtyantare kāṣāyadaṇḍādiliṅgadhāraṇaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor niṣiddham |
mukhajānām ayaṃ dharmo yad viṣṇor liṅgadhāraṇam |
bāhujātorujātānāṃ nāyaṃ dharmo vidhīyate ||
iti |
apare punaḥ saṃnyāsaṃ traivarṇikādhikāram icchanti, adhītavedasya dvijātimātrasya samuccayavikalpābhyām āśramacatuṣṭayasya bahusmṛtiṣu vidhānāt | ata eva yājñavalkyena saṃnyāsaprakaraṇe dvijaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ |
saṃnirudhyendriyagrāmaṃ rāgadveṣau prahāya ca |
bhayaṃ hṛtvā ca bhūtānām amṛtībhavati dvijaḥ || (YDh 3.61)
iti | smṛtyantaraṃ tu śṛṅgagrāhikayaiva varṇatrayasya saṃnyāsaṃ vidadhāti |
ṛṇatayam apākṛtya nirmamo nirahaṃkṛtiḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vātha vaiśyo vā pravrajed gṛhāt ||
iti | kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi dvijagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam |
agnīn ātmani saṃsthāpya dvijaḥ pravrajito bhavet |
yogābhyāsarataḥ śānto brahmavidyāparāyaṇaḥ || (KūPu 1.2.28.2)
iti | yāni pūrvodāhṛtavacanāni tāni kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ [155] kāṣāyadaṇḍaniṣedhaparāṇi | tathā ca “mukhajānām” iti vacanam udāhṛtam | baudhāyano ‘pi |
brāhmaṇānām ayaṃ dharmo yad viṣṇor liṅgadhāraṇam |
rājanyavaiśyayor neti dattātreyamuner vacaḥ ||
iti | parivrāḍbubhūṣuḥ sarvasvadakṣiṇāṃ prājāpatyām iṣṭiṃ nirvapet | tad āha manuḥ |
prājāpatyāṃ nirūpyeṣṭiṃ sarvavedasadakṣiṇām |
ātmany agnīn samāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajed gṛhāt || (MDh 6.38)
iti | yad vā āgneyīm iṣṭiṃ kuryāt | tad uktaṃ śrutyā: “tad vaike prājāpatyam eveṣṭiṃ kurvanti tad u tathā na kuryāt āgneyīm eva kuryāt | agnir hi prāṇaḥ prāṇam evaitayā karoti” (JābUp 4) iti | kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
prājāpatyāṃ nirūpyeṣṭim āgneyīm atha vā punaḥ |
antaḥpakvakaṣāyo ‘sau brahmāśramam upāśrayet || (KūPu 1.2.28.4)
[156] iti | tatra prājāpatyeṣṭir āhitāgniviṣayā “agnīn samāropya” ity agnibahutvābhidhānāt | āgneyī tv anāhitāgniviṣayā tadvākyaśeṣe “agnim ājighret” ity ekāgnyabhidhānāt | sā ceṣṭiḥ śrāddhādipuraḥsaraṃ prakartavyā | tathā ca nṛsiṃhapurāṇam |
evaṃ vanāśrame tiṣṭhan tapasā dagdhakilbiṣaḥ |
caturtham āśramaṃ gacchet saṃnyasya vidhinā dvijaḥ ||
deyaṃ pitṛbhyo devebhyaḥ svapitṛbhyo ‘pi yatnataḥ |
datvā śrāddham ṛṣibhyaś ca manujebhyas tathātmane ||
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyām athāpi vā |
agniṃ svātmani saṃsthāpya mantravat pravrajet punaḥ || iti | (NṛPu 60.2–4)
śrāddhāni cāṣṭau daivādīni | tathā cāha baudhāyanaḥ |
daivam ārṣaṃ tathā divyaṃ pitryaṃ mātṛkamānuṣe |
bhautikaṃ cātmanaś cānte aṣṭau śrāddhāni nirvapet || iti |
uktaśrāddhādau yogyatā kṛcchraiḥ saṃpādanīyā | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
[157]        kṛcchrāṃs tu caturaḥ kṛtvā pāvanārtham anāśramī |
āśramī cet taptakṛcchraṃ tenāsau yogyatāṃ vrajet || iti |
śrāddhānantarabhāvinīm iṣṭeḥ prācīnām itikartavyatām āha baudhāyanaḥ |
kṛtvā śrāddhāni sarvāṇi pitrādibhyo ‘ṣṭakaṃ pṛthak |
vāpayitvā ca keśādīn mārjayen mātṛkā imāḥ ||
trīn daṇḍān aṅgulisthūlān vaiṇavān mūrdhasaṃmitān |
ekādaśanavadvitricatuḥsaptāṣṭaparvakān ||
satvakkān avraṇān saumyān samasaṃnataparvakān |
veṣṭitān kṛṣṇagovālarajvā tu caturaṅgulān ||
eko vā tādṛśo daṇḍo govālarahito bhavet |
kuśakārpāsasūtrair vā kṣaumasūtrair athāpi vā ||
kuśalair grathitaṃ śikyaṃ padmākārasamanvitam |
ṣaṭpādaṃ pañcapādaṃ vā muṣṭidvayavisarpitam ||
vikeśaṃ sitam aspaṣṭam ubhayadvādaśāṅgulam |
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ vāpi sarvato ‘ṣṭāṅgulaṃ tu vā ||
prādeśamātraṃ vā sūkṣmakārpāsaiḥ kṛtam avraṇam |
[158]        caṇḍālādyahataṃ caitat smṛtaṃ jalapavitrakam ||
gṛhītaṃ mantravat tac cet saśikyaṃ ca kamaṇḍalum |
dāravaṃ vaiṇavaṃ vāpi mṛdalābumayaṃ tu vā ||
pātraṃ śilāmayaṃ tāmraṃ parṇādimayam eva vā |
caturasraṃ vartulaṃ vāpy āsanaṃ dāravaṃ śubham ||
kaupīnācchādanaṃ vāsaḥ kanthāṃ śītanivāraṇīm |
pāduke cāpi śaucārthaṃ daśa mātrā udāhṛtāḥ ||
chatraṃ pavitraṃ saucīṃ ca triviṣṭabdhaṃ tathājinam |
padmāni cākṣasūtraṃ ca mṛtkhanitrī kṛpāṇikā ||
yogapaṭṭaṃ bahirvāsa ity etā ekaviṃśatiḥ |
tāsāṃ pañcādikā nityā daśa vā sarvaśo ‘pi vā ||
gṛhītvemā athāgatya devāgāre ‘gniveśmani |
grāmānte grāmasīmānte yad vā śucimanohare ||
ājyaṃ payo dadhīty etat trivṛd vā jalam eva vā |
oṃ bhūr ityādinā prāśya rātriṃ copavaset tataḥ ||
etāvataiva vidhinā bhikṣuḥ syād āpadi dvijaḥ |
athādityasyāstamayāt pūrvam agnīn vihṛtya saḥ ||
[159]        ājyaṃ ca gārhapatye tu saṃskṛtyaitena ca sruvā |
pūrṇayāhavanīye tu juhuyāt praṇavena tat ||
brahmānvādhānam etat syād agnihotre hute tataḥ |
stīrtvā tu gārhapatyasya darbhān uttarato ‘tra tu ||
pātrāṇi sādayitvātha brahmāyatana eva tu |
stīrṇeṣu darbheṣv āsīta tv ajināntariteṣu vā ||
jāgṛyād rātrim etāṃ tu yāvad brāhmo muhūrtakaḥ |
etām avasthāṃ saṃprāpya mṛto ‘py ānantyam aśnute ||
agnihotraṃ svakāle tu hutvā prātastanaṃ tataḥ |
iṣṭiṃ vaiśvānarīṃ kuryāt prājāpatyām athāpi vā || iti |
ājyādiprāśanānantaram agnivaharaṇāt pūrvaṃ sāvitrīpraveśaṃ kuryāt | tad uktaṃ baudhāyanadharme:
oṃ bhūḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi – tatsavirur vareṇyam
oṃ bhuvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi – bhargo devasya dhīmahi
oṃ suvaḥ sāvitrīṃ praviśāmi – dhiyo yo naḥ pracodayāt
iti ardharcaśaḥ samastayā vā | (BDh 2.10.14)
iti | iṣṭiṃ parisamāpyāgnīṃś cātmani samāropya praiṣam uccārayet | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ātmany agnīn samāropya vedimadhye sthito harim |
dhyātvā hṛdi tv anujñāto guruṇā praiṣam īrayet ||
[160] iti | atha praiṣam uccāryābhayadānaṃ kuryāt | tad uktaṃ kapilamate |
vidhivat praiṣam uccārya trir upāṃśu dvir uccakaiḥ |
abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo mattaḥ svāhety apo bhuvi ||
ninīṣan daṇḍaśikyādi gṛhītvātha bahir vrajet || iti |
daṇḍādīnāṃ mantrān āha baudhāyanaḥ |
sakhā metyādinā daṇḍaṃ gṛhṇīyād guruṇārpitam |
yena devāḥ pavitreṇety uktvā jalapavitrakam ||
yad asya pāre rajasaḥ śukram ity api śikyakam |
vyāhṛtībhis tathā pātram atha kaupīnam ity ṛcā ||
yuvā suvāsā ity evaṃ tac chaṃ yor upavītakam |
etad gṛhītvā niṣkramya svagrāmaṃ bāndhavāṃs tyajet ||
niḥspṛho ‘nyatra gatvaiva vrataiḥ svair vartayet sadā || iti |
etac ca jalapavitrādikaṃ caturvidheṣu bhikṣuṣu prathamadvitīyaviṣayam | cāturvidhyaṃ tu bhikṣūṇāṃ hārīta āha |
caturvidhā bhikṣavas tu proktāḥ sāmānyaliṅginaḥ |
teṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak jñānaṃ vṛttibhedādikaṃ śrutam ||
kuṭīcako bahūdako haṃsaś caiva tṛtīyakaḥ |
caturthaḥ paramo haṃso yo yaḥ paścāt sa uttamaḥ || iti | (HārSm 10.13–14)
pitāmaho ‘pi |
[161]        caturvidhā bhikṣavas tu prakhyātā brahmaṇo mukhāt |
kuṭīcako bahūdako haṃsaś caiva tṛtīyakaḥ ||
caturthaḥ parahaṃsaś ca saṃjñābhedaiḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
tatrādhikāraviśeṣaḥ purāṇe darśitaḥ |
viraktir dvividhā proktā tīvrā tīvratareti ca |
satyām eva tu tīvrāyāṃ nyased yogī kuṭīcakaḥ ||
śakto bahūdake tīvratayāyāṃ haṃsasaṃjñite |
mumukṣuḥ parame haṃse sākṣād vijñānasādhane || iti | (NṛPu 60–13?)
tatra kuṭīcakasya vṛttiviśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ: “kuṭīcako nāma svagṛhe vartamānaḥ śucir akaluṣa āhitāgniṣu bhikṣāṃ bhuñjāno ‘pagatakāmakrodhalobhamoho ‘haṃkāravarjita ātmānugrahaṃ kurute” iti | vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi: “kuṭīcakā nāma putrādibhiḥ kuṭīṃ kārayitvā kāmakrodhalobhamohamadamātsaryādīn hitvā vidhivat saṃnyāsaṃ gṛhītvā tridaṇḍajalapavitrakāṣāyavastradhāriṇaḥ snānaśaucācamanajapasvādhyāyabrahmacaryadhyāna-tatparāḥ putrāder eva [162] bhikṣākāle ‘nnaṃ yātrāmātram upabhuñjānāḥ tasyāṃ kuṭyāṃ nityaṃ vasanta ātmānaṃ mokṣayanti” iti | skandapurāṇe ‘pi |
kuṭīcakas tu saṃnyasya sve ca sadmani nityaśaḥ |
bhikṣām ādāya bhuñjīta svabandhūnāṃ gṛhe ‘tha vā ||
śikhī yajñopavītī syāt tridaṇḍī sakamaṇḍaluḥ || iti |
kuṭīcakasyāśaktaviṣayatvād yukto gṛhavāsādiḥ | tad uktaṃ baudhāyanena |
kuṭīcakāḥ parivrajya sve sve veśmani nityaśaḥ |
bhikṣāṃ bandhubhya ādāya bhuñjate śaktisaṃkṣayāt || iti |
bahūdakasya vṛttiviśeṣam āha skandaḥ |
bahūdakas tu saṃnyasya bandhuputrādivarjitaḥ |
saptāgāraṃ cared bhaikṣyam ekānnaṃ ca parityajet ||
govālarajjusaṃbaddhaṃ tridaṇḍaṃ śikyam uddhṛtam |
jalapātraṃ pavitraṃ ca khanitraṃ ca kṛpāṇikām ||
śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ ca devatārādhanaṃ caret || iti |
vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi: “tatra bahūdakā nāma tridaṇḍakamaṇḍalupadmapavitrapātrakāṣāyavastradhāriṇo vedāntārthāvabodhakāḥ [163] sādhuvṛtteṣu brāhmaṇagṛheṣu bhaikṣacaryāṃ caranta ātmānaṃ mokṣayante” iti | pitāmaho ‘pi |
bahūdakaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sarvasaṅgavivarjitaḥ |
bandhuvarge na bhikṣeta svabhūmau naiva saṃvaset ||
niścalaḥ sthāṇubhūtaś ca sadā mokṣaparāyaṇaḥ |
na kuṭyāṃ nodake saṅgaṃ kuryād vastre ca cetasā ||
nāgāre nāsane nānne nāstare ca tridaṇḍake |
svamātrāyāṃ na kuryād vai rāgaṃ daṇḍādike yatiḥ || iti |
haṃsavṛttiḥ skandapurāṇe darśitā |
haṃsaḥ kamaṇḍaluṃ śikyaṃ bhikṣāpātraṃ tathaiva ca |
kanthāṃ kaupīnam ācchādanam aṅgavastraṃ bahiḥpaṭam ||
ekaṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍaṃ dhārayen nityam ādarāt |
devatānām abhedena kuryād dhyānaṃ samarcayet || iti |
viṣṇur api |
yajñopavītaṃ daṇḍaṃ ca vastraṃ jantunivāraṇam |
tāvān parigrahaḥ prokto nānyo haṃsaparigrahaḥ || iti |
pitāmaho ‘pi |
haṃsas tṛtīyo vijñeyo bhikṣur mokṣaparāyaṇaḥ |
nityaṃ triṣavaṇasnāyī tv ārdravāsā bhavet sadā ||
cāndrāyaṇena varteta yatidharmānuśāsanāt |
vṛkṣamūle vasen nityaṃ guhāyāṃ vā sarttaṭe || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
[164]        haṃsā kamaṇḍaluṃ śikyaṃ daṇḍapātrāṇi bibhrataḥ |
grāmatīrthaikarātrāś ca nagare pañcarātrakāḥ ||
triṣaḍrātropavāsāś ca pakṣamāsopavāsinaḥ |
kṛcchrasāntapanādyaiś ca vrataiḥ kṛśavapurdharāḥ || iti |
paramahaṃsavṛttiḥ skandapurāṇe darśitāḥ |
kaupīnācchādanaṃ vastraṃ kanthāṃ śītanivāriṇīm |
akṣamālāṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍam avraṇam ||
mādhūkaram athaikānnaṃ parahaṃsaḥ samācaret |
parahaṃsas tridaṇḍaṃ ca rajjuṃ govālanirmitām ||
śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ ca nityaṃ karma parityajet || iti |
atra kecic chraddhājāḍyena saṃdhyāvandanagāyatrīśikhāyajñopavītatyāgam asahamānāḥ yathāvarṇitaṃ pāramahaṃsyaṃ vidviṣanti | udāharanti ca kānicd vacanāni | tatra hārītaḥ |
catvāro ‘py āśramā hy ete saṃdhyāvandanavarjitāḥ |
brāhmaṇyād eva hīyante yady apy ugratapodharāḥ || iti | (HārSm 14.18)
baudhāyano ‘pi |
anāgatāṃ tu ye pūrvām anatītāṃ tu paścimām |
samdhyāṃ nopāsate viprāḥ kathaṃ te brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti | (BDh 2.4.15)
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
[165]        sāyaṃ prātaḥ sadā saṃdhyāṃ ye viprā no upāsate |
kāmaṃ tān dhārmiko rājā śūdrakarmasu yojayet || iti | (KSm 1.5)
manur api |
sāvitrīpatitā vrātyā bhavanty āryavigarhitāḥ | iti | (MDh 2.39)
atrir api |
yajñopavītaṃ sarveṣāṃ dvijānāṃ muktisādhanam |
parityajanti ye mohān narā nirayagāminaḥ || iti | (AtrSm 3)
padmapurāṇe ‘pi |
śikhāyajñopavītena tyaktenāsau kathaṃ dvijaḥ | iti |
maivam, eteṣāṃ vacanānāṃ paramahaṃsavyatiriktaviṣayatvenāpy upapatteḥ | pāramahaṃsyaṃ tu bahuṣu pratyakṣaśrutiṣūpalabhyamānaṃ kena pradveṣṭuṃ śakyam | tathā ca jābālaśrutiḥ: “tatra paramahaṃsā nāma saṃvartaka-āruṇi-śvetaketu-durvāsā-ṛbhu-nidāgha-jaḍabharata-dattātreya-raivatakaprabhṛtayo ‘vyaktaliṅgā avyaktācārā anunmattā unmattavad ācaranti” iti (JābUp 6) | teṣāṃ ca śikhādityāga ātmajñānādidharmāś ca tatraiva śrutāḥ: [166]
daṇḍaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ śikyaṃ pātraṃ jalapavitrakam | śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ ca – ity etat sarvaṃ bhūḥ svāhety apsu parityajyātmānam anvicchet | yathājātarūpadharo nirdvandvo niṣparigrahaḥ tattvabrahmamārge samyak saṃpannaḥ śuddhamānasaḥ prāṇasaṃdhāraṇārthaṃ yathoktakāle vimukto bhaikṣyam ācaran karapātreṇa lābhālābhayoḥ samo bhūtvā śūnyāgāradevagṛha-tṛṇakūṭavalmīkavṛkṣamūlakulālaśālāgnihotranadīpulinagirikuharakandara-sthaṇḍileṣv aniketavāsy aprayatno nirmamaḥ śukladhyānaparāyaṇo ‘dhyātmaniṣṭho ‘śubhakarmanirmūlanaparaḥ saṃnyāsena dehatyāgaṃ karoti sa paramahaṃso nāma | iti | (JābUp 6)
tasyām eva ca śrutau janaka-yājñavalkyasaṃvādarūpeṇa yajñopavītatyāgam ākṣipya samāhitam: “pṛcchāmi tvāṃ yājñavalkya ayajñopavītī kathaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ iti | sa hovāca yājñavalkyaḥ | idam evāsya tad yajñopavītaṃ ya ātmeti” iti (JābUp 5) | āruṇiśrutāv api pāramahaṃsyaṃ prapañcitam:
[167]        āruṇiḥ prajāpater lokaṃ jagāma | taṃ gatvovāca kena bhagavan karmāṇy aśeṣato
visṛjānīti | taṃ hovāca prajāpatiḥ | tava putrān bhrātṛvyabandhvādīn śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ yāgaṃ sūtraṃ svādhyāyaṃ ca bhūrlokaṃ bhuvarlokaṃ svarlokaṃ cātalavitalasutalatalātalamahātalarasātalapātālaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ ca visarjayet | daṇḍam ācchādanaṃ kaupīnaṃ parigrahet | śeṣaṃ visṛjet | brahmacārī gṛhastho vānaprastho vā laukikāgnīn udarāgnau samāropayet | upavītaṃ bhūmau vāpsu vā visṛjet | kuṭīcako brahmacārī kuṭumbaṃ visṛjet | pātraṃ visṛjet | daṇḍān lokāgnīn visṛjed iti hovāca | ata ūrdhvam amantravad ācared ūrdhvagamanaṃ visṛjet trisamdhyādau snānam ācaret | saṃdhiṃ samādhāv [168] ātmany ācaret | sarveṣu vedeṣv āraṇyakam āvartayed upaniṣadam āvartayet | (ĀrUp 1)
iti | maitrāyaṇaśrutāv api: “indrasya vajro ‘sīti trīn vaiṇavān daṇḍān dakṣiṇapānau dhārayed ekaṃ vā | yady ekaṃ tadā saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā visṛjya yajñopavītam” iti | pippalādaśākhāyām api |
saśikhaṃ vapanaṃ kṛtvā bahiḥsūtraṃ tyajed budhaḥ |
yad akṣaraṃ paraṃ brahma tat sūtram iti dhārayet ||
sūcanāt sūtram ity āhuḥ sūtraṃ nāma paraṃ padam |
tat sūtraṃ viditaṃ yena sa vipro vedapāragaḥ ||
yena sarvam idaṃ protaṃ sūtre maṇigaṇā iva |
tat sūtraṃ dhārayed yogī yogavit tattvadarśivān ||
bahiḥsūtraṃ tyajed vidvān yogam uttamam āsthitaḥ |
brahmabāvam idaṃ sūtraṃ dhārayed yaḥ sa cetanaḥ ||
dhāraṇād asya sūtrasya nocchiṣṭo nāśucir bhavet |
sūtram antargataṃ yeṣāṃ jñānayajñopavītinām ||
[169]        te vai sūtravido loke te ca yajñopavītinaḥ |
jñānaśikhino jñānaniṣṭhā jñānayajñopavītinaḥ ||
jñānam eva paraṃ teṣāṃ pavitraṃ jñānam ucyate |
agner iva śikhā nānyā yasya jñānamayī śikhā ||
sa śikhīty ucyate vidvān netare keśadhāriṇaḥ |
karmaṇy adhikṛtā ye tu vaidike brāhmaṇādayaḥ ||
ebhir dhāṛyam idaṃ sūtraṃ kriyāṅgaṃ tad dhi vai smṛtam |
śikhā jñānamayī yasya upavītaṃ ca tanmayam ||
brāhmaṇyaṃ sakalaṃ tasya iti brahmavido viduḥ |
idaṃ yajñopavītaṃ tu paramaṃ yat parāyaṇam ||
vidvān yajñopavītī syāt sa yajñaḥ sa ca yajñavit || iti |
vyāsasmṛtāv api |
yajñopavītaṃ karmāṅgaṃ vadanty uttamabuddhayaḥ |
upapūrvanayāt pūrvaṃ yato loke na dṛśyate ||
yāvat karmāṇi kurute tāvad evāsya dhāraṇam |
tasmād asya parityāgaḥ kriyate karmabhiḥ saha ||
[170]        agnihotravināśē tu juhvādīni yathā tyajet |
yathā ca mekhalādīni gṛhasthāśramavāñchayā ||
patnī yoktraṃ yatheṣṭyante somānte ca yathā grahān |
tadvad yajñopavītasya tyāgam icchanti yoginaḥ || iti |
viśvāmitro ‘pi: “athāparaṃ parivrājakaliṅgaṃ sarvataḥ parimokṣam eke | satyānṛte sukhaduḥkhe vedān imaṃ lokam amuṃ ca parityjyātmānam anvicchet | śikhāyajñopavītakamaṇḍalukapālānāṃ tyāgī” iti | baudhāyano ‘pi: “ata ūrdhvaṃ yajñopavītaṃ mantram ācchādanaṃ yaṣṭayaḥ śikyaṃ jalapavitraṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ pātram ity etāni varjayitvā ekaṃ daṇḍam ādatte sakhā ma gopāyeti” iti | smṛtyantare ‘pi |
yadā tu viditaṃ tat syāt paraṃ brahma sanātanam |
tadaikadaṇḍaṃ saṃgṛhya upavītaṃ śikhāṃ tyajet || iti |
atra kecid āhuḥ – upavītatyāgavacanāni purātanayajñopavītaviṣayāṇi | tathā ca smṛtiḥ: [171] “nakhāni nikṛtya purāṇaṃ vastraṃ yajñopavītaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ tyaktvā navāni gṛhītvā grāmaṃ praviśet” iti | yadācamanāṅgayajñopavītaṃ syāt tadā brāhmaṇyād eva hīyate | tasmād asti paramahaṃsasyāpi yajñopavītam – iti |
tad ayuktam, udāhṛtasmṛter bahūdakādiviṣayatvāt, “na yajñopavītaṃ nācchādanaṃ carati paramahaṃsaḥ” (ParUp 2) iti śruteḥ, “ayajñopavītī śaucaniṣṭhaḥ kāmam ekaṃ vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍam ādadīta” (ĀśUp 4) iti śrutyantarāc ca | na ca – bāhyopavītam antareṇācamanādyasaṃbhavaḥ, kauṣītakibrāhmaṇe praśnottarābhyāṃ tadupapādanāt: “kim asya yajñopavītaṃ | kā vāsya śikhā | kathaṃ vāsyopasparśanam” iti praśnaḥ, “idam evāsya tad yajñopavītaṃ yad ātmadhyānaṃ vidyā śikhā” (KaṭhUp 1) ityādyuttaram | brāhmaṇyaṃ tu jñānamayaśikhāyajñopavitina eva puṣkalam iti pippalādaśrutāv udāhṛtam |
kecit tu – paramahaṃsasyāpi tridaṇḍam icchanti | udāharanti ca [172] vacanāni | tatra dakṣaḥ sarveṣām āśramiṇāṃ krameṇa lakṣaṇam abhidadhānaḥ “tridaṇḍena yatiś caiva” (DaSm 1.14) iti yater lakṣaṇam abhidhāya tridaṇḍarahitasya yatitvaṃ niṣedhati: “yasyaital lakṣaṇaṃ nāsti prāyaścittī na cāśramī” (DaSm 1.15) iti | hārīta-dattātreya-pitāmahāḥ kuṭīcakādīn caturo ‘py upanyasya sarveṣāṃ tridaṇḍam eva vidadhate |
vṛttibhedena bhinnāś ca naiva liṅgena te dvijāḥ |
liṅgaṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ teṣāṃ tridaṇḍaṃ sapavitrakam || iti |
atrir api |
śikhinas tu śrutāḥ kecit kecit muṇḍāś ca bhikṣukāḥ |
caturdhā bhikṣukāḥ proktāḥ sarve caiva tridaṇḍinaḥ || iti | (AtrSm 1.13)
ekadaṇḍavacanāni tu tridaṇḍālābhaviṣayāṇi | tad āha medhātithiḥ |
yāvan na syus tridaṇḍās tu tāvad ekena paryaṭet | iti |
hārīto ‘pi |
naṣṭe jalapavitre vā tridaṇḍe vā pramādataḥ |
ekaṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍaṃ pālāśaṃ bailvam eva vā ||
gṛhītvā vicaret tāvad yāval labhyet tridaṇḍakam || (HārSm 11.14)
[173] iti |
atrocyate – paramahaṃsasyaikadaṇḍanirākaraṇr bahvāgamavirodhaḥ syāt | udāhṛtāś ca paramahaṃsasyaikadaṇḍapratipādikāḥ śrutismṛtayaḥ | evaṃ sati medhātithi-hārītābhyāṃ yad ekadaṇḍasyānukalpatvam uktaṃ tad bahūdakādiviṣayaṃ bhavaiṣyati | paramahaṃsasya tu naikadaṇḍo ‘nukalpaḥ | yato vyāsa āha |
tridaṇḍasya parityāga ekadaṇḍasya dhāraṇam |
ekasmin dṛśyate vāke tasmād asya pradhānatā || iti |
yat tu “sarve caiva tridaṇḍinaḥ” iti tad vāgdaṇḍādiviṣayam, na tu yaṣṭitrayābhiprāyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
vāgdaṇḍo ‘tha manodaṇḍaḥ kāyadaṇḍas tathaiva ca |
yasyaite nihitā buddhau tridaṇḍīti sa ucyate ||
tridaṇḍam etaṃ niḥkṣipya sarvabhūteṣu mānavaḥ |
kāmakrodhau tu saṃyamya tataḥ siddhiṃ nigacchati || iti | (MDh 12.10–11)
dakṣo ‘pi |
vāgdaṇḍo ‘tha manodaṇḍaḥ karmadaṇḍas tathaiva ca |
yasyaite niyatā daṇḍās tridaṣḍīti sa ucyate || iti |
eteṣāṃ ca trayāṇāṃ daṇḍānāṃ savrūpaṃ sa evāha |
vāgadaṇḍo maunatā proktā karmadaṇḍas tv anīhatā |
mānasasya tu daṇḍasya prāṇāyāmo vidhīyate ||
[174] iti | yad api pitāmahenoktam,
paraḥ paramahaṃsas tu turyākhyaḥ śrutir abravīt |
yamaiś ca niyamair yukto viṣṇurūpī tridaṇḍabhṛt ||
iti, tad apy uktarītyā vāgdaṇḍādyabhiprāyam | yad api,
liṅgaṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ teṣāṃ tridaṇḍaṃ sapavitrakam |
iti yaṣṭitrayābhidhānam, tad api bahūdakaviṣayatvenopapannam | yo hi bahūdaka eva san grāmaikarātrādikāṃ haṃsavṛttim ācarati sa vṛttito haṃso bhavati | vedāntaśravaṇādikāṃ paramahaṃsavṛttiṃ ced āśrayati tadā vṛttitaḥ paramahaṃso bhavati | teṣāṃ haṃsādīnāṃ tridaṇḍam eva liṅgam | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa,
vṛttibhedena bhinnāś ca naiva liṅgena te dvijāḥ |
ity uktam | anyathā mukhyayor haṃsaparahaṃsayor ekadaṇḍavidhāyakāny udāhṛtāni vacanāni nirviṣayāṇi syuḥ | tasmād eka eva daṇḍaḥ paramahaṃsasya |
nanu – paramahaṃsopaniṣadi ekadaṇḍo ‘py amukhyatvenaiva śrūyate: “kaupīnaṃ daṇḍam ācchādanaṃ ca svaśarīropabhogārthāya ca lokasyopakārārthāya ca parigrahet | tac ca na [175] mukhyam asti | ko mukhyaḥ iti cet, ayaṃ mukhyo – na daṇḍaṃ na śikyaṃ nācchādanaṃ carati paramahaṃsaḥ” iti (ParUp 1)
bāḍham | nāsty eva vidvatparamahaṃsasya bāhyayaṣṭhyupayogaḥ | ata eva ca vākyaśeṣe jñānam eva tasya daṇḍaḥ ity uktam:
jñānadaṇḍo dhṛto yena ekadaṇḍo sa ucyate | iti | (ParUp 3)
yaṣṭidhāraṇaṃ tu vividiṣoḥ paramahaṃsasya | na ca vidvadvividiṣubhedena pāramahaṃsyadvividhye mānābhāvaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ, vājasaneyibrāhmaṇe tadupalambhāt | “etaṃ vai tam ātmānaṃ viditvā brāhmaṇāḥ putreṣaṇāyāś ca vitteṣaṇāyāś ca lokeṣaṇāyāś ca vyutthāyātha bhikṣācaryaṃ caranti” (BāUp 3.5.1) iti vidvatsaṃnyāse pramāṇam | “etam eva viditvā munir bhavati | etam eva pravrājino lokam icchantaḥ pravrajanti” (BāUp 4.4.22) [176] ity etadvākye vidvadvividiṣusaṃnyāsau ubhāv api vispaṣṭam avagamyete | “etat sarvaṃ bhūḥ svāhety apsu parityajyātmānam anvicchet” (JābUp 6) iti jābālavākye tridaṇḍādiparityāgātmakaṃ vividiṣupāramahaṃsyam āmnātam | śrutiś ca “nyāsa iti brahmā brahmā hi paraḥ paro hi brahmā tāni vā etāny avarāṇi tapāṃsi nyāsa evātyarecayat” (MNU 21.2) ity agnihotrayajñadānāditaponindāpuraḥsaraṃ pāramahaṃsyaṃ vidhāya tasya paramahaṃsasya vividiṣor ātmavidyādhikāraṃ darśayati “om ity etam ātmānaṃ yuñjīta” (MNU 24.2) iti | tasmād dvaividhyasadbhāvād daṇḍādinivāraṇam aśeṣakarmaśūnyavidvatparamahaṃsaviṣayam | viduṣaḥ kartavyaśūnyatāṃ bhagavān āha |
yas tv ātmaratir eva syād ātmatṛptaś ca mānavaḥ |
ātmany eva ca saṃtuṣṭas tasya kāryaṃ na vidyate || (BGh 3.17)
[177] iti | dakṣo ‘pi |
nādhyetavyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ na śrotavyaṃ kadācana |
etaiḥ sarveṣu niṣṇāto yatir bhavati nānyathā || iti | (DaSm 7.33)
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
jñānāmṛtena tṛptasya kṛtakṛtyasya yoginaḥ |
naivāsti kiṃcit kartavyam asti cen na sa sarvavit || iti |
bahvṛcabrāhmaṇe ‘pi: “etad dha sam vai tadvidvāṃsa āhur ṛṣayaḥ kāvaṣeyāḥ kimarthā vayam adhyeṣyāmahe kimarthā vayaṃ yakṣyāmahe” iti | vividiṣos tu śravaṇamananādikartavyasadbhāvāt tadupakāritvena daṇḍadhāraṇādiniyama upapadyate |
nanu – jñānarahitasyāpi daṇḍapratiṣedha āmnāyate:
kāṣṭhadaṇḍo dhṛto yena sarvāśī jñānavarjitaḥ |
sa yāti narakān ghorān mahārauravasaṃjñitān || iti | (ParUp 3)
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, vidyāṃ vividiṣāṃ ca vinā jīvanārtham eva kevalam ekadaṇḍaṃ yo dhatte tadviṣayatvāt pratiṣedhasya | ata eva sarvāśīti viśeṣitam | smṛtāv api |
[178]        ekadaṇḍaṃ samāśritya jīvanti bahavo narāḥ |
narake raurave ghore karmatyāgāt patanti te || iti |
yuktaś ca narake pātaḥ | saty api bāhyadaṇḍe pāpanivartakānām āntaradaṇḍānām abhāvāt | pāpanivartakatvaṃ ca kālikāpurāṇe darśitam |
vaiṇavā ye smṛtā daṇḍā liṅgamātraprabodhakāḥ |
liṅgavyaktyai hi dhāryās te na punar dharmahetavaḥ ||
kāyajā ye budhair nityaṃ nṛṇāṃ pāpapramokṣaṇāt |
jitendriyair jitakrodhair dhāryā vai tattvadarśibhiḥ || iti |
ye kāyajās trayo daṇḍās te pāpavimokṣaṇāya dhāryā ity anvayaḥ |
nanu – ekadaṇḍatridaṇḍayor vikalpaḥ kvacit smaryate | tatra viṣṇu-baudhāyanau |
ekadaṇḍī bhaved vāpi tridaṇḍo vā munir bhavet | iti |
vyāsaḥ |
tridaṇḍam ekadaṇḍaṃ vā vratam āsthāya tattvavit |
paryaṭet pṛthivīṃ nityaṃ varṣākāle sthiro bhavet || iti |
śaunako ‘pi: “atha kāṣāyavāsāḥ sakhā ma gopāyeti tridaṇḍam ekadaṇḍaṃ vā gṛhṇāti” iti | āśvamedhike bhagavadvacanam |
[179]        ekadaṇḍī tridaṇḍī vā śikhī muṇḍita eva vā |
kāṣāyamātrasāro ‘pi yatiḥ pūjyo yudhiṣṭhira || iti | (MBh āśva 105.8–9)
bāḍham | uktarītyā tayor vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | tatra kuṭīcakabahūdakayos tridaṇḍaḥ | haṃsaparamahaṃsayor ekadaṇḍaḥ | tathā ca sati tatra tatrodāhṛtāni vacanāni upapadyante |||
tad evaṃ caturvidhaḥ saṃnyāso nirūpitaḥ
atha taddharmā nirūpyante | tatra baudhāyanaḥ |
uṣaḥkāle samutthāya śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi |
dantān vidhāvya cācamya parvavarjaṃ yathāvidhi ||
snātvā cācamya vidhivat tiṣṭhann āsīna eva vā |
bibhaj jalapavitraṃ vāpy akṣasūtraṃ karadvaye ||
tadvat pavitre govālaiḥ kṛte duṣkṛtanāśane |
udaye vidhivat saṃdhyām upāsya trikajapyavān ||
mitrasya carṣaṇītyādyair upasthāya pavitribhiḥ |
pūrvavat tarpayitvātha japet samyak samāhitaḥ || iti |
manur api |
eka eva caren nityaṃ siddhyartham asahāyakaḥ |
siddhim ekasya saṃpaśyan na jahāti na hīyate || (MDh 6.42)
[180] iti | ekasyāsahāyasya vicarato rāgadveṣādipratibandhābhāvāt jñānalakṣaṇāṃ siddhiṃ niścinvan tāṃ siddhiṃ na jahāti | tasyāṃ siddhāv apratyūhena pravartate | pravṛttaś ca tasyāḥ siddher na hīyate kiṃ tu pāraṃ gacchati | yadā tu dvitīyatṛtīyapuruṣasahāyavān vicaret tadā rāgadveṣasaṃbhavād uktasiddher hīyate | ata eva dakṣaḥ |
eko bhikṣur yathoktas tu dvāv eva mithunaṃ smṛtam |
trayo grāmaḥ samākhyāta ūrdhvaṃ tu nagarāyate ||
nagaraṃ na hi kartavyaṃ grāmo ‘pi mithunaṃ tathā |
etat trayaṃ prakurvāṇaḥ svadharmāc cyavate yatiḥ ||
rājavārtā hi teṣāṃ ca bhikṣāvārtā parasparam |
snehapaiśunyamātsaryaṃ saṃnikarṣān na saṃśayaḥ || iti | (DaSm 7.35–37)
yadā tu śravaṇādisaṃpattyabhāvād ātmajñānasiddhau svayam aśaktaḥ syāt tadā tatra śaktena dvitīyena saha vicaret | yathāha śrutiḥ: “varṣāsu dhruvaśīlo ‘ṣṭau māsam ekākī yatiś caret dvau vā” iti (ĀrUp 3) | caret caretām ity arthaḥ | śaunako ‘pi: “ekākī vicaret sarvabhūtebhyo hitam ācaret” [181] iti | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sarvabhūtahitaḥ śāntaḥ tridaṇḍī sakamaṇḍaluḥ |
ekārāmaḥ parivrajya bhikṣārthī grāmam āśrayet || iti | (YDh 3.58)
hitācaraṇaṃ nāma hiṃsānanuṣṭhānamātram | na punar upakāreṣu pravṛttiḥ, “hiṃsānugrahayor anārambhaḥ” (GDh 3.24) iti gautamasmaraṇāt | ata eva ahiṃsādīn āha atriḥ |
ahiṃsā satyam asteyaṃ brahmacaryāparigrahau |
bhāvaśuddhir harer bhaktiḥ saṃtoṣaḥ śaucam ārjavam ||
āstikyaṃ brahmasaṃsparśaḥ svādhyāyaḥ samadarśanam |
anauddhatyam adīnatvaṃ prasādaḥ sthairyamārdave ||
svasneho guruśuśrūṣā śraddhā kṣāntir damaḥ śamaḥ |
upekṣā dhairyamādhurye titikṣā karuṇā tathā ||
hrīs tapo jñānavijñāne yogo laghvaśanaṃ dhṛtiḥ |
bhikṣāṭanaṃ japaḥ saṃdhyā tyāgaḥ karmaphalasya ca |
eṣa svadharmo vikhyāto yatīnāṃ niyatātmanām || iti |
pravrajyāṃ kṛtvāpi guroḥ samīpe brahmajñānaparyantaṃ nivaset | tad uktaṃ liṅgapurāṇe |
[182]        āśramatrayam uktasya prāptasya paramāśramam |
tataḥ saṃvatsarasyānte prāpya jñānam anuttamam ||
anujñāpya guruṃ caiva cared dhi pṛthivīm imām |
tyaktasaṅgo jitakrodho laghvāhāro jitendriyaḥ ||
pidhāya buddhyā dvārāṇi dhyānenaikamanā bhavet || iti | (LiṅPu 1.90.15)
matsyapurāṇe ‘pi |
guror abhihite yuktaḥ sa tu saṃvatsaraṃ vaset |
niyameṣv apramattas tu yameṣu ca sadā bhavet ||
prāpya cānte tataś caiva jñānayogam anuttamam |
avirodhena dharmasya vicaret pṛthivīṃ yatiḥ || iti | (LiṅPu 1.89.5–6)
saṃvatasaram ity upalakṣaṇam | yāvaj jñānaṃ tāvan nivaset | gurusamīpavāsasya jñānārthatvāt | pṛthivīvicaraṇe viśeṣam āha kaṇvaḥ |
ekarātraṃ vaset grāme nagare pañcarātrakam |
varṣābhyo ‘nyatra varṣāsu māsāṃś ca caturo vaset || iti |
matsyapurāṇe ‘pi |
aṣṭau māsān vihāraḥ syād yatīnāṃ saṃyatātmanām |
[183]        ekatra caturo māsān vārṣikān nivaset punaḥ ||
na deho bhavitā tatra dṛṣṭaṃ śāstraṃ purātanam || iti | (MatPu 184.21–23)
cāturmāsyanivāse prayojanam āha medhātithiḥ |
saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jantūnāṃ vasudhātalacāriṇām |
āṣāḍhādīṃś ca catura ā māsāt kārtikād yatiḥ ||
dharmāḍhye jalasaṃpanne grāmānte nivasec chuciḥ || iti |
anyān api heyopādeyāṃś c dharmān saṃgṛhyāha sa eva |
śraddhayā parayopetaḥ paramātmaparāyaṇaḥ |
sthūlasūkṣmaśarīrābhyāṃ mucyante daśaṣaṭkavit ||
tridaṇḍaṃ kuṇḍikāṃ kanyāṃ bhaikṣabhājanam āsanam |
kaupīnācchādanaṃ vāsaḥ ṣaḍ etāni parigrahet ||
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ bījaṃ taijasaṃ viṣam āyudham |
ṣaḍ etāni na gṛhṇīyād yatir mūtrapurīṣavat ||
rasāyanakriyāvādaṃ jyotiṣaṃ krayavikrayam |
vividhāni ca śilpāni varjayet paradāravat ||
bhikṣāṭanaṃ japaṃ snānaṃ dhyānaṃ śaucaṃ surārcanam |
kartavyāni ṣaḍ etāni sarvathā nṛpadaṇḍavat ||
[184]        naṭādiprekṣaṇaṃ dyūtaṃ pramadāṃ suhṛdaṃ tathā |
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam udakyāṃ ca ṣaṇ na paśyet kadācana ||
skandhāvāre khale sārthe pure grāme vasadgṛhe |
na vaseta yatiḥ ṣaṭsu sthāneṣv eteṣu karhicit ||
rāgaṃ dveṣaṃ madaṃ māyāṃ dambhaṃ mohaṃ parātmasu |
ṣaḍ etāni yatir nityaṃ manasāpi na cintayet ||
mañcakaṃ śuklavastraṃ ca strīkathāṃ laulyam eva ca |
divā svāpaṃ ca yānaṃ ca yatīnāṃ patanāni ṣaṭ ||
saṃyogaṃ ca viyogaṃ ca viyogasya ca sādhanam |
jīveśvarapradhānānāṃ svarūpāṇi vicintayet ||
āsanaṃ pātralopaś ca saṃcayaḥ śiṣyasaṃgrahaḥ |
divāsvāpo vṛthājalpo yater bandhakarāṇi ṣaṭ ||
ekāhāt parato grāme pañcāhāt parataḥ pure |
varṣābhyo ‘nyatra yat sthānam āsanaṃ tad udāhṛtam ||
uktānāṃ yatipātrāṇām ekasyāpi na saṃgrahaḥ |
bhikṣor bhaikṣabhujaś cāpi pātralopaḥ sa ucyate ||
gṛhītasya tridaṇḍāder dvitīyasya parigrahaḥ |
kālāntaropabhogārthaṃ saṃcayaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
[185]        śuśrūṣālābhapūjārthaṃ yaśo’rthaṃ vā parigrahaḥ |
śiṣyāṇāṃ na tu kāruṇyāt sa snehaḥ śiṣyasaṃgrahaḥ ||
vidyā dinaṃ prakāśatvād avidyā rātrir ucyate |
vidyābhyāse pramādo yaḥ sa divāsvāpa ucyate ||
ādhyātmakīṃ kathāṃ muktvā bhaikṣacaryāṃ surastutim |
anugrahaparaḥ praśno vṛthā jalpaḥ sa ucyate ||
ajihvaḥ ṣaṇḍhakaḥ paṅgur andho badhira eva ca |
mugdhaś ca mucyate bhikṣuḥ ṣaḍbhir etair na saṃśayaḥ ||
idaṃ mṛṣṭm idaṃ neti yo ‘śnann api na sajjati |
hitaṃ satyaṃ mitaṃ vakti tam ajihvaṃ pracakṣate ||
sadyojātāṃ yathā nārīṃ tathā ṣoḍaśavārṣikīm |
śatavarṣāṃ ca yo dṛṣṭvā nirvikāraḥ sa ṣaṇḍhakaḥ ||
bhikṣārtham aṭanaṃ yasya viṇmūtrakaraṇāya ca |
yojanān na paraṃ yāti sarvathā paṅgur eva saḥ ||
tiṣṭhato vrajato vāpi yasya cakṣur na dūragam |
caturyugād bhayaṃ tyaktvā parivrāṭ so ‘ndha ucyate ||
[186]        hitāhitaṃ manorāmaṃ vacaḥ śokāvahaṃ tu yat |
śrutvā yo na śṛṇotīva badhiraḥ sa prakīrtitaḥ ||
sāṃnidhye viṣayāṇāṃ yaḥ samartho ‘vikalendriyaḥ |
suptvad vartate nityaṃ sa bhikṣur mugdha ucyate || iti |
bhikṣāṭanavidhim āha manuḥ |
ekakālaṃ cared bhaikṣaṃ na prasajyeta vistare |
bhaikṣaprasakto hi yatir viṣayeṣv api sajjate ||
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |
vṛtte śarāvasaṃpāte bhikṣāṃ nityaṃ yatiś caret ||
alābhe na viṣādī syāl lābhe caiva na harṣayet |
prāṇayātrikamātraḥ syān mātrāsaṅgād vinirgataḥ || iti | (MDh 6.55–57)
mīyante iti mātrā viṣayās teṣāṃ saṅgād vinirgataḥ | yatatasto (?) na harṣaviṣādau kāryau ity arthaḥ | yamo ‘pi |
snātvā śuciḥ śucau deśe kṛtajapyaḥ samāhitaḥ |
bhikṣārthī praviśed grāmaṃ rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ ||
caren mādhūkaraṃ bhaikṣaṃ yatir mlecchakulād api |
ekānnaṃ na tu bhuñjīta bṛhaspatisamo yadi ||
[187]        medhyaṃ bhakṣaṃ caren nityaṃ sāyāhne vāgyataḥ śuciḥ |
ekavāsā viśuddhātmā mandagāmī yugāntadṛk ||
yathālabdhaṃ tathāśnīyād ājyasaṃskāravarjitam |
bhaikṣaṃ mādhūkaraṃ nāma sarvapātakanāśanam || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
vidhūme sannamusale vyaṅgāre bhuktavajjane |
kāle ‘parāhṇabhūyiṣṭhe bhikṣāṭaṇam athācaret ||
ūrdhvaṃ jānvor adho nābheḥ paridhāyaikam ambaram |
dvitīyam āntaraṃ vāsaḥ pātrī daṇḍī ca vāgyataḥ ||
savye cādāya pātraṃ tu tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
upatiṣṭheta sūryaṃ tu dhyātvā caikatvam ātmanā ||
uktvā virajasaṃ mantram ākṛṣṇena pradakṣiṇam |
kṛtvā punar japitvā ca ye te panthāna ity api ||
yo ‘sau viṣṇvākhyaṃ āditye puruṣo ‘ntarhṛdi sthitaḥ |
so ‘haṃ nārāyaṇo deva iti dhyātvā praṇamya tam ||
bhikṣāpātrādiśuddhyartham avamucyāpy upānahau |
tato grāmaṃ vrajen mandaṃ yugamātrāvalokakaḥ ||
dhyāyan hariṃ ca taccitta idaṃ ca samudīrayet |
viṣṇus tiryag adhordhvaṃ me vaikuṇṭho vidiśaṃ diśam ||
pātu māṃ sarvato rāmo dhanvī cakrī ca keśavaḥ |
abhigamya gṛhād bhikṣāṃ bhavatpūrvaṃ pracodayet ||
[188]        godohamātraṃ tiṣṭhec ca vāgyato ‘dhomukhas tataḥ |
dṛṣtvā bhikṣāṃ dṛṣṭipūtāṃ dātuś ca karasaṃsthitām ||
tridaṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇe tv aṅge tataḥ sandhāya bāhunā |
utpāṭayec ca kavacaṃ dakṣiṇena kareṇa saḥ ||
pātraṃ vāmakare kṣiptvā śleṣayed dakṣiṇena tu |
prāṇayātrikam annaṃ tu bhikṣeta vigataspṛhaḥ || iti |
bhikṣasya pañcavidhatvam āha uśanāḥ |
mādhūkarama asaṃkḷptaṃ prākpraṇītam ayācitam |
tātkālikopapannaṃ ca bhaikṣaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam ||
manaḥsaṃkalparahitān gṛhāṃs trīn sapta pañcakān |
madhuvad āharaṇaṃ yat tu mādhūkaram iti smṛtam ||
śayanotthāpanāt prāg yat prārthitaṃ bhaktisaṃyutaiḥ |
tat prākpraṇītam ity āha bhagavān uśanā muniḥ ||
bhikṣāṭanasamudyogāt prāk kenāpi nimantritam |
ayācitaṃ hi tad bhaikṣaṃ bhoktavyaṃ manur abravīt ||
upasthāne ca yat proktaṃ bhikṣārthaṃ brāhmaṇena ha |
tātkālikam iti khyātaṃ tad attavyaṃ mumukṣuṇā ||
siddham annaṃ bhaktajanair ānītaṃ yan maṭhaṃ prati |
upapannaṃ tad ity āhur munayo mokṣakāṅkṣiṇaḥ || iti |
bhkṣānnaṃ praśaṃsati yamaḥ |
yaś caret sarvavarṇeṣu bhaikṣam abhyavahārataḥ |
na sa kiṃcid upāśniyād āpo bhaikṣam iti sthitiḥ ||
[189]        abbinduṃ yaḥ kuśāgreṇa māsi māsi trayaṃ pibet |
nyāyato yas tu bhikṣāśī pūrvoktāt tu viśiṣyate ||
taptakāñcanavarṇena gavāṃ mūtreṇa yāvakam |
pibet dvādaśa varṣāṇi na tad bhaikṣasamaṃ bhavet ||
śākabhakṣāḥ payobhakṣā ye ‘nye yāvakabhakṣakāḥ |
