sarve bhaikṣabhujas tasya kalāṃ nārhanti ṣoḍaśīm ||
na bhaikṣaṃ parapākānnaṃ na ca bhaikṣaṃ pratigrahaḥ |
somapānasamaṃ bhaikṣaṃ tasmād bhaikṣeṇa vartayet || iti |
atra sarvavarṇeṣv ity āpadviṣayam | ata eva baudhāyanaḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ medhyānām annam āharet |
asaṃbhave tu pūrvasyāpy ādadītottarottaram ||
sarveṣām apy abhāve tu bhaikṣadvayam anaśnatā |
bhaikṣaṃ śūdrād api grāhyaṃ rakṣyāḥ prāṇā vijānatā || iti |
na ca bhikṣāṃ labdhum ulkāpātādyutpātakathanaṃ grahadausthyādikathanam anyaṃ vā kaṃcid upādhiṃ saṃpādayet | tad āha manuḥ |
na cotpātanimittābhyāṃ na nakṣatrāṅgavidyayā |
nānuśāsanavādābhyāṃ bhikṣāṃ lipseta karhicit || iti | (MDh 6.50)
varjyam annam āha atriḥ |
hitaṃ mitaṃ sadāśnīyād yat sukhenaiva jīryati |
dhātuḥ prakupyate yena tad annaṃ varjayed yatiḥ ||
[190]        udakyā coditaṃ cānnaṃ dvijānnaṃ śūdracoditam |
prāṇyaṅge vāyase kḷptaṃ tad annaṃ varjayed yatiḥ ||
pitrarthaṃ kalpitaṃ pūrvam annaṃ devādikāraṇāt |
varjayet tādṛśīṃ bhikṣāṃ parabādhākarīṃ tathā || iti |
parabādhāprasaktim evābhipretya manur āha |
na tāpasair brāhmaṇair vā vayobhir atha vā śvabhiḥ |
ākīrṇaṃ bhikṣukair vānyar agāram upasaṃvrajet || iti | (MDh 6.51)
yas tu bhikṣāṃ dātuṃ śakto ‘pi nāstikyān na prayacchati tadgṛhaṃ varjayed ity āha baudhāyanaḥ |
bhikṣāṃ na dadyuḥ pañcāhaṃ saptāhaṃ vā kadācana |
yasmin gṛhe janā maurkhyāt tyajec cāṇḍālaveśvavat || iti |
anindyagṛhasya varjane bādham āha sa eva |
sādhuṃ cāpatitaṃ vipraṃ yo yatiḥ parivarjayet |
sa tasya sukṛtaṃ datvā duṣkṛtaṃ pratipadyate || iti |
yas tu daridraḥ śraddhālutayā svayam upoṣyāpi bhikṣāṃ prayacchati tasya bhikṣā na grāhyā | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
ātmānaṃ pīḍayitvāpi bhikṣāṃ yaḥ saṃprayacchati |
sā bhikṣā hiṃsitā jñeyā nādadyāt tādṛśīṃ yatiḥ ||
[191] iti | bhikṣārthaṃ bahuṣu gṛheṣu paryaṭitum alasam praty āha baudhāyanaḥ |
ekatra lobhād yo bhikṣuḥ pātrapūraṇam icchati |
dātā svargam avāpnoti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam || iti |
yatipātraṃ vivinakti manuḥ |
ataijasāni pātrāṇi tasya syur nirvraṇāni ca |
teṣām adbhiḥ smṛtaṃ śaucaṃ camasānām ivādhvare ||
alābuṃ dārupātraṃ vā mṛṇmayaṃ vaidalaṃ tathā |
etāni yatipātrāṇi manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt || iti | (MDh 6.53–54)
yamo ‘pi |
hiraṇmayāni pātrāṇi kṛṣṇāyasamayāni ca |
yatīnāṃ tāny apātrāṇi varjayet tāni bhikṣukaḥ || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
svayam āhṛtaparṇeṣu svayaṃ śīrṇeṣu vā punaḥ |
bhuñjīta na vaṭāśvatthakarañjānāṃ tu parṇake ||
kumbhītindukayor vāpi kovidārārkayos tathā |
āpady api na kāṃsye tu malāśī kāṃśyabhojanaḥ ||
sauvarṇe rājate tāmramaye vā trapusīsayoḥ || iti |
bhojananiyamam āha sa eva: “bhaikṣacaryād upāvṛtya … hastau pādau ca prakṣālya [192] ācamyādityasyāgre nivedayan ud u tyaṃ citram iti | … brahajajñānam iti ca | … ud vayaṃ tamasas parīti ca … japitvā | … bhuñjīta” iti (BDh 2.10.18.7–12) | nṛsiṃhapurāṇe ‘pi |
tato nivṛtya tatpātraṃ saṃsthāpyācamya saṃyamI |
caturaṅgulena prakṣālya grāsamātraṃ samāhitaḥ ||
sarvavyañjanasaṃyuktaṃ pṛthak pātre nivedayet |
sūryādidevabhūtebhyo datvānnaṃ prokṣya vāriṇā ||
bhuñjīta parṇapuṭake pātre vā vāgyato yatiḥ |
bhuktvā pātraṃ yatir nityaṃ kṣālayen mantrapūrvakam ||
na duṣyet tasya tat pātraṃ yajñeṣu camasā iva |
athācamya niruddhāsur upatiṣṭheta bhāskaram ||
japadhyānaviśeṣeṇa dinaśeṣaṃ nayed budhaḥ |
kṛtasaṃdhyas tato rātriṃ nayed devagṛhādiṣu ||
[193]        hṛtpuṇdarīkanilaye dhyātvātmānam akalmaṣam |
yatir dharmarataḥ śāntaḥ sarvabhūtasamo vaśī ||
prāpnoti paramaṃ sthānaṃ yat prāpya na nivartate || iti | (NṛPu 60.13–20)
kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
āditye darśayitvānnaṃ bhuñjīta prāṅmukho ‘tvaraḥ |
hutvā prāṇāhutīḥ pañca grāsān aṣṭau samāhitaḥ ||
ācamya devaṃ brahmāṇaṃ dhyāyīta parameśvaram |
prāgrātre ‘prararātre ca madhyarātre tathaiva ca ||
saṃdhyāsv ahni viśeṣeṇa cintayen nityam īśvaram || iti | (KūPu 1.2.22.7–10)
athānye ‘pi yatidharmāḥ | tatrāha atriḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi ācāro yo yateḥ smṛtaḥ |
abhyutthānapriyālāpair guruvat pratipūjanam ||
yatīnāṃ vratavṛddhānāṃ svadharmam anuvartinām |
viṣṇurūpeṇa vai kuryān namaskāraṃ vidhānataḥ || iti |
manur api |
kṛttakeśanakhaśmaśruḥ pātrī daṇḍī kusumbhavān |
vicaren niyato nityaṃ sarvabhūtāny apīḍayan ||
[194]        kapālaṃ vṛkṣamūlāni kucelam asahāyatā |
samatā caiva sarvasminn etan muktasya lakṣaṇam ||
nābhinandeta maraṇaṃ nābhinandeta jīvitam |
kālam eva pratīkṣeta nideśaṃ bhṛtako yathā ||
dṛṣṭipūtaṃ nyaset pādaṃ vastrapūtaṃ jalaṃ pibet |
satyapūtāṃ vaded vācaṃ manaḥpūtaṃ samācaret ||
ativādāṃs titikṣeta nāvamanyeta kathaṃcana |
na cekaṃ deham āśritya vairaṃ kurvīta kenacit ||
krudhyantaṃ na pratikrudhyed ākruṣṭaḥ kuśalaṃ vadet |
saptadvārāvakīrṇāṃ ca na vācam anṛtāṃ vadet ||
adhyātmaratir āsīno nirapakṣo nirāmiṣaḥ |
ātmanaiva sahāyena sukhārthī vicared iha ||
saṃrakṣaṇārthaṃ jantūnāṃ rātrāv ahani saṃdhyayoḥ |
śarīrasyātyaye caiva samīkṣya vasudhāṃ caret ||
alpānnābhyavahāreṇa rahaḥsthānāsanena ca |
hriyamāṇāni viṣayair indriyāṇi nivartayet ||
indriyāṇāṃ nirodhena rāgadveṣakṣayeṇa ca |
ahiṃsayā ca bhūtānām amṛtatvāya kalpate || (MDh 6.44–68)
[195] iti | dakṣo ‘pi |
nādhyetavyaṃ na vaktavyaṃ na śrotavyaṃ kathaṃcana |
etaiḥ sarvaiḥ suniṣpanno yatir bhavati netaraḥ || iti | (DaSm 7.33)
bṛhaspatir api |
na kiṃcid bheṣajād anyad anyad vā dantadhāvanāt |
vinā bhojanakālaṃ tu bhakṣayed ātmavān yatiḥ ||
naivādadīta pātheyaṃ yatiḥ kiṃcid anāpadi |
pakvam āpatsu gṛhṇīyād yāvad ahnopabhujyate ||
na tīrthavāsī nityaṃ syān nopavāsaparo yatiḥ |
na cādhyayanaśīlaḥ syān na vyākhyānaparo bhavet || iti |
nādhyetavyam ity etat karmakāṇḍaviṣayam, anyathā “upaniṣadam āvartayet” (ĀśUp 2) iti śrutir bādhyeta | na śrotavyam ity etad brahmamīmāṃsāvyatiriktaviṣayam, “śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ” iti tanmīmāṃsāyā vihitatvāt | upapannaś ca tīrthopavāsādhyayanavyākhyāna-tātparyaniṣedho nivṛttidharmapradhānatvāt kaivalyāśramasya | yataḥ sa evāha |
[196]        yasmin vācaḥ praviṣṭāḥ syuḥ kūpe prāstāḥ śilā iva |
na vaktāraṃ punar yānti sa kaivalyāśrame vaset ||
yasmin kāmāḥ praviśanti viṣayebhyopasaṃhṛtāḥ |
viṣayān na punar yānti sa kaivalyāśrame vaset ||
yasmin krodhaḥ śamaṃ yāti viphalaḥ samyag utthitaḥ |
ākāśe ‘sir yathā kṣiptaḥ sa kaivalyāśrame vaset ||
yasmin kṣāntiḥ śamaḥ śaucaṃ satyaṃ saṃtoṣa ārjavam |
ākiṃcanyam adambhaś ca sa kaivalyāśrame vaset ||
vṛthā pralāpī yo na syān na lokārādhane rataḥ |
nānyavidyābhiyuktaś ca sa kaivalyāśrame vaset ||
atītān na smared bhogāṃs tathaivānāgatān api |
prāptāṃś ca nābhinandeta sa kaivalyāśrame vaset ||
andhavan mūkavat paṅgubadhiraklībavac ca yaḥ |
āste vrajati yo nityaṃ sa kaivalyāśrame vaset || iti |
uktadharmopetaṃ yatiṃ praśaṃsati dakṣaḥ |
saṃcitaṃ yad gṛhasthasya pāpam āmaraṇāntikam |
nirdahiṣyati tat sarvam ekarātroṣito yatiḥ ||
saṃnyasyantaṃ dvijaṃ dṛṣṭvā sthānāc calati bhāskaraḥ |
eṣa me maṇḍalaṃ bhitvā paraṃ sthānaṃ prayāsyati || iti | (DaSm 7.45–46)
tad evaṃ yatidharmā nirūpitāḥ
uktānāṃ brahmacaryādīnāṃ saṃnyāsāntānāṃ caturṇām āśramāṇāṃ pratyekam avāntarabhedāś caturvidhāḥ | tad uktaṃ mahābhārate |
[197]        brahmacārī gṛhasthaś ca vānaprastho ‘tha bhikṣukaḥ |
catvāra āśramāḥ proktā ekaikasya caturvidhāḥ || iti
tatra cāturvidhyaṃ kātyāyanasmṛtir vyācacakṣe:
brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthaparivrājakāś catvāra āśramāḥ ṣoḍaśabhedā bhavanti | tatra brahmacāriṇaś caturvidhā bhavanti | gāyatro brāhmaḥ prājāpatyo bṛhann iti | upananaynād ūrdhvaṃ trirātram akṣāralavaṇāśī gāyatrīm adhīte sa gāyatraḥ | aṣṭācatvāriṃśadvarṣāṇi vedabrahmacaryaṃ caret prativedaṃ dvādaśa dvādaśa vā yāvadgrahaṇāntaṃ vā vedasya sa brāhmaḥ | svadāranirato ṛtukālagāmī sadā paradāravarjī sa prājāpatyaḥ | atha vā caturviṃśativarṣāṇi gurukulavāsī brāhmaḥ | aṣṭācatvāriṃśadvarṣavāsī ca prājāpatyaḥ | [197] ā prāyaṇād guror aparityāgī sa naiṣṭhiko bṛhann iti | [=ĀśrUp 1]
gṛhasthā api caturvidhā bhavanti | vārtākavṛttayaḥ śālīnavṛttayo yāyāvarā ghorasaṃnyāsikāś cet | tatra vārtākavṛttayaḥ kṛṣigorakṣavāṇijyam agarhitam upayuñjānāḥ śatasaṃvatsarābhiḥ kriyābhir yajanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | śālīnavṛttayo yajanto na yājayanto ‘dhīyānā nādhyāpayanto dadato na pratigṛhṇantaḥ śatasaṃvatsarābhiḥ kriyābhir yajanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | yāyāvarā yajanto yājanato ‘dhīyānā adhyāpayanto dadataḥ pratigṛhṇantaḥ śatasaṃvatsarābhiḥ kriyābhir yajanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | ghorasaṃnyāsikā udbhūtaparipūtābhir adbhiḥ kāryaṃ kurvantaḥ pratidivasam āhṛtoñchavṛttim upayuñjānāḥ śatasaṃvatsarābhiḥ kriyābhir yajanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante || [ĀśrUp 2]
vānaprasthā api caturvidhā bhavanti | vaikhānasā audumbarā vālakhilyāḥ phenapāś ceti | tatra vaikhānasā akṛṣṭapacyoṣadhivanaspatibhir grāmabahiṣkṛtābhir agniparicaraṇaṃ kṛtvā [199] pañcamahāyajñakriyā nirvartayanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | audumbarāḥ prātar utthāya yāṃ diśam abhiprekṣante tadāhṛtodumbarabadaranīvāraśyāmākair agniparicaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pañcamahāyajñakriyā nirvartayanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | vālakhilyā jaṭādharāś cīracarmavalkalaparivṛtāḥ kārtikyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ puṣpaphalam utsṛjantaḥ śeṣān aṣṭau māsān vṛttyupārjanaṃ kṛtvāgniparicaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pañcamahāyajñakriyā nirvartayanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | phenapā unmattakāḥ śrīrṇaparṇaphalabhojino yatra tatra vā vasanto ‘gniparicaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pañcamahāyajñakriyā nirvartayanta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | (ĀśrUp 3)
parivrājakā api caturvidhā bhavanti | kuṭīcakā bahūdakā haṃsāḥ paramahaṃsāś ceti | [200] kuṭīcakāḥ svaputragṛheṣu bhaikṣacaryaṃ caranta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | bahūdakās tridaṇḍakamaṇḍaluśikyapadmajalapavitar-pādukāsanaśikhāyajñopavītakaupīnakāṣāyaveṣadhāriṇaḥ sādhuvṛtteṣu brāhmaṇakuleṣu bhaikṣavaryaṃ caranta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | haṃsā ekadaṇḍadharāḥ śikhāvarjitā yajñopavītadhāriṇaḥ śikyakamaṇḍaluhastā grāmaikarātravāsino nagare tīrtheṣu pañcarātraṃ vasanta ekarātradvirātrakṛcchracāndrāyaṇādi caranta ātmānaṃ prārthayante | paramahaṃsā na daṇḍadharā muṇḍāḥ kanthākaupīnavāsaso ‘vyaktaliṅgā avyaktācārā anunmattā unmattavad ācarantas tridaṇḍakamaṇḍaluśikyapadma-jalapavitrapādukāsanaśikhāyajñopvītatyāginaḥ [201] śūnyāgāradevagṛhavāsino na teṣāṃ dharmo nādharmo na satyaṃ nāpi cānṛtaṃ sarvasahāḥ sarvasamāḥ samalokṣṭāśmakāñcanā yathopapannacāturvarṇye bhaikṣacaryaṃ caranta ātmānaṃ mokṣayante | (ĀśrUp 4) iti |
kātyāyano ‘pi |
teṣām upaśamo dharmo niyamo vanavāsinām |
dānam ekaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ śuśrūṣā brahmacāriṇām || iti |
yuktaṃ ca parivrājakāṇām ātmamokṣaṇam | tattvajñānaparyavasāyitvāt pārivrajyasya | etad evābhipretya tannirvacanaṃ smṛtyantare darśitam |
paribodhāt paricchedāt paripūrṇāvalokanāt |
paripūrṇaphalatvāc ca parivrājaka ucyate ||
parito vrajate nityaṃ paraṃ vā vrajate punaḥ |
hitvā caivāparaṃ janma parivrājaka ucyate || iti |
tad evam adhyāyādau mūlavacane “cāturvarṇyāśramāgatam” ityāśramaśabdena buddhisthā āśramacatuṣṭayadharmāḥ parisamāpitā iti |
dvitīye tv adhyāye sphuṭam abhihito jīvanakṛter
upāyaḥ kṛṣyādiḥ punar atha samastāśramagatāḥ | [202]
garīyāṃso dharmāḥ kim api vivṛtās tv āśramapadāḥ
tam evaṃ vyākārṣīn mahitadhiṣaṇo mādhavavibhuḥ ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārga-pravarttikaśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhvāmātyasya kṛtau parāśrasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ dvitīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ || 1 ||
atha tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ
prathamadvitīyābhyām adhyāyābhyāṃ cāturvarṇyaśramāḥ sākṣāt pratipāditāḥ | āśramadharmāś ca sūcitāḥ | teṣu ca dharmeṣu śuddhasyaivādhikāraḥ, “śucinā karma kartavyam” iti smṛteḥ | sā ca śuddhir yady api puruṣasya svābhāvikī tathāpi kenacid āgantukenāśaucākhyena doṣarūpeṇa puruṣagatātiśayena kaṃcit kālaṃ pratibadhyate | tac cāśaucaṃ kāleyattāsnānādyapanodyam | atas tṛtīye ‘dhyāye tat pratipipādayiṣur ādau pratibandhāpagamenottambhitāṃ śuddhiṃ pratijānīte:
ataḥ śuddhiṃ pravkṣyāmi janane maraṇe tathā |
yato jananamaraṇayor dharmādhikāraparipanthiny aśuddhiḥ [204] prāpnoti atas tannivartakopāyapratibodhanena śuddhiṃ pravakṣyāmi | jananamaraṇayoś ca aśuddhiprāpakatvaṃ manunā darśitam |
dantajāte ‘nujāte ca kṛtacaule ca saṃsthite |
aśuddhā bāndhavāḥ sarve sūtake ca tathocyate || iti | (MDh 5.58)
pratijñātāṃ śuddhiṃ varṇānukrameṇa darśayati |
dinatrayeṇa śudhyanti brāhmaṇāḥ pretasūtake || 1 ||
kṣatriyo dvādaśāhena vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhakaiḥ |
śūdraḥ śudhyati māsena parāśaravaco yathā || 2 ||
nanu – dinatrayeṇa śudhyanti brāhmaṇāḥ ity etad bahusmṛtiviruddham | tathā ca dakṣaḥ |
śudhyed vipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudhyati || iti | (DaSm 6.7)
devalo ‘pi |
daśāhaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tu kṣatriyāṇāṃ tripañcakam |
viṃśadrātraṃ tu vaiśyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ māsam eva tu || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: [205] “brāhmaṇo daśarātreṇa | pañcadaśarātreṇa kṣatriyaḥ | vaiśyo viṃśatirātreṇa | śūdro māsena śudhyati” iti (VaDh 4.27–30) |
naiṣa doṣaḥ, vipreṣu tryahāśaucasya samānodakaviṣayatvāt | athā ca manuḥ: “tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ” iti (MDh 5.64) | daśāhāśaucapratipādakāni dakṣādivacanāni sapiṇḍaviṣayāṇi,
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate | (MDh 5.59)
iti manusmaraṇāt | kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
daśāham āhur āśaucaṃ sapaiṇḍeṣu vipaścitaḥ | iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.1)
bṛhaspatir api |
daśāhena sapiṇḍās tu śudhyanti pretasūtake |
trirātreṇa sakulyās tu snātvā śudhyanti gotrajāḥ ||
[206] iti |
nanu – “kṣatriyo dvādaśāhena” ity etad apy anekasmṛtiviruddham | tatra vasiṣṭha-devalābhyāṃ kṣatriyasya pañcadaśāhāśaucam uktam | tadvacanaṃ codāhṛtam | śātātapas tv ekādaśāham āha |
ekādaśāhād rājanyo vaiśyo dvādaśabhis tathā |
śūdro viṃśatirātreṇa śudhyate mṛtasūtake || iti |
vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi |
kṣatriyas tu daśāhena svakarmanirataḥ śuciḥ |
tathaiva dvādaśāhena vaiśyaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyāt || iti |
atrocyate – vidyātapasos tāratamyena virodhaḥ samādheyaḥ | yāvad yāvad vidyātapasī vivardhete tāvat tāvad āśaucaṃ saṃkucyate | ata eva yājñavalkyo nyāyavartinaḥ śūdrasyāpy ardham āśaucam āha |
kṣatrasya dvādaśāhāni viśaḥ pañcadaśaiva tu |
triṃśaddināni śūdrasya tadardhaṃ nyāyavartinaḥ || iti | (YDh 3.22)
devalo ‘py etad evābhipretya viprādīnām āśaucatāratamyam āha |
catvāry adhītavedānām ahāny āśaucam iṣyate |
vedāgniyuktaviprasya tryaham āśaucam iṣyate ||
[207]        etābhyāṃ śrutayuktasya dinam ekaṃ vidhīyate |
etaiḥ sākaṃ karmayuktaḥ sadyaḥ śucir asaṃśayaḥ ||
etair yuktasya rājñas tu dvādaśaikādaśā daśa |
vaiśyasyaivaṃ pañcadaśadvādaśaikādaśa kramāt ||
ardhamāsaṃ tu śuśrūṣoḥ śūdrasyāśaucam iṣyate || iti |
yat tu kṣatriyādes tripañcakādikaṃ tenaivoktaṃ tad vidyātaporahitaviṣayam,
prākṛtānāṃ tu varṇānām āśaucaṃ saṃprakīrtitam |
iti vākyaśeṣāt | dakṣo ‘pi daśa pakṣān upanyasya guṇotkarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ vyavasthām āha |
sadyaḥśaucaṃ tathaikāhaṃ tryahaś caturahas tathā |
ṣaḍdaśadvādaśāhaṃ ca pakṣo māsas tathaiva ca ||
maraṇāntaṃ tathā cānyat pakṣāś ca daśa sūtake |
upanyāsakrameṇaiva vakṣyāmy aham aśeṣataḥ ||
granthārthato vijānāti vedam aṅgaiḥ samanvitam |
sakalpaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca kriyāvāṃś cen na sūtakam ||
rājartvigdīkṣitānāṃ ca bāle deśāntare tathā |
vratināṃ satriṇāṃ caiva sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
[208]        ekāhāc chudhyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
hīne hīnatare vāpi tryahaś caturahas tathā ||
tathā hīnatame caiva ṣaḍahaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
ye daśāhādayaḥ proktā varṇānāṃ te yathākramam ||
asnātvā cāpy ahutvā ca adatvāśṇaṃs tathā dvijaḥ |
evaṃvidhasya viprasya sarvadā sūtakaṃ bhavet || iti | (DaSm 6.2–9)
atra “catvāry adhītavedānām” ityādinokto ‘ghasaṃkoco yugāntaraviṣayaḥ,
vṛttasvādhyāyasāpekṣam aghasaṃkocanaṃ tathā |
ityādy anukramya,
kalau yuge tv imān dharmān varjyān āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
iti smṛtyantare ‘bhidhānāt,
daśāha eva viprasya sapiṇḍamaraṇe sati |
kalpāntarāṇi kurvāṇaḥ kalau vyāmohakilbiṣī || (HārSm 18.32)
iti hārītavacanāc ca | uktarītyā kṣatriyavad vaiśye ‘pi vacanāntaravirodhaḥ parihartavyaḥ | evaṃ ca sati viprasya samānodakeṣu [209] trirātraṃ sapiṇḍeṣu daśarātram | kṣatriyādīnāṃ dvādaśāhādi yan mūlavacanoktaṃ tad eva sthitam | yady api kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ pañcadaśāhaviṃśatirātra-vacanānusāreṇa dvādaśāhapañcadaśāhāśaucavacanaṃ guṇavadaghasaṃkocaparam ivābhāti devalaś ca guṇavadviṣayatvenaivodājahāra tathāpi śiṣṭācārād bahusmṛtyanugrahāc ca kṣatriyavaiśyayor mūlavacanoka eva mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | ata eva manu-kūrma-dakṣāḥ |
śudhyed vipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudhyati || iti | (MDh 5.83)
mārkaṇḍeyao ‘pi |
daśāhaṃ brāhmaṇas tiṣṭhed dānahomādivarjitaḥ |
kṣatriyo dvādaśāhaṃ tu vaiśyo māsārdham eva ca |
śūdras tu māsam āsīta nijakarmavivarjitaḥ || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
kṣatriyo dvādaśāhena śudhyate mṛtasūtake |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śudhyati || iti |
viṣṇur api: “brāhmaṇasya sapiṇḍānām jananamaraṇayor daśāham āśaucam | dvādaśāhaṃ rājanyasya | pañcadaśāhaṃ vaiśyasya | māsaṃ śūdrasya” iti (ViDh 22.1–4) | pañcādaśāhaviṃśatirātravacanaṃ tu yāvajjīvāśaucavākyam [210] iva nindāparatvena yugāntaraviṣayatvena vā vyākhyeyam | “parāśravaco yathā” ity anena svamatatvaṃ darśayan matāntareṣv aghasaṃkocavikāsaparāṇi vacanāni santīti sūcayati | tāni cāsmābhir vyavasthāpitāni || 2 ||
uktasyāśaucasya karmādhikāraparipanthitvāt saṃdhyādyupāsanasyāpi nivṛttiprāptāv apavādam āha |
upāsane tu viprāṇām aṅgaśuddhiś ca jāyate |
upāsanaṃ saṃdhyāvandanāgnihotrādyanuṣṭhānaṃ tasmin prasakte tātkālikī śarīraśuddhir bhavati | tad āha gobhilaḥ |
agnihotrādihomārthaṃ śuddhis tātkālikī smṛtā |
pañcayajñāṇ na kurvīta hy aśuddhaḥ punar eva saḥ || iti |
yāvatkālenāgnihotraṃ niṣpadyate tāvad eva śuddhir na tūpari | pulastyo ‘pi |
saṃdhyāsiddhiṃ caruṃ homaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ samācaret |
na tyajet sūtake vāpi tyajan gacched adho dvijaḥ ||
sūtake mṛtake caiva saṃdhyākarma na saṃtyajet |
manasoccārayet mantrān prāṇāyāmam ṛte dvijaḥ || iti |
añjaliprakṣepe tu vācoccāraṇam abhipretya paiṭhīnasir āha: “sūtake sāvitryāñjaliṃ prakṣipya pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā sūryaṃ dhyāyan namaskuryāt”[211] iti | manasoccāraṇasya mārjanādimantreṣv api siddhatvād añjalau viśeṣavidhānaṃ vācikābhiprāyam | yat tu manunoktam,
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate || (MDh 5.62)
iti, tat smārtavaiśvadevādiviṣayam | tad āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
pañcayajñavidhānaṃ ca na kuryāt mṛtyujanmanoḥ | iti |
yat tu jābālenoktam,
saṃdhyāṃ pañcamahāyajñān naityakaṃ smṛtikarma ca |
tanmadhye hāpayed eva āśaucānte tu tatkriyā ||
iti, tad vācikasaṃdhyābhiprāyam | smārtakarmavarjanaṃ svayaṃkartṛkaviṣayam | anyena tu kārayed eva | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sūtake mṛtake caiva hy aśaktau śrāddhabhojane |
pravāsādinimitteṣu hāvayen na tu hāpayet || iti |
jātūkarṇyo ‘pi |
sūtake tu samutpanne smārtaṃ karma kathaṃ bhavet |
piṇḍayajñaṃ caruṃ homam asagotreṇa kārayet || iti |
mūlavacane vipragrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyādīnām upalakṣaṇam | [212] dvividhaṃ cāśucitvaṃ karmānadhikāralakṣaṇam aspṛśyatvalakṣaṇaṃ ca | tatrāṅgaśuddhir ity anenaikasya nivṛttir uktā cakāreṇāparasyāpi |
yathā śāve tātkālikī dvividhāśuddhis tathā janane ‘pi tatprāptau viśeṣam āha |
brāhmaṇānāṃ prasūtau tu dehasparśo vidhīyate || 3 || iti |
janane sapiṇḍānāṃ sārvakāliko ‘ṅgasparśaḥ | na tu śāvavat tātkālikaḥ | ata eva aṅgirāḥ |
sūtake sūtikāvarjaṃ saṃsparśo na niṣidhyate |
saṃsparśe sūtikāyās tu snānam eva vidhīyate || iti |
kaurme ‘pi |
sūtake tu sapiṇḍānāṃ saṃsparśo naiva duṣyati | iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.5)
paiṭhīnasir api |
janau sapiṇḍāḥ śucayo mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti |
janane mātāpitṛvyatiriktāḥ sarve sapiḍṇāḥ spṛśyāḥ | mātāpitros tu nāsti spṛśyatvam | tatrāpi pitā snānena spṛśyo bhavati | daśāham aspṛśyatvaṃ mātur eva | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
nāśaucaṃ vidyate puṃsaḥ saṃsargaṃ cen na gacchati |
rajas tatrāśuci jñeyaṃ tac ca puṃsi na vidyate || (VaDh 4.23)
[213] iti | saṃvarto ‘pi |
jāte putre pituḥ snānaṃ sacailaṃ tu vidhīyate |
mātā śuddhyed daśāhena snānāt tu sparśanaṃ pituḥ || iti | (SaṃSm 1.43)
|| 3 ||
maraṇe varṇānukrameṇa śuddhir darśitā | idānīṃ janane ‘pi varṇakrameṇa śuddhiṃ darśayati |
jātau vipro daśāhena dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ |
vaiśyaḥ pañcadaśāhena śūdro māsena śuddhyati || 4 || iti |
jātau janane | spaṣṭam anyat | iyaṃ ca śuddhiḥ karmādhikāraviṣayā, samantarātītena vacanena sparśaviṣayāyāḥ śuddher uktatvāt | janmadivase tu nāsty aśuddhir dānādiviṣaye | ata eva manuḥ |
jāte kumāre tad ahaḥ kāmaṃ kuryāt pratigraham |
hiraṇyadhānyagovāsastilānāṃ guḍasarpiṣām || iti |
śaṅkhalikhitau: “kumāraprasave nābhyām acchinnāyāṃ guḍatilahiraṇyavastraprāvaraṇa-godhānyānāṃ pratigraheṣv adoṣaḥ | tad ahar ity eke” | [214] iti | vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
kumārajanmadivase vipraiḥ kāryaḥ pratigrahaḥ |
hiraṇyabhūgavāśvājavāsaḥśayyāsanādiṣu ||
tatra sarvaṃ pratigrāhyaṃ kṛtānnaṃ tu na bhakṣayet |
bhakṣayitvā tu tan mohāt dvijaś cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret || iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
guḍtailahiraṇyānāṃ godhānyānāṃ ca vāsasām |
tasminn ahani dānaṃ ca kāryaṃ vipraiḥ pratigrahaḥ |
prāṅ nābhicchedanād grāhyāṇy etānīty apare jaguḥ || iti |
yās tu janmadākhyāḥ sūtikāgṛhābhimāninyo devatāḥ tāsāṃ pūyāyāṃ prathamaṣaṣṭhadaśamādi-divaseṣv aśuddhir nāsti | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
sūtikāvāsatilayā janmadā nāma devatāḥ |
tāsāṃ yāganimittaṃ tu śuddhir janmani kīrtitā ||
prathame divase ṣaṣṭhe daśame caiva sarvadā |
triṣv eteṣu na kurvīta sūtakaṃ putrajanmani || iti |
mārkaṇḍeyo ‘pi |
rakṣaṇīyā tathā ṣaṣṭhī niśā tatra viśeṣataḥ |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryāj janmadānāṃ tathā balim ||
[215]         puruṣāḥ śastrahastāś ca nṛtyagītaiś ca yoṣitaḥ |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kuryur daśamyāṃ caiva sūtake || iti |
yat tv aṅgirasoktam,
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ kriyāvatām |
iti, tat pūrvoktāgnihotrādiviṣayatvena vā samanantaroktajanmadānāṃ baliviṣayatvena vā netavyam | anyathā “jātau vipro daśāhena” ity etad vacanaṃ nirviṣayaṃ syāt || 4 ||
uktasya pretāśaucasya jātāśaucasya ca kvacit saṃkocam āha |
ekāhāc chudhyate vipro yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ |
tryahāt kevalavedas tu dvihīno daśabhir dinaiḥ || 5 || iti |
atrāgniśabdenāhavanīyādayo gṛhyante | taiś ca tatsādhyā darśapūrṇamāsādaya upalakṣyante | dvihīno dvābhyām agnivedābhyāṃ hīnaḥ | ayam āśaucasaṃkocaḥ svādhyāyadurmedhaso bahutarasapiṇḍasya saṃkucitavṛtteś ca pratigrahādau draṣṭavyaḥ | na tu sarvakarmasu | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “brāhmaṇasya svādhyāyānivṛttyartham” iti (GDh 14.21) | ayam arthaḥ – brāhmaṇasya saṃpūrṇāśauce svīkriyamāṇe [216] svādhyāyo nivarteta tan mā bhūd iti | svādhyāyānivṛttigrahaṇaṃ pratigrahasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham | anyathā aśvastanikādīnāṃ saṃkucitavṛttīnāṃ saṃpūrṇāśauce svīkriyamāṇe jīvanam eva na syāt | evaṃ ca sati ekāhavidhānam aśvastanikaviṣayaṃ tryahavidhānaṃ tyāhikaviṣayaṃ asaṃkucitavṛttes tu daśāham – iti vyavasthā | evamuktarītyā,
sadyaḥśaucaṃ tathaikāhaṃ tryahaś caturahas tathā |
ṣaḍdaśadvādaśāhaṃ ca pakṣo māsas tathaiva ca || (DaSm 6.2)
iti dakṣoktāḥ pakṣā vyavasthāpanīyāḥ | vṛttisaṃkocenāśaucasaṃkocam āha saṃgrahakāraḥ |
śiloñchāyācitair jīvan sadyaḥ śudhyed dvijottamaḥ | iti |
nanu – vidvadavidvadviṣayatvenaivāyam āśaucasaṃkocaḥ sarvakarmasu kiṃ neṣyate, “yo ‘gnivedasamanvitaḥ” iti viśeṣaṇasāmarthāt |
tan na –
daśāhaṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ sapiṇḍeṣu vidhīyate | (MDh 5.59)
ity aviśeṣeṇa daśāhāśaucavidhānāt | na ca – sāmānyaprāptasya daśāhāśaucasya vidvadviṣaye bādhaḥ – iti śaṅkanīyam, bādhasyānupapattihetukatvāt | yāvaty abādhite ‘nupapattir na śāmyati [217] tāvad bādhanīyam | atra cādhyayanapratigrahādimātra eva | daśāhāśaucabādhenaikāhāśaucavidhānasya caritārthatvān na sarvatra daśāhāśaucabādhaḥ | agnivedasamanvitatvam aśvastanikasyaikāhāśaucavidhistutyartham, na tv ekāhāśaucavidhyadhikāriviśeṣaṇam | yat tu,
ubhayatra daśāhāni kulasyānnaṃ na bhujyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaś ca nivartate || (MDh 5.62)
iti manunā pratigrahādiniṣedhanaṃ kṛtam, tad asaṃkucitavṛttiviṣayam | yad vā uktāpavādapratisavābhiprāyeṇa vā neyam || 5 ||
yadi vṛttisaṃkocāsaṃkocāv evāśaucasaṃkocāsaṃkocayoḥ kāraṇaṃ, tarhy atyantāsaṃkucita-vṛtter nirguṇasyāmaraṇam āśaucaṃ prāpnotīty āśaṅkya āśaucāvadhiṃ darśayati |
janmakarmaparibhraṣṭaḥ saṃdhyopāsanavarjitaḥ |
nāmadhārakavipras tu daśāhaṃ sūtakī bhavet || 6 || iti |
janmakarmaparibhraṣṭaḥ garbhādhānādisaṃskārarahitaḥ | saṃdhyopāsanavarjitaḥ saṃdhyopāsanādinityanaimittikakarmāṇy [218] akurvāṇaḥ ata evāsau nāmadhārakavipro bhavati | tasyāpi daśāham evāśaucam | nāmadhārakaviprasvarūpaṃ darśayati vyāsaḥ |
brahmabījasamutpanno mantrasaṃskāravarjitaḥ |
jātimātropajīvī ca sa bhaven nāmadhārakaḥ ||
garbhādhānādibhir yuktas tathopanayanena ca |
na karmavit na vādhīte sa bhaven nāmadhārakaḥ || iti | (VyāSm 4.42–43)
nanu – saṃskārarahitasya nāmadhārakaviprasya maraṇāntikam āśaucaṃ kūrmapurāṇe’bhihitam |
kriyāhīnasya mūrkhasya mahārogiṇa eva ca |
yatheṣṭācaraṇasyāhur maraṇāntam aśaucakam || iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.9)
dakṣo ‘pi |
vyādhitasya kadaryasya ṛṇagrastasya sarvadā |
kriyāhīnasya mūrkhasya strījitasya viśeṣataḥ ||
vyasanāsaktacittasya parādhīnasya nityaśaḥ |
śraddhātyāgavihīnasya bhasmāntaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet ||
nāsūtakaṃ kadācit syād yāvajjīvaṃ tu sūtakam || (DaSm 6.9–11)
[219] tat kathaṃ daśāhāśaucam – iti |
ucyate | nindārthavādatvād eteṣāṃ vacanānāṃ na yāvajjīvāśaucavidhiparatvam | anyathā
nāmadhārakavipras tu daśāhaṃ sūtakī bhavet | (ParSm 3.6)
ity etad vacanaṃ virudhyeta || 6 ||
caturṇām api varṇānām āśaucam abhidhāya adhunottamavarṇena hīnavarṇāsūtpannānām uttamavarṇasaṃbandhini janane maraṇe cāśaucam āha |
ekapiṇḍās tu dāyādāḥ pṛthagdāraniketanāḥ |
janmany api vipattau ca teṣāṃ tat sūtakaṃ bhavet || 7 || iti |
ekaḥ piṇḍa uttamavarṇadeha utpādako yeṣāṃ te tathā | pṛthagdāraniketanāḥ hīnavarṇāḥ striyaḥ niketanāni utpattisthānāni yeṣāṃ te tathā | dāyādāḥ putrāḥ | teṣām uttamavarṇasaṃbandhini janane maraṇe ca sati tat sūtakam uttamavarṇasaṃbandhyāśaucaṃ bhavet | tathā ca manuḥ |
sarveṣūttamavarṇānām āśaucaṃ kuryur ādṛtāḥ |
tadvarṇavidhidṛṣṭen svāśaucaṃ tu svayoniṣu || iti | (MDh 5.87)
ayam arthaḥ – sarve hīnavarṇā uttamavarṇānāṃ saṃbandhini janane maraṇe vā uttamavarṇavidhidṛṣṭena daśarātrādikāśaucaṃ kuryuḥ | svayoniṣu jāteṣu mṛteṣu ca svāśaucaṃ kuryuḥ | kaurme ‘pi |
[220]        śūdraviṭkṣatriyāṇāṃ tu brāhmaṇe saṃsthite sati |
daśarātreṇa śuddhiḥ syād ity āha kamalodbhavaḥ || iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.45)
devalo ‘pi |
sarvavarṇeṣu dāyādā ye syur viprasya bāndhavāḥ |
teṣāṃ daśāham āśaucaṃ viprāśauce vidhīyate || iti |
etac cāśaucam avibhaktaviṣayam | tathā ca āpastambaḥ |
kṣatraviṭśūdrajātīnāṃ yadi stho mṛtasūtake |
teṣāṃ tu paitṛkāśaucaṃ vibhaktānāṃ tv apaitṛkam || iti |
apaitṛkaṃ mātṛjājīyam ity arthaḥ | adhamavarṇasaṃbandhini jananādau uttamavarṇasya yadāśaucaṃ tad uktaṃ kūrmapurāṇe |
ṣaḍrātraṃ vā trirātraṃ syād ekarātraṃ krameṇa tu |
vaiśyakṣatriyaviprāṇāṃ śūdre cāśaucam eva hi || iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.42)
viṣṇur api: “brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyaviṭśūdreṣu sapiṇḍeṣu ṣaḍrātratrirātraikarātraiḥ | kṣatriyasya viṭśūdreṣu ṣaḍrātratrirātrābhyām | vaiśyasya śūdreṣu ṣaḍrātreṇa” iti (ViDh 22.22–24) | bṛhaspatis tu prakārāntareṇāśaucam āha |
[221]        daśāhāc chudhyate vipro janmahānyoḥ svayoniṣu |
saptapañcatrirātrais tu kṣatraviṭśūdrayoniṣu || iti |
atra ṣaḍrātrasaptarātrādipakṣayor vikalpaḥ | snehādinā vā vyavasthān || 6 ||
uktasya bhinnajātīyaviṣayasyāśaucasya sajātīyeṣv iva sāptapuruṣatvaprāptau tadavadhim āha |
tāvat tat sūtakaṃ gotre caturthapuruṣeṇa tu | iti |
tat sūtakaṃ bhinnajātīyasantativiṣayoktam āśaucam | tāvat yāvat tripuruṣam | caturthapuruṣeṇa tu nivartate, tatra sāpiṇḍyanivṛttaḥ,
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
sajātīyeṣu varṇeṣu caturthe bhinnajātiṣu ||
iti vṛddhaparāśaravacanāt | śātātapo ‘pi |
yady ekajātā bahavaḥ pṛthakkṣetrāḥ pṛthagdanāḥ |
ekapiṇḍāḥ pṛthakśaucāḥ piṇḍas tv āvartate triṣu || iti |
sajātīyeṣu pañcamādiṣv āśaucatāratamyaṃ vaktuṃ sāpiṇḍyanivṛttim āha |
dāyād vicchedam āpnoti pañcamo vātmavṃśajaḥ || 8 || iti |
dāyaśabdena piṇḍo lakṣyate | tasmād vicchedam avāpnoti | ātmavaṃśajaḥ pañcamaḥ | vāśabdāt ṣaṣṭhasaptamau vā | tatra sāpiṇḍyaṃ nivartate – iti | tad uktaṃ gautamena: [222] “piṇḍanivṛttiḥ pañcame saptame vā” iti (GDh 14.7) | vāśabdāt ṣaṣṭhe || 8 ||
yadarthaṃ sāpiṇḍyanivṛttir abhihitā tad idānīm āha |
caturthe daśarātraṃ syāt ṣaṇṇiśāḥ puṃsi pañcame |
ṣaṣṭhe caturahāc chuddhiḥ saptame tu dinatrayāt || 9 || iti |
pitṛpakṣe kūṭastham ārabhya gaṇanāyāṃ caturthe daśarātram āśaucam | pañcame ṣaḍrātraṃ ṣaṣṭhe catūrātraṃ saptame trirātram iti |
nanu – sāpiṇḍyasya saptapuruṣaparyantatvāt sapiṇḍeṣu cāviśeṣeṇa daśāhāśaucavidhānād āśaucasya saṃkocavidhānam anupapannam | sāpiṇḍyasya saptapuruṣaparyantatvaṃ matsyapurāṇe ‘bhihitam |
lepabhājaś caturthādyāḥ pitrādyāḥ piṇḍabhāginaḥ |
piṇḍadaḥ saptamas teṣāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ sāptapauruṣam || iti | (MatsPu 18.29)
manur api |
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu janmanāmnor avedane || (MDh 5.60)
[223] iti |
satyam – tathāpi pañcamādiṣu sāpiṇḍyanivṛtter vikalpena smṛtatvāt tadanurodhenāśaucasaṃkocavidhānaṃ vikalpena yujyate | udāhṛtaṃ ca gautamavacanam: “piṇḍanivṛttiḥ pañcame saptame vā” iti (GDh 14.7) | paiṭhīnasir api: “trīn atītya mātṛtaḥ pañcātītya pitṛtaḥ” iti |
nanu – evaṃ tarhi pañcamādīnāṃ samānodakatvena “tryahāt tūdakadāyinaḥ” iti trirātram āśaucaṃ prāpnuyāt ataḥ ṣaḍrātrādividhānam anupapannam iti |
satyam – pañcamādiṣu trirātrāśaucaṃ prāpnoti tathāpi viśeṣavidhānād apodyate | sāmānyaśāstrasya viśeṣaśāstraviṣayetaraparatvasya yuktatvāt || 9 ||
uktasya pretāśaucasya kvacid apavādam āha |
bhṛgvagnimaraṇe caiva deśāntaramṛte tathā |
bāle prete ca saṃnyaste sadhyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyat || 10 || iti |
bhṛguḥ prapātaḥ | agniḥ prasiddhaḥ | bhṛgvagnimaraṇaṃ pramādādina vinā durmaraṇamātropalakṣaṇam, prāyaścittānurodhāt | etannimitte [224] maraṇe sati tatsaṃbandhināṃ sarveṣāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ na tu daśāhāśaucam iti | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
hatānāṃ nṛpagoviprair anvakṣaṃ cātmaghātinām | iti | (YDh 3.21)
nṛpo ‘bhiṣiktaḥ kṣatriyaḥ | gośabdaḥ śṛṅgidaṃṣṭryādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ tiraścām upalakṣakaḥ | vipragrahaṇaṃ caṇḍālādyupalakṣaṇārtham | etair nṛpādibhir hatānāṃ vidhim antareṇātmatyāgakāriṇāṃ ca ye saṃbandhinaḥ sapiṇḍāḥ teṣāṃ anvakṣaṃ yāvacchavadarśanam āśaucaṃ na tu daśāhādiparyantam ity arthaḥ | durmṛtānām udakadānādikam api nāsti | tathā ca yamaḥ |
caṇḍālād udakāt sarpād brāhmaṇād vaidyutād api |
daṃṣṭribhyaś ca paśubhyaś ca maraṇaṃ pāpakarmaṇām ||
udakaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ ca pretebhyo yat pradīyate |
nopatiṣṭhati tat sarvam antarikṣe vinaśyati ||
nāśaucaṃ nodakaṃ nāśru na dāhādyantakarma ca |
brahmadaṇḍahatānāṃ ca na kuryāt kaṭadhāraṇam || iti |
brahmadaṇḍo brāhmaṇaśāpaḥ abhicāro vā | kaṭaśabdena śavavahanopayogikaṭādikam abhidhīyate | āpastambo ‘pi |
vyāpādayed ya ātmānaṃ svayam agnyudakādibhiḥ |
vihitaṃ tasya nāśaucaṃ nāpi kāryodakakriyā ||
[225] iti | etac ca buddhipūrvakamaraṇaviṣayam | ata eva gautamaḥ: “gobrāhmaṇahatānām anvakṣam | rājakrodhāc ca | yuddhe | prāyo’nāśakaśastrāgniviṣodakodbandhanaprapatanaiś cecchatām” iti (GDh 14.6–9) | prāyo mahāprasthānam | anāśakam anaśanam | prapatanaṃ bhṛgupatanam | etair buddhipūrvakaṃ hatānāṃ sapiṇḍasyānvakṣam āśaucam ity arthaḥ | ataś caitad uktaṃ bhavati | sarpādinā caṇḍālādinā vā vigrahaṃ kurvan yas tair hataḥ tasyaivāyaṃ piṇḍadānādiniṣedhaḥ | evaṃ duṣṭadaṃṣṭryādīn grahītum ābhimukhyena gacchato maraṇe ‘yam āśaucādiniṣedhaḥ | evaṃ rājñaḥ prātikūlyam ācarato maraṇe | evaṃ bāhubhyāṃ nadītaraṇe ‘pi | evaṃ sarvatrānusaṃdheyam | ata eva brahmapurāṇam |
śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrinakhivyālaviṣavahnimahājalaiḥ |
sudūrāt parihartavyaiḥ kurvan krīḍāṃ mṛtas tu yaḥ ||
[226]         nāgānāṃ vipriyaṃ kurvan dagdhaś cāpy atha vidyutā |
nigṛhītāś ca ye rājñā cauryadoṣeṇa kutracit ||
paradārān harantaś ca roṣāt tatpatibhir hatāḥ |
asamānaiś ca saṃkīrṇaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca vigraham ||
kṛtvā tair nihatās tadvac caṇḍālādīn samāśritāḥ |
krodhāt prāyaṃ viṣaṃ vahniṃ śastram udbandhanaṃ jalam ||
girivṛkṣaprapātaṃ ca ye kurvanti narādhamāḥ |
mahāpātakino ye ca patitās te prakīrtitāḥ ||
patitānāṃ na dāhah syān nāntyeṣṭir nāsthisaṃcayaḥ|
na vāśrupātaḥ piṇḍo vā kāryaṃ śrāddhādikaṃ kvacit || iti |
nanu – ayaṃ caṇḍālādihatānām agnisaṃskāraniṣedho ‘nāhitāgniviṣayaḥ | āhitāgniviṣayatve “āhitāgnim agnibhir yajñapātraiś ca dahet” iti śrutivihitāgniyajñapātrādi-pratipattilopaprasaṅgād iti |
maivam, smṛtyantare caṇḍālādihatāhitāgnisaṃbandhinām agnīnāṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ ca pratipattyantaravidhānāt |
vaitānaṃ prakṣiped apsu āvasathyaṃ catuṣpathe |
pātrāṇi tu dahed agnau yajamāne vṛthā mṛte ||
ātmanas tyāgināṃ nāsti patitānāṃ tathā kriyā |
teṣām api tathā gaṅgātoye saṃsthāpanaṃ hitam || iti |
tasmāt sarveṣāṃ durmṛtānām aviśeṣeṇa dāhādiniṣedhaḥ | ayam āśaucaśrāddhādiniṣedho yāvat saṃvatsaram | pūrṇe tu [227] saṃvatsare pretasya śrāddhādisaṃpradānayogyatāsiddhyarthaṃ nārāyaṇabaliṃ kṛtvā sarvam aurdhvadaihikaṃ kāryam eva | tad uktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
gobrāhmaṇahatānāṃ ca patitānāṃ tathaiva ca |
ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāt kāryaṃ sarvam evaurdhvadaihikam || iti |
nārāyaṇabaleś ca pretaśuddhyāpādakatvaṃ vyāsenoktam |
nārāyaṇaṃ samuddiśya śivaṃ vā yat pradīyate |
tasya śuddhikaraṃ karma tad bhaven na tad anyathā || iti |
sarpahate tv ayaṃ viśeṣaḥ – saṃvatsaraparyantaṃ pañcabhyāṃ nāgapūjāṃ kṛtvā saṃvatsarānantaraṃ nārāyaṇabaliṃ kṛtvā sauvarṇaṃ nāgaṃ dadyāt pratyakṣāṃ ca gām | tad uktaṃ bhaviṣottarapurāṇe |
suvarṇākāraniṣpannaṃ nāgaṃ kṛtvā tathaiva gām |
viprāya datvā vidhivat pitur ānṛṇyam āpnuyāt || iti |
pramādamaraṇe tv āśaucam asty eva | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
yadi kaścit pramādena mriyate ‘gnyudakādibhiḥ |
tasyāśaucaṃ vidhātavyaṃ kartavyā codakakriyā || iti |
brahmapurāne ‘pi |
pramādād atha niḥśaṅkam akasmāt vidhicoditaḥ |
śṛṅgidaṃṣṭrinakhivyālaviṣavidyujjalāgnibhiḥ ||
[228]        caṇḍālair atha vā caurair nihato yata kutracit |
tasya dāhādikaṃ kāryaṃ yasmān na patitas tu saḥ || iti |
vidhito bhṛgvagnimaraṇe tu asti viśeṣaḥ | tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
vṛddhaḥ śaucakriyāluptaḥ pratyākhyātabhiṣakkriyaḥ |
ātmānaṃ ghātayed yas tu bhṛgvagnyanaśanādibhiḥ ||
tatra trirātram āśaucaṃ dvitīye tv asthisaṃcayaḥ |
tṛtīye tūdakaṃ kṛtvā caturthe śrāddham ācaret || iti |
asti ca bhṛgvādau vidhiḥ | tathā ca ādityapurāṇam |
duścikitsvyair mahārogaiḥ pīḍitas tu pumān yadi |
praviśej jvalanaṃ dīptaṃ kuryād anaśanaṃ tathā ||
agādhatoyarāśiṃ vā bhṛgoḥ patanam eva vā |
gacchen mahāpathaṃ vāpi tuṣāragirim ādarāt ||
prayāgavaṭaśākhāyāṃ dehatyāgaṃ karoti vā |
uttamān āpnuyāl lokān nātmaghātī bhavet kvacit ||
[229]        vārāṇasyāṃ mṛto yas tu pratyākhyātabhiṣakkriyaḥ |
kāṣṭhapāṣāṇamadhyastho jāhnavījalamadhyagaḥ ||
avimuktonmukhas tasya karṇamūlagato haraḥ |
praṇavaṃ tārakaṃ brūte nānyathā kutracit kvacit || iti |
brahmagarbhaḥ |
yo ‘nuṣṭhātuṃ na śaknoti mahāvyādhyupapīḍitaḥ |
so ‘gnivārimahāyātrāṃ kurvan nāmutra duṣyati || iti |
“deśāntaramṛta” it asapiṇḍe deśāntaramṛte sadyaḥ śaucam ity arthaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
bāle deśāntarasthe ca pṛthakpiṇḍe ca saṃsthite |
savāsā jalam āplutya sadya eva viśudhyati || iti | (MDh 5.78)
deśāntarasthatvena vā sapiṇḍo viśiṣyate | deśāntaralakṣaṇaṃ vṛddhamanunoktam |
mahānadyantaraṃ yatra girir vā vyavadhāyakaḥ |
vāco yatra vibhidyante tad deśāntaram ucyate || iti |
bṛhaspatināpi |
deśāntaraṃ vadanty eke ṣaṣṭiyojanam āyatam |
catvāriṃśad vadanty eke anye triṃśat tathaiva ca || iti |
yojanalakṣaṇaṃ tu smṛtyantare ‘bhihitam |
tiryagyavodarāṇy aṣṭau ūrdhvā vā vrīhayas trayaḥ |
[230]        pramāṇam aṅgulasyokaṃ vitastir dvādaśāṅgulam ||
vitaster dviguṇo ‘ratnis tasmāt kiṣkus tato dhanuḥ |
dhanuḥsahasre dve krośaś catuḥkrośaṃ tu yojanam || iti |
bālo ‘trākṛtanāmā | tasmin mṛte sati tatsapiṇḍānāṃ maraṇanimitte sadyaḥ śaucam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ: “prāṅnāmakaraṇāt sadyaḥ śuddhiḥ” iti | kātyāyano ‘pi |
anivṛtte daśāhe tu pañcatvaṃ yadi gacchati |
sadya eva viśuddhiḥ syāt na pretaṃ nodakakriyā || iti |
mātāpitṛsahodaravyatiriktaviṣayam etat | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
bāle mṛte sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
daśāhenaiva dampatyoḥ sodarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca || iti |
jāte mṛte mṛtajāte vā sapiṇḍānāṃ sadhyaśaucam | janmadivase śiśumaraṇe mātrādīnāṃ daśāhenaiva śuddhiḥ | divasāntaramaraṇe tu śeṣāhobhir viśuddhiḥ | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
antardāhe jātasya śiśor niṣkramaṇaṃ yadā |
sūtakenaiva śuddhiḥ syāt pitroḥ śātātapo ‘bravīt || iti |
smṛtyantaram api: “antardaśāhoparatasya yat pitrādīnāṃ maraṇāśaucaṃ tat sūtakāhobhiḥ” [231] iti | gacchatīti śeṣaḥ | janananimittaṃ tv āśaucaṃ sarvām asty eva | tathā ca hārītaḥ: “jātamṛte mṛtajāte vā sapiṇḍānāṃ daśāhaḥ” iti | bṛhanmanur api |
daśāhābhyantare bāle pramīte tasya bāndhavaiḥ |
śāvāśaucaṃ na kartavyaṃ sūtyāśaucaṃ samācaret || iti |
etac ca nābhicchedād ūrdhvaṃ veditavyam | tathā ca jaiminiḥ |
yāvan na chidyate nālaṃ tāvan nāpnoti sūtakam |
chinne nāle tataḥ paścāt sūtakaṃ tu vidhīyate || iti |
nābhicchedāt prāg bṛhanmanur āha |
jīvan jāto yadi tato mṛtaḥ sūtaka eva tu |
sūtakaṃ sakalaṃ mātuḥ pitrādīnāṃ trirātrakam || iti |
yat tu bṛhatpracetovacanam,
muhūrtaṃ jīvato bālaḥ pañcatvaṃ yadi gacchati |
mātuḥ śaucaṃ daśāhena sadyaḥśaucās tu gotriṇaḥ ||
iti, tad agnihotrārthaṃ sadyaḥśaucapratipādanaparam | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ: “agnihotrādyanuṣṭhānārthaṃ snātvopasparśanāt tat kālaṃ śaucam” iti | saṃnyaste mṛte sati tatsapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam | tathā ca vāmanapurāṇam |
[232]        bāle pravrajite caiva deśāntaramṛte tathā |
sadyaḥśaucaṃ samākhyātaṃ vidyutpātamṛte tathā || iti | (VāPu 14.99–100)
smṛtyantaram api |
sarvasaṅganivṛttasya dhyānayogaratasya ca |
na tasya dahanaṃ kāryaṃ nāśaucaṃ nodakakriyā || iti || 10 ||
pūrvam asapiṇḍasya deśāntaragatasya maraṇaśravaṇe tatsapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam abhidhāya adhunā deśāntaragatasya sapiṇḍasya saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ maraṇaśravaṇe ‘pi tatsapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidadhāti |
deśāntaramṛtaḥ kaścit sagotraḥ śrūyate yadi |
na trirātram ahorātraṃ sadyaḥ snātvā śucir bhavet || 11 || iti |
sagotraḥ sapiṇḍaḥ | tasya deśāntaragatasya saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ maraṇaśravaṇe tatsapiṇḍānāṃ na trirātram ahorātraṃ vāśucaṃ kiṃ tu sadyaḥśaucam | daśāhād ūrdhvam arvāk tripakṣāt trirātraṃ ṣaṇmāsād arvāk pakṣiṇī arvāk saṃvatsarād ekāham ity arthaḥ | tathā ca devalaḥ: “ā tripakṣāt trirātraṃ syāt ṣaṇmāsāt pakṣiṇī tataḥ | param ekāham ā varṣād ūrdhvaṃ snāto viśudhyati” iti | viṣṇur api: [233] “arvāk tripakṣāt triṇiśaṃ ṣaṇmāsāc ca divāniśam | ahaḥ saṃvatsarād arvāg deśāntaramṛteṣv api” iti | atra divāśabdenāhardvayam ucyate, “ṣaṇmāsāt pakṣiṇī” iti vacanāntarāt | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
proṣite kālaśeṣaḥ syāt pūrṇe datvodakaṃ śuciḥ | iti | (YDh 3.21)
proṣite deśāntarasthe sapiṇḍe mṛte āśaucamadhye śrute sati tatkālaśeṣeṇaiva śuddhiḥ | pūrṇe saṃvatsare vyatīte tanmaraṇaśravaṇe snātvodakaṃ datvā śucir bhavatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
saṃvatsare vyatīte tu spṛṣṭvaivāpo viśudhyati | iti | (MDh 5.76)
yat tu gautamenoktam: “śrutvā cordhvaṃ daśamyāḥ pakṣiṇī” (GDh 14.11) iti, tat tripakṣād ūrdhvam arvāk ṣaṇmāsād veditavyam, “ṣaṇmāsāt pakṣiṇī” iti devalasmaraṇāt | yat punar vasiṣṭhavacanam: “deśāntarasthe mṛte ūrdhvaṃ daśāhāt śrutvā ekarātram” (VaDh 4.36) iti, yac ca gadyaviṣṇuvacanam: [234] “vyatīte tv āśauce saṃvatsarasyāntas tv ekarātreṇa | tataḥ paraṃ snānena” (VaDh 22.40–41) iti, tat ūrdhvaṃ ṣaṇmāsād arvāk saṃvatsarād veditavyam, “param ekāham ā varṣāt” iti smaraṇāt | yad api śaṅkhavacanam “atīte daśarātre tu trirātram aśucir bhavet” (ŚaṅSm 15.12) iti, tat tripakṣād arvāg draṣṭavyam, “arvāk tripakṣāt triṇiśam” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | atra mūlavacanoktaṃ sadyaḥśaucavidhānaṃ jñātimātraviṣayam | pitrādiviṣaye tu asti viśeṣaḥ | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pitarau cen mṛtau syātāṃ dūrastho ‘pi hi putrakaḥ |
śrutvā tad dinam ārabhya daśāhaṃ sūtakī bhavet || iti |
dakṣo ‘pi |
mahāgurunipāte tu ārdravastropavāsinā |
atīte ‘bde ‘pi kartavyaṃ pretakāryaṃ yathāvidhi || iti |
saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam apy āśaucodakadānādikaṃ kāryaṃ na punaḥ snānamātrāc chuddhir ity arthaḥ | pitṛpatnyāṃ mātṛvyatiriktāyāṃ viśeṣo dakṣeṇa darśitaḥ |
[235]        pitṛpatnyām atītāyāṃ mātṛvarjaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
saṃvatsare vyatīte ‘pi trirātram aśucir bhavet || iti |
idaṃ cātikrāntāśaucam upanītoparamaviṣayam | tathā ca vyāghrapāt |
tulyaṃ vayasi sarveṣām atikrānte tathaiva ca |
upanīte tu viṣamaṃ tasminn evātikālajam || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – ṣaṇmāsādirūpe vayasi yad āśaucaṃ “ā dantajanmanaḥ sadyaḥ” ityādivacanavihitaṃ tat sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ tulyam aviśiṣṭam | atikrānte daśāhādike trirātrādyāśaucaṃ yat tat sarveṣāṃ samānam | upanīte tu mṛte daśadvādaśapañcadaśatriṃśaddinānīty evaṃ viṣamam āśaucaṃ brāhmaṇādīnām | atikālajam atikrāntāśaucaṃ tasminn eva upanītoparama eva | nānupanītoparame – iti | janane tv atikrāntāśaucaṃ nāsti | tad āha devalaḥ |
nāśuddhiḥ prasavāśauce vyatīteṣu dineṣv api | iti |
manur api |
nirdaśaṃ jñātimaraṇaṃ śrutvā putrasya janma ca |
savāsā jalm āplutya śuddho bhavati mānavaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.77)
atra putragrahaṇāt nirdaśe ‘pi pituḥ snānena śuddhiḥ | sapiṇḍānāṃ tv atikrāntāśaucaṃ nāstīty arthaḥ | antardaśāhe tu śeṣāhobhir viśuddhiḥ | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ |
[236]        deśāntaragataṃ śrutvā kalyāṇaṃ maraṇaṃ tathā |
yac cheṣaṃ daśarātrasya tāvad evāśucir bhavet || iti | (ŚaṅSm 15.11)
dvividho hi deśāntaramṛtaḥ | kṛtasaṃskāro ‘kṛtasaṃskāraś ca | tatra kṛtasaṃskārasya maraṇaśravaṇe saṃvatsarād arvāg ūrdhvaṃ vāśaucaṃ vacanadvaye vyasthāpitam | akṛtasaṃskārasya maraṇaśravaṇe tv āśaucagrahaṇapiṇḍadānādeḥ kālaviśeṣo vivicyate | akṛtasaṃskāro ‘pi dvividhaḥ, maraṇadivasajñānājñānabhedāt | yasya hi maraṇadivaso vijñātaḥ tasya pratyābdikādiśrāddhaṃ tad divasa eva kartavyam | āśaucagrahaṇapiṇḍodakadānaṃ tv aniṣiddhanakṣatrādikaṃ paryālocya tatrānuṣṭheyam, śiṣṭācārasya tathā pravṛttatvāt || 11 ||
yasya tu divaso na vijñātaḥ taṃ praty etad ucyate |
deśāntaragato vipraḥ prayāsāt kālakāritāt |
dehanāśam anuprāptas tithir na jñāyate yadi || 12 ||
kṛṣṇāṣṭamī tv amāvāsyā kṛṣṇā caikādaśī ca yā |
udakaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ ca tatra śrāddhaṃ ca kārayet || 13 ||
tīrthayātrādinā kenacin nimittena deśāntaragatasya viprasya [237] cirakālabahudeśa-paryaṭanādisaṃpāditād āyāsabāhulyād yatra kvāpi dehanāśo bhavati | ata eva tanmaraṇatithir na jñāyate | maraṇavārtā ca yadā kadācit śrutā bhavati | tatra tadīyāśaucasvīkāras tilodakapiṇḍadānopakramādikaṃ cety etad ubhayaṃ kṛṣṇāṣṭamyādiṣu tisṛṣu tithiṣv icchayā kasyāṃcit tithau kartavyam | tasyām eva tithāv ābdikaśrāddhaṃ ca kartavyam |
yady apy asmin vacane āśaucasvīkāraḥ sākṣān nopāttaḥ tathāpi pūrvottaravacanayor āśaucaviṣayatvena tatprakaraṇatvād āśaucasvīkāram antareṇa tilokadapiṇḍadānāsaṃbhavāc cāśaucasvīkāro ‘py atra vivakṣitaḥ – iti gamyate | udakādibahukartavyopanyāsena śrāddhaprakaraṇasya kṛtsnasyāpy atra saṅgraho vivakṣitaḥ | saṃgṛhītaṃ ca tat prakaraṇam upariṣṭād asmābhiḥ prapañcayiṣyate || 12–13 ||
pūrvam akṛtanāmno bālasya maraṇe sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśuddhir abhihitā | idānīṃ kṛtanāmno ‘py ajātadantasya bālasya maraṇe saha saṃskāreṇāśaucaṃ niṣedhati |
ajātadantā ye bālā ye ca garbhād viniḥsṛtāḥ |
na teṣām agnisaṃskāro nāśaucaṃ nodakakriyā || 14 ||
ajātadantā anutpannadantāḥ kṛtanāmāno ye bālā mṛtāḥ ye ca garbhād viniḥsṛtāḥ patitāḥ teṣāṃ tatsapiṇḍair nāgnisaṃskārādikaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca brahmapurāṇam |
[238]        strīṇāṃ tu patito garbhaḥ sadyojāto ‘tha vā mṛtaḥ |
ajātadanto māsair vā mṛtaḥ ṣaḍbhir gatas tathā ||
vastrādyair bhūṣitaṃ kṛtvā tyaktavyas tu sa kāṣṭhavat |
khanitvā tu śanair bhūmiṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate || iti |
strīṇāṃ yo garbhaḥ patitaḥ yaś ca jananakṣaṇa eva mṛtaḥ yaś ca ṣaṇmāsāt prāk mṛtaḥ yaś ca ṣaṇmāsād ūrdhvam apy ajātadantaḥ san mṛtaḥ sa kāṣṭhavad bhūmiṃ khanitvā nikṣeptavyaḥ | mātrādivyatiriktaiḥ sapiṇḍair nāśaucādikaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | viṣṇur api: “ajātadante bāle prete sadya eva | nāgnisaṃskāro nodakakriyā” iti (ViDh 22.27–28) || 14 ||
pūrvatra garbhapāte sapiṇḍānāṃ bandhūnāṃ sadhyaśuddhim abhidhāya, adhunā mātus tannimittam āśaucam astīty āha |
yadi garbho vipadyeta sravate vāpi yoṣitaḥ |
yāvan māsaṃ sthito garbho dinaṃ tāvat tu sūtakam || 15 ||
yadi garbhasya srāvapātau syātāṃ tadā yāvatsu māseṣu garbhaḥ sthitas tanmāsasaṃkhyāsamadinaṃ yoṣito mātuḥ sūtakaṃ sūtyāśaucam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
garbhasrāve māsatulyā niśāḥ śuddhes tu kāraṇam | (YDh 3.20)
[239] iti | “māsatulyā niśāḥ” iti caturthamāsaprabhṛtyā saptamād veditavyam | arvāk tu yathāvarṇaṃ trirātrādayaḥ | tathā ca marīciḥ |
garbhasrutyāṃ yathāmāsam acire tūttame tryaham |
rājanye tu catūrātraṃ vaiśye pañcāham eva tu ||
aṣṭāhena tu śūdrasya śuddhir eṣā prakīrtitā || iti |
acire māsatraye garbhasrāve uttame brāhmaṇe tryaham | gautamo ‘pi: “garbhamāsasamā rātriḥ saṃsrave garbhasya | tryahaṃ vā” iti (GDh 14.10–11) | atra garbhamāsasamarātritryahayor vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | māsatrayaṃ yāvat tryahaṃ tataḥ paraṃ māsasamā trātraya iti | ādipurāṇe ‘pi |
ṣaṇmāsābhyantaraṃ yāvad garbhasrāvo bhaved yadi |
tadā māsasamais tāsāṃ divasaiḥ śuddhir iṣyate || iti |
etac ca srāvanimittāśaucaṃ mātur eva | pātanimittaṃ tu pitrādīnām apy asti | tathā ca marīciḥ
srāve mātus trirātraṃ syāt sapiṇḍāśaucavarjanam |
pāte mātur yathāmāsaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ dinatrayam || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “ūnadvivarṣe prete garbhapāte vā sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātram” (VaDh. 4.34) [240] iti | srvāve pitur viśeṣam āha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ: “garbhasrāve māsatulyā rātrayaḥ strīṇām | snānamātram eva puruṣasya” iti || 15 ||
nanu – srāvapātayor aprāptaprasavakālatvāviśeṣād anayoḥ ko viśeṣa ity ata āha |
ā caturthād bhavet srāvaḥ pātaḥ pañcamaṣaṣṭhayoḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ prasūtiḥ syād daśāhaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet || 16 || iti |
caturthamāsābhyantare garbhanāśaḥ srāvaḥ | pañcamaṣaṣṭhayor garbhanāśaḥ pātaḥ | tatra māsasaṃkhyayā vihitam āśaucaṃ mātur bhavet | ata ūrdhvaṃ saptamamāsaprabhṛti garbhanirgamaḥ prasavaḥ | tatra mātuḥ prasavanimittam āśaucaṃ daśāhaṃ bhaved ity arthaḥ | yat tu caturviṃśatimate,
adhastān navamān māsāc chuddhiḥ syāt prasave katham |
mṛte jīvati vā tasmin ahobhir māsasaṃkhyayaā || iti |
asyāyam arthaḥ – navamān māsād arvāk saptamamāsād ārabhya prasave sati tannimittam āśaucaṃ sūtikāvyatiriktasarvasapiṇḍānāṃ māsasaṃkhyākair ahobhir vidhīyata iti | sūtikāviṣayatve daśāhavidhivirodhaḥ prasajyeta |
nanu – evaṃ tarhi “jātau vipro daśāhena” iti sarvasapiṇḍānāṃ janananimittadaśāhāśauca-vidhāyakavacanaṃ [241] virudhyeta |
tan na, tasya navamadaśamamāsaprasavaviṣayatvenopapatteḥ | atha vā ekaviṣayatve ‘pi vikalpena vyavasthāstu || 15 ||
bālasyāgnisaṃskāre saty āśaucaṃ darśayati |
dantajāte ‘nujāte ca kṛtacūḍe ca saṃsthite |
agnisaṃskaraṇe teṣāṃ trirātram aśucir bhavet || 16 || iti |
jātā dantā yasyāsau dantajātaḥ | tad anu paścāj jāto ‘nujātaḥ anutpannadanta iti yāvat | kṛtaṃ cūḍākhyaṃ karma yasyāsau kṛtacūḍaḥ | tatra jātadantasyākṛtacūḍasyānujātasya ca saty agnisaṃskāre tṛtīyavarṣakṛtacūḍe ca saṃsthite teṣāṃ sapiṇḍas trirātram aśucir bhaved ity arthaḥ | tatrākṛtacūḍasya jātadantasya dāhapakṣe trirātrāśaucam aṅgirasoktam |
yady apy akṛtacūḍo vai jātadantas tu saṃsthitaḥ |
dāhayitvā tathāpy enam āśaucaṃ tryaham ācaret || iti |
purāṇe ‘pi |
anatītadvivarśas tu preto yatrāpi dahyate |
āśaucaṃ bāndhavānāṃ tu trirātraṃ vidyate || iti |
yat tu viṣṇuvacanam “dantajāta tv akṛtacūḍe tva ahorātreṇa” (ViDh 22.29) [242] iti, tat khananapakṣe veditavyam | ajātadantasya kṛtacūḍasya dahane trirātrāśaucaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘bhihitam |
yady apy ajātadantaḥ syāt kṛtacūḍas tu saṃsthitaḥ |
tathāpi dāhayed enaṃ tryahaṃ cāśaucam ācaret || iti |
yat tu yamenoktam,
ajātadante tanaye śiśau garbhacyute tathā |
sapiṇḍānāṃ tu sarveṣām ahorātram aśaucakam ||
iti, tad akṛtacūḍaviṣayam |
nanu – ajātadantasya kṛtacūḍatvaṃ katham, tasya tṛtīye vihitatvād iti cet,
na,
cūḍākarma dvijātīnāṃ sarveṣām eva dharmataḥ |
prathame ‘bde tṛtīye vā kartavyaṃ śruticodanāt || (MDh 2.35)
iti manunā vikalpena vikalpena smṛtatvāt | “agnisaṃskaraṇe” ity etad vikalpenābhidhānaṃ jātadantānujātayor eva | na trivarṣakṛtacūḍe, tatrāgnisaṃskārasya niyatatvāt | itaratrāgnisaṃskāravikalpo manunā darśitaḥ |
nātrivarṣasya kartavyā bāndhavair udakakriyā |
jātadantasya vā kuryān nāmni vāpi kṛte sati || (MDh 5.70)
[243] iti | udakakriyeti agnisaṃskāropalakṣaṇārtham || 16 ||
vayo’vasthāviśeṣeṇāśaucaviśeṣaṃ darśayati |
ā dantajanmanaḥ sadya ā cūḍān naiśikī smṛtā |
trirāram ā vratādeśād daśarātram ataḥ param || 17 || iti |
dantajananāt prāg atītasya bālasya saṃbandhināṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ sadyaḥśaucam | dantajananād ūrdhvaṃ prāk cūḍākaraṇād atītasya saṃbandhināṃ naiśikī niśāyāṃ bhavā ahorātram aśuddhiḥ | vratādeśa upanayanam | ato ‘rvāk cūḍāyāś cordhvam atītasya saṃbandhināṃ trirātram aśuddhiḥ | tataḥ paraṃ daśarātram ity arthaḥ | tathā ca saṃgrahakāraḥ |
nāmno dantodbhavāc cauḍād upanīter adhaḥ kramāt |
sadyaḥ śaucam ahas tryaho niyatāgnyudakaḥ paraḥ || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi
ajātadante tanaye sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
ahorātrāt tathā śuddhir bāle tv akṛtacūḍake ||
tathaivānupanīte tu tryahāc chudhyanti bāndhavāḥ || iti | (ŚaṅSm 15.4–5)
yat tu kāśyapavacanam: “bālānām ajātadantānāṃ trirātreṇa śuddhiḥ” (KāśSm 6.8) [244] iti, tan mātāpitṛviṣayam | ata eva manuḥ |
nirasya tu pumān śukram upaspṛśya viśudhyati |
baijikād abhisaṃbandhād anurundhyād aghaṃ tryaham || iti | (MDh 5.63)
baijikasaṃbandho janyajanakabhāvaḥ | yat tu smṛtyantaram,
prāṅ nāmakaraṇāt sadya ekāho dantajanmanaḥ |
iti, tad dahane veditavyam | khanane tu sadyaḥ śuddhiḥ, “ajātadante bāle prete sadya eva | nāsty agnisaṃskāro nodakakriyā” (ViDh 22.27–28) iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | yat tu vasiṣṭhavacanam: “ūnadvivarṣe prete garbhapatane vā sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātram” (ViDh 4.34) iti taj jātadantasyāgnisaṃskāre draṣṭavyam | tataś caivaṃ vyavasthā – nāmakaraṇāt prāk sadyaḥśaucaṃ niyatam | tadūrdhvaṃ prāk dantajananād agnisaṃskārakriyāyām ekāhaḥ | anyathā sadyaḥ śuddhiḥ | tasyāpy ajātadantasya cūḍākaraṇe trirātram | dantajananād ūrdhvam arvāk cūḍākaraṇād ekāhaṃ khanane | agnisaṃskāre tu tryahaḥ | ūrdhvaṃ cūḍāyāḥ prāg upanayanāt tryahaḥ | upanayanād ūrdhvaṃ brāhmanādīnāṃ [245] daśāhādikam iti | iyaṃ vyavasthā pumapatyamaraṇe draṣṭavyā | stryapatye tu viśeṣo vṛddhamanunā darśitaḥ |
aprauḍhāyāṃ ca kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
ahas tv adattakanyāsu prattāsu ca tryahaṃ tathā || iti |
aprauḍhāyāṃ akṛtacūḍāyām ity arthaḥ,
acūḍāyāṃ tu kanyāyāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
ity āpastambasmaraṇāt | adattakanyāsu vācādattāsu ahorātram | dattāsu vāgdattāsu tryaham | tathā ca marīciḥ: “cūḍākaraṇe sadyaḥśaucam | prāg vāgdānād ekāhaḥ | dattānāṃ prāk pariṇayanāt tryaham” iti | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
ā janmanas tu caulāntaṃ kanyā yadi vipadyate |
sadyaḥśaucaṃ bhavet tatra sarvavarṇeṣu nityaśaḥ ||
tato vāgdānaparyantaṃ yāvd ekāham eva hi |
tataḥ paraṃ pravṛddhāyāṃ trirātram iti niścayaḥ ||
vākpradāne kṛte tatra jñeyaṃ cobhayatas tryaham |
pitur varasya ca tato dattānāṃ bhartur eva hi ||
svajātyuktam aśaucaṃ syān mṛtake jātake tathā || iti |
pulastyo ‘pi |
sadyas tv aprauḍhakanyāyāṃ prauḍhāyāṃ vāsarāc chuciḥ |
pradattāyāṃ trirātreṇa dattāyāṃ pakṣiṇī bhavet ||
[246] iti | pradattāyāṃ prakrāntadānāyāṃ vācādattāyām iti yāvat | vāgdānānantaraṃ mṛtāyāṃ trirātram | manur apy āha |
strīṇām asaṃskṛtānāṃ tu tryahāc chudhyanti bāndhavāḥ |
yathoktenaiva kalpena śudhyanti tu sanābhayaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.72)
bāndhavāḥ patisapiṇḍāḥ | sanābhayaḥ pitṛsapiṇḍāḥ | yathoktena kalpena trirātreṇa | ata eva marīciḥ |
avāripūrvaṃ prattā tu yā naiva pratipāditā |
asaṃskṛtā tu sā jñeyā trirātram ubhayoḥ smṛtam || iti |
ubhayor varapitṛpakṣayoḥ | akṛtacūḍāyāṃ yat sadyaḥśaucavidhānaṃ kṛtacūḍāyāṃ ca yad ekāhavidhānaṃ tanmātāpitṛvyatiriktaviṣayam |
prattāprattāsu yoṣitsu saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtāsu ca |
mātāpitros trirātraṃ syād itareṣāṃ yathāvidhi || iti |
“ajātadantāsu pitror ekarātram” iti śaṅkha-kārṣṇājinibhyāṃ viśeṣasmaraṇāt | adattāsu trirātravidhānaṃ jātadantaviṣayam, ajātadantāsv ekāhavidhānāt | saṃdkṛtāsu pitros trirātraṃ tadgṛhamaraṇe veditavyam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “saṃskṛtāsu strīṣu nāśaucaṃ pitṛpakṣe | [247] tatprasavamaraṇe ca bandhuvargasyaikarātraṃ pitros trirātram iti vyavasthā” iti (ViDh 22.31–34) | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
dattā nārī pitur gehe sūyetātha mriyeta vā |
tadbandhuvargas tv ekena śucis tajjanakas tribhiḥ || iti |
pitror uparame saṃskṛtānāṃ strīṇāṃ trirātram | tathā ca vṛddhamanuḥ |
pitror uparame strīṇām ūḍhānāṃ tu kathaṃ bhavet |
trirātreṇaiva śuddhiḥ syād ity āha bhagavān yamaḥ || iti |
pitror mātāpitror uparame vivāhasaṃskārasaṃskṛtānāṃ duhitṝṇāṃ trirātreṇa śuddhir iti | dauhitrabhaginīsutayor asaṃskṛtayoḥ pakṣiṇyāśaucam saṃskṛtayos trirātram | tathā ca vṛddhamanuḥ |
saṃsthite pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ dauhitre bhaginīsute |
saṃskṛte tu trirātraṃ syād iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ || iti |
dauhitre bhaginīsute vānupanīte mṛte sati pakṣiṇīm āgāmivartamānāhardvayayuktāṃ rātriṃ mātāmahādiḥ kṣapayet | upanīte tu tasmin mṛte sati mātāmahādīnāṃ trirātram āśaucaṃ bhaved ity arthaḥ | mātāmahādīnāṃ maraṇe dauhitrādīnāṃ trirātram āśaucam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
tryahaṃ mātāmahācāryaśrotriyeṣv aśucir bhavet | iti |
ācāryo ‘trāsapiṇḍaḥ sann upanayanādikartā | śrotriyas [248] tv ekaśākhādhyāyī maitrīprātiveśyatvādinopasaṃpannaḥ | eteṣu mātāmahādiṣu mṛteṣu trirātram iti | viṣṇur api: “ācārye mātāmahe ca vyatīte trirāreṇa” iti (ViDh 22.42) | manur api |
śrotriye tūpasaṃpanne trirātram aśucir bhavet | iti | (MDh 5.81)
etat trirātrāśaucaṃ parakartṛkadahanādau veditavyam,
guroḥ pretasya śiṣyas tu pitṛmedhaṃ samācaran |
pretahāraiḥ samaṃ tatra daśarātreṇa śudhyati || (MDh 5.65)
iti svakartṛkadāhādau manunā viśeṣasmaraṇāt | mātṛṣvasrādiṣu trirātram āśaucam | tad āha pracetāḥ |
mātṛṣvasāmātulayoḥ śvaśrūśvaśurayor guroḥ |
mṛte cartviji yājye ca trirātreṇa viśudhyati || iti |
gurur ācāryaḥ | ṛtvik kulaparamparāyātaḥ | yājyo ‘pi tathāvidhaḥ | yat tu yājñavalkyavacanam,
gurvantevāsyanūcānamātulaśrotriyeṣu ca | (YDh 3.24)
iti, yac ca viṣṇuvacanam: [249] “ācāryapatnīputropādhyāyamātulaśvaśuraśvaśrūśvaśurya-sahādhyāyiśiṣyeṣv atīteṣv ekarātreṇa” (ViDh 22.44) iti, tatra gurur upādhyāyaḥ | antevāsī anyopanītaḥ śiṣyaḥ, svopanīte tu: “śiṣyasatīrthyasabrahmacāriṣu trirātram ahorātram ekāhaḥ” (BDh 1.5.11.30) iti baudhāyanena trirātravidhānāt | mātulaḥ anupakārī videśastho vā | śrotriyo ‘nupasaṃpannaḥ | śvaśrūśvaśurāv apy anupakāriṇau videśasthau vā | ekasmin gurukule ‘lpakālaṃ sahādhyāyī | eteṣv ekarātram iti vyavasthā | yat tu manunoktam,
mātule pakṣiṇī rātriḥ śiṣyartvigbāndhaveṣu ca | (MDh 5.81)
iti, tasyāyam arthaḥ – svalpopakārake mātule śiṣyo ‘nyopanītasāṅgavedādhyāyī ṛtvik ādhānaprabhṛti yāvajjīvam ārtvijyakārī bāndhavāḥ mātṛpitṛbāndhavāḥ eteṣu pakṣiṇyāśaucam iti | anaurasaputrādiṣu trirātram āśaucam | tad āha viṣṇuḥ |
anauraseṣu putreṣu jāteṣu ca mṛteṣu ca |
parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu prasūtāsu mṛtāsu ca || iti | (ViDh 22.43)
trirātram ity anuvartate | hārīto ‘pi |
[250]        parapūrvāsu bhāryāsu putreṣu kṛtakeṣu ca |
mātāmahe trirātraṃ syād ekāhaṃ tu sapiṇḍataḥ || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi |
anauraseṣu putreṣu bhāryāsv anyagatāsu ca |
parapūrvāsu ca strīṣu trirātrāc chuddhir iṣyate || iti | (ŚaṅSm 15.13)
anaurasāḥ kṣetrajādayaḥ | parapūrvā punarbhuvaḥ | anyagatā svairiṇyaḥ | eteṣv anaurasādiṣu yat pratiyogikaṃ bhāryātvaṃ putratvaṃ ca tasyaivedaṃ trirātram āśaucam ity arthaḥ | yat tv ekāhavidhānam,
anauraseṣu putreṣu bhāryāsv anyagatāsu ca | (YDh 3.25)
iti, tad asaṃnidhiviṣayam | saṃnidhāv api pitṛsapiṇḍānām ekāha eva | tathā ca marīciḥ |
ekāhas tu sapiṇḍānāṃ trirātraṃ yatra vai pituḥ | iti |
yat tu prajāpatinoktam,
anāśriteṣu dāreṣu parapatnīsuteṣu ca |
gotriṇaḥ snānaśuddhāḥ syus trirātreṇaiva tatpitā ||
iti snānād eva śuddhir iti, tat samānodakaviṣayaṃ asaṃnidhiviṣayaṃ vā | ekasyāṃ mātari pitṛdvayotpāditayor anyatarasmin mṛte ‘nyatarasya trirātram āśaucaṃ bhavati | tathā ca marīciḥ |
[251]        mātraikayā dvipitṛkau bhrātarāv anyagotrajau |
ekāhaṃ sūtakaṃ tatra trirātraṃ mṛtake tayoḥ || iti |
asapiṇḍayonisaṃbandhimaraṇe pakṣiṇy āśaucam | tad āha gautamaḥ: “pakṣiṇy asapiṇḍe yonisaṃbandhe sahādhyāyini ca” iti (GDh 14.12) | ayam arthaḥ – asapiṇḍaḥ svaveśmani mṛtaḥ | yonisaṃbandhā mātṛṣvasrīyapitṛṣvasrīyādayaḥ | sahādhyāyī gurukule saha kṛtsnavedādhyāyī | cakārād gurvaṅganādayo ‘pi saṃgṛhyante | teṣu pakṣiṇīṃ tatsaṃbandhapratiyogī kṣapayed iti | tathā ca vṛddhamanuḥ:
mātule śvaśure mitre gurau gurvaṅganāsu ca |
āśaucaṃ pakṣiṇīṃ rātriṃ mṛtā mātāmahī yadi ||
śvaśurayoś ca bhaginyāṃ ca mātylānyāṃ ca mātule |
pitroḥ svasari tadvac ca pakṣiṇīṃ kṣapayen niśām || iti |
yat tu viṣṇunoktam: “asapiṇḍe svaveśmani mṛte ekarātram” (ViDh 22.46) iti, tad apradhānagṛhamaraṇe veditavyam | yad apy aṅgirasoktam,
[252]        gṛhe yasya mṛtaḥ kaścid asapiṇḍaḥ kathaṃcana |
tasyāpy aśaucaṃ vijñeyaṃ trirātraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
tad asapiṇḍaśrotriyaviṣayam | yat tu bṛhanmanunoktam,
bhaginyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu bhrātary api ca saṃsthite |
mitre jāmātari prete dauhitre bhaginīsute ||
śyālake tatsute caiva sadyaḥ snānena śudhyati |
iti, tatra bhaginyādau sadyaḥśuddhyabhidhānaṃ deśāntaramaraṇaviṣayam | jāmātṛśyālasutayoḥ saṃnidhāv eva sadhyaśuddhir iti | nivāsarājany ahani mṛte ‘har āśaucaṃ rātrau ced rātrimātram iti | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
nivāsarājani prete tad ahaḥ śuddhikāraṇam | iti | (YDh 3.25)
yasmin deśe nivasati sa nivāsaḥ svadeśa ucyate | tasya rājani mṛte taddeśanivāsināṃ yasminn ahany asau mṛtas tadaharmātraṃ śuddhikāraṇaṃ rātrau ced rātrimātram iti | ata eva manuḥ |
prete rājani sajyotir yasya syād viṣayate sthitaḥ | iti | (MDh 5.82)
jyotiṣā saureṇa nākṣatreṇa vā saha vartate yad āśaucaṃ tat sajyotiḥ | ahani ced yāvat sūryadarśaṇaṃ rātrau ced yāvan nakṣatradarśanam ity arthaḥ | grāmamadhye śave sthite grāmasya tāvad āśaucam | tad āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
[253]        grāmamadhyagato yāvac chavas tiṣṭhati kasyacit |
grāmasya tāvad āśaucaṃ nirgate śucitām iyāt || iti |
grāmeśvarādāv api sajyotir āśaucam | tad āha sa eva |
grāmeśvare kulapatau śrotriye ca tapasvini |
śiṣye pañcatvam āpanno śuddhir nakṣatradarśanāt || iti |
kulapatiḥ samūhapatiḥ | śrotriyo deśāntarasthaḥ || 17 ||
uktasyāśaucasyāgnihotribrahmacāriṇor apavādam āha |
brahmacārī gṛhe yeṣāṃ hūyate ca hutāśanaḥ |
saṃparkaṃ na ca kurvanti na teṣāṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet || 18 || iti |
brahmacārī upakurvāṇako naiṣṭhikaś ca | yeṣāṃ gṛhe hutāśano [254] hūyate agnihotram anuṣṭhīyate teṣām agnihotrānuṣṭhānakāle nāsty āśaucaṃ yadi te sūtakibhiḥ saha saṃsargaṃ na kuryuḥ | tad uktaṃ kaurme |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
nāśaucaṃ kīrtitaṃ sadbhiḥ patite ca tathā mṛte || iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.71)
devalo ‘pi |
naiṣṭhikānāṃ vanasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ brahmacāriṇām |
nāśaucaṃ sūtake proktaṃ śāve vāpi tathaiva ca || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
svādhyāyaḥ kriyate yatra homaś cobhayakālikaḥ |
sāyaṃprātar vaiśvadevaṃ na teṣāṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet || iti || 18 ||
saṃsargasyāspṛśyatvakarmānadhikāralakṣaṇāśaucāpādakatvam anvayavyatirekābhyām upapādayati |
saṃparkād duṣyate vipro janane maraṇe tathā |
saṃparkāc ca nivṛttasya na pretaṃ naiva sūtakam || 19 || iti |
spaṣṭārtham etat || 19 ||
kiṃ ca –
śilpinaḥ kārukā vaidyā dāsīdāsāś ca nāpitāḥ |
rājānaḥ śrotriyāś caiva sadyaḥśaucāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 20 ||
savrataḥ satrapūtaś ca āhitāgniś ca yo dvijaḥ |
[255]        rājñaś ca sūtakaṃ nāsti yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ || 21 ||
udyato nidhane dāne ārto vipro nimantritaḥ |
tadaiva ṛṣibhir dṛṣṭaṃ yathākālena śudhyati || 22 || iti |
śilpinaś citrakārādyāḥ | kārukāḥ sūpakāraprabhṛtayaḥ | vaidyāś cikitsakāḥ | śrotriyāḥ sadyaḥprakṣālikāḥ | vratena cāndrāyaṇādiniyamena saha vartate iti savrataḥ | sattraputo gavāmayanādyadhikṛtaḥ | ete svasvakarmaṇi sadyaḥśaucāḥ | rājñaḥ, rājasaṃbandhino mānyasya, yasya ca purohitasyānanyasādhyamantrābhicārādikarmasiddhyartham āśaucābhāvam icchati, tayor api tattatkarmaṇi sūtakaṃ nāsti | nidhanaśabdena tatsādhanabhūtaḥ saṃgrāmo lakṣyate | tatra annādidāne codyataḥ kṛtopakramaḥ ārtaḥ āpadaṃ prāptaḥ śrāddhādau nimantrito vipraś ca tadaiva sadya eva śudhyatīti ṛṣibhir dṛṣṭam | yathākālena dvādaśarātrādinā tathety arthaḥ | tathā ca ādipurāṇe |
śilpinaś citrakārādyāḥ karma yat sādhayanty alam |
tat karma nānyo jānāti tasmāc chuddhāḥ svakarmaṇi ||
sūpakāreṇa yat karma karaṇīyaṃ nareṣv iha |
tad anyo naiva jānāti tasmāc chuddhaḥ sa sūpakṛt ||
cikitsako yat kurute tad anyena na śakyate |
tasmāc cikitsakaḥ sparśe śuddho bhavati nityaśaḥ ||
dāsyo dāsāś ca yat kiṃcit kurvanty api ca līlayā |
[256]        tad anyo na kṣamaḥ kartuṃ tasmāt te śucayaḥ sadā ||
rājā karoti yat karma svapne ‘py anyasya tat katham |
evaṃ sati nṛpaḥ śuddhaḥ saṃsparśe mṛtasūtake ||
yat karma rājabhṛtyānāṃ hastyaśvagamanādikam |
tan nāsti yasmād anyasya tasmāt te śucayaḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
viṣṇur api: “āśaucam | na rājñāṃ rājakarmaṇi | na vratināṃ vrate | na sattriṇāṃ sattre | na kārūṇāṃ kārukarmaṇi | na rājājñākāriṇāṃ tadicchāyām” iti (ViDh 22.47–52) | pracetā api |
kāravaḥ śilpino vaidyā dāsīdāsās tathaiva ca |
rājāno rājabhṛtyāś ca sadyaḥśaucāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
vṛddhaparāśaro ‘pi |
rājñāṃ tu sūtakaṃ nāsti vratināṃ na ca sattriṇām |
dīkṣitānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ yasya cecchati pārthivaḥ ||
tapodānapravṛtteṣu nāśaucaṃ mṛtasūtake || iti |
smṛtyantaram api |
nityam annapradasyāpi kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādiṣu |
pravṛtte kṛcchrahomādau brāhmaṇādiṣu bhojane ||
[257]        gṛhītaniyamasyāpi na syād anyasya kasyacit |
nimantriteṣu vipreṣu prārabdhe śrāddhakarmaṇi || iti |
brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
nimantritasya viprasya svādhyāyaniratasya ca |
dehe pitṛṣu tiṣṭhatsu nāśaucaṃ vidyate kvacit ||
prāyaścittapravṛttānāṃ dātṛbrahmavidāṃ tathā || iti |
manur api |
na rājñām aghadoṣo ‘sti vratināṃ na ca sattriṇām |
aindraṃ sthānam upāsīnā brahmabhūtā hi te sadā ||
rājño māhātmike sthāne sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate |
prajānāṃ parirakṣārtham āsanaṃ tatra kāraṇam || iti | (MDh 5.93–94)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
ṛtvijāṃ dīkṣitānāṃ ca yajñiyaṃ karma kurvatām |
sattrivratibrahmacāridātṛbrahmavidāṃ tathā ||
dāne vivāhe yajñe ca saṃgrāme deśaviplave |
āpady api ca kaṣṭāyāṃ sadaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate || (YDh 3.28–29)
[258] iti | hārīto ‘pi |
saṃgrāmasthaś ca rājanyo vaiśyo madhye gavāṃ sthitaḥ |
sattrī ca brāhmaṇo nityaṃ brahmacārī ca vai śuciḥ || iti |
paiṭhīnasir api |
vivāhayajñadurgeṣu yātrāyāṃ tīrthakarmaṇi |
na tatra sūtakaṃ tadvat karma yajñādi kārayet || iti |
brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
atha devapratiṣṭhāyāṃ gaṇayāgādikarmaṇi |
śrāddhādau pitṛyajñe ca kanyādāne ca no bhavet || iti |
aṅgirā api |
janane maraṇe caiva triṣv āśaucaṃ na vidyate |
yajñe vivāhakāle ca devayāge tathaiva ca || iti |
atra vivāhādau sadyaḥśaucam upakrāntavivāhādiviṣayam | atra nṛpādīnām asādhāraṇakṛtyavyatiriktaviṣayeṣv āśaucam asty eva | tathā ca brāhme purāṇe |
rājyanāśas tu yena syād vinā rājñā svamaṇḍale |
prayāsyataś ca saṃgrāme home prāsthānike sati ||
mantrāditarpaṇair vāpi prajānāṃ śāntikarmaṇi |
gomaṅgalādau vaiśyānāṃ kṛṣikālātyayeṣv api ||
āśaucaṃ na bhavel loke sarvatrānyatra vidyate || iti || 20–22 ||
[259]
kiṃ ca –
prasave gṛhamedhī tu na kuryāt saṃkaraṃ yadi |
daśāhāc chudhyate mātā tv avagāhya pitā śuciḥ || 23 || iti |
prasave janane | gṛhamedhī gṛhasthaḥ | pitā sūtikayā saha yadi saṃsargaṃ na kuryāt tadā snānena śuddho bhavati | mātā tu daśāhena śuddhā bhavatīty arthaḥ || 23 ||
nanv evaṃ tarhi pituḥ karmānadhikāralakṣaṇam apy āśaucaṃ na syād ity ata āha |
sarceṣāṃ śāvam āśaucaṃ mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syāt upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || 24 || iti |
yathā sapiṇḍānāṃ karmānadhikāralakṣaṇam āśaucaṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ tadvat pitur api | mātāpitros tu sūtakam aspṛśyatvalakṣaṇam āśaucam | tatrāpi daśāham aspṛśyatvaṃ mātur eva | pitus tu snānaparyantam evety arthaḥ | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
janau sapiṇḍāḥ śucayo mātāpitros tu sūtakam |
sūtakaṃ mātur eva syād upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – janane mātāpitṛvyatiriktāḥ sarve sapiṇḍāḥ spṛśyāḥ | mātāpitros tu nāsti spṛśyatvam | tatrāpi pitā snānena spṛśyo bhavati | daśāhaṃ tv aspṛśyatvaṃ mātur eva | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ |
[260]        nāśaucaṃ vidyate puṃsaḥ saṃsargaṃ cen na gacchati |
rajaś cātrāśuci jñeyaṃ tac ca puṃsi na vidyate || iti | (VaDh 4.23)
saṃvarto ‘pi |
jāte putre pituḥ snānaṃ sacailaṃ tu vidhīyate |
mātā śuddhyed daśāhena snānāt tu sparśanaṃ pituḥ || iti | (SaṃSm 143)
bṛhaspatir api |
jātāśaucaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ sūtakaṃ mātur eva ca |
snānaṃ prakuryāt tu pitā jñātayo na sacailinaḥ || iti |
gautamo ‘pi: “mātāpitros tu sūtakam upaspṛśya pitā śuciḥ” iti (GDh 14.23) | ādipurāṇe ‘pi |
sūtakī tu mukhaṃ dṛṣṭvā jātasya janakas tataḥ |
kṛtvā sacailaṃ snānaṃ tu śuddho bhavati tatkṣaṇāt || iti | || 24 ||
sūtikayā saha saṃsargakaraṇe tannimittam aspṛśyatvaṃ daśāham astīty āha |
yadi patnyāṃ prasūtāyāṃ saṃparkaṃ kurute dvijaḥ |
sūtakaṃ tu bhavet tasya yadi vipraḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit || 25 || iti |
sūtikāyā patnyā saha patiḥ saṃsargaṃ yadi kuryāt tadā [261] vidyākarmayuktasya viprasyāpy aspṛśyatvalakṣaṇaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavet kim utānyasyety arthaḥ | tathā ca sumantuḥ: “mātur eva sūtakam | tāṃ spṛśataś ca | netareṣām” iti | sūtikāṃ spṛśato janakasyāspṛśyatvalakṣaṇaṃ sūtakaṃ bhavati nānyeṣām ity arthaḥ || 25 ||
nanu – janananimittam evāspṛśyatvaṃ bhartuḥ snānānantaram api kiṃ na syāt | ata āha |
saṃparkāj jāyate doṣo nānyo doṣo ‘sti vai dvije |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena saṃparkaṃ varjayed budhaḥ || 26 || iti |
snānānantaraṃ bhartuḥ saṃsarganimittaka eva doṣo ‘spṛśyatvāpādako jāyate na janananimittako doṣo ‘sti | tasmād vidvān saṃparkaṃ sahaśayanāsanabhojanādikaṃ varjayed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
yas taiḥ saha sapiṇḍo ‘pi prakuryāc chayanāśanam |
bāndhavo vā paro vāpi sa daśāhena śudhyati || iti |
viṣṇur ap: “brāhmaṇādīnām āśauce yaḥ sakṛd evānnam aśnīyāt tasya tāvad āśaucaṃ yāvat teṣām” iti (ViDh 22.8) | atrir api |
saṃparkāj jāyate doṣaḥ pārakye mṛtajanmani |
tadvarjanād pitur api sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
[262] iti || 26 ||
prārabdhe yajñādau kartuḥ śuddhir uktā | idānīṃ kalpitadravyasyāpi śuddhir astīty āha |
vivāhotsavayajñeṣu tv antarā mṛtasūtake |
pūrvasaṃkalpitaṃ dravyaṃ dīyamānaṃ na duṣyati || 27 || iti |
atra vivāhagrahaṇaṃ pūrvapravṛttacaulopanayanādisaṃskārakarmopalakṣaṇārtham | utsavo devatotsavaḥ | tena ca devapratiṣṭhādikam upalakṣyate | yajño jyotiṣṭomādiḥ | teṣu prārabdheṣu antarā madhye yadi mṛtasūtake maraṇajanane syātāṃ tadā pūrvasaṃkalpitaṃ dravyaṃ devatāyai brāhmaṇebhyo dīyamānaṃ na duṣyatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca kratuḥ |
pūrvasaṃkalpitaṃ dravyaṃ dīyamānaṃ na duṣyati | iti |
pakve tu viśeṣaḥ smṛtyantare darśitaḥ |
vivāhotsavayajñādiṣv antarā mṛtasūtake |
śṛtam annaṃ parair deyaṃ dātṝn bhoktṝṃś ca na spṛśet || iti |
kṛtānnam asūtakibhir deyam | sūtakī tu dātṝn bhoktṝṃś ca na spṛśed ity arthaḥ | yat tu smṛtyantaram,
dravyāṇi svāmisaṃbandhād aghāni tv aśucīni ca |
svāmiśuddhyaiva śudhyanti vāriṇā prokṣitāny api ||
iti, tad asaṃkalpitadravyaviṣayam | kācinid asaṃkalpitāny api dravyāṇi sarvadā śuddhāni | tathā ca marīciḥ |
[263]        lavaṇe madhumāṃse ca puṣpamūlaphaleṣu ca |
śākakāṣṭhatṛṇeṣv apsu dadhisarpiḥpayaḥsu ca ||
tailauṣadhyajine caiva pakvāpakve svayaṃgrahaḥ |
paṇyeṣu caiva sarveṣu nāśaucaṃ mṛtasūtake || iti || 27 ||
anekanimittāśaucasaṃnipāte pratinimittaṃ naimittikāvṛttau tāṃ nivārayati |
antarā tu daśāhasya punarmaraṇajanmanī |
tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anirdaśam || 28 || iti |
yadā daśāhāśaucakālamadhye tattulyasya tato ‘lpasya vāśaucasya nimitte jananamaraṇe syātāṃ tadā pūrvapravṛttaṃ tadāśaucaṃ yāvad anirdaśam anirgatadaśāhaṃ syāt vipras tāvad evāśucir bhavati na punar madhyotpannamaraṇādinimittakadaśāhādyāśaucavān iti arthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
antardaśāhe syātāṃ cet punar maraṇajanmanī |
tāvat syād aśucir vipro yāvat tat syād anidaśam || iti | (MDh 5.79)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
antarā janmamareṇe śeṣāhobhir viśudhyati | iti | (YDh 3.20)
viṣṇur api: [264] “jananāśaucamadhye yady aparaṃ jananāśaucaṃ syāt tadā pūrvāśaucavyapagame śuddhiḥ | maraṇāśaucamadhye jñātimaraṇe py evam” iti (ViDh 22.35–38) | api śabdāj janane ‘pi maraṇāśaucakālenaiva śuddhir ity arthaḥ | yadā janananimittadaśāhāśaucamadhye maraṇam āpatati tadā maraṇād ārabhya daśāhaṃ kāryam | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
sūtake mṛtakaṃ cet syān mṛtake tv atha sūtakam |
tatrādhikṛtya mṛtakaṃ śaucaṃ kuryān na sūtakam || iti |
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ‘pi |
śāvāśauce samutpanne sūtakaṃ tu yadā bhavet |
śāvena śudhyate sūtir na śāvaśodhinī || iti |
caturviṃśatimate ‘pi |
mṛtajātakayor yoge yā śuddhiḥ sā tu kathyate |
mṛtena śudhyate jātaṃ na mṛtaṃ jātakena tu || iti |
alpāśaucamadhye dīrghakālāśaucaprātau na pūrveṇa śuddhiḥ | tad uktam uśanasā |
svalpāśaucasya madhye tu dīrghāśaucaṃ bhaved yadi |
na pūrveṇa viśuddhiḥ syāt svakālenaiva śudhyati || iti |
yamenāpi |
aghavṛddhimad āśaucaṃ paścimena samāpayet |
[265]        yathā trirātre prakrānte daśāhaṃ praviśed yadi |
āśaucaṃ punar āgacchet tat samāpya viśudhyati || iti |
prathamapravṛttāśaucakālāpekṣayā dīrghakālānuvartena vṛddhimad āśaucaṃ yadi madhye samutpayeta tadā paścimena svakālenaiva samāpayed ity arthaḥ | śaṅkho ‘pi |
samānāśaucasaṃpāte prathamena samāpayet |
asamānaṃ dvitīyena dharmarājavaco yathā || iti | (ŚaṅSm 15.10)
asamānaṃ dīrghakālāśaucam ity arthaḥ | hārīto ‘pi |
śāvāntaḥ śāva āyāte pūrvāśaucena śudhyati |
guruṇā laghu śudhyet tu laghunā naiva tadguru || iti |
kaurme ‘pi |
aghānāṃ yaugapadye tu jñeyā śuddhir garīyasā | iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.24)
gurulaghutve tu samānajātīyayoḥ kālāpekṣayā | vijātīyayoḥ svarūpeṇaiva | tad uktam tenaiva |
maraṇotpattiyoge tu garīyo maraṇaṃ bhavet | iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.6)
kvacit kālāpekṣayā laghvāśaucamadhyavartino gurvāśaucasya pūrvāśaucakālenāpagamo ‘sti | tad āha devalaḥ |
parataḥ parato ‘śuddhir aghavṛddhau vidhīyate |
syāc cet tat pañcamād ahnaḥ pūrveṇaivātra śiṣyate ||
[266] iti | vartamānāśaucamadhyavartini jananādau yadāghavṛddhir dīrghakālam āśaucaṃ tadā parataḥ prāptaṃ jananādikam ārabhyāśuddhir vidhīyate | tad yadi pūrvapravṛttam āśaucaṃ pañcamadināt parato ‘py anuvarteta tadā pūrveṇaiva pūrvāśaucakālenaiva daśāhāśaucasyāpi śuddhir viśiṣyate vidhīyate | etad uktaṃ bhavati – antarā patitasyāśaucasya dīrghakālatve ‘pi yadi pūrvapravṛttam āśaucam uttarāśaucakālād ardhādhikakālaṃ syāt tadā pūrvapravṛttāśaucakālenaivottarasyāpi śuddhir bhavati | tad yathā – garbhapātanimittaṣaḍahāśaucamadhye yadi daśāhāśaucam āpatet tadā ṣaḍahāśaucaśeṣeṇaiva daśāhāśaucasyāpi nivṛttir iti | evam anyatrāpi ardhādhikakālāśaucaśeṣeṇaivādhikakālāśaucasyāpi nivṛttir avagantavyā |
antarā patitasyāśaucasya śeṣeṇa śuddhir ity atra viśeṣo gautamenoktaḥ: “rātriśeṣe dvābhyām | prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ” iti (GDh 14.7–8) | rātriśabdenāhorātraṃ lakṣyate | rātriḥ śeṣo yasyāśaucasya tasmin vidyamāne yadāśaucāntaram āpatet tadā pūrvāśaucakālānantaraṃ dvābhyāṃ rātribhyāṃ śuddhiḥ | prabhāte tasyā rātreś carame yāme purā sūryodayād āśaucasaṃnipāte tisṛbhī rātribhiḥ śuddhiḥ | na tu pūrvāśaucakālaśeṣeṇeti | tathā śaṅkhalikhitābhyām api: “atha ced antarā pramīyeta jāyeta vā śiṣṭair eva divasaiḥ śudhyet | ahaḥśeṣe dvābhyām | prabhāte tisṛbhiḥ” [267] iti | śātātapenāpi |
rātriśeṣe dvyahāc chuddhir yāmaśeṣe tryahāc chuciḥ | iti |
baudhāyanenāpi: “atha yadi daśarātrasaṃnipāte yad ādyaṃ daśarātrasamāśaucam ā navamād divasāt” (BDh 1.5.11.18) | asyārthaḥ – yāvan navamadivasaparisamāptis tāvat na pūrvāśaucakālaśeṣeṇottarāśaucasya nivṛttir iti | navamaśabdenopāntyadivasa upalakṣyate | tataś ca kṣatriyādīnām apy antyadivasāśaucasaṃnipāte dvirātraṃ prabhāte trirātram ity avagantavyam | devalenāpi |
punaḥ pāte daśāhāt prāk pūrveṇa saha gacchati |
daśame ‘hni pated yasya dvyahāt tu sa viśudhyati ||
prabhāte tu trirātreṇa daśarātreṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
daśāhāt prāg ity atra daśāhaśabdo ‘ntyadivasopalakṣakaḥ | daśarātreṣv ity etad api dvādaśarātrādyupalakṣaṇam | samānāśaucayoḥ saṃnipāte pūrvaśeṣeṇa śuddhir ity asya kvacid apavādaḥ śaṅkhena darśitaḥ |
mātary agre pramītāyām aśuddhau mriyate pitā |
pituḥ śeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ syān mātuḥ kuryāt tu pakṣiṇīm || iti | (ŚaṅSm 15.14)
mātari pūrvaṃ mṛtāyāṃ yadi tannimittāśaucamadhye pitā mriyeta tadā na pūrvāśaucaśeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ | kiṃ tu pitrāśaucakālenaiva śuddhiḥ | tathā pūrvaṃ pitari mṛte tannimittāśaucamadhye mātari [268] pramītāyām api na pitrāśaucakālaśeṣeṇa śuddhiḥ | kiṃ tu pitrāśaucaṃ samāpya pakṣiṇīṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ || 28 ||
uktasya daśāhādyāśaucasya viṣayāntare ‘py apavādam āha |
brāhmaṇārthaṃ vipannānāṃ bandigograhaṇe tathā |
āhaveṣu vipannānām ekarātram aśaucakam || 29 || iti |
brāhmaṇaprāṇarakṣaṇārthaṃ bandigrahaṇe gograhe ca sati tadvimocanārthaṃ hatānām āhaveṣv ābhimukhyena hatānāṃ ye sapiṇḍās teṣām ekarātram evāśaucaṃ na daśarātrādikam ity arthaḥ | yat tu ‘sadyaḥśaucam’ ity anuvṛttau manunoktam,
ḍimbhāhavahatānāṃ ca vidyutā pārthivena ca |
gobrāhmaṇasya caivārthe yasya cecchati bhūmipaḥ || (MDh 5.95)
iti, tad asaṃnidhiviṣayam || 29 ||
raṇahatasapiṇḍānām ekāhāśaucavidhiśeṣatayā navabhiḥ ślokair āhave hataṃ praśaṃsati | tatra prathamaṃ parivrājakadṛṣṭāntenādityamaṇḍalabheditvaṃ darśayann arthād brahmalokaprāptiṃ darśayati |
dvāv imau puruṣau loke sūryamaṇḍalabhedinau |
parivrāḍ yogayuktaś ca raṇe cābhimukho hataḥ || 30 || iti |
yogābhyāseneśvaram upāsīnaḥ parivrājako ‘rcirādimārgeṇa brahmalokaṃ gacchan mārgamadhye vāyvādityacandrāṇāṃ maṇḍalāni [269] krameṇa bhittvā tatra tebhya uttarottarādhikebhyaḥ chidrebhyo nirgatya krameṇa vidyudādilokān saṃcaran brahmalokaṃ prāpnoti | chidranirgamanaṃ ca vājasaneyibrāhmaṇe śrutam |
sa vāym āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā rathacakrasya khaṃ tena sa ūrdhvam ākramate sa ādityam āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā lambarasya khaṃ tena sa ūrdhvam ākramate sa candramasam āgacchati tasmai sa tatra vijihīte yathā dundubheḥ khaṃ tena na ūrdhva ākramate | iti (BāUp 5.10.1) || 30 ||
tatra – cirakālaṃ mahatā prayāsena yogam abhyasyatā parivrājakena saha samānagatitvaṃ raṇahatasyāyuktam | tasmād alpakālaprayāsatvād ity āśaṅkya kālālpatve ‘pi dhairyātiśayena prayāsasāmyaṃ sūcayitum abhimukha ity uktam | tam eva sūcitam arthaṃ viśadīkaroti |
yatra yatra hataḥ śūraḥ śatrubhiḥ pariveṣṭitaḥ |
akṣayān labhate lokān yadi klībaṃ na bhāṣate || 31 || iti |
loke śastradhāriṇam ekam api dṛṣṭvā mahatī prāṇabhītir jāyate | yuddhakāle tu pratisainyagatāḥ sarve ‘pi śatravaḥ śastradhāriṇo [270] māraṇodyatā enaṃ pariveṣṭayanti | tadānīm utpadyamānāyā bhīter iyattaiva nāsti | tādṛśīṃ bhītiṃ soḍhvā pratibhaṭābhimukhyaṃ gacchataḥ śūrasya dhairyaṃ yogidhairyād apy adhikam | na hi yogino yamaniyamādiṣu kvacit prāṇabhītiḥ saṃbhāvitā | tato yathā jāgaraṇe bahuṣu vatsareṣu anubhavanīyasya bhogasya muhūrtamātravartini svapne sākalyaṃ dṛśyante tathā cirabhāviyogasāmyaṃ raṇe dhairyavataḥ kiṃ na syāt | dhairyātiśayena sāmyam atra vivakṣitam iti darśayituṃ “yadi klībaṃ na bhāṣate” ity uktam | klībaṃ napuṃsakatvaṃ vikalatā tatsūcakaṃ bhītyāviṣkāri vākyaṃ yadi na bhāṣate tadānīṃ yogisāmyād akṣayān brahmalokāvāntaraviśeṣān sālokyādīn labhate || 31 ||
parivrājakadṛṣṭānte sūryamaṇḍalabheditvaṃ saṃbhāvayati |
saṃnyastaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā sthānāc calati bhāskaraḥ |
eṣa me maṇḍalaṃ bhittvā paraṃ sthānaṃ prayāsyati || 32 || iti |
yady api maṇḍalasyācetanaraśmisamūharūpatvāt tadbhede ‘pi nāsti kācid ādityasya vedanā tathāpi pūrvam atyantanīcapade vartamānasyedānīm uccapadaprāptiś cittakleśahetur bhavati | ata eva bhittvā paraṃ sthānaṃ prayāsyatīty uktam | etad evābhipretya vyāsa āha |
kriyāvadbhir hi kaunteya devalokaḥ samāvṛtaḥ |
na caitad iṣṭaṃ devānāṃ martyair upari vartanam ||
[271] iti | mumukṣuparivrājakadarśanamātreṇa niṣpannasya bhāskaracalanasyopanyāsād yogino yathoktaphalaṃ dṛḍhīkṛtaṃ bhavati || 32 ||
raṇe cābhimukho hata iti dārṣṭāntike ‘bhihitaṃ tac ca hatatvaṃ dhairyātiśayasyopalakṣaṇam | asaty api svavadhe paratrāṇapravṛttasya dhīrasya yathoktaphalasadbhāvād ity āha |
yas tu bhagneṣu sainyeṣu vidravatsu samantataḥ |
paritrātā yadā gacchet sa ca kratuphalaṃ labhet || 33 || iti |}
kratur atrāśvamedhaḥ, brahmalokaprāptiphalatvāt | aśvamedhasya ca tatphalatvaṃ vājasaneyiśākhāyāṃ bhujyubrāhmaṇe: “kva nv aśvamedhayājino gacchanti” (BāUp 3.3.2) ityādipraśnaprativacanayor vispaṣṭam avagamyate || 33 ||
yaḥ paritrāṇārthaṃpravṛttaḥ tasya pravṛttimātreṇa kratuphalam uktam | pravṛttasya gātracchede sati hatatvābhāve ‘pi phalātiśayo ‘stīty āha |
yasya chedakṣataṃ gātraṃ śaramudgarayaṣṭibhiḥ |
devakanyāsu taṃ vīraṃ haranti ramayanti ca || 34 || iti |
gātraṃ śarīram | chedakṣataṃ hastapādādyavayavachedenopadrutam || 34 ||
[272]
paritrāṇāya pravṛttasya gātrachede yat phalaṃ tato ‘py atiśayaṃ maraṇe darśayati |
devāṅganāsahasrāṇi śūram āyodhane hatam |
tvaramāṇāḥ pradhāvanti mama bhartā mameti ca || 35 ||
iti || 35 ||
yady api yajñasaṅghādikaṃ yuddhamaraṇaṃ cobhayam apy ekavidhasya phalasya samānaṃ sādhanaṃ tathāpi yuddhamaraṇasyātyalpakālasādhyatvena vaikalyāsaṃbhavād uttamasādhanatvam ity āha |
yaṃ yajñasaṅghais tapasā ca viprāḥ
svargaiṣiṇo vātra yathaiva yānti |
kṣaṇena yānty eva hi tatra vīrāḥ
prāṇām suyuddhena parityajantaḥ || 36 || iti |
vipraśabdena niṣkāmā vivakṣitāḥ | tathā ca svargaiṣiṇo veti vikalpa upapadyate | atra puṇyalokeṣu yaṃ lokaviśeṣaṃ yathaiva yena prakāraviśeṣeṇa devakanyāvaraṇādinā yuktāḥ santo yānti tatra teṣu puṇyalokeṣu tam eva lokaviśeṣaṃ tenaiva prakāreṇa yuddhahatā vīrāś ca yānti | kṣaṇenety uktaṃ tena kālālpatvam eteṣv atiṣayaḥ || 36 ||
[273]
nanu – kālasyālpatve ‘pi prāṇabhīter duṣpariharatvāt pūrvoktaṃ yuddhadhairyaṃ durlabham ity āśaṅkya vicāravataḥ puruṣasya tat sulabham ity abhipretya taṃ vicāraṃ darśayati |
jitena labhyate lakṣmīr mṛtenāpi surāṅganāṅ |
kṣaṇadhvaṃsini kāye ‘smin kā cintā maraṇe raṇe || 37 || iti |
jiteneti kartari niṣṭhā | yato jayena lakṣmīlābhaḥ | maraṇena surāṅganālābhaḥ tato yadi kāyajīvanalobhāl lakṣmīdevāṅganālābhau na paryālocyete tadā lābhaparityāgamātraṃ tasya kevalam avaśiṣyate | kāyas tu sarvathā na ciraṃ jīvati | tasya karmaprāpitāyuṣyavaśavartitvena kṣaṇapradhvaṃsisvabhāvatvāt || 37 ||
atyantaniṣiddham api rudhirapānaṃ yatra niratiśayasukṛtatvena pariṇamate tatra puṇalokaprāptau ko vismayaḥ, ity āha |
lalāṭadeśe rudhiraṃ sravac ca
yasyāhave tu praviśec ca vaktram |
tat somapānena kilāsya tulyaṃ
saṃgrāmayajñe vidhivac ca dṛṣṭam || 38 || iti |
saṃgrāmayajñapratipādake nītiśāstrādau purobhāge prahāro [274] vīralakṣaṇatvenopavarṇita iti vivakṣitatvāt vidhivad dṛṣṭam ity uktam |
tad evaṃ navabhiḥ ślokair āśaucavidhis tāvakatvena yuddhamaraṇasya praśaṃsā kṛtā | yasmād raṇahato ‘tyantapuṇyātmā tasmāt tanmṛtau parivrājakamaraṇa ivādhikāśaucābhāva upapadyate | atha vā ta ete nava ślokāḥ prakaraṇād utkṛṣya rājadharmeṣu sthāpanīyāḥ, yuddhasya kṣatriyadharmatvāt | yathā darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇe śrūyamāṇo rajasvalāvratakalāpaḥ prakaraṇād utkṛṣya kratvarthaparihāreṇa puruṣārthatayopavarṇitas tadvat || 38 ||
dharmārtham anāthabrāhmaṇaśavavahanādau praśaṃsāpūrvakaṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidadhāti |
anāthaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ pretaṃ ye vahanti dvijātayaḥ |
pade pade yajñaphalam ānupūrvyāl labhante te || 39 ||
na teṣām aśubhaṃ kiṃcit pāpaṃ vā śubhakarmaṇām |
jalāvagāhanāt teṣāṃ sadyaḥśaucaṃ vidhīyate || 40 || iti |
anāthaṃ bandhurahitam asapiṇḍaṃ brāhmaṇam adṛṣṭārthaṃ ye dvijātayo vahanti spṛśanti dahanti ca te pade pade yajñaphalāni krameṇa prāpnuvanti |
tathā teṣām aśubhādikam api nāsti | teṣāṃ snānād eva sadyaḥśuddhir vidhīyata ity arthaḥ | tathā ca vṛddhaparāśaraḥ |
[275]        pretasparśanasaṃskāre brāhmaṇo naiva duṣyati |
voḍhā caivāgnidātā ca sadyaḥ snātvā viśudhyati || iti |
yat tu hārītenoktam: “pretaspṛśo grāmaṃ na viśeyur ā nakṣatradarśanāt | rātrau ced ādityasya” iti, yac ca devalenoktam,
ahni ced vahanaṃ kuryāt ūrdhvam astamayād raveḥ |
snātvā gṛhaṃ viśed vipro rātrau ced udayād raveḥ ||
iti, tat snehādinākaraṇīyanirhārakaraṇe veditavyam || 39–40 ||
na kevalaṃ nānaṃ kiṃ tu prāṇāyāmo ‘pi kartavya ity āha |
asagotram abandhuṃ ca pretībhūtaṃ dvijottamam |
vahitvā ca dahitvā ca prāṇāyāmena śudhyati || 41 || iti |
asagotram asapiṇḍam abandhuṃ bandhurahitaṃ pretaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ ye vahanti dahanti ca teṣāṃ prāṇāyāmena śuddhir ity arthaḥ | na kevalaṃ snānaprāṇāyāmau, agnisparśo ‘pi kartavyaḥ | tad uktam aṅgirasā |
yaḥ kaścin nirharet pretam asapiṇḍaḥ kathaṃcana |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ tasminn evāhni vai śuciḥ || iti |
snehādinā pretanirharaṇaṃ kurvato ‘sapiṇḍasyāśaucam asti | tathā ca manuḥ |
[276]        asapiṇḍaṃ dvijaṃ pretaṃ vipro nirhṛtya bandhuvat |
viśudhyati trirātreṇa mātur āptāṃś ca bāndhavān ||
yady annam atti teṣāṃ tu daśāhenaiva śudhyati |
anadann annam ahnaiva na cet tasmin gṛhe vaset || iti | (MDh 5.101–02)
yas tu pretanirharaṇaṃ kṛtvā tadgṛhe vasati na ca tadannam aśnāti tasya trirātram āśaucam | yas tu tadgṛhe vasan tadannam aśnāti tasya daśarātram | yaḥ punaḥ pretaṃ nirhṛtya tadgṛhavāsaṃ tadannaṃ ca parityajati tasyaikāham ity arthaḥ | etat savarṇaviṣayam | asavarṇaśavanirhāre tu tajjātīyam āśaucaṃ kāryam | tad āha gautamaḥ: “aparaś ced varṇaḥ pūrvavarṇam upaspṛśet pūrvo vāparaṃ tacchavoktam āśaucam” iti (GDh 14.16) | upasparśanaṃ nirharaṇam | brāhmaṇasya śūdraśavanirhāre māsam āśaucam | śūdrasya brāhmaṇaśavanirhāre daśāham āśaucaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | yas ev arthalobhād asavarṇaśavanirharaṇaṃ karoti tasya dviguṇam āśaucaṃ bhavatīti | tathā ca vyāghraḥ |
avaraś ced varaṃ varo vāpy avaraṃ yadi |
vahec chavaṃ tadāśaucaṃ vṛttyarthe dviguṇaṃ bhavet ||
[277] iti | asavarṇapretanirhāre taduktam āśaucam | tatra vetanāśrayaṇe dviguṇaṃ āśaucaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | yat tu viṣṇupurāṇe,
yo ‘savarṇaṃ tu mūlyena nītvā caiva dahen naraḥ |
āśaucaṃ tu bhavet tasya pretajātisamaṃ sadā ||
iti, tad āpadi draṣṭavyam | arthalobhāt savarṇaśavavahanādau svajātyuktāśaucaṃ kāryam | tathā ca kaurme |
yadi nirharati pretaṃ pralobhākrāntamānasaḥ |
daśāhena dvijaḥ śudhyed dvādaśāhena bhūmipaḥ |
ardhamāsena vaiśyas tu śūdro māsena śudhyati || iti | (KūPu 1.2.23.49–50)
yas tu sapiṇḍa eva pretaṃ nirharati na tasyāśaucādhikyam, pretanirharaṇasya vihitatvāt | tad āha devalaḥ |
vihitaṃ tu sapiṇḍānāṃ pretanirharaṇādikam |
teṣāṃ karoti yaḥ kaścit tasyādhikyaṃ na vidyate || iti |
ādhikyam āśaucādhkyam ity arthaḥ | samānodakapretanirharaṇe daśāham | tad āha sa eva |
yaḥ samānodakaṃ pretaṃ vahed vātha daheta vā |
tasyāśaucaṃ daśāhaṃ tu dharmajñā munayo viduḥ || iti |
brahmacāriṇaḥ pretavahanakaraṇe vratalopaḥ | tad āha devalaḥ |
