[278]        brahmcārī na kurvīta śavavāhādikāḥ kriyāḥ |
yadi kuryāc caret kṛcchraṃ punaḥ saṃskāram eva ca || iti |
pitrādiśavavahane tu na doṣaḥ | tathā ca manu-devalau |
ācāryaṃ svam upādhyāyaṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
nirhṛtya tu vratī pretān na vratena viyujyate || iti | (MDh 5.91)
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “brahmacāriṇaḥ śavakarmaṇā vratanivṛttiḥ | anyatra mātāpitror guror vā” iti (VaDh 23.7–8) | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
ācāryapitrupādhyāyān nirhṛtyāpi vratī vratī |
sakaṭānnaṃ ca nāśnīyān na ca tiḥ saha saṃvaset || iti | (YDh 3.15)
vratī brahmacārī viprādīnāṃ nirharaṇādikaṃ kṛtvā yady āśaucibhiḥ saha vāsaṃ tadannaṃ ca parityajati tadā vratī vratacaryāyā na viyujyate ity arthaḥ | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
[279]        ācāryaṃ vāpy upādhyāyaṃ guruṃ vā pitaraṃ tathā |
mātaraṃ vā svayaṃ dagdhvā vratasthas tatra bhojanam ||
kṛtvā patati vai tasmāt pretānnaṃ na tu bhakṣayet |
anyatra bhojanaṃ kuryān na ca taiḥ saha saṃvaset ||
ekāham aśucir bhūtvā dvitīye ‘hani śudhyati | iti |
brāhmaṇaśavavahanādau śūdraṃ na niyojayet | tad āha manuḥ |
na vipraṃ sveṣu tiṣṭhatsu mṛtaṃ śūdreṇa hārayet |
asvargyā hy āhutiḥ sā syāc chūdrasaṃsparśdūṣitā || iti | (MDh 5.104)
atra sveṣu tiṣṭhatsv ity avivakṣitam, asvargyatvadoṣaśravaṇāt | viṣṇur api: “mṛtaṃ dvijaṃ na śūdreṇa nirhārayet | na śūdraṃ dvijena” (ViDh 19.1–2) [280] iti | yamo ‘pi |
na śūdro yajamānaṃ vai pretībhūtaṃ samudvahet |
yasyānayati śūdro ‘gniṃ tṛṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ havīṃṣi ca |
pretatvaṃ hi sadā tasya sa cādharmeṇa lipyate || iti |
brāhmaṇādiśavanirhāre diṅniyamo darśito manunā |
dakṣiṇena mṛtaṃ śūdraṃ puradvāreṇa nirharet |
paścimottarapūrvais tu yathāyogaṃ dvijanmanaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.92)
hārīto ‘pi: “na grāmābhimukhaṃ pretaṃ hareyuḥ’ iti (HārSm 19.13) || 41 ||
anugamanāśaucam āha |
anugamyecchayā pretaṃ jñātim ajñātim eva vā |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || 42 || iti |
jñātiṃ sapiṇḍavyatiriktaṃ bandhum, sapiṇḍānugamanasya vihitatvāt | ajñātim abandhuṃ vā samānotkṛṣṭajātipretaṃ kāmanayānugamya sacailaṃ snātvāgniṃ spṛṣṭvā ghṛtabhuk śudhyati ity arthaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
anugamyāmbhasi snātvā spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtabhuk śuciḥ | (YDh 3.26)
[281] iti | kaurme ‘pi |
pretībhūtaṃ dvijaṃ yo ‘nugaccheta kāmataḥ |
snātvā sacailaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati | iti | KūPu 1.2.23.53)
atra ca viśeṣaḥ kāvaṣeyeṇoktaḥ |
anugamya śavaṃ buddhyā snātvā spṛṣṭvā hutāśanam |
sarpiḥ prāśya punaḥ snātvā prāṇāyāmair viśudhyati || iti |
na ca – ghṛtaprāśanasya bhojanakārye vidhānād bhojananivṛttir iti vācyam, tasya prāyaścittatvena vidhānāt | prāṇāyāmair iti bahuvacanasya kapiñjalanyāyena tritve paryavasānāt tribhiḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ śudhyati – ity arthaḥ || 42 ||
nikṛṣṭajātyanugamanāśaucam āha |
kṣatriyaṃ mṛtam ajñānād brāhmaṇo yo ‘nugacchati |
ekāham aśucir bhūtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati || 43 ||
[282]        śavaṃ ca vaiśyam ajñānād brāhmaṇo yo ‘nugacchati |
kṛtvāśaucaṃ dvirātraṃ ca prāṇāyāmān ṣaḍ ācaret || 44 ||
pretībhūtaṃ tu yaḥ śūdraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
anugacchen nīyamānaṃ trirātram aśucir bhavet || 45 ||
trirātre tu tataḥ pūrṇe nadīṃ gatvā samudragām |
prāṇāyāmaśataṃ kṛtvā ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati || 46 || iti |
yo brāhmaṇaḥ ajñānān maurkhyāt kṣatriyaṃ pretam anugacchati sa ekāham āśaucaṃ kṛtvā pañcagavyena śudhyati | brāhmaṇo vaiśyaśavānugamanaṃ kṛtvā dvirātrāśaucānantaraṃ ṣaḍbhiḥ prāṇāyāmaiḥ śudhyati | śūdraśavānugamanaṃ kṛtvā trirātram āśaucaṃ samāpya mahānadyāṃ snātvā śataṃ prāṇāyāmān kṛtvā ghṛtaprāśanena śudhyati | uprakramopasaṃhāraparyālokanayā kṣatriyādiśavānugamane ‘pi sacailasnānāgnisparśaghṛtaprāśanāny anusaṃdheyāni | evaṃ ca sati kṣatriyasya vaiśyaśavānugamane ekāham, śūdraśavānugamane dvyaham, vaiśyasya śūdraśavānugamane ekāham āśaucam ity ūhanīyam | tathā ca kaurme |
ekāhāt kṣatriye śuddhir vaiśye syāc ca dvyahena tu |
śūdre dinatrayaṃ proktaṃ prāṇāyāmaśataṃ punaḥ || (KūPu 1.2.13.54)
[283] iti || 43–46 ||
dvijānāṃ śūdraśavānugamananiṣedhe kadā taiḥ śūdrā anusartavyā ity ata āha |
vinirvartya yadā śūdrā udakāntam upasthitāḥ |
dvijais tadānugantavyā eṣa dharmaḥ sanātanaḥ || 47 || iti |
udakaśabdenodakakriyocyate | tasyā antaḥ samāptiḥ tāṃ nirvartya āśaucaṃ parisamāpya yadā sthitāḥ tadā dvijair anugantavyāḥ anusartavyāḥ iti | evaṃ brāhmaṇasyāturakṣatriyavaiśyānusaraṇaṃ kṣatriyasyāpy āturavaiśyānusaraṇam āśaucānantaram evety ūhanīyam | āśaucamadhye āturavyañjane tv āśaucam asti | tatra brāhmaṇamaraṇaviṣayāturavyañjane pāraskaraḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād arvāg ruditvā snānam ācaret |
antardaśāhe viprasya ūrdhvam ācamanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
viprasya mṛtasya daśāhābhyantare ‘sthisaṃcayanād arvāg brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyādir vāturavyañjanaṃ kṛtvā snānam ācaret | tata ūrdhvam ācanam ācared iti | kṣatriyamaraṇaviṣayāturavyañjane kṣatriyādīnāṃ vaiśyamaraṇaviṣayāturavyañjane vaiśyaśūdrayoś ca prāg asthisaṃcayanāt sacailaṃ snānam | tata ūrdhvaṃ snānamātram eva | etat sarvaṃ brahmapurāṇe ‘bhihitam |
[284]        mṛtasya yāvad asthīni brāhmaṇasyāhṛtāni tu |
tāvad yo ‘bāndhavas tatra rauti tad bāndhavaiḥ saha ||
tasya snānād bhavec chuddhis tatas tv ācamanaṃ smṛtam |
savailaṃ snānam anyeṣām akṛte tv asthisaṃcaye ||
kṛte tu kevalaṃ snānaṃ kṣatraviṭśūdrajanmanām | iti |
brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyavaiśyamaraṇaviṣayāturavyañjane asthisaṃcayanād arvāg ekāham āśaucaṃ sacailaṃ snānaṃ ca | tata ūrdhvaṃ sacailaṃ snānamātram | tathā ca brahmapurāṇam |
asthisaṃcayane vipro rauti cet kṣatravaiśyayoḥ |
tadā snātaḥ sacailas tu dvitīye ‘hani sudhyati ||
kṛte tu saṃcaye vipraḥ snānenaiva śucir bhavet | iti |
kṣatriyasya vaiśyamarṇaviṣayāturavyañjane viśeṣāśravaṇe ‘pi brāhmaṇasya samanantarakṣatriyamaraṇaviṣayāturavyañjane yad āśaucaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tad evātreti nyāyato ‘vagamyate | śūdramaraṇaviṣayāturavyañjane ‘sthisaṃcayanāt prāk brāhmaṇasya trirātram āśaucam | kṣatriyavaiśyayor dvirātram | tata ūrdhvaṃ dvijātīnām ekarātram eva | śūdrasya sparśaṃ vināturavyañjane ‘sthisaṃcayanād arvāg ekarātram āśaucam | tata ūrdhvaṃ sajyotir āśaucam iti | tathā ca pāraskaraḥ |
asthisaṃcayanād arvāg yadi vipro ‘śru pātayet |
mṛte śūdre gṛhaṃ gatvā trirātreṇa viśudhyati ||
[285]         asthisaṃcayanād ūrdhvaṃ māso yāvad dvijātayaḥ |
ahorātreṇa śudhyanti vāsasaḥ kṣālanena ca ||
sajāter divasenaiva dvyahāt kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ |
sparśaṃ vinānugamane śūdro naktena śudhyati || iti |
atrāśrupāta āturavyañjanamātropalakṣaṇārthaḥ | sajāteḥ śūdrasyāsthisaṃcayanād arvāk sparśaṃ vinānugamane āturavyañjane divasenāhorātreṇa śuddhiḥ | tata ūrdhvaṃ naktena | rātrau ced rātryā ahani ced ahnā śuddhir iti | etac ca śavanirharṇānugamanasahāturavyañjanādinimittam āśaucam asapiṇḍānām | sapiṇḍānāṃ tu vihitatvāt nāsti | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
vihitaṃ hi sapiṇḍasya pretanirharaṇādikam |
doṣaḥ syād asapiṇḍasya tatrānāthakriyāṃ vinā || iti |
pretanirharaṇādikam ity atrādiśabdena dāhodakadānādikam ucyate | anugamādividhis tu yājñavalkyena darśitaḥ |
ā śmaśānād anuvrajya itaro jñātibhir vṛtaḥ ||
yamasūktaṃ tathā gāthā japadbhir laukikāgninā |
sa dagdhavya upetaś ced āhitāgnyāvṛtārthavat || (YDh 3.1–2)
[286] iti | ūnadvivarṣād itaraḥ saṃpūrṇadvivarṣo mṛto jñātibhiḥ sapiṇḍaiḥ śmaśānabhūmiṃ yāvad anugantavyaḥ | tathā yamasūktaṃ pareyuvāṃsam iti ṣoḍaśarcaṃ tathā yamadaivatyā gāthāś ca japadbhir laukikāgninā sa dagdhavyaḥ | upeta upanītaś cen mṛtaḥ tadā āhitāgnisaṃskāraprakāreṇārthavat prayojanavad yathā bhavati tathā dagdhavyaḥ | ayam abhiprāyaḥ – yeṣāṃ bhūśodhanaprokṣaṇādīnām āhitāgnivihitasaṃskārāṇāṃ karaṇam arthavat dvārakāryarūpaṃ prayojanam asti tāny ananuṣṭheyāni | yāni tu luptārthāni pātraprayojanādīni tāny anuṣṭheyāni | yathā kṛṣṇaleṣv atideśaprāpteṣv avaghātaprokṣaṇādiṣu dvāralopād avaghātādīnām ananuṣṭhānaṃ prokṣaṇādīnāṃ tv anuṣṭhānam iti | atra laukikāgnigrahaṇaṃ jātāraṇer abhāve | tatsadbhāve tu tanmathito ‘gnir grāhyaḥ | na tu laukikāgniḥ | tasyāgnisaṃpādyakāryamātrārthatvenopapatteḥ | laukikāgniś caṇḍālāgnyādivyatirikto grāhyaḥ | teṣāṃ niṣiddhatvāt | tathā ca devalaḥ |
caṇḍālāgnir amedhyāgniḥ sūtikāgniś ca karhicit |
patitāgniś citāgniś ca na śiṣṭagrahaṇocitāḥ || iti |
āhitāgnis tu śrautāgninā dagdhavyaḥ | anāhitāgnir gṛhyāgninā | itaro laukikena | tad āha vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
āhitāgnir yathānyāyaṃ dagdhavyas tribhir agnibhiḥ |
anāhitāgnir ekena laukikenetaro janaḥ ||
[287] iti | ekena gṛhyāgninā | dāhaś ca snapanādyanantaraṃ kartavyaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
durbalaṃ snāpayitvā ca śuddhacailābhisaṃvṛtam |
dakṣiṇāśirasaṃ bhūmau barhiṣmatyāṃ niveśayet ||
ghṛtenābhyaktam āplāvya savastraṃ copavītinam |
candanokṣitasarvāṅgaṃ sumanobhir vibhūṣayet ||
hiraṇyaśakalāny asya kṣiptvā chidreṣu saptasu |
mukhe vastraṃ nidhāyainaṃ nirhareyuḥ sutādayaḥ ||
āmapātre ‘nnam ādāya pretam agnipuraḥsaram |
eko ‘nugacchan tasyārdham ardhapathy utsṛjed bhuvi ||
ardham ādahanaṃ prāptam āsīno dakṣināmukhaḥ |
savyaṃ jānv ācya śanakaiḥ satilaṃ piṇḍadānavat || iti | (KSm 3.21.3–7)
piṇḍadānavidhinā ādahanaṃ śmaśānaparyantam ānītam annaṃ prakṣiped ity arthaḥ | dāhānantaraṃ citim anavekṣamāṇā jñātayo jalasamīpaṃ gatvā snātvodakaṃ sakṛt trir vā dadyuḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
athānavekṣam ety āpaḥ sarva eva śavaspṛśaḥ |
snātvā sacailam ācamya dadyur asyodakaṃ sthale ||
gotranāmapadānte ca tarpayāmīty anantaram |
dakṣiṇāgrān kuśān kṛtvā satilaṃ tu pṛthak sakṛt || (KSm 3.22.1–2)
[288] iti | paiṭhīnasir api: “pretaṃ manasā dhyāyan dakṣiṇābhimukhas trīn udakāñjalīn ninayet” iti | etac cāyugmatithiṣu kāryam, “prathamatṛtīyapañcamasaptamanavameṣūdakakriyā” (GDh 14.19) iti gautamasmaraṇāt | pretopakāraviśeṣāpekṣayā tu yāvanty āśaucadināni tāvad udakadānāvṛttiḥ kāryā | tathā ca pracetāḥ |
dine dine ‘ñjalīn pūrṇān pradadyāt pretakāraṇāt |
tāvad vṛddhiś ca kartavyā yāvat piṇḍaḥ samāpyet || iti |
yāvad daśamaḥ piṇḍaḥ samāpyate tāvad añjalivṛddhiḥ kāryety arthaḥ | atrāparo viśeṣas tenaivoktaḥ |
nadīkūlaṃ tato gatvā śaucaṃ kṛtvā yathārthavat |
vastraṃ saṃśodhayed ādau tataḥ snānaṃ samācaret ||
sacailas tu tataḥ snātvā śuciḥ prayatamānasaḥ |
pāṣāṇaṃ tat ādāya vipre dadyāt daśāñjalīn ||
dvādaśa kṣatriye dadyād vaiśye pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ |
triṃśac chūdrāya dātavyās tataḥ saṃpraviśed gṛham ||
tataḥ snānaṃ punaḥ kāryaṃ gṛhaśaucaṃ ca kārayet || iti |
ajñātibhir api kvacit udakadānaṃ kartavyam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[289]        evaṃ mātāmahācāryapretānām udakakriyā |
kāmodakaṃ sakhiprattāsvasrīyaśvaśurartvijām || iti | (YDh 3.4)
prattā pariṇītaduhitṛbhaginyādiḥ | svasrīyo bhāgineyaḥ | atra pretānāṃ mātāmahādīnāṃ sapiṇḍavad udakadānaṃ nityaṃ kāryam | sakhyādīnāṃ tu kāmataḥ, na nityatayā, akaraṇe pratyavāyābhāvād iti | udakadānānantaraṃ piṇḍadānam api kartavyam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “pretasyodakanirvapaṇaṃ kṛtvā ekaṃ ca piṇḍaṃ kuśeṣu dadyuḥ” iti (ViDh 19.7) | piṇḍodakadānaṃ ca yāvad āśaucaṃ kāryam | tad āha sa eva: “yāvad āśaucaṃ tāvat pretasyodakaṃ piṇḍam ekaṃ ca dadyuḥ” iti (ViDh 19.13) | varṇānukrameṇa piṇḍasaṃkhyāniyamaḥ pāraskareṇoktaḥ |
brāhmaṇe daś piṇḍās tu kṣatriye dvādaś smṛtāḥ |
vaiśye pañcadaś proktāḥ śūdre triṃśat prakīrtitāḥ || iti |
āśaucahrāse “yāvad āśaucam” iti viṣṇuvacanāt piṇḍasaṅkocaprāptau śātātapaḥ |
āśaucasya ca hrāse ‘pi piṇḍān dadyāt daśaiva tu | iti |
trirātrāśaucapakṣe daśapiṇḍadānaprakāraḥ pāraskareṇa darśitaḥ |
[290]        prathame divase deyās trayaḥ piṇḍāḥ samāhitaiḥ |
dvitīye caturo dadyād asthisaṃcayanaṃ tathā |
trīṃs tu dadyāt tṛtīye ‘hni vastrādīn kṣālayet tathā || iti |
udakadānavat piṇḍadānaṃ na sarvaiḥ kartavyam | api tu putreṇaiva | tadabhāve saṃnihitena sapiṇḍena | tadabhāve mātṛsapiṇḍādinā | tad āha gautamaḥ: “putrābhāve sapiṇḍā mātṛsapiṇḍāḥ śiṣyāś ca dadyuḥ | tadabhāve ṛtvigācāryau” iti (GDh 15.25–26) | putreṣv api jyeṣṭha eva piṇḍaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca marīciḥ |
sarvair anumatiṃ kṛtvā jyeṣṭhenaiva tu yat kṛtam |
dravyeṇa vāvibhaktena sarvair eva kṛtaṃ bhavet || iti |
yadā tu putrāsaṃnidhānādinānyaḥ piṇḍadānaṃ karoti tadā daśāhamadhye putrasāṃnidhye ‘pi sa eva daśāhaṃ piṇḍaṃ dadyāt | tad uktaṃ gṛhyapariśiṣṭe |
asagotraḥ sagotro vā yadi strī yadi vā pumān |
prathame ‘hani yaḥ kuryāt sa daśāhaṃ samāpayet || iti |
yathā daśāhaṃ piṇḍadāne kartṛniyamaḥ tathā dravyaniyamo ‘pi | tad hāha śunaḥpucchaḥ |
śālinā saktubhir vāpi śākair vāpy atha nirvapet |
prathame ‘hani yad dravyaṃ tad eva syād daśāhikam ||
[291] iti | yadā tu daśāhamadhye darśapātas tadā darśa evottaraṃ tantraṃ piṇḍodakadānarūpaṃ samāpayet | tad āha ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
āśaucam antarā darśo yadi syāt sarvavarṇinaḥ |
samāptiṃ pretatantrasya kuryur ity āha gautamaḥ || iti |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe ‘pi |
pravṛttāśaucatantras tu yadi darśaṃ prapadyate |
samāpya codakaṃ piṇḍān snānamātraṃ samācaret || iti |
paiṭhīnasir api |
ādyendāv eva kartavyā pretapiṇḍodakakriyā |
dviraindave tu kurvāṇaḥ punaḥ śāvaṃ samaśnute || iti |
mātāpitṛviṣaye tu viśeṣo gālavenoktaḥ |
pitror āśaucamadhye tu yadi darśaḥ samāpatet |
tāvad evottaraṃ tantraṃ paryavasyet tryahāt param || iti |
pitror āśaucamadhye tu trirātrāt paraṃ yadi darśaḥ samāpayet tadaivottaraṃ tantraṃ darśe samāpayet nāvarg darśāpāte | yat tu ślokagautamenoktam,
antardaśāhe darśe tu tatra sarvaṃ samāpayet |
pitros tu yāvad āśaucaṃ dadyāt piṇḍān jalāñjalīn ||
iti, tat trirātrād arvāg darśāpāte veditavyam, tryahāt param iti gālavena viśeṣitatvāt | piṇḍodakadānānantaraṃ bāndhavair āturāśvāsanaṃ kāryam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
[292]        kṛtodakān samuttīrṇān mṛduśādvalasaṃsthitān |
snātān apavadeyus tān itihāsaiḥ purātanaiḥ || (YDh 3.7)
itihāsas tu tenaiva darśitaḥ |
mānuṣye kadalīstambhaniḥsāre sāramārgaṇam |
karoti yaḥ sa saṃmūḍho jalabudbudasaṃnibhe ||
pañcadhā saṃbhṛtaḥ kāyo yadi pañcatvam āgataḥ |
karmabhiḥ svaśarīrotthais tatra kā paridevanā ||
gantrī vasumatī nāśam udadhir daivatāni ca |
phenaprakhyaḥ kathaṃ nāma martyaloko na yāsyati || iti | (YDh 3.8–10)
kātyāyano ‘pi |
evaṃ kṛtodakān samyak sarvān śādvalasaṃsthitān |
āplutān punar ācāntān vadeyus te ‘nuyāyinaḥ ||
mā śokaṃ kurutānitye sarvasmin prāṇadharmiṇi |
dharmaṃ kuruta yatnena yo vaḥ saha gamiṣyati || iti | (KSm 3.22.3–4)
śoke doṣo ‘pi yājñavalkyena darśitaḥ |
śleṣmāśru bāndhavair muktaṃ preto bhuṅkte yato ‘vaśaḥ |
ato na roditavyaṃ hi kriyāḥ kāryāḥ svaśaktitaḥ || (YDh 3.11)
[293] iti | āturāśvāsanānantarakṛtyaṃ yājñavalkyenoktam |
iti saṃcintya gaccheyur gṛhaṃ bālapuraḥsarāḥ |
vidaśya nimbapatrāṇi niyatā dvāri veśmanaḥ ||
ācamyāgnyādi salilaṃ gomayaṃ gaurasarṣapān |
praviśeyuḥ samālabhya kṛtvāśmani padaṃ śanaiḥ || iti | (YDh 3.12–13)
atrāparo viśeṣaḥ śaṅkhena darśitaḥ: “dūrvāpravālam agniṃ vṛṣabhaṃ cālabhya gṛhadvāre pretāya piṇḍaṃ datvā paścāt praviśeyuḥ” iti | āśaucaniyamā manunā darśitāḥ |
akṣāralavaṇānnāḥ syur nimajjeyuś ca te tryaham |
māṃsāśanaṃ ca nāśnīyuḥ śayīraṃś ca pṛthak kṣitau || iti | (MDh 5.73)
mārkaṇḍeyenāpi |
krītalabdhāśanāś caiva bhaveyuḥ susamāhitāḥ |
na caiva māṃsam aśnīyur vrajeyur na ca yoṣitam || iti |
gautamenāpi “adhaḥśayyāsanā brahmacāriṇaḥ sarve samāsīran | māṃsaṃ na bhakṣayeyur ā pradānāt | [294] prathamatṛtīyapañcamasaptamanavameṣūdakakarma | navame vāsasāṃ tyāgaḥ | antye tv antyānām” iti (GDh 14.27–31) | pradānaṃ mahaikoddiṣṭaśrāddham | vāsasāṃ tyāgas tu prakṣālanārthaṃ rajakārpaṇam | antyaṃ daśamam ahaḥ | tatra antyānām atyantaparityājyānāṃ vāsasāṃ tyāga ity arthaḥ | prathame ‘hani pretam uddiśya jalaṃ kṣīraṃ cākāśe śikyādau pātradvaye sthāpanīyam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
jalam ekāham ākāśe sthāpyaṃ kṣīraṃ ca mṛṇmaye | iti | (YDh 3.17)
prathamatṛtīyasaptamanavamadivasānām anyatamasminn asthisaṃcayanaṃ kāryam | tad āha saṃvartaḥ |
prathame ‘hni tṛtīye vā saptame navame tathā |
asthisaṃcayanaṃ kāryaṃ dine tadgotrajaiḥ saha || (SaṃSm 139–40)
[295] iti | caturthe divase ‘sthisaṃcayanam āha viṣṇuḥ: “caturthe divase ‘sthisaṃcayanaṃ kuryuḥ | teṣāṃ gaṅgāmbhasi prakṣepaḥ” iti (ViDh 19.10–11) | asthisaṃcayane tithivāranakṣatraniṣedho yamenoktaḥ |
bhaumārkamandavāreṣu tithiyugmeṣu varjayet |
varjayed ekapādarkṣe dvipādarkṣe ‘sthisaṃcayam ||
pradātṛjanmanakṣatre tripādarkṣe viśeṣataḥ || iti |
vṛddhamanuḥ |
vasvanārdhāditaḥ (?) pañcanakṣatreṣu trijanmasu |
dvitripādṛkṣayoś caiva nandāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||
ajacaraṇādidvitīye hy āṣāḍhādvayam eva ca |
puṣye ca hastanakṣatre phalgunīdvayam eva ca ||
bhānubhaumārkibhṛguṣu ayugmatithisaṃdhyayoḥ |
caturdaśyāṃ trayodaśyāṃ naidhane ca vivarjayet ||
asthisaṃcayanaṃ kāryaṃ kulakṣayakaraṃ bhavet |
[296] iti | vāpanaṃ daśame ‘hani kāryam | tad āha devalaḥ |
daśame ‘hani saṃprāpte snānaṃ grāmād bahir bhavet |
tatra tyājyāni vāsāṃsi keśaśmaśrunakhāni ca || iti |
smṛtyantare tu ekādaśāhād arvāg aniyamena vāpanaṃ kāryam ity uktam |
dvitīya ‘hani kartavyaṃ kṣurakarma prayatnataḥ |
tṛtīye pañcame vāpi saptame vāpradānataḥ || iti
pradānam ekādaśāhikaṃ śrāddham | atra ‘āpradānataḥ’ iti vacanāt aniyamo ‘vagamyate | baudhāyanenāpi |
aluptakeśo yaḥ pūrvaṃ so ‘tra keśān pravāpayet |
dvitīye ‘hni tṛtīye ‘hni pañcame saptame ‘pi vā ||
yāvac chrāddhaṃ pradīyeta tāvad ity aparaṃ matam | iti |
vāpanaṃ ca putrāṇāṃ kaniṣṭhabhrātṝṇāṃ ca | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “anubhāvināṃ ca parivāpanam” iti (ĀpDh 1.3.10.6) | anu paścād bhavanti jāyante iti putrāḥ kaniṣṭhabhrātaraś ca | atha vā, anubhāvina iti putrā eva nirdiśyante,
gaṅgāyāṃ bhāsakarkṣetre mātāpitror guror mṛtau |
ādhānakāle some ca vapanaṃ saptasu smṛtam ||
[297] ity atra “mātāpitror mṛtau” it viśeṣeṇopādānāt | evaṃ hi niyataḥ san sarvo ‘pi svāśaucānte piṇḍodakadānaṃ samāpayet | tad ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu śrāddham ekādaśe ‘hni kuryāt | tathā ca marīciḥ |
āśaucānte tataḥ samyak piṇḍadānaṃ samāpyate |
tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pradātavyaṃ sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
āśaucānantaram ekādaśe ‘hni brāhmaṇaḥ ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhaṃ kuryāt | ekoddiṣṭam ekādaśe ‘hani kuryād ity ayaṃ vidhiḥ sarvavarṇeṣu kṣatriyādiṣu samāna ity arthaḥ |
nanu – āśaucasamāptyanantaram evaikoddiṣṭavidhiḥ sarveṣv api varṇeṣu kiṃ na syāt, ekādaśe ‘hni adhikakālāśaucināṃ kṣatriyādīnāṃ śuddhyabhāvāt, “śucinā karma kartavyam” iti śuddheḥ karmāṅgatvena vidhānāt, “athāśaucāpagame” (ViDh 21.1) iti sādhāraṇyenopakramyaikoddiṣṭasya viṣṇunābhihitatvāc ca |
ādyaśrāddham aśuddho ‘pi kuryād ekādaśe ‘hani |
karttus tātkālikī śuddhir aśuddhaḥ punar eva saḥ ||
iti śaṅkhavacanenāśaucamadhye ekādaśe ‘hni ekoddiṣṭavidhānān maivam iti cet — na, mātary agne pramītāyāṃ tadāśaucamadhye yadi [298] pitā mriyeta tato mātur ekoddiṣṭaśrāddham ekādaśe ‘hni aśuddho ‘pi kuryād iti viṣayāntarasaṃbhavāt | yat tu,
ekādaśe ‘hni yac chrāddhaṃ tat sāmānyam udāhṛtam |
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ sūtakaṃ tu pṛthak pṛthak ||
iti paiṭhīnasivacanaṃ tasyāyam arthaḥ – āśaucānantaradine yac chrāddhaṃ vihitaṃ tac caturṇām api varṇānāṃ sādhāraṇam, na brāhmaṇasyaiveti | kathaṃ tarhy ekādaśāhaśabdasyopapattiḥ iti cet — na, lakṣaṇayā tasyāśaucānantaradinaparatvenopapatteḥ |
atrocyate — ekādaśāhakālaviśiṣṭam ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhaṃ caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ vidhīyate, “na vidhau paraḥ śabdārthaḥ” iti nyāyenaikādaśāhaśabdasya lakṣaṇayāśaucānantaradinaparatvānupapatteḥ | sati mukhye vṛttyantarakalpanāyā anyāyyatvāc ca ekādaśāha eva kṣatriyādibhir apy ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhaṃ kartavyam | nanu — ekādaśe ‘hni kṣatriyādīnāṃ śuddhyabhāvāc chrāddhe ‘dhikāro nāsti ity uktam iti cet, — na, “karttus tātkālikī śuddhiḥ” iti vacanāt tātkālikyāḥ śuddheḥ sattvāt | — yat tu śaṅkhavacanasyāśaucamadhye āśaucāntaraprātāv ekoddiṣṭam ekādaśe ‘hni aśuddho ‘pi kuryād iti viṣayaviśeṣe tātparyam uktam | — tan na, tatrāpi śuddhyabhāvād ity asya codyasya samānatvāt | sāmānyena pravṛttasya śaṅkhavacanasya vinā kāraṇaṃ viśeṣaparatvena saṅkocāyogāc ca | — yat tūktam “athāśaucāpagame” iti sāmānyenopakramya viṣṇunaikoddiṣṭavidhānād [299] āśaucānantaram eva sarvair ekoddiṣṭaṃ kartavyam iti | — tan na, viṣṇuvacanasya daśāhāśaucibrāhmaṇaviṣatvenopapatteḥ | tasmād ekādaśāha eva kṣatriyādibhir apy ekoddiṣṭaṃ kartavyam iti suṣṭūktam |
atha saṃgṛhītaśrāddhanirṇayaḥ prapañcyate
pretoddeśena śraddhayā dravyatyāgaḥ śrāddham | tad uktaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
deśe kāle ca pātre ca śraddhayā vidhinā ca yat |
pitiṝn uddiśya viprebhyo dattaṃ śrāddham udāhṛtam || iti |
tac ca pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭabhedena dvividham | puruṣatrayam uddiśya yat kriyate tat pārvaṇam | ekapuruḍoddeśena yat kriyate tad ekoddiṣṭam | evaṃ dvividham api śrāddhaṃ nityanaimittikakāmyabhedena tridhā bhidyate | tatra jīvanopādhau coditaṃ nityam, yathā amāvāsyādu coditam | aniyatanimittakaṃ naimittikam, yathoparāgādau | kāmanopādhikaṃ kāmyam, yathā tithinakṣatrādiṣu | yat tu viśvāmitreṇa dvādaśavidhatvam uktam,
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍanam |
pārvaṇaṃ ceti vijñeyaṃ goṣṭhyāṃ śuddhyartham aṣṭamam ||
karmāṅgaṃ navamaṃ proktaṃ daivikaṃ daśamaṃ smṛtam |
yātrāsv ekādaśaṃ proktaṃ puṣṭyarthaṃ dvādaśaṃ matam ||
[300] iti, tan nityanaimittikakāmyāvāntarabhedavivakṣayaiva | na tu tataḥ pārthakyavivakṣayā | tathā hi – tatra nityam ity aharahaḥ śrāddham ucyate | naimittikam ity ekoddiṣṭam | tad āha pāraskaraḥ |
ahany ahani yac chrāddhaṃ tan nityam iti kīrtitam |
vaiśvadevavihīnaṃ tu aśaktāv udakena tu ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ tan naimittikam ucyate |
tad apy adaivaṃ kartavyam ayugmān āśayed dvijān || iti |
kāmyam ity abhimatārthasiddhyartham | vṛddhiśrāddham iti putrajanmavivāhādau kriyamāṇam | sapiṇḍanaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | pāravaṇam iti pratiparva kriyamāṇam | goṣṭhyām iti goṣṭhyām kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddham | tad āha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
abhipretārthasiddhyarthaṃ kāmyaṃ pārvaṇavat smṛtam |
putrajanmavivāhādau vṛddhiśrāddham udāhṛtam ||
navānītārghapātraṃ ca piṇḍaś ca parikīryate |
pitṛpātreṣu piṇḍeṣu sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tu tat ||
pratiparva bhaved yasmāt procyate pārvaṇaṃ tu tat |
goṣṭhyāṃ yat kriyate śrāddhaṃ goṣṭhīśrāddhaṃ tad ucyate ||
bahūnāṃ viduṣāṃ prāptau sukhārthaṃ pitṛtṛptaye || iti |
śuddhyartham iti śuddhaye kriyamāṇam | tad āha pracetāḥ |
[301]        kriyate śuddhaye yat tu brāhmaṇānāṃ tu bhojanam |
śuddhyartham iti tat proktaṃ śrāddhaṃ pārvaṇavat smṛtam || iti |
karmāṅgam iti yāgādau kriyamāṇam | daivikam iti devān uddiśya kriyamāṇam | yātrāśrāddham iti praveśanirgamayoḥ kriyamāṇam | tad āha pāraskaraḥ |
niṣekakāle some ca sīmantonnayane tathā |
jñeyaṃ puṃsavane śrāddhaṃ karmāṅgaṃ vṛddhivat kṛtam ||
devān uddiśya kriyate yat tad daivikam ucyate |
tan nityaśrāddhavat kuryāt dvādaśyādiṣu yatnataḥ ||
gacchan deśāntaraṃ tad dhi śrāddhaṃ kuryāt tu sarpiṣā |
tad yātrārtham iti proktaṃ praveśe ca na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
atra karmāṅgam iti vacanam akaraṇe karmavaiguṇyajñāpanārtham | sarpiṣā sarpiḥ pradhānakenety arthaḥ | anvarthe kevalena tṛpter asaṃbhavāt |
atha deśakathanam
śrāddhaṃ ca dakṣiṇāpravaṇe gomayādyupalipte deśe kāryam | tathā ca viṣṇudharmottare |
dakṣiṇāpravaṇe deśe tīrthādau vā gṛhe ‘pi vā |
bhūsaṃskārādisaṃyukte śrāddhaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ || (ViDhPu 1.144.13)
[304]        nadītaṭeṣu tīrtheṣu śaileṣu pulineṣu ca |
vivikteṣv eva tuṣyanti datteneha pitāmahāḥ || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
puṣkare ‘py akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ japahomatapāṃsi ca |
mahodadhau prayāge ca kāśyāṃ ca kurujāṅgale || iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi |
gaṅgāyamanayos tīre payoṣṇyamarakaṇṭake |
narmadābāhudātīre bhṛgutuṅge himālaye ||
gaṅgādvāre prayāge ca naimiṣe puṣkare tathā |
saṃnihatyāṃ gayāyāṃ ca dattam akṣayatāṃ vrajet || iti | (ŚaṃSm 14.2–3)
brahmāṇdapurāṇe ‘pi |
nadīsmudratīre vā hrade goṣṭhe ‘tha parvate |
samudragānadītīre sindhusāgarasaṃgame ||
nadyor vā saṃgame śastaṃ śālagrāmaśilāntike |
puṣkare vā kurukṣetre prayāge naimiṣe ‘pi vā ||
śālagrāme ca gokarṇe gayāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
[305]        kṣetreṣv eteṣu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ pitṛbhaktisamanvitaḥ ||
karoti vidhivan martyaḥ kṛtakṛtyo vidhīyate || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
kāṃkṣanti pitaraḥ putrān narakāpātabhīravaḥ |
gayāṃ yāsyati yaḥ kaścit so ‘smān saṃtārayiṣyati ||
kariṣyati vṛṣotsargam iṣṭāpūrtaṃ tathaiva ca |
pālayiṣyati vṛddhatve śrāddhaṃ dāsyati cānvaham ||
gayāyāṃ dharmapṛṣṭhe ca sadasi brahmaṇas tathā |
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe caiva pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti |
viṣṇur api |
gayāśīrṣe | vaṭe | amarakaṇṭakaparvate | yatra kvacana narmadātīre | yamunātīre | gaṅgāyāṃ viśeṣataḥ | gaṅgādvāre | prayāge | gaṅgāsāgarasaṃgame | kuśāvarte | bilvake | nīlaparvate | kanakhale | kubjāmre | bhṛgutuṅge | kedāre | [306] mahālaye | naḍantikāyām | sugandhāyām | śākambharyām | phalgutīrthe | mahāgaṅgāyām | tandulikāśrame | kumāradhārāyām | prabhāse | yatra kvacana sarasvatyām | viśeṣato naimiṣāraṇye | vārāṇasyām | agastyāśrame | kaṇvāśrame | kauśikyām | śarayūtīre | śoṇasya jyotirathyāyāś ca saṃgame | śrīparvate | kālodake | uttaramānase | vaḍavāyām | mataṅgavāpyām | saptārṣe | viṣṇupade | svargamārgapradeśe | godāvaryām | gomatyām | vetravatyām | vipāśāyām | vitastāyām | śatadrūtīre | candrabhāgāyām | irāvatyām | [307] sindhos tīre | dakṣiṇe pañcanade | aujase | evamādiṣv athānyeṣu tīrtheṣu | saridvarāsu | saṅgameṣu | prabhaveṣu | pulineṣu | prasravaṇeṣu | parvatanikuñjeṣu | vaneṣūpavaneṣu ca | gomayenopalipteṣu gṛheṣu | manojñeṣu ca | atrāpi pitṛgītā gāthā bhavanti |
kule ‘smākaṃ sa jantuḥ syād yo no dadyāj jalāñjalim |
nadīṣu bahutoyāsu śītalāsu viśeṣataḥ ||
api jāyeta so ‘smākaṃ kule kaścin narottamaḥ |
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe śrāddhaṃ yo no dadyāt samāhitaḥ ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady eko ‘pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet || iti | (ViDh 85.4–70)
gayāśīrṣapramāṇaṃ ca ādipurāṇe ‘bhihitam |
pañcakrośaṃ gayākṣetraṃ krośam ekaṃ gayāśiraḥ |
mahānadyāḥ paścimena yāvad gṛdhreśvaro giriḥ ||
[308]        uttare brahmayūpasya yāvad dakṣiṇamānasam |
etad gayāśiro nāma triṣu lokeṣu viśrutam || iti |
atha śrāddhakālāḥ
śrāddhakālās tv amāvāsyāṣṭakādayaḥ | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
amāvāsyāṣṭakā vṛddhiḥ kṛṣṇapakṣo ‘yanadvayam |
dravyaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpattir viṣuvat sūryasaṃkramaḥ ||
vyatīpāto gamachāyā grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ |
śrāddhaṃ prati ruciś caiva śrāddhakālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.217–18)
yasmin dine candramā na dṛśyate sā amāvāsyā | tatra śrāddhaṃ nityam | tathā ca laugākṣiḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kuryād avaśyaṃ tu pramītapitṛko dvijaḥ |
indukṣaye māsi mādi vṛddhau pratyabdam eva ca || iti |
aṣṭakāś catasraḥ mārgaśīrṣādicatuṣṭayāparapakṣāṣṭamyaḥ |
hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakāḥ ||
iti śaunakasmaraṇāt | tatrāpi śrāddhaṃ nityam | tad āha pitāmahaḥ |
amāvāsyāvyatīpātapaurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca |
vidvān śrāddham akurvāṇaḥ prāyaścittīyate tu saḥ || iti |
vṛddhiḥ putrajanmādiḥ | tena tadviśiṣṭaḥ kālo lakṣyate | kṛṣṇapakṣo ‘parapakṣaḥ | ayanadvayaṃ dakṣiṇāyanam uttarāyaṇaṃ [309] ca | dravyaṃ kṛsaramāṃsādi | brāhmaṇaḥ śrutādhyayanasaṃpannaḥ | tayoḥ saṃpattir lābho yasmin kāle sa tathoktaḥ | viṣuvanmeṣatulāsaṃkrāntī | sūryasaṃkramaḥ sūryasya rāśe rāśyantaraprāptiḥ | sūryasṃkramaśabdenaivāyanaviṣuvator upādāne siddhe pṛthag upādānaṃ phalātiśayajñāpanārtham | vyatīpāto yogaviśeṣaḥ | mahāvyatīpāto vā,
siṃhasthau gurubhaumau cen meṣasthe ca ravau hi vā |
dvādaśī hastasaṃyuktā vyatīpāto mahān hi saḥ ||
śravaṇāśvidhāniṣṭhārdrānāgadaivatamastakaiḥ |
yadyamā ravivāreṇa vyatīpātaḥ sa ucyate ||
iti vṛddhamanuvacanāt | nāgadaivatam āśleṣānakṣatram | mastakaṃ mṛgaśiraḥ | yadyamā amāvāsyā ravivāreṇa śravaṇādīnām anyatamena nakṣatreṇa yuktā sa vyatīpāta ity arthaḥ | gajachāyālakṣaṇaṃ smṛtyantare darśitam |
yadenduḥ pitṛdaivatye haṃsaś caiva kare sthitaḥ |
yāmyā tithir bhavet sā hi gagachāyā prakīrtitā || iti |
pitṛdaivatyaṃ maghānakṣatram | haṃsaḥ sūryaḥ | karo hastanakṣatram | yāmyā tithis trayodaśī | purāṇe ‘pi |
[310]        haṃse hastasthite yā tu maghāyuktā trayodaśī |
tithir vaivasvatī nāma sā chāyā kuñjarasya tu || iti |
“grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ” iti grahaṇam uparāgaḥ | atrāpi kālaviśeṣaḥ śrāddhāṅgatvena svīkāryaḥ | tad āha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
tridaśāḥ sparśasamaye tṛpyanti pitaras tathā |
manuṣyā madhakāle tu mokṣakāle tu rākṣasāḥ || iti |
“śrāddhaṃ prati ruciḥ” iti yadā śrāddhaṃ pratīcchā tadaiva kartavyam iti | cakāreṇānye ‘pi śrāddhakālāḥ saṃgṛhyante | ata eva yamaḥ |
āṣāḍhyām atha kārtikyāṃ mādhyāṃ trīn pañca vā dvijān |
tarpayet pitṛpūrvaṃ tu tad asyākṣayam ucyate || iti |
devalo ‘pi |
tṛtīyā rohiṇīyuktā vaiśākhasya sitā tu yā |
maghābhiḥ sahitā kṛṣṇā nabhasye tu trayodaśī ||
tathā śatabhiṣagyuktā kārtike navamī tathā |
indukṣayagajachāyā vaidhṛteṣu yugādiṣu ||
ete kālāḥ samuddiṣṭāḥ pitṝṇāṃ prītivardhanāḥ || iti |
yugādayo ‘pi śrāddhakālāḥ | te ca matsyapurāṇe darśitāḥ |
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyā tu navamī kārtikasya tu |
māghe pañcadaśī caiva nabhasye ca trayodaśī ||
yugādayaḥ smṛtā hy etā dattasyākṣayakārakāḥ | (MatsPu 17.4–5)
[311] iti | viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
vaiśākhamāsasya ca yā tṛtīyā navamy asau kārtikaśuklapakṣe |
nabhasyamāsasya ca kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśī pañcadaśī ca māghe ||
pānīyam apy atra tilair vimiśraṃ dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ prayato manuṣyaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ tena samāḥ sahasraṃ rahasyam etat pitaro vadanti || iti |
(ViPu 3.14.12–14)
manvādayo ‘pi śrāddhakālāḥ | tad uktaṃ matsyapurāṇe |
aśvayukśuklanavamī kārtike dvādaśī sitā |
tṛtīyā caitramāsasya sitā bhādrapadasya ca ||
phālgunasya tv amāvāsyā pauṣasyaikādaśī sitā |
āṣāḍhasyāpi daśamī māghamāsasya saptamī ||
śrāvaṇasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathāṣāḍhī ca pūrṇimā |
kārtikī phālgunī caitrī jyaiṣṭhī pañcadaśī sitā ||
manvantarādayaś caitā dattasyākṣayakārakāḥ || iti | (MatsPu 17.6–9)
kṛttikādinakṣatrāṇi kāmyaśrāddhakālāḥ | tathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[312]        svargaṃ hy apatyam ojaś ca śauryaṃ kṣetraṃ balaṃ tathā |
putraṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ ca saubhāgyaṃ samṛddhiṃ mukhjyatāṃ śubhām ||
pravṛttacakratāṃ caiva vāṇijyaprabhṛtīn api |
arogitvaṃ yaśo vītaśokatāṃ paramāṃ gatim ||
dhanaṃ vedān bhiṣaksiddhiṃ kupyaṃ gām apy ajāvikam |
aśvān āyuś ca vidhivad yaḥ śrāddhaṃ saṃprayacchati ||
kṛttikādibharaṇyantaṃ sa kāmān āpnuyād imān || iti | (YDh 1.265–68)
mārkaṇḍeyo ‘pi |
kṛttikāsu pitṝn arcya svargam āpnoti mānavaḥ |
apatyakāmo rohiṇyāṃ saumye tv aujasvitāṃ labhet ||
ārdrāyāṃ śauryam āpnoti kṣetrādi tu punarvasau |
puṣṭiṃ puṣye pitṝn arcan nāśleṣāsu varān sutān ||
maghāsu svajanaśraiṣṭhyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ phalgunīṣu ca |
pradānaśīlo bhavati sāpatyaś cottarāsu ca ||
prāpnoti śreṣṭhatāṃ satsu haste śrāddhaprado naraḥ |
rūpavanti ca citrāsu tathāpatyāny avāpnuyāt ||
vāṇijyalābhadā svātir viśākhā putrakāmadā |
kurvatāṃ cānurādhāś ca dadyuś cakrapravartanam ||
[313]        jyeṣṭhāsv arthādhipatyaṃ ca mūle cārogyam uttamam |
āṣāḍhāsu yaśaḥprāptir uttarāsu viśokatā ||
śravaṇe ca śubhāṃl lokān dhaniṣṭhāsu dhanaṃ mahat |
vedavittvam abhijiti bhiṣaksiddhiṃ ca vāruṇe ||
ajāvikaṃ prauṣṭhapade vinded bhāryāṃ tathottare |
revatīṣu tathā raupyam aśvinīṣu turaṅgamān ||
śrāddhaṃ kurvaṃs tathāpnoti bharaṇīṣv āyur uttamam |
tasmāt kāmyāni kurvīta ṛkṣeṣv eteṣu tattvavit || iti | (MārkPu 33.8.16)
saumyaṃ somadaivatyaṃ mṛgaśīrṣam ity arthaḥ | cakrapravartanaṃ sarvatrājñāyāḥ pratighātābhāvena pravartanam | abhijit abhijitsaṃjñakaṃ nakṣatram | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “upariṣṭād āṣāḍhānām adhastāt śroṇāyāḥ” iti | etat tu vedhanirūpaṇam | bhiṣaksiddhir auṣadhaphalāvāptiḥ | vāruṇaṃ śatabhiṣaṅ nikṣatram | kupyaṃ trapusīsādikam | viṣṇur api |
svargaṃ kṛttikāsu | apatyaṃ rohiṇīṣu | brahmavarcasaṃ saumye | karmaṇāṃ siddhiḥ raudre | bhuvaṃ punarvasau | puṣṭiṃ puṣye | śriyaṃ sārpe | sarvān kāmān [314] paitrye | saubhāgyaṃ phalgunīṣu | dhanam āryamṇe | jñātiśreiṣṭhyaṃ haste | rūpavataḥ sutāṃs tvāṣṭre | vāṇijyavṛddhiṃ svātau | kanakaṃ viśākhāsu | mitrāṇi maitre | śākre rājyam | kṛṣiṃ mūle | samudrayānasiddhim āpye | sarvān kāmāñ chravaṇe | balaṃ vāsave | ārogyaṃ vāruṇe | kupyadravyam āje | gṛham āhirbudhnye | gāḥ pauṣṇe | turaṅgamam āśvine | jīvitaṃ yāmye | iti | (ViDh 78.8–35)
ādityādivārāś ca kāmyaśrāddhakālāḥ | tad āha viṣṇuḥ: “satatam āditye ‘hni śrāddhaṃ kurvann ārogyam āpnoti | saubhāgyaṃ cāndre | samaravijayaṃ kauje | sarvān kāmān baudhe | vidyām abhīṣṭāṃ jaive | dhanaṃ śaukre | jīvitaṃ śanaiścare” iti (ViDh 78.1–7) | kūrmapurāṇe ‘pi |
[315]        ādityavāre tv ārogyaṃ candre saubhāgym eva ca |
kuje sarvatra vijayaṃ sarvān kāmān budhasya tu ||
vidyāṃ viśiṣṭāṃ ca gurau dhanaṃ vai bhārgave punaḥ |
śanaiścare bhaved āyur ārogyaṃ ca sudurlabham || iti | (KūPu 1.2.20.16–17)
viṣṇudharmottare |
ataḥ kāmyāni vakṣyāmi śrāddhāni tava pārthiva |
ārogyam atha saubhāgyaṃ samare vijayaṃ tathā |
sarvakāmāṃs tathā vidyāṃ dhanaṃ jīvitam eva ca ||
ādityādidineṣv evaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvan sadā naraḥ |
krameṇaitāny avāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || iti | (ViPu 1.142.30–32)
pratipadāditithayo ‘pi kāmyaśrāddhakālāḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
kurvan pratipadi śrāddhaṃ surūpān vindate sutān |
kanyakāṃ tu dvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ tu bandinaḥ ||
paśūn kṣudrāṃś caturthyāṃ tu pañcamyāṃ śobhanān sutān |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ dūtam avāpnoti saptamyāṃ labhate kṛṣim |||
aṣṭamyām api vāṇijyaṃ labhate śrāddhadaḥ sadā |
syān navamyām ekakhuraṃ daśamyāṃ dvikhuraṃ bahu ||
[316]        ekādaśyāṃ tathā rūpyaṃ brahmacarcasvinaḥ sutān |
dvādaśyāṃ jātarūpaṃ tu rajataṃ kupyam eva ca ||
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ tu suprajāḥ. |
prīyante pitaraś cāsya ye śastreṇa raṇe hatāḥ ||
śrāddhadaḥ pañcadaśyāṃ tu sarvān kāmān samaśnute || iti | (MDh 3.281–86)
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
kanyāṃ kanyāvedinaś ca paśūn kṣudrān sutān api |
dyūtaṃ kṛṣiṃ ca vāṇijyaṃ tathaikadviśaphān api ||
brahmavarcasvinaḥ putrān svarṇarūpye sakupyake |
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ sarvakāmān āpnoti śrāddhadaḥ sadā ||
pratipatprabhṛtiṣv ekāṃ varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śastreṇa tu hatā ye vai tebhyas tatra pradīyate || iti | (Ydh 1.262–64)
kanyāvedino jāmātāraḥ | etāni phalāni kṛṣṇapakṣapratipatpbhṛtiṣv amāvāsyāparyantāsu tithiṣu śrāddhadaḥ krameṇa prāpnoti | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ: “striyo ‘tirūpāḥ pratipadi | dvitīyāyāṃ strījanma | aśvas tṛtīyāyām | caturthyāṃ kṣudrapaśavaḥ | putrāḥ pañcamyām | dyūtaṃ ṣaṣṭhyām | kṛṣiḥ saptamyām | [317] vāṇijyam aṣṭamyām | ekaśaphaṃ navamyām | daśamyāṃ gāvaḥ | paricārakā ekādaśyām | dvādaśyāṃ dhanadhānyarūpyam | jñatiśraiṣṭhyaṃ hiraṇyāni trayodaśyām | yuvānas tatra mriyante śastrahatāś caturdaśyām | amāvāsyāyāṃ sarvam” iti | ayaṃ ca kṛṣṇapakṣapratipadāditithiṣu śrāddhavidhiḥ sarveṣv evāparapakṣeṣu | na bhādrapadāparapakṣa eva | ata eva śaunakaḥ: “prauṣṭhapadāparapakṣe māsi māsi caivam” iti | āpastambo ‘pi |
sarveṣv evāparapakṣasyāhaḥsu kriyamāṇe pitṝn prīṇāti | kartus tu kālābhiniyamāt phalaviśeṣaḥ | prathame ‘hani kriyamāṇe strīprāyam apatye jāyate | dvitīye stenāḥ | tṛtīye brahmavarcasvinaḥ | caturthe kṣudrapaśumān | pañcame pumāṃsaḥ | bahvapatyo na cānapatyaḥ pramīyate | ṣaṣṭhe ‘dhvaśīlo ‘kṣaśīlaś ca | saptame karṣe rāddhiḥ | aṣṭame puṣṭiḥ | navama ekakhurāḥ | daśame vyavahāre rāddhiḥ | ekādaśe kṛṣṇāyasaṃ trapusīsam | dvādaśe paśumān | trayodaśe bahuputro [318] bahumitro darśanīyāpatyaḥ yuvamāriṇas tu bhavanti | caturdaśa āyudharāddhiḥ | pañcadaśe puṣṭiḥ” iti (ĀpDh 2.7.16.7–22) |
bhādrapadāparapakṣaviṣaye mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
kanyāgate savitari dināni daśa pañca ca |
pārvaṇenaiva vidhinā tatra śrāddhaṃ vidhīyate ||
pratipad dhanalābhāya dvitīyā dvipadapradā |
varārthināṃ tṛtīyā ca caturthī śatrunāśinī ||
śriyaṃ prāpnoti pañcamyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ pūjyo bhaven naraḥ |
gaṇādhipatyaṃ pañcamyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ buddhim uttamām ||
striyo navamyāṃ prāpnoti daśamyāṃ pūrṇakāmatām |
vedāṃs tathāpnuyāt sarvān ekādaśyāṃ kriyāparaḥ ||
dvādaśyāṃ hemalābhaṃ ca prāpnoti pitṛpūjakaḥ |
prajāṃ medhāṃ paśuṃ puṣṭiṃ svātantryaṃ vṛddhim uttamām ||
dīrgham āyur athaiśvaryaṃ kurvāṇas tu trayodaśīm |
avāpnoti na saṃdehaḥ śrāddhaṃ śrāddhaparo naraḥ ||
yuvānaḥ pitaro yasya mṛtāḥ śastreṇa vai hatāḥ |
tena kāryaṃ caturdaśyāṃ teṣām ṛddhim abhīpsatā ||
[319]        śrāddhaṃ kurvann amāvāsyām annena puruṣaḥ śuciḥ |
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti svayaṃ cānantyam aśnute || iti | (MārPu 33.1–8)
nabhasyakṛṣṇapakṣapratipatprabhṛtidaśapañcadināni kṛtsno ‘pi pakṣaḥ savitari kanyāgate sati mahālaya iti proktaḥ | tatra pārvaṇenaiva vidhinā śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | tad āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
nabhasyasyāparaḥ pakṣo yatra kanyāṃ vrajed raviḥ |
sa mahālayasaṃjñaḥ syād gajachāyāhvayas tathā || iti |
yat tu śāṭhyāyaninoktam,
nabhasyasyāpare pakṣe tithiṣoḍaśakaṃ tu yat |
kanyāsthārkānvitaṃ cet syāt sa kālaḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
iti, tat tithivṛddhāv adhikadivase ‘pi śrāddhaṃ kuryān na tu pañcadaśadineṣv evety anenābhiprāyeṇa | atha vā, āśvayujaḥ śuklapratipadā saha nabhasyāparapakṣasya ṣoḍaśadinātmakatvam | tasyā api kṣīṇacandratvāviśeṣeṇāparapakṣānupraveśasaṃbhavāt | tad āha devalaḥ |
ahaḥṣoḍaśakaṃ yat tu śuklapratipadā saha |
candrakṣayāviśeṣeṇa sāpi darśātmikā smṛtā || iti |
nanu – etasmin pakṣe “dināni daśa pañca ca” iti vacansya kā gatiḥ |
ucyate – dvādaśasu kapāleṣv aṣṭākapālavat ṣaḍaśasu divaseṣu pañcadaśadinavcanam avayutyānuvādo [320] bhaviṣyati | atha vā, pañcadaśadivasaśoḍaśadivasavidhyor vrīhiyavavad vikalpo ‘stu | nabhasyāparapakṣasya kanyāsthārkānvitatvena praśastataratocyate | tadabhāve ‘pi tasya praśastatvāt | tad āha jābāliḥ |
agate ‘pi ravau kanyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvīta sarvathā |
āṣāḍhyāḥ pañcamaḥ pakṣaḥ praśastaḥ pitṛkarmasu ||
putrān āyus tathārogyam aiśvaryam atulaṃ tathā |
prāpnoti pañcame datvā śrāddhaṃ kāmāṃs tathāparān ||
[321] iti | bṛhanmanur api |
āṣāḍhīm avadhiṃ kṛtvā pañcamaṃ pakṣam āśritāḥ |
kāṅkṣanti pitaraḥ kliṣṭā annam apy anvahaṃ jalam ||
tasmāt tatraiva dātavyaṃ dattam anyatra niṣphalam |
āṣāḍhīm avadhiṃ kṛtvā yaḥ pakṣaḥ pañcamo bhavet ||
tatra śrāddhaṃ prakurvīta kanyāstho ‘rko bhaven na vā || iti |
asya pakṣasya raveḥ kanyāgatatvena praśastataratvaṃ ca ādipurāṇe darśitam |
pakṣāntare ‘pi kanyāsthe ravau śrāddhaṃ praśasyate |
kanyāgate pañcame tu viśeṣeṇaiva kārayet || iti |
ślokagautamo ‘pi |
kanyāgate savitari yāny ahāni tu ṣoḍaśa |
kratubhis tāni tulyāni saṃpūrṇavaradakṣiṇaiḥ || iti | (ŚlGauSm 8.15)
śāṭhyāyanir api |
puṇyaḥ kanyāgataḥ sūryaḥ puṇyaḥ pakṣaś ca pañcamaḥ |
kanyāsthārkānvitaḥ pakṣaḥ so ‘tyantaṃ puṇya ucyate || iti |
ādimadhyāvasāneṣu yatra kvacana kanyārkānvitatvena kṛtsnaḥ pakṣaḥ puṇya ity arthaḥ | ata eva kārṣṇājiniḥ |
ādau madhye ‘vasāne vā yatra kanyāṃ vrajed raviḥ |
sa pakṣaḥ sakalaḥ puṇyaḥ śrāddhaṣoḍaśakaṃ prati || iti |
pratipadādidarśāntaṃ śrāddhaṃ kartum asamarthaś cet [322] pañcamyādidarśāntam aṣṭamyādidarśāntaṃ vā yathāśakti śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | tad āha gautamaḥ: “aśrāddham amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt | pañcamīprabhṛtiṣu vāparapakṣasya | yathāśraddhaṃ sarvasmin vā” iti (GDh 15.1–3) | brahmāṇḍapurāṇe ‘pi |
nabhasyakṛṣṇapakṣe tu śrāddhaṃ kuryād dine dine |
tribhāgahīnapakṣaṃ vā tribhāgaṃ tv ardham eva vā || iti |
atra pratipadādidarśāntam ity asmin pakṣe nandādikaṃ na varjyam | tad āha kārṣṇājiniḥ |
nabhasyasyāpare pakṣe śrāddhaṃ kuryād dine dine |
naiva nandādi varjyaṃ syān naiva varjyā caturdaśī || iti |
nanu – etat
pratipatprabhṛtiṣv ekāṃ varjayitvā caturdaśīm | (YDh 1.264)
iti yājñavalkyavacanena virudhyeta – iti cet,
na, tasya vacanasya pañcamyādipakṣaviṣayatvenopapatteḥ | anyathā kārṣṇājinivacanasyānarthakyaṃ prasajyeta | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ: “aparapakṣe śrāddhaṃ kurvīta | ūrdhvaṃ caturthyāḥ [323] yad ahaḥ saṃpadyate saptamyā ūrdhvaṃ yad ahaḥ saṃpadyate ṛte caturdaśīm | śākenāpy aparapakṣaṃ nātikrāmet” iti | manur api |
kṛṣṇapakṣe daśamyādau varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śrāddhe praśastād tithayo yathaitā na tathetarāḥ || iti |
tatrāpy asāmarthye pañcamapakṣasya pañcamīm ārabhyānantaraprakṣapañcamīparyantāsu tithiṣv aniṣiddhāyām ekasyāṃ tithau yathāsaṃbhavaṃ gṛhī śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | tad āha yamaḥ |
haṃse varṣāsu kanyāsthe śākenāpi gṛhe vasan |
pañcamyor antare dadyāt ubhayor api pakṣayoḥ || iti |
aśaktyādinā pañcamapakṣe śrāddhakaraṇe yāvat kanyārāśau sūryas tiṣṭhati tāvac chrāddhaṃ dadyāt | tatrāpy akaraṇe yāvad vṛścikadarśanam iti | tad āha sumantuḥ |
kanyārāśau mahārāja yāvat tiṣṭhed vibhāvasuḥ |
tasmāt kālād bhaved deyaṃ vṛścikaṃ yāvad āgatam || iti |
purāṇe ‘pi |
kanyāgate savitari pitaro yānti vai sutān |
śūnyā pretapurī sarvā yāvad vṛścikadarśanam ||
tato vṛścikasaṃprāptau nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ |
punaḥ svabhavanaṃ yānti śāpaṃ datvā sudāruṇam ||
sūrye kanyāgate śrāddhaṃ yo na kuryād gṛhāśramī |
dhanaṃ putrāḥ kutas tasya pitṛniḥśvāsapīḍanāt ||
[324]        vṛścike samatikrānte pitaro daivataiḥ saha |
niḥśvasya pratigacchanti śāpaṃ datvā sudāruṇam || iti |
ādipurāṇe ‘pi |
prāvṛḍṛtau yamaḥ pretān pitrṝṃś cātha yamālayāt |
visarjayitvā mānuṣye kṛtvā śūnyaṃ svakaṃ puram ||
kṣudhārtāḥ kīrtayantaś ca duṣkṛtaṃ ca svayaṃ kṛtam |
kāṅkṣantaḥ putrapautrebhyaḥ pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyutam ||
tasmāt tāṃs tatra vidhinā tarpayet pāyasena tu |
madhvājyatilamiśreṇa tathā śītena cāmbhasā ||
grāsamātraṃ paragṛhād annaṃ yaḥ prāpnuyān naraḥ |
bhikṣāmātreṇa yaḥ prāṇān saṃdhārayati vā svayam ||
yo vā saṃvardhate dehaṃ pratyakṣaṃ svātmavikrayāt |
śrāddhaṃ tenāpi kartavyaṃ tais tair dravyaiḥ susaṃcitaiḥ || iti |
yamālayād visarjayitvā svakaṃ puraṃ śūnyaṃ kṛtvā manuṣyaloke pitṝn vāsayatīty adhyāhṛtya yojanā | pāyasaṃ kāṅkṣantaḥ pitaras tiṣṭhantīty adhyāhāraḥ | tatra varjyān āha gārgyaḥ |
nandāyāṃ bhārgavadine trayodaśyāṃ trijanmani |
eṣu śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta gṛhī putradhanakṣayāt || iti |
trijanmani janmanakṣatraṃ tatpurvottaraṃ cety arthaḥ | vṛddhagārgyo ‘pi |
[325]        prājāpatye tu pauṣṇe ca pitryarkṣe bhārgave tathā |
yas tu śrāddhaṃ prakurvīta tasya putro vinaśyati || iti |
prājāpatyaṃ rohiṇī | pauṣṇaṃ revatī | pitryarkṣaṃ maghā | aṅgirā api |
trayodaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute naraḥ |
pañcatvaṃ tasya jānīyāt jyeṣṭhaputrasya niścitam ||
madhāsu kurvataḥ śrāddhaṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ putro vinaśyati || iti |
atra maghātrayodaśyāṃ śrāddhaniṣedhaḥ kevalapitṛvargoddeśena śrāddhaviṣayaḥ | na tu kevalaśrāddhaniṣedhaḥ |
nanu – kevalapitṛvargoddeśena śrāddhaprāptau satyāṃ tanniṣedho yuktaḥ,
pitaro yatra pūjyante tatra mātāmahā dhruvam |
aviśeṣeṇa kartavyaṃ viśeṣān narakaṃ vrajet ||
iti dhaumyavacanena kevalaikavargoddeśaśrāddhaniṣedhāt prāptir eva nāsti | ato naivaṃ vyavasthā vivaṣyate – iti |
maivam – satyām api dhaumyasmṛtau vyāmohād eva prāptasyaikavargaśrāddhasya niṣedhāt | tathā rāgaprāptasya kalañjabhakṣaṇasya “na kalañjaṃ bhakṣayet” iti niṣedhas tadvat | ata eva kārṣṇājiniḥ |
śrāddhaṃ tu naikavargasya trayodaśyām upakramet |
atṛptās tatra ye ‘sya syuḥ prajāṃ hiṃsanti tatra te || iti |
smṛtyantaram api |
[326]        icchet trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ putravān yaḥ sutāyuṣoḥ |
ekasyaiva tu no dadyāt pārvaṇaṃ tu samācaret || iti |
yaḥ putravān sutāyuṣor abhivṛddhim icchet sa ekasyaikavargasyaiva śrāddhaṃ no dadyāt | api tu mātāmahavargoddeśenāpi pārvaṇaṃ samācared ity arthaḥ | tasmād ekavargoddeśenaiva maghātrayodaśyāṃ śrāddhaniṣedho na tu śrāddhasyaiva | tatra śrāddhasya praśastatvāt | tathā ca śaṅkhaḥ |
prauṣṭhapadyām atītāyāṃ maghāyuktāṃ trayodaśīm |
prāpya śrāddhaṃ tu kartavyaṃ madhunā pāyasena ca ||
prajām iṣṭāṃ yaśaḥ svargam ārogyaṃ ca dhanaṃ tathā |
nṛṇāṃ śrāddhe sadā prītāḥ prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ || iti | (ŚaṅSm 14.6–7)
mahābhārate ‘pi |
jñātīnāṃ tu bhavec chreṣṭhaḥ kurvan śrāddhaṃ trayodaśīm |
nāvaśyaṃ tu yuvāno ‘sya pramīyante narā gṛhe || iti |
atra maghātrayodaśyāṃ śrāddhe piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ na kuryāt, tasyāṃ yugāditvena piṇḍanirvapaṇaniṣedhāt | tathā ca pulastyaḥ |
ayanadvitaye śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaddvitaye (?) tathā |
yugādiṣu ca sarvāsu piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte || iti |
kartavyam ity adhyāhāraḥ | maghānvitatvenāpi piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ nāsti | tathā ca ādipurāṇe |
[327]        saṃkrāntāv upavāsena pāraṇena ca bhārata |
maghāyāṃ piṇḍadānena jyeṣṭhaḥ putro vinaśyati || iti |
caturdaśyāṃ śrāddhaniṣedho ‘py aśastrahataviṣayaḥ | apamṛtyuhatānāṃ tu caturdaśyām api śrāddhaṃ kāryam | tad āha marīciḥ |
viṣaśastraśvāpadāhitiryagbrāhmaṇaghātinām |
caturdaśyāṃ kriyā kāryā anyeṣāṃ tu viharhitā || iti |
pracetā api |
vṛkṣārohaṇalohādividyujjalaviṣādibhiḥ |
nakhidaṃṣṭrivipannānām eṣāṃ śastā caturdaśī || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
śastreṇa tu hatāye vai tebhyas tatra pradīyate | iti | (YDh 1.264)
atra caturdasyāṃ śastrādihatānām eveti niyamyate | na punaś caturdaśyām eveti | evaṃ ca sati dināntare ‘pi pitāmahāditṛptisiddhyarthaṃ mahālayaśrāddhaṃ kāryam | caturdaśyāṃ mahālayaśrāddhasyaikoddiṣṭatvena vihitatvāt tenātra pitāmahāditṛpter abhāvāt | tasya caikoddiṣṭarūpatvaṃ sumantur āha |
[328]        samatvam āgatasyāpi pituḥ śastrahatasya tu |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ sutaiḥ kāryaṃ caturdaśyāṃ mahālaye || iti |
samatvam āgatasya sapiṇḍīkṛtasya śastrahatasya pituś caturdaśyāṃ mahālaye sutair ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ | yasya pitāmaho ‘pi śastrādinā hataḥ tena dvayor api caturdaśyām ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhaṃ kāryam | tathā ca smṛtyantaram: “ekasmin dvayor vaikoddiṣṭavidhiḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – ekasmin pitari śastrādinā hate dvayor vā pitṛpitāmahayoḥ śastrādinā hatayoś caturdaśyāṃ putreṇa tayoḥ pratyekam ekoddiṣṭaṃ śrāddhaṃ kāryam iti | yasya pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahās trayo ‘pi śastrahatāḥ tena caturdaśyāṃ pārvaṇenaiva vidhinā śrāddhaṃ kārya,| ekasmin dvayor vaikoddiṣṭavidhir iti viśeṣopādānāt | idaṃ ca candrikākāra-aparārkayor matam | triṣv api pitrādiṣu śatrahateṣu trayāṇām api pṛthak pṛthag ekoddiṣṭam eva kāryam iti devasvāmimatam | atra – trayāṇāṃ śastrahatatve pārvaṇaśrāddhasya [329] sākṣad vidhāyakavacanābhāvād ekasmin dvayor vety asyopalakṣaṇārthatvenāpy upapatter ekoddiṣṭatrayam eva kāryam – iti devasvāmimataṃ yuktam iti pratibhāti | śastrādihatānāṃ dināntare pārvaṇavidhinaiva śrāddhaṃ kāryam | ata eva prajāpatiḥ |
saṃkrāntāv uparāge ca varṣotsavamahālaye |
nirvaped atra piṇḍāṃs trīn iti prāha prajāpatiḥ || iti |
bhrātṛbhaginyādīnāṃ mahālayaśrāddham ekoddiṣṭavidhānena kāryam | tathā ca sumantuḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ yatra yatra pradīyate |
bhrātre bhaginyai putrāya svāmine mātulāya ca ||
mitrāya gurave śrāddham ekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇm || iti |
āpastambo ‘pi |
aputrā ye mṛtāḥ kecit striyaś ca puruṣāś ca ye |
teṣām api ca deyaṃ syād ekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam || iti |
striyo bhaginyādayaḥ | puruṣā bhrātrādayaḥ | kātyāyano ‘pi |
saṃbandhibāndhavādīnām ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu sarvadā | iti |
yāni punar ekoddiṣṭaniṣedhakāni vākyāni yathā sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ prakṛtya jātūkarṇyaḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ na kartavyam ekoddiṣṭaṃ kadācana |
[330]        sapiṇḍīkaraṇāntaṃ ca tat proktam iti mudgalaḥ ||
pretatvaṃ caiva nistīrṇaḥ prāptaḥ pitṛgaṇaṃ tu saḥ |
cyavate pitṛlokāt tu pṛthakpiṇḍe niyojitaḥ ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ pṛthaktvenopapadyate |
pṛthaktve tu kṛte paścāt punaḥ kāryā sapiṇḍatā || iti |
kārṣṇāginir api |
ata ūrdhvaṃ na kartavyam ekoddiṣṭaṃ kadācana |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇāntaṃ ca pretasyaitad amaṅgalam || iti |
yamo ‘pi |
yaḥ sapiṇḍīkṛtaṃ pretaṃ pṛthakpiṇḍe niyojayet |
vidhighnas tena bhavati pitṛhā copajāyate || iti |
purāṇe ‘pi |
pradānaṃ yatra yatraiṣāṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt param |
tatra pārvaṇavat śrāddham ekoddiṣṭaṃ tyajed budhaḥ || iti |
tāni sarvāṇy api pratpadoktaikoddiṣṭavidhyabhāve draṣṭavyāni | etac chrāddhaṃ sūtakādinā mukhyakālātikrame tv āśaucāpagamānantarakāla eva kāryam | tathā ca ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
deye ptiṝṇāṃ śrāddhe tu āśaucaṃ jāyate tadā |
āśauce tu vyatikrānte tebhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ pradīyate || iti |
yat tv atrivacanam |
[331]        tad ahaś cet praduṣyeta kenacit sūtakādinā |
sūtakānantaraṃ kuryāt punas tad ahar eva vā || iti |
sūtakānantarakāle vā anantare vā māsi tatpakṣe tattithau veti pakṣadvayam upanyastam | tatrādye pakṣe virodha eva nāsti | punas tad ahar eva vety ayaṃ pakṣaḥ sūtakavyatiriktanimittāntareṇa vighne samutpanne pratimāsaṃ kṣayāhe vihitaikoddiṣṭamāsikaśrāddhaviṣaya iti ṛṣyśṛṅgavacanāvirodhāya vyavasthāpyate | ata eva devalaḥ |
ekoddiṣṭe tu saṃprāpte yadi vighnaḥ prajāyate |
anyasmiṃs tattitau tasmin śrāddhaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ || iti |
anyasminn anantare māsi | tattithau mṛtatithau | yasimin śukle kṛṣṇe vā mṛtas tasmin pakṣe śrāddhaṃ vighnavaśāt kuryād ity arthaḥ | āśaucanimittakavighne tu māsikaśrāddham api sūtakānantaram eva ṛṣyaśṛṅgavacanabalād anuṣṭheyam | devasvāminā ‘py evam eva viṣayavyavasthā kṛtā: “etat ṛṣyaśṛṅgavacanam āśaucavighnaviṣayam | nimittāntaratas tadaharvighāte ‘ekoddiṣṭe tu saṃprāpte yadi vighnaḥ prajāyate’ ityādismṛtyantaravacanam” iti | yat tu vyāsenoktam,
śrāddhavighne samutpanne antarā mṛtasūtake |
amāvāsyāṃ prakurvīta śuddhāv eke manīṣiṇaḥ ||
[332] iti | antarā prayogamadhye pākopakramāt prāk mṛtake sūtake vā jāte amāvāsyām amāvāsyāyāṃ śuddhau śuddhyanantaraṃ vā śrāddhaṃ prakurvīteti | etad anumāsikasāṃvatsarikaśrāddhaviṣayam | ata evoktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
māsike ‘bde tu saṃprāpte antarā mṛtasūtake |
vadanti śuddhau tatkāryaṃ darśe vāpi vicakṣaṇāḥ || iti |
darśagrahaṇaṃ śuklakṛṣṇaikādaśyor upalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva marīciḥ |
śrāddhavighne samutpanne avijñāte mṛte ‘hani |
ekādaśyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ || iti |
kṛṣṇapakṣe yā ekādaśī tasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ kartavyam iti yojanā, pitṛkārye kṛṣṇapakṣasyaiva viśeṣato grāhyatvāt | kṛṣṇaikādaśīto ‘pi amāvāsyāyā mukhyatvaṃ pitṛkārye daṇḍāpūpanyāyasiddham | etad uktaṃ bhavati – āśaucasamantarakālo mukhyakālasaṃnikṛṣṭatvāc chreṣṭhatamaḥ | darśakālas tu mukhyakālapratyāsattyabhāvāt tato jaghanya iti | ata eva ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
śucir bhūtena dātavyaṃ yā tithiḥ pratipadyate |
sā tithis tasya kartavyā na cānyā vai kadācana || iti |
śucinā tāvac chrāddhaṃ kartavyam | tatrāśaucavaśān mukhyakāle śuddhyabhāve śuddhyanantaraṃ yā tithiḥ pratipadyate labhyate sā tithis tasya karmaṇo ‘ṅgatvena svīkartavyā | āśaucādyanupaghāte tu mukhyakālo nālasyādinātikramaṇīyaḥ | tad āha sa eva |
[333]        tithichedo na kartavyo vināśaucaṃ yadṛcchayā |
piṇḍaḥ śrāddhaṃ ca dātavyaṃ vicchittiṃ naiva kārayet || iti |
cakāreṇāgnaukaraṇaṃ samuccinoti | śrāddhaśabdenātra brāhmaṇatarpaṇamātraṃ vivakṣitam, piṇḍadānasya pṛthaguāttatvāt | “vicchittiṃ naiva kārayet” iti brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ kartum asamarthaś cet piṇḍapradānamātram api kuryāt | sarvathā pitrarcanasya vicchedaṃ na kuryād ity arthaḥ | ata eva nigamaḥ: “āhitāgneḥ pitrarcanaṃ piṇḍair eva brāhmaṇān api vā bhojayet” iti | atra vyavasthito vikalpaḥ | sati sāmarthye brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ piṇḍapradānaṃ ca kuryāt | tatrāsāmarthye piṇḍapradānamātram iti | yat tu hārītena śrāddhavighne samutpanne amāvāsyādiṣv āmaśrāddhaṃ vihitam |
śrāddhavighne dvijātīnām āmaśrāddhaṃ prakīrtitam |
amāvāsyādiniyataṃ māsasāṃvatsarād ṛte || iti | (HārSm 21.13)
māsaṃ māsikam | sāṃvatsaraṃ sāṃvatsarikam | tat bhāryārajodarśanakṛtavighnaviṣayam | tathāha uśanāḥ |
apatnīkaḥ pravāsī ca yasya bhāryā rajasvalā |
siddhānnena na kurvīta āmaṃ tasya vidhīyate || iti | (UśSm 5.82)
[334] kātyāyano ‘pi |
āpady anagnau tīrthe ca pravāse putrajanmani |
āmaśrāddhaṃ prakurvīta yasya bhāryā rajasvalā || iti |
vyāghrapādo ‘pi |
ārtave deśakālānāṃ viplave samupasthite |
āmaśrāddhaṃ dvijaiḥ kāryaṃ śūdraḥ kuryāt sadaiva hi || iti |
na ca kātyāyana-vyāghravacanaparyālocanayā māsikapratyābdikayor apy āmaśrāddhaṃ prāpnotīti mantavyam, “māsasāṃvatsarād ṛte” iti viśeṣavacanenāmaśrāddhasya tadvyatiriktaviṣayatvāvagamāt | ata eva marīciḥ |
anagnikaḥ pravāsī ca yasya bhāryā rajasvalā |
āmaśrāddhaṃ dvijaḥ kuryān na tat kuryān mṛte ‘hani || iti |
tat śmaśrāddhaṃ mṛte ‘hani na kuryāt | kiṃ tu pakvānnenaiva kuryād ity arthaḥ | laugākṣir api |
puṣpavatsv api dāreṣu videśastho ‘py anagnikaḥ |
annenaivābdikaṃ kuryāt hemnā nāmena vā kvacit || iti |
yas tu smṛtyantare bhāryāyāṃ rajasvalāyāṃ mṛte ‘hani śrāddhaniṣedhaḥ,
mṛte ‘hani tu saṃprāpte yasya bhāryā rajasvalā |
śrāddhaṃ tadā na kartavyaṃ kartavyaṃ pañcame ‘hani ||
[335] iti, tasyāyaṃ viṣayaḥ — aputrāyāḥ patnyā eva patyur mṛtāhaśrāddhe ‘dhikārād yadā sā svayam eva rajasvalā syāt tadā mṛte ‘hani śrāddhaṃ na kartavyam | kiṃ tu pañcame ‘hanīti | tathā ca ślokagautamaḥ |
aputrā tu yadā bhāryā saṃprāpte bhartur ābdike |
rajasvalā bhavet sā tu kuryāt tat pañcame ‘hani || iti | (ŚloGSm 5.14)
prabhāsakhaṇḍe ‘pi |
śuddhā syāt tu caturthe ‘hni snātā nārī rajasvalā |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca pañcame ‘hani śudhyati || iti |
anye tu,
śrāddhīye ‘hani saṃprāpte yasya bhāryā rajasvalā |
śrāddhaṃ tatra na kartavyaṃ kartavyaṃ pañcame ‘hani || (ŚlGSm 5.19)
iti ślokagautamavacanam anyathā paṭhitvā śrāddhādau karmaṇi bhāryayā sahaivādhikāraśravaṇāt tasyāṃ rajodarśanadūṣitāyām adhikāranivṛtter mukhyakālam atikramya pañcame ‘hani śrāddhaṃ kartavyam – iti manyante |
nanu – asmin pāṭhe ‘māvāsyādiśrāddhasyāpi pañcame ‘hany utkarṣaḥ prāpnoti – iti |
maivam, [336] “śrāddhavighne dvijātīnām” iti hārītavacanenāmāvāsyādiṣv āmasyānnakārye somakārye pūtīkavad vihitatvāt | śrāddhīye ‘hnīty asya vacanasya mṛtāhavyatiriktaviṣayatvena sārthakatvam astu – iti cet |
bhaved etad evaṃ yadi viṣayāntaraṃ vaktuṃ śakyeta | na tv etad asti | mṛtāhaviṣayatvaṃ tu “mṛte ‘hani tu saṃprāpte” iti smṛtyantaravacanād evāvagamyate | tasmād ekabhāryeṇa mṛtāhaśrāddhaṃ rajodarśanarūpavighnoparamakāla eva kartavyam | bhāryāntarayuktena tv adhikārānapagamān mukhya eva kāle kartavyam iti | yad atra yuktaṃ tad grāhyam ||
atha śrāddhe brāhmaṇaparīkṣā
śrāddhe bhojanīyabrāhmaṇaparīkṣā kartavyā | tatra śrāddhaṃ prakṛtya yamaḥ |
pūrvam eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇān vedapāragān |
śarīraprabhavair doṣair viśuddhāṃś caritavratān ||
dūrād eva parīkṣeta brāhmaṇān vedapāragān |
iṣṭān vā yadi vāniṣṭāṃs tatkāle nāvamānayet || iti |
pūrvam iti nimantarṇāt pūrvam ity arthaḥ | śarīraprabhavā doṣāḥ kuṣṭāpasmārādayaḥ | dūrād iti prapitāmahād ārabhya bhojanīyabrāhmaṇaparyantam | tathā ca chāgaleyaḥ |
[337]        uktalakṣaṇasaṃpannair vidyāśīlaguṇānvitaiḥ |
puruṣatrayavikhyātaiḥ sarvaṃ śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
sarvaṃ pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭātmakam | ata eva manunāpi pituḥ śrotriyatvena puruṣasya śraiṣṭhyam uktam |
aśrotriyaḥ pitā yasya putraḥ syād vedapāragaḥ |
aśrotriyo vā putraḥ syāt pitā syād vedapāragaḥ ||
jyāyāṃsam anayor vidyād yasya syāc chrotriyaḥ pitā | iti | (MDh 3.136–37)
śrāddhe bhojanīyā brāhmaṇā yājñavalkyena darśitāḥ |
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu śrotriyo brahmavid yuvā |
vedārthavit jyeṣṭhasamā trimadhus trisuparṇikaḥ ||
karmaniṣṭhās taponiṣṭhāḥ pañcāgnir brahmacāriṇaḥ |
pitṛmātṛparāś caiva brāhmaṇāḥ śrāddhasaṃpade || (Ydh 1.219–221)
[338] iti | ṛgvedādisarvavedeṣv askhalitādhyayanakramāḥ | śrotriyaḥ śrutādhyayanasaṃpannaḥ | brahmavit brahmajñānavān | yuvā madhyamavayaskaḥ | yuvatvaṃ ca sarvaviśeṣaṇam | vedārthavid dharmajñānavān | jyeṣṭhasāmeti sāmaviśeṣas tadvrataṃ ca | tadvratācaraṇena yas tad adhīte sa jyeṣṭhasāmā | trimadhuḥ ṛgvedaikadeśaḥ tadvrataṃ ca | tavratācaraṇena tadadhyāyī trimadhuḥ | trisuparṇam ṛgyajuṣayor ekadeśaḥ tadvrataṃ ca | tadācaraṇena yas tad adhīte sa trisuparṇikaḥ | brāhmaṇā na kṣatriyādayaḥ | uktalakṣaṇā ete brāhmaṇāḥ śrāddhasyākṣayaphalasaṃpādakā ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatir api |
yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe chandogaṃ tatra bhojayet |
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni tritayaṃ tatra vidyate ||
aṭeta pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām |
yadi labhyeta pitrarthe sāmnām akṣaracintakaḥ ||
ṛcā tu tṛpyati pitā yajuṣā tu pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ pitāmahaḥ sāmnā chandogo ‘bhyadhikas tataḥ || iti |
śātātapo ‘pi |
bhojayed yas tv atharvāṇaṃ daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
anantam akṣayaṃ caiva phalaṃ tasyeti vai śrutiḥ ||
[339] iti | yamo ‘pi |
vedavidyāvratasnātāḥ śrotriyā vedapāragāḥ |
svadharmaniratāḥ śāntāḥ kriyāvantas tapasvinaḥ ||
tebhyo havyaṃ ca kavyaṃ ca prasannebhyaḥ pradīyate | iti |
manur api |
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni dātṛbhiḥ |
arhattamāya viprāya tasmai dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
ekaikam api vidvāṃsaṃ daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti nāmantrajñān bahūn api || iti | (MDh 3.128–29)
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “yatīn gṛhasthān sādhūn vā” iti | bhojayed iti śeṣaḥ | brāhmāṇḍapurāṇe ‘pi |
śikhibhyo dhāturaktebhyaḥ tridaṇḍebhyaś ca dāpayet | iti |
śikhino brahmacāriṇḥ | dhāturaktāḥ dhāturaktavastradhāriṇo vānaprasthāḥ | tridaṇḍino vākkāyamanodaṇḍair upetā yatayaḥ | atra paraḥ paraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | ata eva nāradaḥ |
yo vai yatīn anādṛtya bhojayed itarān dvijān |
vijānan vasato grāme kavyaṃ tad yāti rākṣasān || iti |
brāhmaṇḍapurāṇē ‘pi |
[340]        alābhe dhyānibhikṣūṇāṃ bhojayed brahmacāriṇam |
tadalābhe ‘py udāsīnaṃ gṛhastham api bhojayet || iti |
udāsīno hy asaṃbandhaḥ | ata eva āpastambaḥ: “brāhmaṇān bhojayed brahmavido yonigotramantrāntevāsyasaṃbandhān” iti (ĀpDh 2.7.17.4) | yonisaṃabdhā mātulādayaḥ | gotrasaṃbandhāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ | mantrasaṃbandhā vedādhyāpakādayaḥ | antevāsisaṃbandhāḥ śiṣyaśāstropādhyāyāḥ | evaṃvidhasaṃbandhavyatiriktān brāhmaṇān gṛhasthādīn bhojayed ity arthaḥ | śrāddhe śrotriyādīnāṃ paṅktipāvanatvenāpi pātraviśeṣatāṃ sa evāha |
apāṅktyopahtā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yair dvijottamaiḥ |
tān nibodhata kārtsnyena dvijāgryān paṅktipāvanān ||
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca |
śrotriyānvayajāś caiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgniḥ trisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
[341]        brahmadeyātmasantānaś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ ||
vedārthavit pravaktā ca brahmcārī sahasradaḥ |
śatāyuś caiva vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ || iti |
brahmadeyātmasantānaḥ brāhmavivāhoḍhāputraḥ | sahasradaḥ gavāṃ suvarṇasya vā | yamo ‘pi |
ye somapā virajaso dharmajñāḥ śāntabuddhayaḥ |
vratino niyamasthāś ca ṛtukālābhigāminaḥ ||
pañcāgnir apy adhīyāno yajurvedavid eva ca |
bahvṛcaś ca trisauparṇaḥ trimadhur vātha yo bhavet ||
triṇāciketo virajāś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
atharvaśiraso ‘dhyetā sarve te paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
śiśur apy agnihotrī ca nyāyavic ca ṣaḍaṅgavit |
mantrabrāhmaṇavic caiva yaś ca syād dharmapāṭhakaḥ ||
brahmadeyāsutaś caiva bhāvaśuddhaḥ sahasradaḥ |
cāndrāyaṇavratacaraḥ satyavādī purāṇavit ||
niṣṇātaḥ sarvavidyāsu śānto vigatakalmaṣaḥ |
guruvedāgnipūjāsu prasakto jñānatatparaḥ ||
vimuktaḥ sarvadā dhīro brahmabhūto dvijottamaḥ |
anamitro na cāmitro maitra ātmavid eva ca ||
snātako japyanirataḥ sadā puṣpabalipriyaḥ |
ṛjur mṛduḥ kṣamī dāntaḥ śāntaḥ satyavrataḥ śuciḥ ||
[342]        vedajñaḥ sarvaśāstrajñaḥ upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ |
gṛhastho brahmcārī ca caturvedavid eva ca ||
vedavidyāvratasnātā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ || iti |
paiṭhīnasir api:
athātaḥ paṅktipāvanā bhavanti | triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇaś cīrṇavrataś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago brahmadeyānusantānaḥ sahasrado vedādhyāyī caturvedaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit atharvaśiraso ‘dhyāyī pañcāgnir vedajāpī ceti | teṣām ekaikaḥ punāti paṅktim | niyukto mūrdhani sahasrair apy upahatām | iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi |
brahmadeyānusantāno brahmadeyāpradāyakaḥ |
brahmadeyāpatiś caiva brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
ṛgyajuḥpārago yaś ca sāmnāṃ yaś cāpi pāragaḥ |
atharvaśiraso ‘dhyetā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
nityaṃ yogaparo vidvān samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
dhyānaśīlo yatir vidvān brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ || iti | (ŚaṅSm 13.6–8)
baudhāyano ‘pi: [343] “trimadhus triṇāciketas trisuparṇaḥ pañcāgniḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit triśīrṣako jyeṣṭhasāmagā it paṅktipāvanāḥ” iti (BDh 2.8.14.2) | hārīto ‘pi |
sthitir avacchinnavedaveditāyonisaṅkaritvam ārṣeyatvaṃ ceti kulaguṇāḥ | vedo ‘ṅgāni dharmo ‘dhyātmavittvaṃ vijñānaṃ smṛtiś ceti ṣaḍvidhaṃ śrutam | brahmaṇyatā devapitṛbhaktatā | samatā saumyatā parānupatāpitānasūyatānuddhatatā pāruṣyaṃ mitratā priyavāditvaṃ kṛtajñatā śaraṇyatā praśāntiś ceti trayodaśavidhaṃ śīlam |
kṣamā damo dayā dānam ahiṃsā gurupūjanam |
śaucaṃ snānaṃ japo homas tapaḥ svādhyāya eva ca ||
satyavacanaṃ santoṣo dṛḍhavratatvam upavratatvam iti ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ vṛttam | tasmāt kulīnāḥ [344] śrutaśīlavanto vṛttasthāḥ satyavādino ‘vyaṅgāḥ pāṅkteyāḥ | dvādaśobhayataḥ śrotriyas triṇāciketas trisuparṇas trimadhus triśīrṣako jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ pañcāgniḥ ṣaḍaṅgavid rudrajāpy ūrdhvaretāḥ ṛtukālagāmī tattvavic ceti paṅktipāvanā bhavanti” iti (HārSm 13.15–22) |
sthitir avicchinnasantānatā avicchinnavedveditety atra havirāsādanārthadeśaviśeṣavācī vediśabdo haviḥsādhyaṃ yāgaṃ lakṣayati | ārṣeyatvaṃ pravaravartiṛṣijñātṛtvam | dharmo dharmaśāstram | vijñānaṃ vaiśeṣikādiśāstrābhijñānam | smṛtir vedaśāstrāvismaraṇam | upavratatvaṃ daśamyādāv ekabhaktatā | atrānukalpo yājñavalkyena darśitaḥ |
svasrīyaṛtvikjāmātṛyājaśvaśuramātulāḥ |
triṇāciketadauhitraśiṣyasaṃbandhibāndhavāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.220)
ata eva manuḥ |
anukalpas tv ayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ |
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svasrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum ||
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhum ṛtvigyājyau ca bhojayet | iti | (MDh 3.147–48)
viṭpatir jāmātā atithir vā | tad uktam devasvāminā: “viṭpatir atithir anye vadanti” iti | āpastambo ‘pi: [345] “guṇavadalābhe sodaryo ‘pi bhojayitavyaḥ | etenāntevāsino vyākhyātāḥ” iti (ĀpDh 2.6.17.5–6) | baudhāyano ‘pi: “tadabhāve rahasyavit | ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmānīti śrāddhasya mahimā tasmād evaṃvidhaṃ sapiṇḍam apy āśayet” iti (BDh 2.8.14.3–4) | viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
pitṛvyagurudauhitrān ṛtviksvasrīyamātulān |
pūjayed dhavyakavyena vṛddhān atithibāndhavān || iti |
atra ṛtvikpitṛvyasodaryasapiṇḍā vaiśvadevasthāne niyoktavyāḥ | na pitrādisthāne | tathā ca atriḥ |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā putro vātha sapiṇḍakaḥ |
na parasparam arghyāḥ syur na śrāddhaṃ ṛtvijas tathā ||
ṛtvikputrādayo hy ete sakulyā brāhmaṇā dvijāḥ |
vaiśvadeve niyoktavyā yady ete guṇavattarāḥ || iti |
śiṣyasyāpi vaiśvadevasthāna eva niveśaḥ, sodarye vihitasyārthasya [346] “etenāntevāsino vyākhyātāḥ” (ĀpDh 2.7.17.6) ity āpastambena śiṣye ‘tideśāt | yat tu manunā kalpāntaram uktam,
kāmaṃ śrāddhe ‘rcayen mitraṃ nābhirūpam api tv arim |
dviṣatā hi havir bhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalam || (MDh 3.144)
iti, tan na sākṣād anukalpābhiprāyeṇa | kiṃ tv anukalpānukalpābhiprāyeṇa, “na śrāddhe bhojayen mitram” (MDh 3.144) iti svenaiva niṣiddhasya mitrasya “kāmam arcayet” iti sānuśayam evānujñānāt | vasiṣṭho ‘py anukalpānukalpam āha |
ānṛśaṃsyaṃ paro dharmo yācate yat pradīyate |
ayācataḥ sīdamānān sarvopāyair nimantrayet || iti |
ānṛśaṃsyam utkṛṣṭo dharmaḥ | tenāyācataḥ ayācanaśīlān ata eva sidamānān nirguṇān api saguṇānām anukalpānām abhāve [347] sarvopāyair yathā te nimantraṇam aṅgīkurvanti tādṛśair upāyair nimantrayet | ayācanaśīlānām abhāve yācamānāya nirguṇāya pradīyate – iti etad apy anukalpo bhavatīty arthaḥ | saṃbhavati mukhyakalpe nānukalpo ‘nuṣṭheyaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
prabhuḥ prathamakalpasya yo ‘nukalpena vartate |
na sāmparāyikaṃ tasya durmater vidyate phalam || iti | (MDh 11.30)
sāmparāyikam uttarakālikaṃ svargādikaṃ phalam iti | bhaviṣatpurāṇe ‘pi |
brāhmanātikramo nāsti mūrghe vedavivarjite |
jvalantam agnim utsṛjya na hi bhasmani hūyate || iti |
vedavivarjite iti nirguṇamātropalakṣaṇārtham | ata evoktaṃ tatraiva |
vyatikrāntur na doṣo ‘sti nirguṇam prati karhicit |
yasya tv ekagṛhe mūrkho dūrasthaś ca guṇānvitaḥ ||
guṇānvitāya dātavyaṃ nāsti mūrkhe vyatikramaḥ || iti |
yat tu purāṇantare ‘bhihitam,
yas tv āsannam atikramya brāhmaṇaṃ patitād ṛte |
dūrasthaṃ bhojayen mūḍho guṇāḍhyaṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||
tasmāt saṃpūjayed enaṃ guṇaṃ tasya ca cintayet |
kevalaṃ cintayej jātiṃ na guṇānvitatāṃ khaga ||
[348]        saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ dvijaṃ yas tu yuktajātiṃ priyaṃvadam |
mūrkhaṃ vā paṇḍitaṃ vāpi vṛttihīnam athāpi vā ||
nātikrāmen naro vidvān dāridyābhihataṃ tathā |
iti, tad dauhitrajāmātrādiviṣayam | ata eva manuḥ |
vratastham api dauhitraṃ śrāddhe yatnena bhojayet | iti | (MDh 3.234)
vratasthaṃ kevalavratastham adhyayanādirahitam ity arthaḥ | guṇavatsaṃnikṛṣṭātikrame tu pratyavāyo ‘sti | tathā ca purāṇam |
sapta pūrvān sapta parān puruṣān ātmanā saha |
atikramya dvijavarān narake pātayet khaga ||
tasmān nātikramet prājño brāhmaṇān prātiveśikān |
saṃbandhinas tathā sarvān dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ tathā ||
bhāgineyaṃ viśeṣeṇa tathā bandhuṃ khagādhipa |
nātikrāmen naraś caitān amūrkhān api gopate ||
atikramya mahāraudraṃ rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet | iti |
apir evakārārthaḥ | amūrkhān evātikramya narakaṃ vrajet | na mūrkhān ity abhiprāyaḥ | śrāddhe varjanīyā brāhmaṇā yājñavalkyena darśitāḥ |
rogī hīnātiriktāṅgaḥ kāṇaḥ paunarbhavas tathā |
avakīrṇī kuṇḍagolau kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ ||
[349]        bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ klībaḥ kanyādūṣy abhiśastakaḥ |
mitradhruk piśunaḥ somavikrayī privindakaḥ ||
mātāpitṛgurutyāgī kuṇḍāśī vṛṣalātmajaḥ |
parapūrvāpatiḥ stenaḥ karmaduṣṭaś ca ninditāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.222–24)
rogī unmādādipāparogavān | te ca devalena darśitāḥ: “unmādas tvagdoṣo rājayakṣmā śvāso madhumeho bhagandaro mahodaro ‘śmarīty aṣṭau pāparogāḥ” iti | hīnaṃ nyūnam adhikam atiriktam aṅgaṃ yasyāsau hīnātiriktāṅgaḥ | ekenāpy akṣṇā yo na paśyati asau kāṇaḥ | tena ca badhiramūkādayo lakṣyante | dvirūḍhā punarbhūs tasyāṃ jātaḥ paunarbhavaḥ | avakīrṇī kṣatavrataḥ | vetanaṃ gṛhītvā yo ‘dhyāpayati sa bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ | asatā satā vā doṣeṇa kanyāṃ dūṣayitā kanyādūṣī | mahāpātakābhiśasto ‘bhiśastakaḥ | parivindakaḥ parivettā | kuṇḍasyānnaṃ yo ‘śnāti sa kuṇḍāśī | kuṇḍaśabdo golakasyāpy upalakṣakaḥ | vihitakarmaparityāgī vṛṣalaḥ tatsuto vṛṣalātmajaḥ | parapūrvāpatiḥ punarbhūpatiḥ | adattādāyī stenaḥ | karmaduṣṭāḥ śāstraviruddhācāropetāḥ | ete śrāddhe ninditā varjyā ity arthaḥ | manur api |
[350]        ye stenapatitaklībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
tān havyakavyayor viprān anarhān manur abravīt ||
jaṭilaṃ cānadhīyānaṃ durvālaṃ kitavaṃ tathā |
yājayanti ca ye pūgāṃs tāṃś ca śrāddhe na bhojayet ||
cikitsakān devalakān māṃsavikrayiṇas tathā |
vipaṇena ca jīvanto varjyāḥ syur havyakavyayoḥ ||
preṣyo grāmasya rājñaś ca kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ |
pratiroddhā guroś caiva tyaktāgnir vārdhuṣis tathā ||
yakṣmī ca paśupālaś ca parivettā nirākṛtiḥ |
brahmadviṭ parivittiś ca gaṇābhyantara eva ca ||
kuśīlavo ‘vakīrṇī ca vṛṣalīpatir eva ca |
paunarbhavaś ca kāṇaś ca yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitas tathā |
śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva vāgduṣṭaḥ kuṇḍagolakau ||
akāraṇaparityaktā mātāpitror guros tathā |
brāhmair yaunaiś ca saṃbandhaiḥ saṃyogaṃ patitair gataḥ ||
agāradāhī garadaḥ kuṇḍāśī somavikrayī |
samudrayāyī bandī ca tailakaḥ kūṭakārakaḥ ||
pitrā vivadamānaś ca kekaro madyapas tathā |
kekaraḥ tiryagdṛṣṭiḥ |
[351]        pāparogy abhiśastaś ca dāmbhiko rasavikrayī ||
dhanuḥśarāṇāṃ kartā ca yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ |
mitradhruk dyūtavṛttiś ca putrācāryas tathaiva ca ||
bhrāmarī gaṇḍamālī ca śvitry atho piśunas tathā |
unmatto ‘ndhaś ca varjyāḥ syur vedanindaka eva ca ||
hastigo’śvoṣṭradamako nakṣatrair yaś ca jīvati |
pakṣiṇāṃ poṣako yaś ca yuddhācāryas tathaiva ca ||
srotasāṃ bhedakaś caiva teṣāṃ cāvaraṇe rataḥ |
gṛhasaṃveśako dūto vṛkṣaropaka eva ca ||
śvakrīḍī śyenajīvi ca kanyādūṣaka eva ca |
hiṃsro vṛṣalavṛttiś ca gaṇānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ ||
ācārahīnaḥ klībaś ca nityaṃ yācanakas tathā |
kṛṣijīvī śilpajīvī sadbhir nindita eva ca ||
aurabhriko māhiṣikaḥ parapūrvāpatis tathā |
pretaniryātakaś caiva varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
etān vigarhitācārān apaṅkteyān dvijādhamān |
dvijātipravaro vidvān ubhayatrāpi varjayet || iti | (MDh 3.150–67)
steno ‘tra brahmasvavyatiriktadravyāpahārī vivakṣitaḥ, [352] taddravyāpahāriṇaḥ patitaśabdenopāttatvāt | pāralaukikaphaladaṃ karma nāstīti manyamānā nāstikāḥ | tebhyo vṛttir jīvikā yeṣāṃ te nāstikavṛttayaḥ | jaṭilo brahmacārī | “anadhīyānaḥ” iti jaṭilaviśeṣaṇam | ataś cānadhīyāno brahmacārī pratiṣidhyate | na tu brahmacārimātram, tasya śrāddhe “pañcāgnir brahmacāriṇaḥ” iti pātratvavidhānāt | na ca – adhyayanarahitasya brahmacāriṇo ‘śrotriyatvena śrāddhe prasaktyabhāvāt pratiṣedho ‘nupapannaḥ – iti mantavyam | yataḥ,
vratastham api dauhitraṃ śrāddhe yatnena bhojayet | (MDh 3.234)
ity atra dauhitragrahaṇam avivakṣitam iti bhrāntyā adhyayanarahito ‘pi brahmacārī śrāddhe bhojanīyatayā prasaktaḥ pratiṣidhyata iti | durvālaḥ khalvāṭaḥ kapilakeśo vā | tad uktaṃ saṃgrahakāreṇa |
khalvāṭakaś ca durvālaḥ kapilaś caṇḍa eva ca | iti |
kitavo dyūtāsaktaḥ | pūgayājakāḥ gaṇayājakāḥ | atra śrāddhe iti viśeṣopādānād durvālādīnāṃ śrāddha eva varjyatvaṃ na daiva ity avagamyate | anyathā prakaraṇād evobhayatra niṣedhāvagamād viśeṣopādānam anarthakaṃ syāt | ata eva gautamaḥ: “haviḥṣu caivaṃ durvālādīn śrādda evaike” iti (GDh 15.27) | haviḥṣu ca daive ‘pi | evaṃ pitryavat parīkṣya durvālādīn [353] varjayet | eke manvādayaḥ | śrāddha eva na bhojayet daive tu bhojayed ity abhiprāyaḥ | cikitsakāḥ jīvanārtham adṛṣṭārthaṃ ca bheṣajakāriṇaḥ,
tasmād brāhmaṇena bheṣajaṃ na kāryam |
apūto hy eṣo ‘medhyo yo bhiṣak |
iti aviśeṣeṇaiva nindārthavādadarśanāt | dhanārthaṃ saṃvatsaratrayaṃ devārcako devalakaḥ | tad uktaṃ devalena |
devārcanaparo nityaṃ vittārthī vatsaratrayam |
asau devalako nāma havyakavyeṣu garhitaḥ ||
apāṅkteyaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sarvakarmasu sarvadā | iti |
āpady api māṃsavikrayiṇaḥ | anāpadi vipaṇajīvitvenaiva niṣedhe siddhe “māṃsavikrayiṇaḥ” iti punar viśeṣopādānasya vaiyarthyāt | anāpadi vāṇijyena jīvanto vipaṇajīvinaḥ | na tv āpady api | tatra,
kṣātreṇa karmaṇā jīved viśāṃ vāpy āpadi dvijaḥ | (YDh 3.35)
iti vāṇijyasyāpatkalpatayā vihitatvāt | vihitatyāgakāraṇam vinā śrautasmārtāgniparityaktā parityaktāgniḥ | alapavṛddhyā dhanaṃ svīkṛtyādhikavṛddhyā dhanaprayojako vārdhuṣikaḥ | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
samarghaṃ dhanam uddhṛtya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
sa vai vārdhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ || (VaDh 2.41)
[354] iti | yakṣmī kṣayarogī | anāpadi paśupālaḥ | avivāhite jyeṣṭhe anāhitāgnau vā sati yaḥ kanīyān kṛtadāraparigraha āhitāgnir vā bhavet sa parivettā | tajjyeṣṭhas tu parivittiḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
dārāgnihotrsaṃyogaṃ kurute yo ‘graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.171)
agrajaḥ sodaryo vivakṣitaḥ | tathā ca gargaḥ |
sodarye tiṣṭhati jyeṣṭhe na kuryād dārasaṃgraham |
āvasathyaṃ tathādhānaṃ patitas tv anyathā bhavet || iti |
āvasathyam āvasathyādhānam | ādhānaṃ gārhapatyādyādhānam | asodarye tu na doṣaḥ | tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
pitṛvyaputrān sāpatnān paranārīsutāṃs tathā |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge na doṣaḥ parivedane || iti |
paranārīsutāḥ kṣetrajā bhrātaraḥ | yamo ‘pi |
pitṛvyaputrān sāpatnān paranārīsutāṃs tathā |
dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge ca doṣaḥ parivedane || iti |
paraputrā dattakrītādayaḥ bhrātaraḥ | sodaryaviṣaye ‘pi kvacid doṣo nāsti | tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
krībe deśāntarasthe ca patite bhikṣuke ‘pi vā |
yogaśāstrābhiyukte ca na doṣaḥ parivedane || iti |
bhikṣukaḥ pravrajitaḥ | yogaśāstrābhiyukto viraktaḥ | kātyāyano ‘pi |
[355]        deśāntarasthaklībaikavṛṣaṇān asahodarān |
veśyātisaktapatitaśūdratulyātirogiṇaḥ ||
jaṣamūkāndhabadhirakubjavāmanakhoḍakān |
ativṛddhān abhāryāṃś ca kṛṣisaktān nṛpasya ca ||
dhanavṛddhiprasaktāṃś ca kāmato ‘kāriṇas tathā |
kuhakonmattacorāṃś ca parivindan na duṣyati || iti |
khoḍo bhagnapādadvayaḥ | abhāryā naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇaḥ | kāmato ‘kāriṇaṇ svecchayaiva vivāhān nivṛttāḥ | deśāntaragatādiṣu kālapratīkṣām antareṇa parivedane doṣo ‘sti | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ: “aṣṭau daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaram aniviṣṭam apratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittī bhavati” iti | aniviṣṭam akṛtavivāham | atreyaṃ vyavsthā – adṛṣṭārtham arthārthaṃ vā dvādaśavarṣapratīkṣaṇaṃ deśāntaragatajyeṣṭhaviṣayam | aṣṭau daśeti pakṣadvayaṃ kāryāntarārthaṃ deśāntaragataviṣayam | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayor gataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ || iti |
klībādayas tu na pratīkṣaṇīyāḥ | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klība eva vā |
rājayakṣmy āmayāvī ca na nyāyyaḥ syāt pratīkṣitum ||
[356] iti | viraktaveśyātisaktādiṣu tu cirakānānuvṛttyā vivāhasaṃbhāvanānivṛttāv adhivedanaṃ na doṣāya | tatra kālāvadher aśrutatvāt | ādhānaviṣaye ‘pi jyeṣṭhānumatyādhivedane na doṣaḥ | tathā ca vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
agrajaś ca yadānagnir ādadhyād anujaḥ katham |
agrajānumataḥ kuryād agnihotraṃ yathāvidhi || iti |
ādhānādhikāriṇi jyeṣṭhe ‘nāhitāgnāv api kaniṣṭhas tadanumatyādhānaṃ kuryād ity abhiprāyaḥ | ayaṃ nyāyaḥ pitrādiṣu draṣṭavyaḥ | tathā ca uśanāḥ |
pitā pitāmaho yasya agrajo vātha kasyacit |
tapo’gnihotramantreṣu na doṣaḥ parivedane || iti |
yasya kasyacit pitā pitāmaho vāgrajo vāhitāgnir na bhavati tasya tadanymatyādhānakaraṇe ‘pi na doṣa ity arthaḥ | evam eva kanyāparivedane ‘pi doṣatadapavādau draṣṭavyau | adhītavismṛtavedo nirākṛtiḥ | tathā ca devalaḥ |
adhītya vismṛte vede bhaved dvijo nirākṛtiḥ | iti |
nānājātīyā aniyatavṛttayo gaṇās teṣāṃ madhyavartī gaṇābhyantaraḥ | kuśīlavo gāyakādiḥ | vṛṣalīpatis tu rajasvalāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ patiḥ | tad uktaṃ devalena |
vandhyā tu vṛṣalī jñeyā vṛṣalī ca mṛtaprajā |
aparā vṛṣalī jñeyā kumārī yā rajasvalā ||
[357]        yas tv enām udvahet kanyāṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
aśrāddheym apāṅkteyaṃ taṃ vidyād vṛṣalīpatim || iti |
yasya gṛhe upapatir jāraḥ sadā saṃvaset so ‘pi varjyaḥ | tad uktaṃ devalena |
paradārābhigo mohāt puruṣo jāra ucyate |
sa evopapatir jñeyo yaḥ sadā saṃvased gṛhe || iti |
vāgduṣṭo niṣṭhuravāk | patitair mahāpātakisaṃsargibhiḥ saha brāhmair yaunaiś ca saṃbandhair vidyāyonisaṃbandhair yaḥ saṃyogaṃ gataḥ so ‘tra vivakṣitaḥ | na tu sākṣāt saṃsargī | tasya patitaśabdenaivopāttatvāt | kekaro ‘dhyardhadṛṣṭiḥ | agredidhiṣvāḥ patir agredidhiṣūpatiḥ | jyeṣṭhāyām anūḍhāyām ūḍhā kaniṣṭhā yā sāgredidhiṣuḥ | tad uktaṃ devalena |
jyeṣṭhāyāṃ yady anūḍhāyāṃ kanyāyām uhyate ‘nujā |
sā cāgredidhiṣūr jñeyā pūrvā tu didhiṣūr matā || iti |
putrācāryo ‘kṣarapāṭhakaḥ | bhrāmarī vṛttyartham eva bhramaravad arthārjakaḥ | gṛhasaṃveśako vardhakivṛttyā vartamānaḥ | urabhrāḥ avayaḥ | ta eva vṛttyarthaṃ pālanīyā yasyāsāv aurabhrikaḥ | mahiṣyaḥ pālyā yasyāsau māhiṣakaḥ | athavā vyabhicāriṇīputraḥ | tad āha devalaḥ |
mahiṣīty ucyate bhāryā sā caiva vyabhicāriṇī |
tasyāṃ yo jāyate garbhaḥ sa vai māhiṣakaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
etān pūrvoktān ubhayatra daive pitrye ca varjayed ity arthaḥ | yamo ‘pi |
[358]        kāṇāḥ kubjāś ca ṣaṇḍhāś ca kṛtaghnā gurutalpagāḥ |
brahmaghnāś ca surāpāś ca stenā goghnāś cikitsakāḥ ||
rāṣṭrakāmās tathonmattāḥ paśuvikrayiṇaś ca ye |
mānakūṭās tulākūṭāḥ śilpino grāmayājakāḥ ||
rājabhṛtyāndhabadhirā mūkakhalvāṭapaṅgavaḥ |
vṛṣalīphenapītāś ca śreṇiyājakayājakāḥ ||
kālopajivinaś caiva brahmavikrayiṇas tathā |
daṇḍapūjāś ca ye viprā grāmakṛtyaparāś ca ye ||
agāradāhinaś caiva garadā vanadāhakāḥ |
kuṇḍāśino devalakāḥ paradārābhimarśakāḥ ||
śyāvadantāḥ kunakhinaḥ śilpinaḥ kuṣṭhinaś ca ye |
vaṇijo madhuhartāro hastyaśvadamakā dvijāḥ ||
kanyānāṃ dūṣakāś caiva brāhmaṇānāṃ ca dūṣakāḥ |
sūcakāḥ preṣyakāś caiva kitavāś ca kuśīlavāḥ ||
samayānāṃ ca bhettāraḥ pradāne ye ca bādhakāḥ |
ajāvikā māhiṣikāḥ sarvavikrayiṇaś ca ye ||
dhanuḥkartā dyūtavṛttir mitradhruk śastravikrayī |
pāṇḍurogī gaṇḍamālī yakṣmī ca bhrāmarī tathā ||
piśunaḥ kūṭasākṣī ca dīrgharogī vṛthāśramī |
pravrajyopanivṛttaś ca vṛthā pravrajitaś ca yaḥ ||
yaś ca pravrajitāj jātaḥ pravrajyāvasitaś ca yaḥ |
[359]        tāv ubhau brahmacaṇḍālāv āha vaivasvato yamaḥ ||
rājñaḥ preṣyakaro yaś ca grāmasya nagarasya vā |
samudrayāyī vāntāśī keśavikrayiṇaś ca ye ||
avakīrṇī ca vīraghno gurughnaḥ pitṛdūṣakaḥ |
govikrayī ca durvālaḥ pūgānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ ||
madyapaś ca kadaryaś ca saha pitrā vivādakṛt |
dāmbhiko vardhakībhartā tyaktātmā dāradūṣakaḥ ||
sadbhiś ca ninditācārḥ svakarmaparivarjakaḥ |
parivittiḥ parivettā bhṛtyācāryo nirākṛtiḥ ||
śūdrācāryaḥ sutācāryaḥ śūdraśiṣyas tu nāstikaḥ |
iṣvastradārakācāryo mānakṛt tailikas tathā ||
corā vārdhuṣikā duṣṭāḥ parasvānāṃ ca dūṣakāḥ |
caturāśramabāhyāś ca sarve te paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
ity etair lakṣaṇair yuktāṃs tān dvijān na niyojayet |
dvijātiguṇayoge ‘py eteṣāṃ ‘py eteṣāṃ varjanīyatvaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe ‘bhihitam |
śrāddhārhaguṇayoge ‘pi naite jātu kathaṃcana |
nimantraṇīyāḥ śrāddheṣu samyakphalam abhīpsatā || iti |
atha brāhmaṇanimantraṇam
evaṃ brāhmaṇān prāg eva samyak parīkṣya pūrvedyur nimantrayīta | tathā ca hārītaḥ: [360] “yatnenaivaṃvidhān śrāddham ācariṣyan pūrvedyur nimantrayet” iti (HārSm 13.29) | asaṃbhave paredyur nimantrayīta | tathā ca kaurme |
śvo bhaviṣyati me śrāddhaṃ pūrvedyur abhipūjayet |
asaṃbhave paredyur vā yathoktair lakṣaṇair yutān || iti | (KūPu 1.2.22.2)
devalo ‘pi |
śvaḥ kartāsmīti niścitya dātā viprān nimantrayet |
nirāmiṣaṃ sakṛd bhuktvā sarvabhuktajane gṛhe ||
asaṃbhave paredyur vā brāhmaṇāṃs tān nimantrayet | iti |
atra viśeṣo manunā darśitaḥ |
pūrvedyur aparedyur vā śrāddhakarmaṇy upasthite |
nimantrayīta tryavarān samyag viprān yathoditān || iti | (MDh 3.187)
varāhapurāṇe |
vastraśaucādi kartavyaṃ śvaḥ kartāsmīti jānatā |
sthānopalepanaṃ bhūmiṃ kṛtvā viprān nimantrayet ||
dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca visṛjet brahmacārī śucir bhavet |
[361] iti | śrāddhabhūmiṃ parigṛhya gomayādinā tatsthānopalepanaṃ kṛtvā viprān rātrau nimantrayed ity arthaḥ | tathā ca brahmāṇḍapurāṇam |
pūrve ‘hni rātrau viprāgryān kṛtasāyantanāśanān |
gatvā nimantrayed devapitruddeśasamanviataḥ || iti |
nimantraṇaprakāraḥ pracetasā darśitaḥ |
kṛtāpasavyaḥ pūrvedyuḥ pitṝn pūrvaṃ nimantrayet |
bhavadbhiḥ pitṛkāryaṃ naḥ saṃpādyaṃ ca prasīdata ||
savyena vaiśvadevārthaṃ praṇipatya nimantrayet | iti |
atra praṇatipūrvakaṃ nimantraṇaṃ śūdraviṣayam | tathā ca purāṇam |
dakṣiṇaṃ caraṇaṃ vipraḥ savyaṃ vai kṣatriyas tathā |
pādāv ādāya vaiśyau dvau śūdraḥ praṇatipūrvakam || iti |
dakṣiṇacaraṇasparśo jānupradeśe kartavyaḥ | tathā ca matsyaḥ |
dakṣiṇaṃ jānum ālabhya tvaṃ mayātra nimantritaḥ | iti | (MatPu 16.19)
“pūrvaṃ nimantrayet” ity atra pūrvapadasya vaiśvadevārthaṃ nimantrayed iti vyavahitenānvayaḥ | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
upavītī tato bhūtvā devatārthaṃ dvijottamān |
apasavyena pitrye ca svayaṃ śiṣyo ‘tha vā sutaḥ || iti |
[362]
atha brāhmaṇasaṃkhyā
pārvaṇaśrāddhe brāhmaṇasaṃkhyām āha paiṭhīnasiḥ: “brāhmaṇān sapta pañca dvau vā śrotriyān āmantrayet” iti | yadā pañca brāhmaṇāḥ tadā daive dvau pitrye traya iti vibhāgaḥ, “dvau daive pitṛkārye trīn” (MDh 3.125) iti manusmaraṇāt | yadā sapta tadā daive catvāraḥ | pitrye trayaḥ, “ayujo bhojayec chrāddhe na samā daivike samāḥ” iti aṅgirasasmaraṇāt |
nanv etad vacanaṃ daive dvau pitrye pañca iti vibhāge ‘py uppadyate, iti cet |
maivam, triṣu pañcānām ayugmasaṅkhyayā vibhāgānupapatteḥ | na ca – ekatra traya itaratraikaika iti vibhāga upapadyata – iti vācyam, “samaṃ syād aśrutatvāt” (PMS 10.3.53) iti nyāyena viṣamavibhāgasyānyāyyatvāt | tasmād ayugmasaṃkhyayā [363] samavibhāgārthaṃ pitrye traya iti yuktam | yat tu śaunakena pitrye ‘pi yugmavidhānam kṛtam, “ekaikasya dvau dvau” iti, tad vṛddhiśrāddhaviṣayam | pitrādisthāneṣu sati sāmarthye ekaikasya trīṃs trīn viprān bhojayet | tathā ca śaunakaḥ, “ekaikam ekaikasya trīṃs trīn vā” iti | atyantavibhave saty ekaikasya pañca sapta vā brāhmaṇān bhojayet | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “navāvarān bhojayed ayujo vā yathotsāham” iti (GDh 15.7) | asyārthaḥ – yathotsāhaṃ yathāvibhavaṃ pitrādisthāneṣu pratyekam ayujaḥ pañca sapta vā brāhmaṇān bhojayed iti |
nanu – ayujo bhojayed iti vacanasya kathaṃ pañcasu saptasu vā brāhmaṇeṣu paryavasānam|
sāmarthye ‘pi navabhyo ‘rvāg bhojayīta sati dvijān |
nordhvaṃ kartavyam ity āhuḥ kecit taddoṣadarśinaḥ ||
iti brāhmaṇādapurāṇavacanād iti brūmaḥ | śaunaka-gautamābhyām ukto ‘yaṃ śrāddhavistaro manunā nādṛtaḥ |
dvau daive pitṛkārye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ‘pi na prasajjeta vistare ||
[364]        satkriyāṃ deśakālau ca śaucaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃpadam |
pañcaitān vistaro hanti tasmān neheta vistaram || (MDh 3.125–26)
iti sakāraṇam eva vistarapratiṣedhāt | ata eva vṛddhabṛhaspatir api |
ekaikam atha vā dvau trīn daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
satkriyākālapātrādi na saṃpadyeta vistare || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
dvau daive pitṛkārye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ‘pi vistaraṃ tu vivarjayet || iti | (VaDh 11.27)
ata eva yājñavalkyenāpi saṅkocapakṣa eva vihitaḥ |
dvau daive prāk trayaḥ pitrye udag ekaikam eva vā |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ tantraṃ vā vaiśvadevikam || iti | (YDh 1.228)
ekaikam ubhayatra veti brāhmaṇādyasaṃbhave veditavyam | yat tu śaṅkhenoktam,
bhojayed atha vāpy ekaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ paṅktipāvanam |
iti, tad apy alābhaviṣayam | yadā tv eka eva bhoktā tadaivam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
[365]        yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe daivaṃ tatra kathaṃ bhavet |
annaṃ pātre samuddhṛtya sarvasya prakṛtasya tu ||
devatāyatane kṛtvā yathāvidhivad arcayet |
prāsyed agnau tad annaṃ tu dadyād vā brahmacāriṇe || iti | (VaDh 11.30–31)
nimantraṇe niyamāntaram āha matsyaḥ |
evaṃ nimantrya niyamān śrāvayet paitṛkān budhaḥ |
akrodhanaiḥ śaucaparaiḥ satataṃ brahmacāribhiḥ |
bhavitavyaṃ bhavadbhiś ca mayā ca śrāddhakāriṇā || iti | (MatPu 16.1–20)
atha nimantritabrāhmaṇakartavyam
nimantritair kartavyaṃ tad āha atriḥ |
te taṃ tathety avighnena gateyaṃ rajanī yadi |
yathāśrutaṃ pratīkṣeran śrāddhakālam atandritāḥ || iti |
te nimantritā viprās taṃ śrāddhakartāram avighnapūrvakaṃ tathāstv ity uktvā yathāśrutaṃ vihitaṃ niyamajātaṃ śrāddhakālaṃ śrāddhe bhuktaṃ yāvat jīryati tāvad anutiṣṭherann ity arthaḥ | tathā ca pracetāḥ |
ā cānnapariṇāmāt tu brahmacaryaṃ dvayoḥ smṛtam | iti |
yamo ‘pi |
[366]        āmantritās tu viprāḥ śrāddhakāla upasthite |
vaseyur niyatāhārā brahmacaryaparāyaṇāḥ ||
ahiṃsā satyam akrodho dūre ca gamanakriyā |
abhārodvahanaṃ ceti śrāddhasyopāsanāvidhiḥ || iti |
tathāstv ity aṅgīkāraḥ sati sāmarthye aninditāmantraṇaviṣayaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ: “anindyenāmantrite nātikrāmet” iti | śaktena na pratyākhyānaṃ kartavyam iti | yas tv āmantraṇam aṅgīkṛtya saty api sāmarthye paścān nivārayati tasya doṣo ‘sti | tathā ca manuḥ |
ketitas tu yathānyāyaṃ havyakavye dvijottamaḥ |
kathaṃcid apy atikrāman pāpaḥ sūkaratāṃ vrajet || iti | (MDh 3.190)
ketito nimantritaḥ | yamo ‘pi |
āmantritaś ca yo vipro bhoktum anyatra gacchati |
narakāṇāṃ śataṃ gatvā caṇḍāleṣv abhijāyate || iti |
nimantritabrāhmaṇaparityāge pratyavāyo ‘sti | tathā ca nārāyaṇaḥ |
niketanaṃ kārayitvā nivārayati durmatiḥ |
brahmahatyām avāpnoti śūdrayonau ca jāyate || iti |
yas ev āmantrito vipraś cāhūto ‘pi śrāddhakālātikramaṃ karoti tasya pratyavāya ādipurāṇe ‘bhihitaḥ |
[367]        āmantritaś ciraṃ naiva kuryād vipraḥ kadācana |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca dātur annasya caiva hi ||
cirakārī bhaved drohī pacyate narakāgninā | iti |
dātṛbhoktror brahmacaryaniyamātikrame pratyavāyas tu tatra tatroktaḥ | tatra vṛddhamanuḥ |
ṛtukālaṃ niyukto vā naiva gacchet striyaṃ kvacit |
tatra gacchan samāpnoti hy aniṣṭaphalam eva tu || iti |
gautamaḥ: “sadyaḥśrāddhī śūdrātalpagatas tatpurīṣe māsaṃ nayati pitṝn” iti (GDh 15.18) | śrāddhī śrāddhakartā | sadyas tatkṣaṇam ārabhyety arthaḥ | manuḥ |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe vṛṣalyā saha modate |
dātur yad duṣkṛtaṃ kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ pratipadyate || iti | (MDh 3.191)
yamaḥ |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe adhvānaṃ pratipadyate |
bhavanti pitaras tasya tan māsaṃ pāṃśubhojanāḥ || iti |
atha śrāddhadinakṛtyam
śrāddhe dinakṛtyaṃ pracetasā darśitam |
