[368]        śrāddhabhuk prātar utthāya prakuryād dantadhāvanam |
śrāddhakartā na kurvīta dantānāṃ dhāvanaṃ budhaḥ || iti |
devalo ‘pi |
tathaiva yantrito dātā prātaḥ snātvā sahāmbaraḥ |
ārabheta navaiḥ pātrair annārambhaṃ svabāndhavaiḥ ||
tilān avakiret tatra sarvato bandhayed ajān |
asurāpahataṃ sarvaṃ tilaiḥ śudhyaty ajena ca ||
tato ‘nnaṃ bahusaṃskāraṃ naikavyañjanabhakṣyavat |
coṣyapeyasamṛddhaṃ ca yathāśakti prakalpayet || iti |
atha śrāddhārhadravyāṇi
atra dravyāṇi precetā āha |
kṛṣṇamāṣās tilāś caiva śreṣṭhāḥ syur yavaśālayaḥ |
mahāyavā vrīhiyavās tathaiva ca madhūlikāḥ ||
kṛṣṇāḥ śvetāś ca lohāś ca grāhyāḥ syuḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi | iti |
yavāḥ sitaśūkāḥ | śālayaḥ kalamādyāḥ | mahāyavā vrīhiyavāś ca yavaviśeṣāḥ | madhūlikā dhānyaviśeṣaḥ | kṛṣṇāḥ sthalajāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇavrīhayaḥ | lohā raktaśālayaḥ | mārkaṇḍeyo ‘pi |
[369]        yavavrīhisagodhūmatilā mudgāḥ sasarṣapāḥ |
priyaṅgavaḥ kovidārā niṣpāvāś cātra śobhanāḥ || iti | (MārPu 32.10)
atra godhūmānām āvaśyakatvam atriṇoktam |
agodhūmaṃ ca yac chrāddhaṃ kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ bhavet | iti |
vāyupurāṇe ‘pi |
bilvāmalakamṛdvīkāpanasāmrātadāḍimam |
cavyaṃ pālevatākṣoṭakharjūrāṇāṃ phalāni ca ||
kaśeru kovidāraś ca tālakandas tathā bisam |
kāleyaṃ kālaśākaṃ ca suniṣaṇṇaṃ suvarcalā ||
kaṭphalaṃ kauṅkaṇī drākṣā lakucaṃ mocam eva ca |
karkandhūgrīvakaṃ cāru tindukaṃ madhusāhvayam ||
vaikaṅkataṃ nālikeraṃ śṛṅgāṭakaparūṣakam |
pippalī maricaṃ caiva paṭolaṃ bṛhatīphalam ||
sugandhimatsyamāṃsaṃ ca kalāyāḥ sarva eva ca |
evamādīni cānyāni svādūni madhurāṇi ca ||
nāgaraṃ cātra vai deyaṃ dīrghamūlakam eva ca | iti |
mṛdvīkā drākṣā | āmrātakaḥ kapītanaḥ | cavyaṃ cavikā | akṣoṭaḥ śailodbhavaḥ pīluḥ | kaśeru bhadramustā | kāleyakaṃ dāruharidrā | suniṣaṇṇaṃ vitunnaśākam | kaṭphalaṃ śrīparṇikā |
[370] lakuco likucaḥ | mocaṃ kadalīphalam | karkaṃdhūr badarī | tindukaḥ śitisārakaḥ | śṛṅgāṭakaṃ jalajaṃ trikaṇṭakam | bṛhatīphalaṃ nidigdhikāphalam | dīrghamūlakaṃ tuṇḍikerīphalam | bilvāmalakādīni prasiddhāni | pālevatagrīvakaparūṣakādīny aprasiddhāni | śaṅkho ‘pi |
āmān pālevatān ikṣūn mṛdvīkāṃ cavyadāḍimam |
vidāryāś ca bhacuṇḍāṃś ca śrāddhakāle ‘pi dāpayet ||
drākṣāṃ madhuyutāṃ dadyāt saktūn śarkarayā saha |
dadyāc chrāddhe prayatnena śṛṅgāṭakakaśerukān || iti | (ŚaṅSm 13.21–22)
ādityapurāṇe ‘pi |
madhukaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ caiva karpūraṃ maricaṃ guḍam |
śrāddhakarmaṇi śastāni saindhavaṃ trapuṣaṃ tathā || iti |
atra viśeṣo mārkaṇḍeyena darśitaḥ |
godhūmair ikṣubhir mudraiḥ kṣīṇakaiś caṇakair api |
śrāddheṣu dattaiḥ prīyante māsam ekaṃ pitāmahāḥ ||
vidāryāś ca bhacuṇḍaiś ca bisaiḥ śṛṅgāṭakais tathā |
kecukaiś ca tathā kandaiḥ karkandhubadarair api ||
pālevatair ātukaś cāpy akṣoṭaiḥ panasais tathā |
kākolyāḥ kṣīrakākolyās tathā piṇḍālakaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
[371]         lājābhiś ca śalābhiś ca trapuṣor vārucirbhaṭaiḥ |
sarṣapārājaśākābhyām iṅgudair ājajambubhiḥ ||
priyālāmalakair mukhyaiḥ pīlubhiś cātilambakaiḥ |
vetrāṅkurais tālakandaiś cakrikākṣīrikāvacaiḥ ||
mocaiḥ samocair lakucais tathā vai bījapūrakaiḥ |
muñjātakaiḥ padmaphalair bhakṣyabojyaiḥ susaṃskṛtaiḥ ||
rāgakhāḍavacoṣyaiś ca trijātakasamanvitaiḥ |
dattais tu māsaṃ prīyante śrāddheṣu pitaro nṝṇām || iti |
vidārī kṛṣṇavarṇabhūkūṣmāṇḍaphalam | kecukaḥ kacūrākhyaśākam | kandaḥ sūraṇaḥ | urvāruḥ svādukarkaṭī | cirbhaṭas tiktakarkaṭī | sarṣapeti dīrghaḥ chāndasaḥ | rājaśākaṃ kṛṣṇasarṣapaḥ | iṅgudaḥ tāpasataruḥ | priyālo rājādanam | cakrikā ciñcā | kṣīrikā phalādhyakṣam | rāgakhāḍavāḥ pānaviśeṣāḥ | trijātakaṃ lavaṅgailāgandhapatrāṇi | matsyapurāṇe ‘pi |
annaṃ tu sadadhikṣīraṃ goghṛtaṃ śarkarānvitam |
māsaṃ prīṇāti sarvān vai pitṝn ity āha keśavaḥ || iti | (MatPu 17.30)
manur api |
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ prīyante vidhivat pitaro nṛṇām ||
[372]        dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākuneneha pañca vai ||
ṣaṇmāsāṃś chāgamāṃsena pārṣateneha sapta vai |
aṣṭāv eṇasya māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu ||
daśa māsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ |
śaśakūrmayos tu māṃsena māsān ekādaśaiva tu ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena vā |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkaṃ khaḍgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.267–72)
vārdhrīṇaso raktavarṇavṛddhachāgaḥ | tad uktaṃ viṣṇudharmottare |
triḥpibaṃ tv indriyakṣīṇaṃ yūthasyāgrasaraṃ tathā |
raktavarṇaṃ tu rājendra chāgaṃ vārdhrīṇasaṃ viduḥ | iti | (ViDhPu 1.41.149)
pakṣiviśeṣo vā,
kṛṣṇagrīvao raktaśirāḥ śvetapakṣo vihaṅgamaḥ |
sa vai vārdhrīṇasaḥ proktaḥ ity eṣā vaidikī śrutiḥ |
iti nigamavacanāt | kālaśākam uttaradeśe prasiddham | mahāśalko [373] matsyaviśeṣaḥ | khaḍgaḥ khaḍgamṛgaḥ | loho lohitavarṇachāgaḥ | munyannāni nīvārādyannāni | śrāddhe kodravādidhānyāni varjayet | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
aśrāddheyāni dhānyāni kodravāḥ pulakās tathā |
hiṅgudravyeṣu śākeṣu kālānalaśubhās tathā || iti |
kodravāḥ koradūṣakāḥ | pulakāḥ pulākāḥ | chāndaso ‘tra hrasvaḥ | saṃskārakadravyeṣu hiṅgudravyam aśrāddheyam | kālaḥ kṛṣṇajīrakaḥ | analaś citrakaḥ | śubhā śubhākhyāḥ śākaviśeṣaḥ | etāni śākāny aśrāddheyāni |
nanu,
madhūkaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ caiva karpūraṃ maricaṃ guḍam |
iti ādipurāṇe hiṅgudravyasya śrāddheyatvam uktaṃ tat kathaṃ tasyāśrāddheyatvam ucyate – iti cet |
satyam, “atirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti | nātirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇāti” itivad, atrāpi vidhipratiṣedhadarśanād vikalpo ‘stu | evam evānyatrāpi | bhāradvājo ‘pi: “mudgāḍhakīmāṣavarjaṃ dvidalāni dadyāt” iti | mudgaḥ kṛṣṇetaraḥ | āḍhakī tuvarī | māṣo rājamāṣaḥ | [374] etair vinā dvidalāni dadyād ity arthaḥ | māṣagrahaṇaṃ kulitthādīnām upalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva caturviṃśatimatam |
kodravān rājamāṣāṃś ca kulitthān varakāṃs tathā |
niṣpāvāṃs viśeṣeṇa pañcaitāṃs tu vivarjayet ||
yānvālān api tathā varjayanti vipaścitaḥ || iti |
varakāḥ vanamudgāḥ | anyat prasiddham | atra niṣpāvaniṣedhaḥ kṛṣṇaniṣpāvaviṣayaḥ,
kṛṣṇadhānyāni sarvāṇi varjayet śrāddhakarmaṇi |
iti smaraṇāt | “niṣpāvāś cātra śobhanāḥ” (MārPu 32.10) iti mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇaṃ kṛṣṇetaraniṣpāvaviṣayatayā vyavasthāpitaṃ bhavati | marīcir api |
kulitthāś caṇakāḥ śrāddhe na deyāś caiva kodravāḥ |
kaṭukāni ca sarvāṇi virasāni tathaiva ca || iti |
viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
śrāddhe na deyā pālaṅkyā tathā niṣpāvakodravāḥ |
masūrakṣāravāstokakulitthaśaṇaśigravaḥ || iti |
pālaṅkyā mukundaḥ | masuro maṅgalyakaḥ | kṣāro yavakṣārādiḥ | viṣṇur api: [375] “bhṛtṛṇaśigrusarṣapasurasārjakakūṣmāṇḍālābuvārttākapālaṅkyātaṇḍulīyakakusumbha-mahiṣīkṣīrādi varjayet” iti (ViDh 79.17) | bhūtṛṇo bhūstṛṇaḥ | chāndasatvāt suḍabhāvaḥ | sarṣapo ‘tra rājasarṣapaḥ, “kusumbhaṃ rājasarṣapam” iti smṛtyantare viśeṣitatvāt | surasā nirguṇḍī | arjakaḥ śvetārjakaḥ | uśanā api |
nālikāśaṇachatrākakusumbhālābuviḍbhavān |
kumbhīkambukavṛntākakovidārāṃś ca varjayet ||
varjayed gṛñjanaṃ śrāddhe kāñcikaṃ pīṇḍamūlakam |
karañjaṃ ye ‘pi cānye vai rasagandhotkaṭās tathā || iti
nālikā dīrghanālāgragatālpapallavā | chatrākaṃ śilīndhraḥ | kumbhī śrīparṇikā | kambukaṃ vṛttālābu | gṛñjano haridraktavarṇaḥ palāṇḍuviśeṣaḥ | kāñjikam āranālakam | karañjaṃ ciribilvaphalam | purāṇe ‘pi |
[376]        vāṃśaṃ karīraṃ surasaṃ sarjakaṃ bhūtṛṇāni ca ||
avedoktāś ca niryāsā lavaṇāny auṣarāṇi ca |
śrāddhakarmaṇi varjyāni yāś ca nāryo rajasvalāḥ || iti | (VāyPu 2.16.11,15)
vāṃśaṃ karīraṃ vaṃśāṅkuraḥ | sarjakaḥ pītasārakaḥ | avedoktā vedaniṣiddhā niryāsāḥ vraścanaprabhavādayaḥ | auṣarāṇi lavaṇāni kṛtalavaṇāni | rajasvalāḥ dinatrayād ūrdhvam anivṛttarajasaḥ | bharadvājo ‘pi |
naktoddhṛtaṃ tu yat toyaṃ palvalāmbu tathaiva ca |
svalpāmbu kūṣmāṇḍaphalaṃ vajrakandaṃ ca pippalī ||
taṇḍulīyakaśākaṃ ca māhiṣaṃ ca payo dadhi |
śimbikāni karīrāṇi kovidāragavedhukam ||
kulitthaśaṇajambīrakarambhāṇi tathaiva ca |
abjād anyad raktapuṣpaṃ śigruḥ kṣāraṃ tathaiva ca ||
nīraśāny api sarvāṇi bhakṣyabhojyāni yāni ca |
etāni naiva deyāni sarvasmin śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
āvikaṃ mārgam auṣṭraṃ ca sarvam aikaśaphaṃ ca yat |
māhiṣaṃ cāmaraṃ caiva payo varjyaṃ vijānatā || iti |
āvikam avīnāṃ payaḥ | mārgaṃ mṛgīṇāṃ payaḥ | auṣṭram uṣṭrīṇāṃ payaḥ | aikaśaphaṃ vaḍavāpayaḥ | māhiṣaṃ mahiṣīpayaḥ | cāmaraṃ camarīpayaḥ | brahmāṇḍapurāṇe ‘pi |
[377]        āsanārūḍham annādyaṃ pādopahatam eva ca |
amedhyād āgataiḥ spṛṣṭaṃ śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ ca yat ||
dviḥsvinnaṃ paridagdhaṃ ca tathaivāgāv alehitam |
śarkarākīṭapāṣāṇaiḥ keśair yac cāpy upadrutam ||
piṇyākaṃ mathitaṃ caiva tathātilavaṇaṃ ca yat |
dadhi śākaṃ tathā bhakṣyam uṣṇaṃ coṣavivarjitam ||
varjayec ca tathā cānyān sarvān abhimatān api |
siddhāḥ kṛtāś ca ye bhakṣāḥ pratyakṣalavaṇīkṛtāḥ ||
vāgbhāvaduṣṭāś ca tathā duṣṭaiś copahatās tathā |
vāsasā copadhūtāni varjyāni śrāddhakarmaṇi || iti |
dviḥsvinnaṃ dviḥpakvam | paridagdham atidagdham | agnāv alehitaṃ pūrvam evānyenāsvāditam | mathitaṃ viloḍitaṃ nirjalaṃ dadhi | siddhā bhakṣā āmalakādayaḥ | pratyakṣalavaṇena miśritāḥ | śaṅkho ‘pi |
kṛṣṇājājīṃ viḍaṃ caiva śītapākīṃ tathaiva ca |
varjayel lavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ tathā jambūphalāni ca ||
avakṣutāvaruditaṃ tathā śrāddheṣu varjayet || (ŚaṃSm 13.18.19)
[378] iti | kṛṣṇājājī kṛṣṇajīrakaḥ | viḍaṃ viḍālākhyam | lavaṇaṃ kṛtalavaṇam | śrāddhe kūṣmāṇḍādiniṣiddhadravyopādāne pratyavāyo ‘sti | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kūṣmāṇḍaṃ mahiṣīkṣīraṃ āḍhakyo rājasarṣapāḥ |
caṇakā rājamāṣāś ca ghnanti śrāddham asaṃśayam ||
piṇḍālukaṃ ca tuṇḍīraṃ karamardāṃś nālikām |
kūṣmāṇḍaṃ bahubījāni śrāddhe datvā prayāty adhaḥ || iti |
karamardaḥ suṣeṇaḥ | bahubījāni bījapūrādīni | nityabhojane pratiṣiddham api śrāddhe na deyam | ata evoktaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
kṣīrtādi (?) māhiṣaṃ varjyam abhakṣyaṃ yac ca kīrtitam | iti |
nityabhojane varjyāni śākāni paiṭhīnasinoktāni: “vṛntākanālikāpautakusuṃbhāśmantakāś ceti śākānām abhakṣyāś ca” iti | pautaṃ pautikā | vṛntākaniṣedhas tu śvetavṛntākaviṣayaḥ | ata eva devalaḥ |
kaṇḍūraṃ śvetavṛntākaṃ kumbhāṇḍaṃ ca vivarjayet | iti |
kaṇḍūrā prāvṛṣāyaṇī tasyāṅ phalaṃ kaṇḍūram | kumbhabudhnavad vartulaṃ vṛttālābusadṛśaṃ kumbhāṇḍam | bhaviṣatpurāṇe ‘pi |
laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ caiva palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca |
vṛntākanālikālābuṃ jānīyāj jātidūṣitam ||
[379] iti | laśunaṃ śvetakandaḥ palāṇḍuviśeṣaḥ,
laśunaṃ dīrghapatraś ca picchagandho mahauṣadham |
karaṇyaś ca palāṇḍuś ca latārkaś ca parārikā ||
gṛñjanaṃ yavaneṣṭaś ca palāṇḍor daś jātayaḥ || iti | SuśruSaṃ 21.60–61)
iti suśrutenoktatvāt | kavakaṃ chatrākam | hārīto ‘pi: “na vaṭaplakṣodumburadadhitthanīpa-mātuluṅgāni vā bhakṣayet” iti (HārSm 13.21) | manur api |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā |
śeluṃ gavyaṃ ca pīyūṣaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || iti | (MDh 5.6)
lohitā vṛkṣaniryāsā lākṣādayaḥ | lohitagrahaṇāt niryāsatve ‘pi pāṭalaśvetavarṇahiṅgukarpūrāder apratiṣedhaḥ | śeluḥ śleṣmātakaḥ | pīyuṣo ‘bhinavaṃ payaḥ | brahmapurāṇe ‘pi |
ghṛtāt phenaṃ ghṛtān maṇḍaṃ pīyūṣam atha cārdragoḥ |
saguḍaṃ maricāktaṃ tu tathā paryuṣitaṃ dadhi ||
dīrṇaṃ takram apeyaṃ ca naṣṭasvādaṃ ca phenavat || iti |
ghṛtād uddhṛtya tat phenamātraṃ na peyam | ghṛtād uddhṛtya maṇḍaṃ tadagraṃ ca na peyam | ārdragoḥ prasavaprabhṛtyanivṛttarajaskāyā [380] goḥ pīyūṣaṃ na peyam | guḍamaricopagataṃ paryuṣitaṃ dadhi ca | dīrṇaṃ sphuṭitaṃ takraṃ dīrghakālasthityā naṣṭasvādaṃ ca phenavac ca na peyam | yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
saṃdhinyanidaśāvatsāgopayaḥ parivrjayet |
auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ straiṇam āraṇyakam athāvikam || iti | (YDh 1.170)
yā vṛṣeṇa saṃdhīyate sā saṃdhinī | anirdaśā anirgatadaśarātrā | avatsā vatsarahitā | etāsāṃ gavāṃ payaḥ parivarjayet | āraṇyakapayoniṣedhaś cāraṇyakamahiṣīvyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | tad āha manuḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ tathā |
āvikaṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca goḥ payaḥ ||
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ mahiṣīr vinā || iti | (MDh 5.8–9)
vasiṣṭho ‘pi: “gomahiṣyajānām anirdaśāhānāṃ payo na peyam” iti (VaDh 14.35) | gautamo ‘pi: “syandinīyamasūsaṃdhinīnāṃ ca” (GDh 17.23) [381] iti | kṣīraṃ na peyam iti śeṣaḥ | syandinī svata eva sravatpayaḥstanī | yamasūr yamalaprasūḥ | baudhāyano ‘pi: “kṣīram apeyam vivatsāyā anyavatsāyāś ca” iti (BDh 1.5.12.9–10) | āpastambo ‘pi |
kṣatriyaś caiva vṛttastho vaiśyaḥ śūdro ‘tha vā punaḥ |
yat pibet kāpilaṃ kṣīraṃ na tato ‘nyo ‘sty apuṇyakṛt || iti |
jātyā viśuddham api keśakīṭādisaṃsargaduṣṭamātraṃ saṃvarjayet | tathā ca devalaḥ |
viśuddham api cāhāraṃ makṣikākṛmijantubhiḥ |
keśaromanakhair vāpi dūṣitaṃ parivarjayet || iti |
atra makṣikākṛmijantavo mṛtā vivakṣitāḥ | etaiḥ keśaromādibhiś ca dūṣitaṃ sati saṃbhave varjayet | asaṃbhave tu keśādikam uddhṛtya saṃprokṣya hiraṇyasparśaṃ kṛtvā bhūñjīta | tathā ca sumantuḥ: “keśakīṭakṣutavacopahataṃ śvabhir āghrātaṃ lehitaṃ vā adadhi paryuṣitaṃ punaḥ siddhaṃ caṇḍānādyavekṣitam abhojyam | anyatra hiraṇyodakaiḥ spṛṣṭvā” iti | kṣtavacaḥ kṣutavāgjāto dhvaniḥ | āpady api śvādibhir avalīḍhaṃ na bhuñjīta | tathā ca devalaḥ |
[382]        avalīḍhaṃ śvamārjāradhvāṃśakukkuṭamūṣakaiḥ |
bhojane nopayuñjīta tad amedhyaṃ hi sarvataḥ || iti |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe ‘pi |
surālaśunasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ pīyūṣādisamanvitam |
saṃsargād duṣyate tad dhi śūdrocchiṣṭavad ācaret || iti |
atrādiśabdena chatrākādiduṣṭadravyaṃ parigṛhyate | smṛtyantare varjyāntaram uktam: “nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyāt | na dviḥpakvam | na paryuṣitam” iti (ĀpDh 1.5.17.14–16) |
ghṛtaṃ vā yadi vā tailaṃ vipro nādyāt nakhacyutam |
yamas tad aśuci prāha tulyaṃ gomāṃsabhakṣaṇaiḥ ||
hastadattāś ca ye snehā lavaṇavyañjanāni ca |
dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhanti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam || (VaDh 14.30–31)
ekena pāṇinā dattaṃ śūdradattaṃ na bhakṣayet | iti |
āpaṇasthānnapratiṣedho ‘trāpūpādivyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | [383] tad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
apupāḥ saktavo dhānās takraṃ dahi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
etat paṇyeṣu bhoktavyaṃ bhāṇḍalepi na ced bhavet || iti |
paryuṣitaniṣedho ‘pi vaṭakādivyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | tad āha yamaḥ |
apūpāś ca karambhāś ca dhānā vaṭakasaktavaḥ |
śākaṃ māṃdam apūpaṃ ca sūpaṃ kṛsaram eva ca ||
yavāgūḥ pāyasaṃ caiva yac cānyat snehasaṃyutam |
sarvaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ śuktaṃ cet parivarjayet || iti |
devalo ‘pi |
abhojyaṃ prāhur āhāraṃ śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ ca yat |
apūpā yavagodhūmavikārā vaṭakādayaḥ || iti |
vaṭakā māṣādipiṣṭamayāḥ prasiddhāḥ | punar apūpagrahaṇaṃ vrīhyādipiṣṭavikāropādānārtham | kṛsaraṃ ghṛṣṭatilacūrṇasaṃyutam odanam | anyad odanādikaṃ snehasaṃyuktaṃ ghṛtena dadhnā vābhighāritam | etat sarvaṃ paryuṣitam aśuktaṃ bojyam | śuktasvarūpaṃ bṛhaspatinoktam |
atyamlaṃ śuktam ākhyātṃ ninditaṃ brahmavādibhiḥ | iti |
anamlam īṣadamlaṃ vā yad vastu kālāntareṇa vā [384] dravyāntarasaṃsargeṇa vātyamlaṃ bhavati tac chuktam | na tu svabhāvato ‘tyamlam | yad agnipakvaṃ sadrātryantaritaṃ tat paryuṣitam | śuktapratiṣedho dadhyādivyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | tad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ ca śukteṣu sarvaṃ ca dadhisaṃbhavam |
ṛjīṣapakvaṃ bhakṣyaṃ syāt sarpiryuktam iti sthitiḥ || iti |
anagnika ūṣmā ṛjīṣaṃ tena pakvam | śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ vāpadi prakṣālitaṃ bhojyam | tad āha yamaḥ |
śuktāni hi dvijo ‘nnāni na bhuñjīta kadācana |
prakṣālitāni nirdoṣāṇy āpaddharmo yathā bhavet ||
masūramāṣasaṃyuktaṃ tathā paryuṣitaṃ ca yat |
tat tu pakṣālitaṃ kṛtvā bhuñjīta hy abhidhāritam || iti |
āśrayaduṣṭam api na bhuñjīta | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
kadaryabaddhacorāṇāṃ klībaraṅgāvatāriṇām |
vaiṇābhiśastavārdhuṣygaṇikāgaṇadīkṣiṇām ||
cikitsakāturakruddhpuṃścalīmattavidviṣām |
krūrograpatitavrātyadāṃbhikocchiṣṭabhojinām ||
avīrāstrīsvarṇakārastrījitagrāmayājinām |
śastravikrayikarmāratantuvāyaśvavṛttinām ||
nṛśaṃsarājarajakakṛtaghnavadhajīvinām |
cailadhāvasurājīvasahopapativeśmanām || (YDh 1.161–65)
[385] iti | kadaryo lubdhakaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
ātmānaṃ dharmakṛtyaṃ ca putradārāṃś ca pīḍayet |
lobhād yaḥ pitarau bhṛtyān sa kadarya ity smṛtaḥ || iti |
baddhaḥ śṛṅkhalitaḥ | raṅgāvatārī naṭaḥ | vaiṇo vīṇāvādanopajīvī | dīkṣī dīkṣāsaṃskāravān | tasya cābhojyānnatvam agnīṣomīyapaśuvapāhomaparyantam, “asaṃsthite ‘gnīṣomīye yajamānasya gṛha nāśitavyam” iti śruteḥ | āturaḥ pāparogagrastaḥ | kruddho dṛḍhatarāntarakopaḥ | matto dhanādinā garvitaḥ | krūro niṣkṛpaḥ | ugraḥ paraduḥkhakārī | rajako vastrarāgakārī | cailadhāvo vastraprakṣālakaḥ | eṣāṃ kadaryādisomavikrayipartyantānāṃ traivarṇikānām annaṃ na bhoktavyam ity arthaḥ | yamo ‘pi |
cakropajīvī gāndharvaḥ kitavas taskaras tathā |
dhvajī dāropajīvī ca śūdrādhyāpakayājakau ||
kulālaś citrakarmā ca vārdhuṣī carmavikrayī || iti |
cakropajīvī śakaṭoajīvī | gāndharvo gāyakaḥ | dhvajī madyavikrayī | itare prasiddhāḥ | ete abhojyānnā ity arthaḥ | [386] āpastambo ‘pi |
dvāv evāśramiṇau bhojyau brahmacārī gṛhī tathā |
muner annam abhojyaṃ syāt sarveṣāṃ liṅgināṃ tathā || iti |
muniśabdena yativānaprasthau gṛhyete | liṅginaḥ pāśupatādayaḥ | aṅgirā api |
ṣaṇmāsān yo dvijo bhuṅkte śūdrasyānnaṃ vigarhitam |
sa tu jīvan bhavec chūdro mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate || iti | (AṅSm 48)
yat tu sumantunoktam,
gorasaṃ caiva saktūṃś ca tailaṃ piṇyākam eva ca |
apūpān bhakṣayec chūrād yac cānyat payasā kṛtam ||
iti, yac ca viṣṇupurāṇe ‘bhihitam |
saṃprokṣya vipro gṛhṇīyāc chūdrānnaṃ gṛham āgatam |
iti, tad āpadviṣayam | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
adattāny agnihīnasya nānnam adyād anāpadi | iti | (YDh 1.160)
agnihīnaḥ śūdraḥ |
[387]
atha varjāvarjyamāṃsāni
māṃseṣv api yad varjyaṃ tad āha manuḥ |
kravyādān śakunīn sarvāṃs tathā grāmanivāsinaḥ |
anirdiṣṭāṃś caikaśaphāṃṣ ṭiṭṭibhaṃ caiva varjayet ||
kalaviṅkaṃ plavaṃ haṃsaṃ cakrāhvaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
sārasaṃ rajjuvālaṃ ca dātyūhaṃ śukasārike ||
pratudān jālapādāṃś ca koyaṣṭinakhaviṣkirān |
nimajjataś ca matsyādān śaunaṃ vallūram eva ca ||
bakaṃ caiva balākāṃ ca kākolaṃ khañjarīṭakān |
matsyādān viḍvarāhāṃś ca matsyān eva ca sarvaśaḥ || iti | (MDh 5.11–14)
kravyadāḥ śakunayo gṛdhrādayaḥ | grāmanivāsinaḥ śakunayaḥ pārāvatādayaḥ | anirdiṣṭā aparijñātajātiviśeṣā mṛgapakṣiṇaḥ | ekaśaphā aśvādayaḥ | ṭiṭṭibhaḥ niṣṭhuraśabdabhāṣī pakṣiviśeṣaḥ | kalaviṅkaś caṭakaḥ | plavo jalakukkuṭaḥ | cakrāhvaś cakravākaḥ | sārasaḥ puṣkarāhvaḥ | rajjuvālako rajjuvatpucchakaḥ | dātyūhaḥ kālakaṇṭakaḥ | pratudaḥ śyenaḥ | jālapādaḥ jājākārapādaḥ | koyaṣṭiḥ pakṣiviśeṣaḥ | nakhaviṣkirāḥ cakorādayaḥ | nimajjanto matsyādā nimajjya matsyabhakṣakāḥ pakṣiviśeṣāḥ | śaunaṃ śunodbhavaṃ māṃsam | vallūraṃ śuṣkamāṃsam | kākolo girikākaḥ | khaṅjarīṭaḥ khañjanaḥ | matsyādā animajjanto matsyādā vivakṣitāḥ | viḍvarāhā grāmasūkarāḥ | [388] atra matsyaniṣedho rājīvasiṃhatuṇḍakādivyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | ata evoktaṃ tenaiva |
rājīvān siṃhatuṇḍāṃś ca saśalkāṃś caiva sarvaśaḥ | iti | (MDh 5.16)
rājīvāḥ padmavarṇā matsyāḥ | siṃhatuṇḍāḥ siṃhamukhāḥ | śalkaiḥ śuktyākārāvayavaiḥ pṛṣṭhabhāgagataiḥ saha vartante iti saśalkāḥ | ete sarvaśaḥ śrāddhe nityabhojane ca bhakṣyā ity arthaḥ | devalo ‘pi |
valūkakuraraśyenagṛdhrakukkuṭavāyasāḥ |
cakoraḥ kokilo rajjudālakaś cāṣamadgukau ||
pārāvatakapotau ca na bhakṣyāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ smṛtāḥ |
abhakṣyāḥ paśujātīnāṃ gokharoṣṭrāśvakuñjarāḥ ||
siṃhavyāghrarkṣaśarabhāḥ sarpājagarakās tathā |
ākhumūṣakamārjāranakulagrāmasūkarāḥ ||
śvaśṛgālavṛkadvīpigolāṅgūlakamarkaṭāḥ | iti |
kuraraḥ utkrośaḥ | madgur jalakākaḥ | dvīpiśabdo vyāghraviśeṣaparaḥ | golāṅgūlo vānaraviśeṣaḥ | markaṭagrahaṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ pañcanakhānām upalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva manuḥ: “sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā” (MDh 5.47) [389] iti | na bhakṣayed iti yojanā | atra pañcanakhānāṃ bhakṣyatvaniṣedho godhādipañcakavyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | tathā ca devalaḥ |
pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyā dharmataḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
godhā kūrmaḥ śaśaḥ śvāviṭ śalyakaś ceti te smṛtāḥ || iti |
dharmata iti hiṃsām akṛtvā krayādiprāptā bhakṣyā ity arthaḥ | na cāyam apūrvavidhiḥ | rāgaprāptatvāt tadbhakṣaṇasya | nāpi niyamaḥ, pakṣaprāptyabhāvāt | ato godhādipañcanakhapañcakavyatiriktā na bhakṣyā iti parisaṅkhyaiva pariśiṣyate | evaṃ ca sati viśeṣaniṣedhabalāt tanmāṃsabhakṣaṇe pratyavāyo netaratrety avagamyate | ata evoktaṃ manunā |
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā bhūtānāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā || iti | (MDh 5.56)
yat tenaivoktam,
nākṛtvā prāṇināṃ hīṃsāṃ māṃsam utpadyate kvacit |
na ca prāṇivadhaḥ svargyas tasmān māṃsaṃ vivarjayet ||
samutpattiṃ ca māṃsasya vadhabandhau ca dehinām |
prasamīkṣya nivarteta sarvamāṃsasya bhakṣaṇāt || (MDh 5.48–49)
iti, yac ca yājñavalkyenāpi,
vaset sa narake chore dināni paśulomabhiḥ |
saṃmitāni durācāro yo hanty avidhinā paśūn || (YDh 1.180)
[390] iti, tan niṣiddhaprāṇihiṃsāpūrvakamāṃsabhakṣaṇaviṣayam | na tu krayādiprāptamāṃsabhakṣaṇaviṣayam | prāṇivadhanindāpūrvakam eva māṃsaniṣedhasmaraṇāt | yac ca manunaivoktam,
phalamūlāśanair medhyair munyannānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ |
na tat phalam avāpnoti yan māṃsaparivarjanāt || (MDh 5.54)
iti, tatra māṃsavarjanasya mahāphalasādhanatvaṃ prokṣitādivyatiriktaviṣayam |  ata evoktaṃ tenaiva |
prokṣitaṃ bhakṣayen māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |
yathāvidhi niyuktaś ca prāṇānām eva cātyaye || iti | (MDh 5.27)
prokṣitam iṣṭaśiṣṭaṃ māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kāmyayā brāhmaṇakāmanayā ca | yathāvidhi niyuktaḥ śrāddhe nimantritaś ca | prāṇātyaye kṣunnimitte vyādhinimitte vā māṃsaṃ bhakṣayed ity alaṃ prasaktānuprasaktyā ||
atha nimantritabrāhmaṇebhyo deyāni
nimantritebhyo brāhmaṇebhyo yad deyaṃ tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
tailam udvartanaṃ snānaṃ dantadhāvanam eva ca |
kṛttaromanakhebhyas tu dadyāt tebhyo ‘pare ‘hani || iti |
snānaṃ snānasādhanam | dantadhāvanaṃ dantadhāvanasādhanaṃ kāṣṭhādi dadyād ity arthaḥ | etat tailādidānam aniṣiddhatithiviṣayam | [391] niṣiddhatithiṣu tilatailapratinidhitvenāmalakodakaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
ahnaḥ ṣaṭsu muhūrtteṣu gateṣv atha ca tān dvijān |
pratyekaṃ preṣayet preṣyān pradāyāmalakodakam || iti |
āmalakodakadānam apy amāvāsyāvyatiriktaviṣayam, “dhātrīphalair amāvāsyāyāṃ na snāyāt” iti smṛtyantare niṣedhāt | tailādidāne viśeṣo devalena darśitaḥ |
tailam udvartanaṃ snānaṃ snānīyaṃ ca pṛthagvidham |
pātrair audumbarair dadyād vaiśvadevikapūrvakam || iti |
audumbaraṃ tāmrapātram | yat tu pracetasoktam,
śrāddhabhug yo nakhaśmaśrucchedanaṃ na tu kārayet |
iti, tan niṣiddhatithiviṣayam | śrāddhadeśe prakalpyāni dravyāṇi purāṇe ‘bhihitāni |
upamūlaṃ sakṛlmūlān kuśāṃs tatropakalpayet |
yavāṃs tilān bṛsīḥ kāṃsyam āpaḥ śuddhaiḥ samāhṛtāḥ ||
pārṇarājatatāmrāṇi pātrāṇi syāt samin madhu |
puṣpadhūpasugandhādi kṣaumasūtraṃ ca mekṣaṇam || iti |
tilā jarttilā grāhyāḥ | tadasaṃbhave grāmyāḥ | jarttilalakṣaṇam uktaṃ satyavratena |
jarttilās tu tilāḥ proktāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇā vanebhavāḥ |
[392] iti | teṣāṃ praśastatvam āpastamba āha |
aṭavyāṃ ye samutpannā akṛṣṭaphalitās tathā |
te vai śrāddhe pavitrās tu tilās te jarttilāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
nimantritabrāhmaṇānām upaveśanārtham āsanaṃ bṛsī | tatra viśeṣo manunoktaḥ: “kutapaṃ cāsanaṃ dadyāt” iti (MDh 3.34) | kutapo nepāladeśaprabhavameṣādiromanirmitakambalaḥ | tad uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
madhyāhnaḥ khaḍgapātraṃ ca tathā nepālakambalaḥ |
rūpyaṃ darbhās tilā gāvo dauhitraś cāṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pāpaṃ kutsitam ity āhus tasya santāpakāraṇam |
aṣṭāv ete yatas tasmāt kutapā iti viśrutāḥ || iti |
kāṃsyapārṇarājatatāmrapātrāṇi bhojanārtham arghyārthaṃ copakalpyāni | atra bhojanārthaṃ palāśapatrapātrāṇy evopakalpyāni | na tv anyaparṇapātrāṇi | tathā ca atriḥ |
na mṛṇmayāni kurvīta bhojane daivapitryayoḥ |
pālāśebhyo vinā na syuḥ parṇapātrāṇi bhojane || iti |
arghyārthaṃ tv anyaparṇapātrāṇy aniṣiddhāni | ata eva baijavāpaḥ |
khādirodumbarāṇy arghyapātrāṇi śrāddhakarmaṇi |
apy aśmamṛṇmayāni syur api parṇapuṭās tathā ||
[393] iti | atropakalpanīyaṃ puṣpaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe darśitam |
śuklāḥ sumanasaḥ śreṣṭhās tathā padmotpalāni ca |
gandharūpopapannāni yāni cānyāni kṛtsnaśaḥ || iti |
mārkṇḍeyapurāṇe ‘pi |
jātyaś ca sarvā dātavyā mallikāḥ śvetapūthikāḥ |
jalodbhavāni sarvāṇi kusumāni ca campakam || iti |
yat tv āṅgirasoktam: “na jātīkusumāni | na kadalīpatram” iti, kratur api |
asurāṇāṃ kule jātā jātī pūrvaparigrahe |
tasyā darśanamātreṇa nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ ||
iti, atra jātīkusumaniṣedho vaikalpikaḥ | tatra varjyāni kusumāni matsyenoktāni |
padmabilvārkadhattūrapāribhadrāṭarūṣakāḥ |
na deyāḥ pitṛkāryeṣu payaś caivāvikaṃ tathā || iti | (MatPu 15.37–38)
pāribhadro mandāraḥ āṭarūṣo | vāsakaḥ | śaṅkho ‘pi |
ugragandhīny agandhīni caityavṛkṣodbhavāni ca |
puṣpāṇi varjanīyāni raktavarṇāni yāni ca || (ŚaṅSm 13.14)
[394] iti | atra raktavarṇaniṣedho jalodbhavavyatiriktaviṣayaḥ | ata evoktaṃ tenaiva |
jalodbhavāni deyāni raktāny api viśeṣataḥ | iti | (ŚaṅSm 13.15)
viṣṇur api: “varjayed ugragandhīny agandhīni kaṇṭakijātāni raktāni puṣpāṇi ca | sitāni sugandhīni kaṇṭakijātāny api dadyāt” iti (ViDh 79.5–6) | dhūpadravyam api viṣṇudharmottare darśitam |
dhūpo guggulako deyas tathā candanasārajaḥ |
agaruś ca sakarpūras turuṣkas tvak tathaiva ca || iti | (ViDhPu 2.141.14)
turuṣkaḥ śallakīvṛkṣaḥ | tvak lavaṅgatvak | marīcir api |
candanāgarūṇī cobhe tamālośīrapadmakam | iti |
varjanīyadhūpadravyaṃ viṣṇur āha: “jīvajaṃ ca sarvaṃ na dhūpārtham” (ViDh 79.9) [395] iti | jīvajaṃ kastūryādi | “candanakuṅkumakarpūrāgarupadmakāny anulepanārthe” iti (ViDh 79.11) | dīpārthaṃ snehadravyam āha marīciḥ |
ghṛtād vā tilatailād vā nānyadravyāt tu dīpakam | iti |
atra cānyadravyaniṣedho vasāmedorūpadravyaviṣayo na punaḥ kausumbhāditailaviṣayaḥ | ata eva śaṅkhaḥ |
ghṛtena dīpo dātavyas tv atha vānyauṣadhīrasaiḥ |
vasāmedodbhavaṃ dīpaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet || iti | (ŚaṅSm 13.16)
kṣaumasūtropakalpanaṃ vastrālābhaviṣayam | sati saṃbhave kṣaumaṃ vastram upakalpanīyam | ata eva smṛtyantaram |
kauśeyaṃ kṣaumakārpāsaṃ dukūlam ahataṃ tathā |
śrāddheṣv etāni yo dadyāt kāmān āpnoti puṣkalān || iti | (VāyPu 2.18.37)
kauśeyaṃ kṛmikośotthatantujam | kṣaumam atasītvaksaṃbhavatantusaṃbhavam | dukūlam iti sūkṣmavastram | ahatam,
[396]        īṣaddhautaṃ navaṃ śvetaṃ sadaśaṃ yan na dhāritam |
ahataṃ tad vijānīyāt sarvakarmasu pāvanam || iti |
atha vāsaḥparidhānam
evaṃ darbhādimekṣaṇāntaṃ dravyam upakalpya snātvā śuklaṃ vāsaḥ paridadhyāt | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
snāto ‘dhikārī bhavati daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
śrāddhakṛc chuklavāsāḥ syāt | iti ||
atha snānānantarakartavyam
snānānantaraṃ yat kartavyaṃ tad āha yamaḥ |
tataḥ snātvā nivṛttebhyaḥ pratyutthāya kṛtāñjaliḥ |
pādyam ācamanīyaṃ ca saṃprayacched yathākramam || iti |
kṛtāñjaliḥ svāgatam ity uktvā adhvakṛtopahatiśuddhyarthaṃ pādaprakṣālanārtham ācamanīyaṃ codakaṃ krameṇa prayacched ity arthaḥ | tadanantaraṃ gṛhāṅgaṇe maṇḍaladvayaṃ kāryam | tathā ca matsyaḥ |
……… bhavanasyāgrato bhuvi |
gomayenānuliptāyāṃ gomūtreṇa tu maṇḍale || (MatPu 16.27–28)
[397] iti | gomayasahitena gosūtreṇa maṇḍale kārye iti śeṣaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha śambhuḥ |
udakplavam udīcyaṃ syād dakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇāplavam | iti |
udīcyaṃ vaiśvadevikaṃ maṇḍalam udakpravaṇam | dakṣiṇaṃ pitryaṃ maṇḍalaṃ dakṣiṇāpravaṇaṃ kuryāt | tatra maṇḍalakaraṇaprabhṛtyā śrāddhaparisamāpter vaiśvadaivikaṃ karma pradakṣiṇaṃ yajñopavītinā kāryam | pitryam apasavyaṃ prācīnāvītinā kāryam | tathā ca manuḥ |
prācīnāvītinā samyag apasavyam atandriṇā |
pitryam ā nidhanāt kāryaṃ vidhivad darbhapāṇinā || iti | (MDh 3.279)
atraiva viśeṣāntaram āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dakṣiṇaṃ pātayet jānuṃ devān paricaran sadā |
pātayed itarañ jānu pitṝn paricarann api || iti | (KSm 1.2.5)
śātātapo ‘pi |
udaṅmukhas tu devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ | iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
pradakṣiṇaṃ tu devānāṃ pitṝṇām apradakṣiṇam |
devānām ṛjavo darbhāḥ pitṝṇāṃ dviguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
maṇḍalakaraṇānantarakartavyam āha śambhuḥ |
uttare ‘kṣatasaṃyuktān pūrvāgrān vinyaset kuśān |
dakṣiṇe dakṣiṇāgrāṃs tu satilān vinyased budhaḥ ||
[398] iti | matsyapurāṇe ‘pi |
akṣatābhiḥ sapuṣpābhis tad abhyarcyāpasavyavat |
viprāṇāṃ kṣālayet pādāv abhivandya punaḥ punaḥ || iti | (MatPu 16.28–29)
apasavyavat pūrvam udīcyamaṇḍalaṃ paścād dakṣiṇamaṇḍalam abhyarcyety arthaḥ | daivapūrvakaṃ viprāṇāṃ pādaprakṣālanaṃ kuryāt | ata eva ca brahmaniruktam |
pādyaṃ caiva tathaivārghyaṃ daivam ādau prayojayet |
śan no devīti mantreṇa pādyam caiva prapādayet || iti |
pādyādidānaṃ nāmagotroccāraṇapūrvakaṃ kartavyam | tad uktaṃ matsyapurāṇe |
nāmagotraṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu prāpakaṃ havyakavyayoḥ || iti | (MatPu 19.4)
pādaprakṣālanānantaraṃ yat kartavyaṃ tad āha sumantuḥ |
darbhapāṇir dvir ācamya laghuvāsā jitendriyaḥ |
pariśrite śucau deśe gomayenopalepite ||
dakṣiṇāpravaṇe samyag ācāntān prayatān śucīn |
āsaneṣu sadarbheṣu vivikteṣūpaveśayet ||
[399] iti | vivikteṣu parasparam asaṃspṛṣṭeṣv ity arthaḥ | manur api |
āsaneṣūpakḷpteṣu barhiṣmatsu pṛthak pṛthak |
upaspṛṣṭodakān samyag viprāṃs tān upaveśayet || iti | (MDh 3.208)
upaveśanaprakāro yamena darśitaḥ: “āsanaṃ saṃspṛśan savyena pāṇinā dakṣiṇena brāhmaṇam upasaṃgṛhya samādhvam iti coktvopaveśayet” iti | dharme ‘pi |
jānvālabhya tato devān upaveśya tataḥ pitṝn |
samastābhir vyāhṛtibhir āsaneṣūpaveśayet || iti | (ViDh 1.141.20)
ādipurāṇe ‘pi |
viprau tu prāṅmukhau tebhyo dvau tu pūrvaṃ niveśayet |
śan no devīti mantreṇa pādyaṃ caiva pradāpayet ||
uttarābhimukhān viprāṃs trīn pitṛbhyaś ca sarvadā || iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
dvau daive prāk trayaḥ pitrya udag ekaikam eva vā |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ tantraṃ vā vaiśvadaivikam || (YDh 1.228)
[400] iti | mātāmahānām apy evam iti saṃkhyādiṅniyamayor atideśaḥ | vaiśvadaivikaṃ karma śrāddhadvayārtham āvṛttyānuṣṭheyam tantreṇa vety abhipretya tantraṃ vā vaiśvadaivikam ity uktam | ata eva marīciḥ |
tathā mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ vaiśvadevasamanvitam |
kurvīta bhaktisaṃpannas tantraṃ vā vaiśvadaivikam || iti |
atra dvayor api śrāddhayor vaiśvadaivikakarmaṇaḥ tantrāvṛttividhānād ekaprayogavidhiprayojyatvaṃ bhinnaprayogavidhiprayojyatvaṃ ca prāptam | tataś caikādhikārāpūrvasādhakatvaṃ bhinnādhikārāpūrvasādhakatvaṃ cāvagamyate |
atha brāhmaṇaniyamāḥ
upaviṣṭabrāhmaṇaniyamāḥ smṛtyantare darśitāḥ |
pavitrapāṇayaḥ sarve te ca maunavratānvitāḥ |
ucchiṣṭocchiṣṭasaṃsaprśaṃ varjayantaḥ parasparam || iti |
maunatvaṃ ca brahmodyakathāvyatiriktaviṣayam | ata eva yamaḥ |
brahmodyāś ca kathāḥ kuryuḥ pitṝṇām etad īpsitam | iti ||
atha yatibrahmacāriṇoḥ bhojanīyatvam
upaviṣṭeṣv api brāhmaṇeṣu yatir brahmacārī vā yady āgacchati tadā so ‘pi śrāddhe bhojayitavyaḥ | tad āha yamaḥ |
bhikṣuko brahmacārī vā bhojanārtham upasthitaḥ |
upaviṣṭeṣv anuprāptaḥ kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet ||
[401] iti | chāgaleyo ‘pi |
pūjayet śrāddhakāle ‘pi yatiṃ ca brahmacāriṇam |
viprān uddharate pāpāt pitṝn mātṛgaṇān api || iti |
manur api |
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ vāpi bhojanārtham upasthitam |
brāhmaṇair anujñātaḥ śaktitaḥ pratipūjayet || iti | (MDh 3.243)
atha brāhmaṇopaveśanānantarakṛtyam
brāhmaṇopaveśanānantarakṛtyaṃ purāṇe ‘bhihitam |
śrāddhabhūmau gayāṃ dhyātvā dhyātvā devaṃ gadādharam |
tābhyāṃ caiva namaskṛtya tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pravartayet || iti |
śrāddhaṃ kariṣya ity evam upaviṣṭān brāhmaṇān pṛcched ity arthaḥ | ata evoktaṃ tatraiva |
ubhau hastau samau kṛtvā jānubhyām antare sthitau |
sapraśrayaś copaviṣṭān sarvān pṛcchet dvijottamān || iti |
kuruṣveti taiḥ anujñāto “devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca” it mantraṃ triḥ paṭhet | tad uktaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaś ca mahāyogibhya eva ca |
namaḥ svadhāyai savāhāyai nityam eva namo namaḥ ||
ādye ‘vasāne śrāddhasya trir āvṛttaṃ japet sadā || iti |
anantaraṃ sarvatas tilān vikiret | tad uktaṃ nigame: [402] “apahatā it tilān vikiret” iti | tilān vikīrya dharbhāsanaṃ dadyāt | tad uktaṃ purāṇe |
kuruṣveti sa tair ukto dadyād darbhāsanaṃ tataḥ | iti |
darbhāsanadānaṃ ca brāhmaṇahaste udakadānapūrvakaṃ kāryam | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
pāṇiprakṣālanaṃ datvā viṣṭarārthān kuśān api | iti | (YDh 1.229)
viṣṭarārthān āsanārthān kuśānā āsaneṣu datvety arthaḥ | tad uktaṃ purāṇe |
āsane cāsanaṃ dadyāt vāme vā dakṣiṇe ‘pi vā | iti |
vāme vā dakṣiṇe ‘pi vety ayaṃ vikalpaḥ pitrarthadevārthabrāhmaṇāsanadānaviṣayatayā vyavasthito draṣṭavyaḥ | ata evoktaṃ tatraiva |
pitṛkarmaṇi vāme vai daive karmaṇi dakṣiṇe | iti |
daive karmaṇy āsanadāne viśeṣaḥ kāṭhake ‘bhihitaḥ: “devānāṃ sayavā darbhāḥ” iti | āsanaṃ datvā punar nimantrayīya | tad āha saṃgrahakāraḥ: “tataḥ punar apo datvā nimantrayet| daive kṣaṇaḥ kriyatām | tataḥ om tatheti vipro brūyāt | prāpnotu bhavān it kartā punar brūyāt | prāpnuvāni iti vipraḥ punar brūyāt” iti | nimantraṇaṃ ca niraṅguṣṭhaṃ hastaṃ gṛhītvā kartavyam | tad uktaṃ purāṇe |
[403]        niraṅguṣṭhaṃ gṛhītvā tu viśvān devān samāhvayet | iti |
aṅguṣṭhavyatiriktaṃ hastaṃ gṛhītvā nimantrya viśvān devān samāhvayed ity arthaḥ | āvāhanetikartavyatām āha yamaḥ |
yavahastas tato devān vijñāpyāvāhanaṃ prati |
āvāhayed anujñāto viśvedevāsa ity ṛcā ||
viśvedevāḥ śṛṇuteti mantraṃ japtvā tato ‘kṣatān |
oṣadhaya iti mantreṇa vikiret tu pradakṣiṇam || iti |
pradakṣiṇaṃ dakṣiṇapādādimastakāntam akṣatān vikiret āropayed ity arthaḥ | viśvedevās tu daś bṛhaspatinā darśitāḥ |
kratur dakṣo vasuḥ satyaḥ kālaḥ kāmas tathaiva ca |
dhuniś ca rocanaś caiva yathā caiva purūravā ||
ārdravaś ca daśaite tu viśvedevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ | iti |
teṣāṃ madhye purūravārdravasaṃjñakā viśvedevā atrāvāhyāḥ, pārvaṇaśrāddhatvāt | ata eva śaṅkhaḥ |
iṣṭiśrāddhe kratur dakṣaḥ saṃkīrtyau vaiśvadaivike |
nāndīmukhe satyavasū kāmye ca dhunirocanau ||
purūravārdravau caiva pārvaṇe samudāhṛtau |
naimittike kālakāmāv evaṃ sarvatra kīrtayet || iti |
iṣṭiśrāddhaṃ karmāṅgaśrāddham | tac ca pāraskareṇa darśitam |
[404]        niṣekakāle some ca sīmantonnayane tathā |
jñeyaṃ puṃsavane śrāddhaṃ karmāṅgaṃ vṛddhivat kṛtam || iti |
vṛddhivad ity anena vṛddiśrāddhāt karmāṅgaśrāddham anyad iti jñāyate | nāndīmukhaṃ vṛddhiśrāddham | tatsvarūpaṃ vṛddhavasiṣṭha āha |
putrajanmavivāhādau vṛddhiśrāddham udāhṛtam | iti |
ādiśabdenānnaprāśanacūḍākaraṇādisaṃskārā gṛhyante | na tu garbhādhānapuṃsavana-sīmantonnayanāni, tatra kriyamāṇasya karmāṅgaśrāddhatvāt | kāmyaṃ phalakāmanopādhikam | pārvaṇam amāvāsyāśrāddham | naimittikaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam |
nanu,
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yat śrāddhaṃ tan naimittikam ucyate |
iti naimittikaśabda ekoddiṣṭe rūḍhatvāt kathaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇe prayujyate iti |
satyam, tathāpi “tad apy adaivaṃ kartavyam” ity ekoddiṣṭasya daivahīnatvena kāmakālasaṃjñakānāṃ viśveṣāṃ devānām anvayānupapatteḥ ekoddiṣṭayuktaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ naimittikaśabdena lakṣaṇayocyate ||
athārghyādidānam
viśvān devān āvāhyārghapātrāsādanādi kuryāt | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
yavair anvavakīryātha bhājane sapavitrake |
[405]        śan no devyā payaḥ kṣiptvā yavo ‘sīti yavāṃs tathā || iti | (YDh 1.230)
matsyapurāṇe ‘pi |
viśvān devān yavaiḥ puṣpair abhyarcyāsanapūrvakam |
pūrayet pātrayugmaṃ tu sthāpya darbhapavitrake || iti | (MatPu 17.14–15)
pracetā api |
ekaikasya tu viprasya arghyaṃ pātre viniḥkṣipet |
yavo ‘sīti yavān kīrttvā gandhapuṣpaiḥ supūjitam || iti |
arghyapātrāṇi sauvarṇarājatādīni | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ: “sauvarṇarājataudumbarakhaḍga-maṇimayānāṃ pātrāṇām anyatameṣu | yāni vā vidyante | patrapūṭake vā” iti | yāni vā taijasāni kāṃsyādīni teṣu vety arthaḥ rājataṃ pātraṃ pitrye viniyujyate | na daive | tad āha matsyo rājataṃ pātram adhikṛtya |
śivanetrodbhavaṃ yasmād atas tat pitṛvallabham |
amaṅgalaṃ tad yatnena devakāryeṣu varjitam || iti | (MatPu 17.23)
arghyapavitrakaṃ pratipātraṃ bhedena kāryam | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
[406]        dve dve śalāke devānāṃ pātre kṛtvā payaḥ kṣipet | iti |
pavitrakaraṇetikartavyatām yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
pavitre stha iti mantreṇa dve pavitre ca kārayet |
antardarbhe kuśacchinne kauśe prādeśasaṃmite || iti |
kuśacchinna iti kuśam antarā kṛtvā chinne | ata eva yajñapārśvaḥ |
auṣadhīm antare kṛtvā aṅguṣṭhāṅguliparvaṇoḥ |
chindyāt prādeśamātraṃ tu pavitraṃ viṣṇudaivatam ||
na nakhena na kāṣṭhena na lohena na mṛṇmayāt | iti |
anantaram “svāhārghyā” iti mantreṇa devatārthaṃ brāhmaṇasamīpe ‘rghyapātraṃ sthāpayet | tathā ca gārgyaḥ |
svāheti caiva devānāṃ homakarmaṇy udāharet | iti |
devānāṃ homakarmaṇy arghyadāne karmaṇy upasthite pātraṃ sthāpayitum “svāhārghyā” iti mantram uccārayed iti prakaraṇād eva gamyate | arghyapātraṃ sthāpayitvā viprahaste ‘rghyaṃ dadyāt | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yā dviyā iti mantreṇa hasteṣv arghyaṃ viniḥkṣipet | iti | (YDh 1.231)
arghyadāne viśeṣam āha gargyaḥ |
datvā haste pavitraṃ tu saṃpūjyārghyaṃ viniḥkṣipet | iti |
hasteṣv iti bahuvacanam upakramagataikavacanānurodhād [407] avivakṣitam | tadanantaram udakapūrvakaṃ gandhādi deyam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
datvodakaṃ gandhamālyaṃ dhūpadānaṃ pradīpakam | iti | (YDh 1.231)
gandhādidānam ācchādanasyāpy upalakṣaṇam, tadantargatatvāt | evam āsanaprabhṛtyācchādanaparyantaṃ vaiśvadevikārcanaṃ kāṇḍānusamayena kṛtvā āsanādyācchādanaparyantaṃ pitrarcanaṃ prācīnāvīty apradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pitṝṇām apradakṣiṇam | iti | (YDh 1.232)
nanu – daive pitrye ca āsanādyācchādanaparyantānāṃ padārthānām api padārthānusamayenaivānuṣṭhānaṃ nyāyyam, na tu kāṇḍānusamayena | padārthānusamaye hi teṣāṃ pradhānapratyāsattir bhavati avaiṣamyaṃ ca | anyathā keṣāṃcit pradhānapratyāsattiḥ keṣāṃcin neti vaiṣamyam āpadyet – iti |
satyam | āsanādipadārtheṣu vacanabalāt kāṇḍānusamaya eva svīkriyate | vācanikatvaṃ ca “apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā” iti vaiśvadaivikāsanādipadārthakāṇḍād ūrdhvaṃ pitrarcanavidhānāt | āsanādidānaprakāram āha sa eva |
dviguṇāṃs tu kuśān datvā hy uśantas tvety ṛcā saha |
āvāhya tadanujñāto japed āyāntu nas tataḥ ||
[408]        yavārthās tu tilaiḥ kāryāḥ kuryād arghyādipūrvavat ||
datvārghyaṃ saṃsravāṃs teṣāṃ pātre kṛtvā vidhānataḥ |
pitṛbhyaḥ sthānam asīti nyubjaṃ pātraṃ karoty adhaḥ || iti | (YDh 1.233–35)
dviguṇān dviguṇabhugnān satilān kuśān āsanārthaṃ dadyāt | tad uktaṃ kāṭhake: “pitṝṇāṃ dviguṇāṃs tilaiḥ” iti | āsanadānāt pūrvaṃ paścāc codakaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca āśvalāyanaḥ: “apaḥ pradāya | darbhān dviguṇabhugnān āsanaṃ pradāya | āpaḥ pradāya” iti (ĀśGṛ 4.7.5–7) | “śrāddhe kṣaṇaḥ kriyatām” iti pūrvavat kṣaṇaṃ datvā | “pitṝn āvāhayiṣye” iti brāhmaṇān pṛṣṭvā “āvāhaya” iti tair anujñātaḥ “uśantas tvā” iti mantreṇāvāhy “namo vaḥ pitaraḥ” itit tilān mastakādidakṣiṇapādāntam avakīrya “āyāntu naḥ pitaraḥ” iti mantreṇopatiṣṭheta | tathā ca pracetāḥ |
śiraḥprabhṛti pādāntaṃ namo va iti paitṛke | iti |
upasthānānantarakṛtyam uktam purāṇe |
japed āyāntu na iti mantraṃ samyag aśeṣataḥ |
rakṣārthaṃ pitṛsatrasya triḥkṛtvaḥ sarvatodiśam ||
tilāṃs tu prakṣipen mantram uccāryāpahatā iti ||
[409] iti | arghyādyācchādanāntaṃ pūrvavat kuryāt | atrārghyapātrāsādanādau viśeṣo viṣṇunā darśitaḥ: “dakṣināgreṣu dakṣiṇāpavargeṣu camaseṣu triṣv apa āsiñcec chinno devīr iti” iti (ViDh 73.12) | ayam arthaḥ – dakṣiṇāgreṣu darbheṣu dakṣiṇāpavargatayāsāditeṣu pavitrāntarhiteṣu camaseṣu triṣv arghyapātreṣu śan no devīr abhiṣṭaya iti mantreṇa pratipātrama apa āsiñced iti | śaunako ‘pi: “pātreṣu darbhāntarhiteṣv apaḥ pradāya śan no devīr abhiṣṭaya ity anumantritāsu tilān āvapati | tilo ‘si somadevatyo gosave devanirmitaḥ | pratnavadbhiḥ prattaḥ svadhayā pitṝn imāṃl lokān prīṇayāhi naḥ svadhā namaḥ” iti | atra ca pitrādīnāṃ trayāṇām ekaikasyānekabrāhmaṇanimantraṇe sarveṣām ekabrāhmaṇanimantraṇe ‘pi trīny evārghyapātrāṇi, na tu brāhmaṇasaṃkhyayā | ata eva baijavāpaḥ |
stīrtvā pitṝṇāṃ trīṇy eva kuryāt pātrāṇi dharmavit |
ekasmin vā bahuṣu vā brāhmaṇeṣu yathāvidhi || iti |
stīrtvā tilān arghyodakeṣu kṣiptvety arthaḥ | arghyapavitraṃ cāyugmasaṃkhyayā kartavyam | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
tisras tisraḥ śalākāḥ syuḥ pitṛpātreṣu pārvaṇe |
[410] iti | tilaprakṣepānantaraṃ gandhapuṣpāṇi prakṣipet | tad uktaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
arghyāḥ puṣpaiś ca gandhaiś ca tāḥ prapūjyāś ca śāstravat | iti |
yā arghyārhā āpas tā gandhādibhiḥ pūjyā ity arthaḥ | anantarakartavyam āha śaunakaḥ: “tāḥ pratigrāhayiṣyan svadhārghyā iti” | tā āpo brāhmaṇaiḥ pratigrāhayiṣyan “svadhārghyā” iti mantreṇa sthāpayed ity arthaḥ | tataḥ pavitrāntarhiteṣūdakapūrvakaṃ “yā divyā” iti mantreṇārghyodakaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ: “tato brāhmaṇahasteṣūdakapūrvaṃ darbhān pradāyodakapūrvakam arghodakaṃ dadāti yā divyā ity uktvāsāv etat te ‘rghodakam ity apa upaspṛśyaivam evetarayoḥ” iti | atra viśeṣam āha dharmaḥ: “yā divyā āpa iti pātraṃ pāṇibhyām uddhṛtya nāma gotraṃ ca gṛhītvā sapavitrahaste ‘rghyaṃ dadyāt” iti | yat tu paiṭhīnasivacane hasteṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ tat trīṃs trīn ekaikatreti  vihitabrāhmaṇasaṃkhyāpakṣe | brāhmaṇahasteṣūdakapūrvaṃ darbhān pradāyeti pitrarthabrāhmaṇārcana eva bahuvacanaprayogāt | [411] anyathā “evam evetarayoḥ” iti pitāmahaprapitāmahārtha-brāhmaṇārcanasya pṛthagvidhānaṃ na syāt | evam arghyaṃ datvā teṣām arghodakānāṃ saṃsravān viprahastebhyaḥ pātreṣu galitān pitṛpātre saṃgṛhya tatpātraṃ nyubjam adhomukham “pitṛbhyaḥ sthānam asi” ity adhaḥ kuryāt bhūmau nidadhyāt ity arthaḥ | nyubjakaraṇānantrakartavyam āha baijavāpaḥ |
tasyopari kuśān datvā pradadyād daivapūrvakam |
gandhapuṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ ca dīpavastropavītakam || iti |
daivapūrvakam ity ayaṃ padārthānusamayo yājñavalkyoktakāṇḍānusamayena saha vikalpate ity avirodhaḥ ||
athāgnaukaraṇam
evaṃ gandhapuṣpādibhir brāhmaṇān abhyarcyāgnaukaraṇākhyaṃ karma kuryāt | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
gandhān brāhmaṇasāt kṛtvā puṣpāṇy ṛtubhavāni ca |
dhūpaṃ caivānupūrvyeṇa agnaukuryād ataḥ param || iti | (KSm 2.17.11)
agnaukaraṇaprakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
agnau kariṣyann ādāya pṛcchaty annaṃ ghṛtaplutam |
kuruṣvety abhyanujñāto hutvāgnau pitṛyajñavat ||
[412]        hutaśeṣaṃ pradadyāt tu bhājaneṣu samāhitaḥ |
yathālābhopapanneṣu raupyeṣu tu viśeṣataḥ || iti | (YDh 1.236–67)
agnau kariṣyan ghṛtaplutam annam ādāya “agnau kariṣye” iti brāhmaṇān pṛcchet | ghṛtaśabdena śākāder nirāsaḥ | tataḥ kuruṣveti tair anujñātaḥ, “sāṃnidhyam upasamādhyāya mekṣaṇenāvadāya somāya pitṛmate svadhā namo ‘gnaye kavyavāhanāya svadhā namaḥ” iti piṇḍapitṛyajñavidhānenāgnau juhuyāt | tato mekṣaṇaṃ hutvā hutaśeṣaṃ yathālābhopapanneṣu pitrādibhājaneṣu dadyāt, na vaiśvadevabhājaneṣu | tad uktaṃ purāṇe |
agnaukaraṇaśeṣaṃ tu dadyād vaiśvadaivike | iti |
agnaukaraṇaṃ ca prācīnāvīty upavītī vā kuryāt | tatprakṛtibhūtasya piṇḍapitṛyajñasya daivikatvapaitṛkatvābhyām ubhayavidhatvena vikalpitobhayadharmakatvāt tadvikṛtibhūtāgnaukaraṇahome ‘pi prācīnāvītitvopavītitvayor vikalpo ‘vagamyate | atra ca yathāśākhaṃ vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | agnaukaraṇaṃ ca smārtatvena vivāhāgnau kartavyam | yadā tu sarvādhānenaupāsanāgnir nāsti tadā dakṣiṇāgnau juhuyāt | tadasaṃnidhāne laukikāgnau | tathā ca vāyupurāṇam |
āhṛtya dakṣiṇāgniṃ tu homārthaṃ vai prayatnataḥ |
agnyarthaṃ laukikaṃ vāpi juhuyāt karmasiddhaye || (VāyPu 2.13.52–53)
[413] iti | agnyartham aupāsanāgnikāryasiddhyartham | dakṣiṇāgnyasaṃnidhāne pāṇau homaḥ kartavyaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
haste ‘gnaukaraṇaṃ kuryād agnau vā sāgniko dvijaḥ | iti |
sāgnikaḥ sarvādhānenāhitāgniḥ | dakṣiṇāgnyasaṃnidhāne haste laukike ‘gnau vāgnaukaraṇaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | yadā tv ardhādhānenāhitāgnir apy anagnimān tadaupāsanāgnāv agnaukaraṇaṃ kuryāt | tadabhāve dvijapāṇāv apsu vā | tathā ca mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
anāhitāgnis tv aupāsanāgnyabhāve dvije ‘psu ca | iti |
caśabdenārdhādhānenānāhitāgnir gṛhyate | apsv agnaukaraṇaṃ jalasamīpe śrāddhakaraṇe veditavyam | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
viṣṇudharmottare vāpsu mārkaṇḍeyena yaḥ smṛtaḥ |
sa yadāpāṃ samīpe syāc chrāddhaṃ jñeyo vidhis tadā || iti |
yat tu manunoktam,
agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇāv evopapādayet | (MDh 3.212)
iti, tad brahmacāriviṣayam | tad āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
agnyabhāve tu viprasya pāṇau dadyāt tu dakṣiṇe |
[414]        agnyabhāvaḥ smṛtas tāvad yāvad bhāryāṃ na vindati || iti |
vidyamāne ‘py agnau kāmyādiṣu caturṣu śrāddheṣu brāhmaṇapāṇāv eva homaḥ | tad āhur gṛhyakārāḥ |
anvaṣṭakyaṃ ca pūrvedyur māsi māsi ca pārvaṇam |
kāmyam abhyudaye ‘ṣṭamyām ekoddiṣṭam athāṣṭamam ||
caturṣv ādyeṣu sāgnīnām agnau homo vidhīyate |
pitryabrāhmaṇahaste syād uttareṣu caturṣv api || iti |
“hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamyo aṣṭakāḥ” iti vihitāny aṣṭakāśrāddhāni | tatrāṣṭakāśrāddhānām uttaradine navamyāṃ kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddham anvaṣṭakam | pūrvedyuḥ saptamyāṃ kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ “pūrvedyuḥ” iti padena lakṣaṇayoktam | pratimāsaṃ kriyamāṇam aparapakṣikaṃ śrāddhaṃ “māsi māsi” ity anenoktam | pārvaṇaṃ sarvaśrāddhaprakṛtibhūtam amāvāsyāśrāddham | kāmyaṃ putrādiphalakāmanayā kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddham | ābhyudikaśrāddham “abhyudaya” iti padenoktam | aṣṭakākhyaśrāddhaṃ aṣṭamyām iti padenoktam | ekoddiṣṭapadena sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ lakṣaṇayoktam | apiṇḍīkaraṇe ekoddiṣṭasyāpi sadbhāvāt | eṣāṃ madhye ādyeṣu caturṣu sāgnikānām agnāv evāgnaukaraṇahomaḥ | uttareṣu sāgnīnāṃ pitryabrāhmaṇahasta eveti | pāṇau home tu viśeṣaḥ kātyāyanena darśitaḥ |
pitrye yaḥ paṅktimūrdhanyas tasya pāṇāv anagnikaḥ |
hutvā mantravad anyeṣāṃ tūṣṇīṃ pātreṣu niḥkṣipet || (KSm 2.17.15)
[415] iti | yat tu yamenoktam,
daivaviprakare ‘nagniḥ kṛtvāgnaukaraṇaṃ dvijaḥ |
iti, tatra vikalpena vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | daivikabrāhmaṇapāṇau homapakṣe ‘pi pitryabrāhmaṇapātreṣv eva śeṣaṃ niḥkṣipet | ata eva vāyupurāṇam |
hutvā daivakare ‘nagniḥ śeṣaṃ pitrye nivedayet |
na hi smṛtāḥ śeṣabhājo viśvedevāḥ purāṇagaiḥ || iti |
pāṇau home yat kartavyaṃ tad āha śaunakaḥ: “anagniś ced ādyaṃ gṛhītvā bhavatsv evāgnaukaraṇam iti pūrvavat tathāstv iti” iti | ayam arthaḥ – ādyaṃ ghṛtaplutam annaṃ gṛhītvā “bhavatsv evāgnaukaraṃahomaṃ kariṣye” iti pūrvavat pṛṣṭvā tathāstv iti tair anujñāto juhuyāt – iti | yamo ‘pi |
agnaukaraṇavat tatra homo daivakare bhavet |
paryastadarbhān āstīrya yato hy agnisamo hi saḥ || iti |
paryastadarbhāḥ paristaraṇyogyā darbhāḥ | daivakaragrahaṇena pitryabrāhmaṇakaro ‘py upalakṣyate, ubhayatrāpi vikalpenāgnaukaraṇasya vidhānāt | pāṇitale hutasyānnasya viniyogam āha gṛhyapariśiṣṭakāraḥ |
yac ca pāṇitale dattaṃ yac cānyad upakalpitam |
ekībhāvena bhoktavyaṃ pṛthagbhāvo na vidyate ||
[416]        annaṃ pāṇitale dattaṃ pūrvam aśnanty abuddhayaḥ |
pitaras tena tṛpyanti śeṣānnaṃ na labhanti te || iti |
yadā daivaviprakare homas tadā pitṛmātāmahaśrāddhadvayārthaṃ sakṛd evānuṣṭheyaḥ, vaiśvadevabhede ‘pi tatrādhikaraṇakārakasya saṃpratipannatvāt | yadā pitryabrāhmaṇakare homas tadā mātāmahabrāhmaṇakare ‘pi pṛthag anuṣṭheyaḥ, vaiśvadaivikatantratve ‘pi tatrādhikaraṇakārakasyāsaṃpratipannatvāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
mātāmahasya bhede ‘pi kuryāt tatraiva sāgnikaḥ | iti |
vaiśvadaivikasya bhede tantre ca mātāmahasya mātāmahārthabrāhmaṇasyāpi pāṇau homaṃ sāgnikaḥ kuryād ity arthaḥ | pātrāṇi dviḥ prakṣālayet | tathā ca brahmāṇḍapurāṇam |
prakṣālya hastapātrādi paścād adbhir vidhānavat |
prakṣālanajalaṃ darbhais tilamiśraṃ kṣipec chucau || iti |
hastena nirmṛṣṭaṃ pātrādīti madhyamapadalopī samāsaḥ | ādiśabdena ghṛtādidhāraṇārthaṃ gṛhyate ||
atha pariveṣaṇam
evaṃ prakṣāliteṣu pātreṣu hutaśeṣaṃ prācīnāvītī pitṛpātreṣu nidhāya saṃpāditān padārthān pariveṣayet | tathā ca śaunakaḥ |
hutvāgnau pariśiṣṭaṃ tu pitṛpātreṣv anantaram |
nivedyaivāpasavyena pariveṣaṇam ācaret ||
[417] iti | apasavyeneti hutaśeṣanivedanenānveti | na tu pariveṣaṇena | ata eva kārṣāṇājiniḥ |
apasavyena kartavyaṃ pitryaṃ kṛtyaṃ viśeṣataḥ |
annadānād ṛte sarvam evaṃ mātāmaheṣv iti || iti |
pariveṣaṇaprakāro manunā darśitaḥ |
pāṇibhyām upasaṃgṛhya svayam annasya vardhitam |
viprāntike pitṝn dhyāyan śanakair upaniḥkṣipet || iti | (MDh 3.224)
annasya vardhitam annena pūrṇaṃ pariveṣaṇapātraṃ viprāntike bhojanārthapātre | svayam iti vacanāt svayaṃ pariveṣaṇaṃ mukhyam | ata eva vāyupurāṇam |
phalasyānantatā proktā svayaṃ tu pariveṣaṇe | iti |
yat tu tatraivoktam,
pariveṣaṇaṃ praśastaṃ hi bhāryayā pitṛtṛptaye |
pitṛdevamanuṣyāṇāṃ strīsahāye yataḥ smṛte ||
iti, tad itarāpekṣayā veditavyam | bhāryayāpi savarṇayaiva pariveṣaṇaṃ kāryam | tathā ca nārāyaṇaḥ |
yad dravyaṃ yat pavitraṃ ca yat pitryaṃ yat sukhāvaham |
dvijātibhyaḥ savarṇāyā hastenaiva tu dīyate ||
[418] iti | hastena hastadvayenety arthaḥ | tad āha matsyaḥ |
ubhābhyām api hastābhyām āhṛtya pariveṣayet | iti | (MatPu 17.28)
hastenāpi na sākṣād deyam | api tu darvyādidvārā | ata eva vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
hastadattās tu ye snehā lavaṇavyañjanādayaḥ |
dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhanti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam || iti | (VaDh 14.31)
viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
nāpavitreṇa hastena naikena ca vinākuśam |
nāyasenaiva pātreṇa śrāddheṣu pariveṣayet || iti |
āyasena ayomayapātreṇa naiva pariveṣayet | pariveṣaṇe kāni pātrāṇi praśastānīty apekṣite viṣṇuḥ: “ghṛtādidāne taijasāni pātrāṇi khaḍgapātrāṇi [419] phalgupātrāṇi ca praśastāni | atra ca pitṛgāthā bhavati |
sauvarṇarājatābhyāṃ ca khaḍgenaudumbareṇa ca |
dattam akṣayatāṃ yāti phalgupātreṇa vāpy atha || iti | (ViDh 79.22–24)
khaḍgena khaḍgamṛgaśṛṅgakṛtadarvyādinā | phalgupātreṇa kākaudumbarikākhyavṛkṣadārukṛta-darvyādinā | anyāny api pariveṣaṇīyāny āha manuḥ |
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca vividhaṃ mūlāni ca phalāni ca |
hṛdyāni caiva māṃsāni pānāni surabhīṇi ca ||
upanīya tu tat sarvaṃ śanakaiḥ susamāhitaḥ |
pariveṣayet tu prayataḥ guṇān sarvān pracodayan || iti | (MDh 3.227–28)
śaunako ‘pi |
pākaṃ sarvam upānīya nivedya ca pṛthak pṛthak |
vidhinā daivapūrvaṃ tu pariveṣaṇam ārabhet ||
[420] iti | saṃpāditaṃ sarvaṃ pātreṣu prakṣipya pātrābhimantraṇaṃ kuryāt | tad āha pracetāḥ: “sarvaṃ prakṛtaṃ datvā pātram ālabhya japet” iti | atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
datvānnaṃ pṛthivīpātram iti pātrābhimantraṇam |
kṛtvedaṃ viṣṇur ity anne dvijāṅguṣṭhaṃ niveśayet || iti | (YDh 1.138)
anantarakartavyam āha atriḥ |
hastena muktam annādyam itdam annam itīrayet |
svāheti ca tataḥ kuryāt svasattāvanivartanam ||
gotrasaṃbandhanāmāni idam annaṃ tataḥ svadhā |
pitṛkramād udīryeti svasattāṃ vinivartayet || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – viśvebhyo devebhya iti devatoddeśena śabdoccāraṇānantaram idam annam ity uccārayet | tataḥ svāheti mantram uccārayet | tato na mameti svatvaparityāgaṃ kuryāt | tataḥ pitrādikramāt saṃbandhagotranāmoccāraṇapūrvakaṃ devatoddeśaṃ kṛtvedam annam [421] iti pradeyaṃ dravyaṃ nirdiśya svadheti kavyadānaprakāśakaṃ mantraṃ uccārya na mameti svatvaparityāgaṃ kuryāt | anantarakṛtyam āha laghuyamaḥ |
annahīnaṃ kriyāhīnaṃ mantrahīnaṃ ca yad bhavet |
sarvam acchidram ity uktvā tato yatnena bhojayet || iti |
“acchidraṃ jāyatām” ity uktyanantarakartavyam āha pracetāḥ |
āpośānaṃ pradāyātha sāvitrīṃ trir japed atha |
madhuvātā iti tṛcaṃ madhv ity etat trikaṃ tathā || iti |
madhu ity etat trir āvartanīyam iti caturthapādasyārthaḥ | sāvitrīṃ savyāhṛtikāṃ japet | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
savyāhṛtikāṃ gāyatrīṃ madhuvātā iti tṛcam |
japtvā yathāsukhaṃ vācyaṃ bhuñjīraṃs te ‘pi vāgyatāḥ || iti | (YDh 1.239)
yathāsukham ity atra juṣadhvam ity adhyāhārah | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
juṣadhvam iti te coktāḥ samyag vidhṛtabhājanāḥ |
kṛtamaunāḥ samaśnīyur apośānād anantaram || iti |
śrāddhabhoktā baliṃ na dadyāt | ata eva atriḥ |
datte vāpy atha vādatte bhūmau yo niḥkṣiped balim |
tad annaṃ niṣphalaṃ yāti nirāśaiḥ pitṛbhir gatam ||
[422] iti | bhojanpakramānantarakartavyam āha kātyāyanaḥ: “aśnatsu japed vyāhṛtipūrvāṃ gāyatrīṃ sapraṇavāṃ sakṛt trir vā rakṣoghnīḥ pitryamantrān puruṣasūktam apratiratham anyāni ca pavitrāṇi” iti | manur api |
svādhyāyaṃ śrāvayet pitrye dharmaśāstrāṇi caiva hi |
ākhyānānītihāsāṃś ca purāṇāni khilāni ca || iti | (MDh 3.232)
atra sūktajapo yajñopavītinā kartavyaḥ | ata eva jamadagniḥ |
apasavyena kartavyaṃ sarvaṃ śrāddhaṃ yathāvidhi |
sūktastotrajapaṃ muktvā viprāṇāṃ ca visarjanam || iti |
atha dātṛbhoktṛniyamāḥ
dātṛbhoktṛniyamam. āha vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
apekṣitaṃ yo na dadyāt śrāddhārtham upakalpitam |
kṛpaṇo mandabuddhis tu na sa śrāddhaphalaṃ labhet ||
apekṣitaṃ yācitavyaṃ śrāddhārtham upakalpitam |
na yācate dvijo mūḍhaḥ sa bhavet pitṛghātakaḥ || iti |
nanu,
[423]        yācate yadi dātāraṃ brāhmaṇo jñānavarjitaḥ |
pitaras tasya kupyanti dātur bhoktur na saṃśayaḥ ||
iti |
kṛcchradvādaśarātreṇa mucyate ‘karmaṇas tataḥ |
tasmād vidvān naiva dadyān na yācen na ca dāpayet ||
iti vāyupurāṇa–smṛtī virudhyeyātām iti cet,
maivam, tayor anupakalpitaviṣayatvenāpy upapatteḥ | upakalpitavastuno ‘py atyantādhikasya dānapratigrahau śaṅkhalikhitābhyāṃ niṣiddhau: “nātyantādhikaṃ dadyāt | na pratigṛhṇīyāt” iti | anapekṣitavastuno nivāraṇaprakāram āha nigamaḥ |
nānnapānādikaṃ śrāddhe vāreyan mukhataḥ kvacit |
aniṣṭatvād bahutvād vā vāraṇaṃ hastasaṃjñayā || iti |
vāraṇaṃ yācanasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham, mukhato yācane maunabhaṅgāviśeṣāt | apekṣitaṃ vastu dadāmīty uktvā na deyam | tathā ca yamaḥ |
yāvad dhaviṣyaṃ bhavati yāvad iṣṭaṃ pradīyate |
tāvad aśnanti pitaro yāvan nāha dadāmy aham || iti |
bhojanakāle dātṛniyamā brahmāṇḍapurāṇe darśitāḥ |
[424]        na cāśru pātayet jātu nāyuktāṃ girim īrayet |
na codvīkṣeta bhuñjānān na ca kurvīta matsaram ||
na dīno nāpi vā kruddho na caivānyamanā naraḥ |
ekāgram ādhāya manaḥ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt sadā budhaḥ || iti |
bhoktṛniyamam āha sumantuḥ |
akrodhano rasān samyag adyād yad yasya rocate |
ā tṛpter bhojanaṃ teṣāṃ kāmato nāvaśeṣaṇam || iti |
nigame ‘pi |
tūṣṇīṃ bhuñjīran na vilokayamānā anuddhṛtya pātram | iti |
baudhāyano ‘pi |
pādena pādam ākramya yo bhuṅkte ‘nāpadi dvijaḥ |
naivāsau bhojayet śrāddhe nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ || iti |
pracetā api |
pītvāpośānam aśnīyāt pātre dattam agarhitam |
sarvendriyāṇāṃ cāpalyaṃ na kuryāt pāṇipādayoḥ || iti |
manur api |
atyuṣṇaṃ sarvam annaṃ syād aśnīraṃś caiva vāgyatāḥ |
na ca dvijātayo brūyur dātrā pṛṣṭā havirguṇān || iti | (MDh 3.236)
aśnīraṃs caivety evakāraḥ pramādāt parasparasaṃsparśe ‘pi bhojanānivṛttyarthaḥ | ata eva śaṅkhaḥ |
[425]        śrāddhapaṅktau tu bhuñjāno brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇaṃ spṛśet |
tad annam atyajan bhuktvā gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet || iti |
punaḥ sa eva |
huṅkāreṇāpi yo brūyād dhastād vāpi guṇān vadet |
bhūtalāc coddharet pātraṃ muñced dhastena vāpi tat ||
pauḍhapādo bahiḥkakṣo bahirjānukaro ‘tha vā |
aṅguṣṭhena vināśnāti mukhaśabdena vā punaḥ ||
pītāvaśiṣṭaṃ toyādi punar uddhṛtya vā pibet |
khāditārdhaṃ punaḥ khāden modakāni phalāni vā ||
mukhena vā dhamed annaṃ niṣṭhīved bhājane ‘pi vā |
ittham aśnan dvijaḥ śrāddhaṃ hatvā gacchaty adhogatim || iti |
prauḍhapādaḥ āsanādyārūḍhapādaḥ | bahiḥkakṣaḥ uttaravāsobahirbhūtakakṣadvitayaḥ | dātṛniyamam āha śaṅkhaḥ |
śrāddhe niyuktān bhuñjānān na pṛcchel lavaṇādiṣu |
ucchiṣṭāḥ pitaro yānti pṛcchato nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
dātuḥ patati bāhur vai jihvā bhoktuś ca bhidyate || iti |
anyān api bhoktṛniyamān āha pracetāḥ |
na spṛśed vāmahastena bhuñjāno ‘nnaṃ kadācana |
na pādau na śiro bastiṃ na padā bhājanaṃ spṛśet ||
bhojanaṃ tu na niḥśeṣaṃ kuryāt prājñaḥ kathaṃcana |
anyatra dadhnaḥ kṣirād vā kṣaudrāt saktubhya eva ca ||
[426] iti | na ca, “na nindeyur nāvaśeṣayeyuḥ” iti jamagagnivacanavirodhaḥ iti vācyam, tasyādhikāvaśeṣaṇaviṣayatvāt | ata evoktaṃ tenaiva: “alpaṃ punar utsraṣṭavyaṃ tasyāsaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ bhāgadeyatvāt” iti | ucchiṣṭasyāsaṃskṛtapramītādibhāgadheyatvaṃ ca manur āha |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syād dharbheṣu vikiraś ca yaḥ || iti | (MDh 3.245)
evaṃ niyamena bhuktavatsu vipreṣu yat kartavyaṃ tad āha pracetāḥ |
tṛptān budhvānnam ādāya satilaṃ pūrvavaj japet | iti |
pūrvavat savyāhṛtikāṃ gāyatrīṃ madhumatīṃ ca japet iti | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ: “gāyatrīṃ madhumatīṃ madhumadhv iti ca japtvā tṛptāḥ stheti pṛcchati” iti | praśnānantaraṃ vyāsaḥ |
tṛptāḥ stheti ca pṛṣṭās te brūyus tṛptāḥ sma ity atha | iti |
atha brāhmaṇabhojanānantrakartavyam
anantarakartavyam āha manuḥ |
[427]        sārvavarṇikam annādyaṃ saṃnīyāplāvya vāriṇā |
samutsṛjed bhuktavatām agrato vikiraṃ bhuvi || iti | (MDh 3.244)
sarvavarṇā vyañjanaviśeṣā yasmiṃs tat sārvavarṇikam | atra viśeṣam āha pracetāḥ: “ye agnīti bhuvi kṣipet” iti | vikiraṃ datvā ācāmet | ata eva marīciḥ |
śrāddheṣu vikiraṃ datvā yo nācāmen matikṣayāt |
pitaras tasya ṣaṇmāsaṃ bhavanty ucchiṣṭabhojinaḥ || iti |
anantaraṃ gaṇḍūṣārtham udakaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca madālasāvākyam |
tadvad ācamanārthāya dadyād āpaḥ sakṛt sakṛt | iti | (MāPu 31.53)
pitryabrāhmaṇapūrvakaṃ hastaprakṣālanācamanārtham udakaṃ dadyāt | tad āha viṣṇuḥ: “udaṅmukheṣv ācamanam ādau dadyāt tataḥ [428] prāṅmukheṣu | tataś ca suprokṣitam iti śrāddhadeśaṃ saṃprokṣya darbhapāṇiḥ sarvaṃ kuryāt” iti (ViDh 73.25) | paitṛkabrāhmaṇeṣu prathamaṃ hastaprakṣālanapūrvakam ācamanārtham udakaṃ datvā paścād vaiśvadaivaikabrāhmaṇeṣu datvānantaraṃ prokṣitam iti mantreṇa śrāddhadeśaṃ saṃprokṣya darbhapāṇiḥ sarvam upari karmajātaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |\
atha piṇḍanirvapaṇam
tataḥ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuryāt | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sarvam annam upādāya satilaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau piṇḍān dadyāt tu pitṛyajñavat || iti | (YDh 1.242)
ayam arthaḥ – brāhmaṇārthapakvaṃ tilamiśraṃ sarvam annam upādāya piṇḍān kṛtvā ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhau piṇḍapitṛyajñakalpena piṇḍān dadyāt | etac ca caruśrapaṇābhāve veditavyam | yadā [429] tu caruśrapaṇasadbhāvas tadāgnaukaraṇaśiṣṭacaruśeṣeṇa saha sarvam annam ādāya agnisaṃnidhau piṇḍān dadyāt | tadāha manuḥ |
trīṃs tu tasmād dhaviḥśeṣāt piṇḍān kṛtvā samāhitaḥ |
audakenaiva vidhinā nirvaped dakṣiṇāmikhaḥ || iti | (MDh3.215)
ucchiṣṭasaṃdhisvarūpam āha atriḥ |
pitṝṇām āsanasthānād agratas triṣv aratniṣu |
ucchiṣṭasaṃnidhānaṃ tu nocchiṣṭāsanasaṃnidhau || iti |
triṣv aratniṣv iti ucchiṣṭasaṃsargarahitāsannadeśopalakṣaṇārtham | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
aratnimātram utsṛjya piṇḍāṃs tatra pradāpayet |
yatropaspṛśatāṃ vāpi prāpnuvanti na bindavaḥ || iti |
piṇḍadāne kālam āha manuḥ |
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kecit purastād eva kurvate | iti | (MDh 3.261)
bhojanāt purastād arcanānantaraṃ agnaukaraṇānantaraṃ vā piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kecid icchanti | apare tu bhojanānantaram ācamanād arvāg ūrdhvaṃ vā brāhmaṇavisarjanāt paścād vā piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kvacid icchantīti kecid ity anenāvagamyate | bhojanāt purastād piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt prāg vihiteṣv apraśasteṣu śrāddheṣu | sapiṇḍīkaraṇādipraśastaśrāddheṣu paścād eva piṇḍanirvapaṇam | tad āha laugākṣiḥ |
[430]        apraśasteṣu yāgeṣu pūrvaṃ piṇḍāvanejanam |
bhojanasya praśaste tu paścād evopakalpayet || iti |
ācamanāt prāg ūrdhvaṃ vā brāhmaṇavisarjanād ūrdhvaṃ vā piṇḍanirvapaṇam iti pakṣāḥ śākhābhedena vyavasthitāḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
munibhir bhinnakāleṣu piṇḍadānaṃ tu yat smṛtam |
tat svaśākhāmataṃ yatra tatra kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ || iti |
tatra āśvalāyanaśākhinām ācamanāt prāg ūrdhvaṃ veti pakṣayoḥ samatvena vikalpaḥ | tathā ca āśvalāyanaḥ: “bhuktavatsv ācānteṣu piṇḍān nidadhyāt | ācānteṣv eke” iti (ĀśGṛ 4.8.12–13) | yasyāṃ śākhāyāṃ kālaviśeṣo na śrutaḥ tatra prayogasaukaryārtham ācamanād ūrdhvaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇapakṣa eva grahītavyaḥ | piṇḍanirvapaṇetikartavyatā brahmāṇḍapurāṇe ‘bhihitā |
savyottarābhyāṃ pāṇibhyāṃ kuryād ullakhanaṃ dvijaḥ |
pragharṣaṇaṃ tataḥ kuryāt śrāddhakarmaṇy atandritaḥ ||
khaṇḍanaṃ peṣaṇaṃ caiva tathaivollekhanakriyā |
vajreṇātha kuśair vāpi ullekhet tu mahīṃ dvijaḥ || iti |
khaṇḍanaṃ kuśādeś chedanam | peṣaṇaṃ bhūgharṣaṇam | vajraśabdena sphya ucyate | ullekhanamantraś ca kātyāyanena darśitaḥ: “ullikhyāpahatā iti” iti | anantaraṃ prokṣayet, [431] “tām abhyukṣya” iti āśvalāyanasmaraṇāt | tatra kuśān āstṛṇuyāt | tad uktaṃ brahmāṇapurāṇe |
kṣipet kuśāṃs tatra ca dakṣiṇāgrān | iti |
tato ‘vanejanaṃ kuryāt | tad āha sumantuḥ |
asāv avaneniṅkṣveti puruṣaṃ puruṣaṃ prati |
tris trir ekena hastena vidadhītāvanejanam || iti |
asāv iti pitrādīn nāmagotrābhyāṃ saṃbodhya ekena dakṣiṇahastena pripuruṣaṃ tris trir avanejanam udakanirvapaṇaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | atra āśvalāyanenāvanejane mantrāntaram uktam: “prācīnāvītī lekhāṃ trir udakenopanayec chundhantāṃ pitaraḥ” ityādi tad aviruddham | śākhābhedena vyavasthopapatteḥ | piṇḍadānamantrāḥ kātyāyanena darśitāḥ: “asāv etat ta iti ye catvā (?) mantrānviti (?) caike” iti | asāv ity amukagotrāmukaśarmann iti saṃbodhya etat te iti mantreṇa piṇḍadānaṃ kuryāt | eke ye ca tvām atrānviti mantreṇa piṇḍadānaṃ kuryād ity āhuḥ | piṇḍadānaṃ ca savyaṃ jānv ācya kartavyam | tad uktaṃ vāyupurāṇe |
madhusarpistilayutāṃs trīn piṇḍān nirvaped budhaḥ |
jānuṃ kṛtvā tathā savyaṃ bhūmau pitṛparāyaṇaḥ || (VāyPu 2.13.22)
[432] iti | piṇḍadānānantarakartavyam āha manuḥ |
nyupya piṇḍān pitṛbhyas tu prayato vidhipūrvakam |
teṣu darbheṣu taṃ hastaṃ nimṛjyāl lepabhāginām || iti | (MDh 3.216)
tatra mantro viṣṇunā darśitaḥ: “atra pitaro mādayadhvaṃ yathābhāgam āvṛṣāyadhvam iti darbhamūleṣu karāvagharṣaṇam” iti | anantarakartavyam āha kātyāyanaḥ: “atra pitara ity uktvodaṅmukha ātamanād āvṛtyāmīmadanta pitara iti japati” iti (ViSm 7.22) | ātamanāt prāṇāyāmotthavāyupīḍāvadhi udaṅmukha āsīt | tato ‘nucchvasann eva paryāvṛttyāmīmadanteti manasā japitvocchvaset | atra viśeṣaḥ karmapradīpe darśitaḥ |
vāmenāvartanaṃ kecid udagantaṃ pracakṣate |
savyaṃ gautamaśāṇḍilyau śāṇḍilyāyana eva ca ||
āvṛtya prāṇam āyamya pitṝn dhyāyan yathārhataḥ |
japaṃs tenaiva cāvṛtya tataḥ prāṇān pramocayet || iti | (KSm 2.17.21–22)
amīmadadantety anumantraṇānantaraṃ viṣṇuḥ [433]: “amīmadantety anumantrya śeṣavaghrāṇaṃ kṛtvā śundhantāṃ pitara iti pūrvavad udakaninayanaṃ piṇḍopari tato ‘sāv abhyaṅkṣvety abhyañjanaṃ dadyād asāv aṅkṣvety añjanam atha vastram abhāve daśām ūrṇāṃ vā etad vaḥ pitaro vāso mānotonyat pitaro yuṅdhvam” iti (ViDh 73.26) | tadanantaraṃ vyāghraḥ |
gandhapuṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ ca vinivedayet |
etad vaḥ pitaro vāso daśāṃ datvā pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
arcanānantaraṃ matsyaḥ |
yat kiṃcit pacyate gehe bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
anivedya ca bhoktavyaṃ piṇḍamūle kathaṃcana || iti | (MatPu 17.36)
tadanantaraṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
anabhyarcyodapātraṃ tu teṣām upari niḥkṣipet | iti |
anantaraṃ viṣṇuḥ: “athaitān upatiṣṭheta namo vaḥ pitara ityādinā manonvāhuvā saha iti tṛcāntenāthaitān piṇḍāṃś cālayet paretana pitara iti” (ViDh 73.27) [434] iti |
atha piṇḍadānānantarakṛtyam
anantarkṛtyam āha āśvalāyanaḥ: “agniṃ pratyeyād agne tamadhāścanna stomair iti agnim agnaukaraṇāgniṃ gārhapatyaṃ yad antarikṣaṃ pṛthivīm” iti | brāhmaṇahasteṣu sakṛt sakṛt apo dadyād ity arthaḥ | etad ācamanāt prāk piṇḍadānapakṣe veditavyam | ācānteṣu tu piṇḍadānapakṣe ācamya piṇḍadānaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇahasteṣūdakaṃ sakṛt sakṛd dadyāt | kuśāś ca deyāḥ | padmapurāṇe ‘pi |
ācānteṣūdakaṃ dadyāt puṣpāṇi sayavāni ca | iti |
atra yavagrahaṇaṃ tilopalakṣaṇārtham | caśabdaḥ punar apy udakadānasamuccayārthaḥ | kṛtakāryatvena teṣu ca yavādiṣu kutracit śucau deśe visṛṣṭeṣu punar udakādikaṃ dadyāt | tathā ca matsyaḥ |
ācānteṣu punar dadyāt jalapuṣpākṣatodakam | iti | (MatPu 16.47)
anantarakartavyam āha sa eva |
datvāśīḥ pratigṛhṇīyāt dvijebhyaḥ prāṅmukho budhaḥ |
aghorāḥ pitaraḥ santu santv ity ukte punar dvijaiḥ ||
gotraṃ tathā vardhatāṃ nas tathety uktaś ca taiḥ punaḥ |
dātāro no ‘bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca ||
[435]        śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahu deyaṃ ca no ‘stv iti |
etāḥ satyā āśiṣaḥ santu santv ity uktaś ca taiḥ punaḥ || iti || (MatPu 17.53–55)
atha svastivācanakam
anantaraṃ pātracālanaṃ kṛtvā svastivācanaṃ kuryāt | ata eva nārāyaṇaḥ |
acālayitvā tat pātraṃ svasti kurvanti ye dvijāḥ |
nirāśāḥ pitaras teṣāṃ śaptvā yānti yathāgatam || iti |
pātracālane viśeṣam āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
pātrāṇi cālayet śrāddhe svayaṃ śiṣyo ‘tha vā sutaḥ |
na strībhir na ca bālena nāsajātyā kathaṃcana || iti |
svastivācanaprakāram āha pāraskaraḥ: “svastīti bhagavan brūhīti vācanam” iti | yajñopavītī vaiśvadaivikahaste udakaṃ datvā purūravārdravasaṃjñakebhyo viśvebhyo devebhyaḥ svastīti bhagavan brūhi – iti kartā brūyāt | vipreṇa ca svasti iti vaktavyam | pitṛbrāhmaṇahasteṣv apy evam eva | anantarakṛtyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
svastivācyaṃ tataḥ kuryād akṣayyodakam eva ca | (YDh 1.243)
[436] iti | akṣayyam astv iti brāhmaṇahaste jalaṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ | mārkaṇḍeyo ‘pi |
pitṝṇāṃ nāmagotreṇa jalaṃ deyam anantaram |
brāhmaṇānāṃ dvijair vācyam akṣayyam idam astv iti || iti |
hasteṣv ity adhyāhṛtya yojanā ||
atha dakṣiṇādānam
anantaraṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt | manuḥ |
svastivācanakaṃ kuryāt akṣayyodakam eva ca |
satilaṃ nāmagotreṇa dadyāc chaktyā ca dakṣiṇām ||
gobhūhiraṇyavāsāṃsi bhavyāni śayanāni ca |
dadyād yad iṣṭaṃ viprāṇām ātmanaḥ pitur eva ca ||
vittaśāṭhyena rahitaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ prītim ācaran | iti |
atra viśeṣam āha pāraskaraḥ: “hiraṇyaṃ viśvebhyo devebhyo rajataṃ pitṛbhyaḥ | anyac ca go kṛṣṇājinaṃ yāvacchakyaṃ dadyāt |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānāṃ viprāṇāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ samaṃ deyaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ tv anusārataḥ” || iti |
anusārata iti nimantritabrāhmaṇavidyāguṇatāratamyenety arthaḥ | putruddeśena dakṣiṇādānaṃ prācīnāvītinā kāryam | ata eva jamadagniḥ |
[437]        apasavyaṃ tu tatrāpi matsyo bhagavān hi me manaḥ || iti |
tatrāpi dakṣiṇādāne ‘pīti | yat tu tenaivoktam,
sarvaṃ karmāpasavyena dakṣiṇādānavarjitam |
iti, tad brāhmaṇoddeśena dakṣiṇādānapakṣe draṣṭavyam | sa ca pakṣo devalena darśitaḥ |
ācāntebhyo dvijebhyas tu prayacched atha dakṣiṇām | iti |
brāhmaṇoddeśena dakṣiṇādāne kramam āha sa eva |
dakṣiṇāṃ pitṛviprebhyo dadyāt pūrvaṃ tato dvayoḥ | iti |
vaiśvadaivikabrāhmaṇayor ity arthaḥ | atra pitṛviprebhya iti samabhivyāhārāc caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī | anantaraṃ yājñavalkyaḥ |
datvā tu dakṣiṇāṃ śaktyā svadhākāram udāharet |
vācyatām ity anujñātaḥ prakṛtebhyaḥ svadhocyatām ||
brūyur astu svadhety ukte bhūmau siñcet tato jalam |
viśvedevāś ca prīyantāṃ vipraiś coktam idaṃ japet ||
dātāro no ‘bhivardhantāṃ vedāḥ santatir eva ca |
śraddhā ca no mā vyagamad bahu deyaṃ ca no ‘stv iti ||
ity uktvoktvā priyā vācaḥ praṇipatya pramādayet |
vāje vāje iti prītaḥ pitṛpūrvaṃ visarjayet || iti | (YDh 1.244–47)
[438]
atha madhyamapiṇḍāśanam
anantaraṃ putrārthi madhyamapiṇḍaṃ patnyai dadyāt | tathā ca vāyupurāṇam |
patnyai prajārthī dadyāt tu madhyamaṃ mantrapūrvakam | iti | (VāyPu 2.14.31)
madhyamaṃ piṇḍam ity arthaḥ | tatprakāraḥ | tatra prācīnāvītī “apāṃ tv auṣadhīnāṃ rasaṃ prāśayāmi bhūtakṛtaṃ garbhaṃ dhatsva” iti patnyā añjalau madhyamapiṇḍaṃ prayacchet | tatprāśanamantras tu matsyena darśitaḥ |
ādhatta pitaro garbham antaḥ santānavardhanam | iti | (MatPu 16.54)
manur api |
pativratā dharmapatnī pitṛpūjanatatparā |
madhyamaṃ tu tataḥ piṇḍaṃ adyāt samyak sutārthinī ||
āyuṣmantaṃ sutaṃ vindet yaśomedhāsamanvitam |
dhanavantaṃ prajāvantaṃ dhārmikaṃ sātvikaṃ tathā || (MDh 3.262–63)
[439] iti | piṇḍapradānasyāyogyatvam āha sa eva |
aprāptayauvane vṛddhe sagarbhe rogasaṃbhave |
bālaputrakalatre ‘pi na dadyāt piṇḍam añjalau || iti |
patnyā asaṃnidhānādau piṇḍapratipattim āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
anyadeśagatā patnī garbhiṇī rogiṇī tathā |
tadā taṃ jīrṇavṛṣabhaḥ chāgo vā bhoktum arhati || iti |
āpastambo ‘pi pratipattyantaram āha |
yadi patnī videśasthā ucchiṣṭā yadi vā mṛtā |
durātmā nānukūlā ca tasya piṇḍasya kā gatiḥ ||
ākāśaṃ gamayet piṇḍaṃ jalastho dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ sthānam ākāśaṃ dakṣiṇā dik tathaiva ca || iti |
yat tu devalenoktam,
tataḥ karmaṇi nirvṛtte tān piṇḍāṃs tadanantaram |
brāhmaṇo ‘gnir ajo gaur vā bhakṣayed apsu vā kṣipet ||
iti, tat putrārthitvābhāvaviṣayam | tīrthaśrāddhe piṇḍānām apsv eva prakṣepaḥ | tad uktaṃ viṣṇudharmottare |
tīrthaśrāddhe sadā piṇḍān kṣipet tīrthe samāhitaḥ |
dakṣiṇābhimukho bhūtvā pitryā dik sā prakīrtitā || iti | (ViDhPu 1.141.8)
[440]
athocchiṣṭamārjanam
anantaram ucchiṣṭaṃ saṃmārjayet | ata eva yājñvalkyaḥ |
[prakṣipet] satsu vipreṣu dvijocchiṣṭaṃ ca mārjayet || iti | (YDh 1.257)
ayam arthaḥ – vipravisarjanāt prāg ucchiṣṭaṃ na mārjayet | api tu teṣu visarjiteṣu piṇḍapratipattau ca kṛtāyām iti | yat tu vyāsenoktam,
ucchiṣṭaṃ na pramṛjyāt tu yāvan nāstam ito raviḥ |
iti, yac ca vasiṣṭhenoktam,
śrāddhe nodvāsanīyāni ucchiṣṭāny ā dinakṣayāt |
cyotante vai svadhākārās tāḥ pibanty akṛtodakāḥ || (VaDh 11,21)
iti, tad gṛhāntarasaṃbhavaviṣayam | ata eva pracetāḥ |
bhṛtyavargāvṛto bhūkkte kavyaśeṣaṃ savgotrajaiḥ |
āsāyaṃ śrāddhakālāyāṃ dvijocchiṣṭaṃ na mārjayet || iti |
atha yajamānaḥ svayaṃ bhuñjīta
ucchiṣṭamārjanānantaraṃ vaiśvadevaṃ kṛtvā śeṣam annaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha bhuñjīta | tathā ca matsyapurāṇam |
[441]        tataś ca vaiśvadevānte sabhṛtyasutabāndhavaḥ |
bhuñjītātithisaṃyuktaḥ sarvaṃ pitṛniṣevitam || iti | (MatPu 16.55)
yadā śrāddhaṃ nirvartya vaiśvadevādikaṃ kriyate tadā taccheṣād eva tat kāryam | tad āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
śrāddhaṃ nirvartya vidhivad vaiśvadevādikaṃ tataḥ |
kuryād bhikṣāṃ tato dadyād dhantakārādikaṃ tathā || iti |
ādiśabdena nityaśrāddhaṃ parigṛhyate | tata iti pitṛpākaśeṣād ity arthaḥ | nityaśrāddhaṃ pṛthakpākena vā kāryam | ata eva tad adhikṛtya mārkaṇḍeyaḥ: “pṛthakpākena vety anye” iti | atra nityaśrāddham apy aniyatam | tad āha sa eva |
nityakriyāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca kecid icchanti mānavāḥ |
na pitṝṇāṃ tathaivānye śeṣaṃ pūrvavad ācaret || iti |
yat tu laugākṣiṇoktam,
[442]        pitrarthaṃ nirvapet pākaṃ vaiśvadevārtham eva ca |
vaiśvadevaṃ na pitrarthaṃ na dārśaṃ vaiśvadaivikam ||
iti, tad āhitāgnikartṛkaśrāddhaviṣayam | āhitāgneḥ śrāddhāt prāg eva vaiśvadevasya tenaiva vihitatvāt |
pakṣāntaṃ karma nirvartya vaiśvdevaṃ ca sāgnikaḥ |
piṇḍayajñaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tato ‘nvāhāryakaṃ budhaḥ || iti |
pakṣāntaṃ karma agnyādhānam | anvāhāryakaṃ darśaśrāddham | yat tu purāṇavacanam,
prativāsariko homaḥ śrāddhādau kriyate yadi |
devā havyaṃ na gṛhṇanti kavyaṃ ca pitaras tathā ||
iti, tat pitṛpākād vaiśvadevakaraṇe veditavyam | ata eva paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pitṛpākāt samuddhṛtya vaiśvadevaṃ karoti yaḥ |
āsuraṃ tad bhavec chrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ nopatiṣṭhate || iti |
yadāgnaukaraṇānantaraṃ vikiraṇānantaraṃ vā vaiśvadevikaṃ karma kriyate tadāpi pṛthakpākād eva tat kāryam | taccheṣād vaiśvadevakaraṇe doṣasyātrāpi samānatvāt | agnaukaraṇānantaraṃ vaiśvadevakaraṇaṃ ca smṛtyantare ‘bhihitam |
vaiśvadevāhutīr agnāv arvāg brāhmaṇabhojanāt |
juhuyād bhūtayajñādi śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ smṛtam || iti |
yadi pitṛpākaśeṣād vaiśvadevaṃ yadi vā pākāntarāt ubhayathāpi pitṛpākaśeṣād eva bhojanam | tad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[443]        pradakṣiṇam anuvrajya bhuñjīta pitṛsevitam | iti | (YDh 1.249)
asati tu pitṛśeṣe pākāntaraṃ kṛtvāpi bhoktavyam eva, abhojanasya niṣiddhatvāt | tathā ca devalaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tu yo vipro na bhuṅkte ‘tha kadācana |
devā havyaṃ na gṛhṇanti kavyāni pitaras tathā || iti |
yataḥ pitṛśeṣabhojanaṃ nityam | ata evaikādaśyādau nityopavāsapakṣe bhojanapratyāmnāyaḥ smaryate |
upavāso yadā nityaḥ śrāddhaṃ naimittikaṃ bhavet |
upavāsaṃ tadā kuryād āghrāya pitṛsevitam || iti |
pitṛsevitabhojananiyamo ‘nujñāpakṣa eva | ata eva śātātapaḥ |
śeṣam annam anujñātaṃ bhuñjīta tadanantaram |
iṣṭaiḥ sārdhaṃ tu vidhivad buddhimān susamāhitaḥ || iti |
bhojanānantaraṃ dātṛbhoktror niyamam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
tāṃ niśāṃ brahmacārī syāc chrāddhakṛc chrāddhikaiḥ saha |
anyathā vartamānau tau syātāṃ nirayagāminau || iti |
matsyapurāṇe ‘pi |
punarbhojanam adhvānaṃ yānam āyāsamaithunam |
śrāddhakṛc chrāddhabhuk caiva sarvam etad vivarjayet ||
svādhyāyaṃ kalahaṃ caiva divāsvāpaṃ ca svecchayā | (MatPu 16.56–57)
[444] iti | śrāddhabhojino viśeṣam āha yamaḥ |
punarbhojanam adhvānaṃ bhāram āyāsamaithunam |
saṃdhyāṃ pratigrahaṃ homaṃ śrāddhabhuk tv aṣṭa varjayet || iti |
saṃdhyāhomayoḥ pratiṣedhas tv akṛtaprāyaścittasya | kṛte tu prāyaścitte kuryād eva | ata eva bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
daśakṛtvaḥ pibec cāpo gāyatryā śrāddhabhug dvijaḥ |
tataḥ saṃdhyām upāsīta japec ca juhuyād api || iti |
evamuktarītyā pārvaṇaṃ kartum asamarthaḥ saṃkalpavidhinā śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | td uktaṃ smṛtyantare |
aṅgāni pitṛyajñasya yadā kartuam na śaknuyāt |
saṃkalpaśrāddham evāsau kuryād arghādivarjitam || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – ālasyena yo ‘gniṃ tyajati sa tyaktāgniḥ | na tasya śrāddhe ‘dhikāraḥ | kiṃ tu tyaktāgnivyatiriktasya sāgnikasya | vidhurādeś ca piṇḍopalakṣitaśrāddhe ‘pi adhikāra uktaḥ | yasmād atyaktāginādhikāriṇā śrāddham avaśyaṃ kartavyaṃ tasmād vistṛte pārvaṇavidhāv aśaktaḥ saṃkalpavidhānena kuryād iti | saṃkalpavidhānalakṣaṇaṃ sa evāha |
[445]        saṃkalpaṃ tu yadā kuryāt na kuryāt pātrapūraṇam |
nāvāhanāgnaukaraṇaṃ piṇḍāṃś caiva na dāpapet || iti |
ucchiṣṭapiṇḍo na dātavyaḥ,
saṃkalpaṃ tu yadā śrāddhaṃ na kuryāt pātrapūraṇam |
vikiraś ca dātavyaḥ  *  *  *  *  ||
iti smṛtyantarāt ||
atha pratyābdikaśrāddham
evaṃ darśe kartavyaṃ pārvaṇaśrāddham uktam | atha tadvikṛtibhūtaṃ pratyābdikaṃ nirūpyate | tatra laugākṣiḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kuryād avaśyaṃ tu pramītapitṛkaḥ svayam |
indukṣaye māsi māsi vṛddhau pratyabdam eva ca || iti |
tatretikartavyatām āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
pituḥ pitṛgaṇasthasya kuryāt pārvaṇavat sutaḥ |
pratyabdaṃ pratimāsaṃ ca vidhir jñeyaḥ sanātanaḥ || iti |
pitṛgaṇasthaḥ sapiṇḍīkṛtaḥ | tasya pratisāṃvatsarikam anumāsikaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ pārvaṇavidhinā kuryāt | jamadagnir api |
āsādya sahapiṇḍatvam auraso vidhivat sutaḥ |
kurvīta darśavac chrāddhaṃ mātāpitror mṛte ‘hani ||
[446] iti |
nanu – darśātideśān mātāmahānām api śrāddhaṃ prasajyeta |
nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, pratyābdike ṣaṭpiṇḍānāṃ paryudastatvāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
karṣūsamanvitaṃ muktvā tathābdaṃ śrāddhaṣoḍaśam |
pratyābdikaṃ ca śeṣeṣu piṇḍāḥ syuḥ ṣaḍ iti sthitiḥ || iti | (KSm 3.24.14)
karṣūsamanvitaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | yat tu yamenoktam,
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ sutaiḥ |
mātāpitroḥ pṛthak kāryam ekoddiṣṭaṃ mṛte ‘hani || iti |
yac ca mṛtāhaṃ prakṛtya vyāsenoktam,
ekoddiṣṭaṃ parityajya pārvaṇaṃ kurute naraḥ |
akṛtaṃ tad vijānīyād bhavec ca pitṛghātakaḥ || iti |
atra pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭayor vikalpaḥ | tatrāpi vṛddhācārato vyavasthā | amāvāsyāyāṃ pretapakṣe ca mṛtānāṃ pārvaṇaṃ niyatam | tad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
amāyāṃ tu kṣayo yasya pretapakṣe ‘tha vā punaḥ |
pārvaṇaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ naikoddiṣṭaṃ kadācana || iti |
yates tu na kvāpy ekoddiṣṭam | kiṃ tu sarvatra pārvaṇam eva | tathā ca pracetāḥ |
daṇḍagrahaṇamātreṇa naiva preto bhaved yatiḥ |
ataḥ sutena kartavyaṃ pārvaṇaṃ tasya sarvadā || iti |
atra kecit pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭayor anyathā vyavasthām āhuḥ,
pratyabdaṃ pārvaṇenaiva vidhinā kṣetrajairasau |
kuryātām itare kuryur ekoddiṣṭaṃ sutā daśa ||
[447] iti jātūkarṇyavacanāt |
tad ayuktam,
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu kartavyam aurasena mṛte ‘hani |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ mātāpitros tu pārvaṇam ||
iti paiṭhīnasivacanavirodhāt | jātūkarṇyavacanaṃ tu kṣayāhavyatiriktapratyabda-kartavyākṣayyatṛtīyādiviṣayatvenāpy upapadyate | yat tu sumantunoktam,
kuryāc ca vidhivac chrāddhaṃ pārvaṇaṃ yo ‘gnimān dvijaḥ |
pitror anagimān dhīra ekoddiṣṭaṃ mṛte ‘hani ||
iti, tad ayuktam,
bahvagnayas tu ye viprā ye caikāgnaya eva ca |
teṣāṃ sapiṇḍanād ūrdhvam ekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam ||
iti smṛtyantare sāgnikasyāpy ekoddiṣṭavidhānāt | ekoddiṣṭalakṣaṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ devahīnam ekārghyaikapavitrakam |
āvāhanāgnaukaraṇarahitaṃ hy apasavyavat ||
upatiṣṭhatām ity akṣayyasthāne vipravisarjane |
abhiramyatām iti vaded brūyus te ‘bhiratāḥ sma ha || iti | (YDh 1.251–52)
kātyāyano ‘pi: “athaikoddiṣṭam ekaṃ pātram eko ‘rghya ekaḥ piṇḍaḥ nāvāhanaṃ nāgnaukaraṇaṃ nātra viśvedevāḥ [448] svaditam iti tṛptipraśnaḥ susvaditam ity anujñānam upatiṣṭhatām ity akṣayyasthāne ‘bhiramyatām iti visarge abhiratāḥ smety apare” iti | tac caikoddiṣṭaṃ trividham navaṃ navamiśraṃ purāṇaṃ ceti | atra prathamāhādyekādaśāhāntavihitaṃ navaśrāddham | tathā ca aṅgirāḥ |
prathame ‘hni tṛtīye ‘hni pañcame saptame ‘pi vā |
navamaikādaśe caiva tan navaśrāddham ucyate || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
saptame ‘hni tṛtīye ‘hni prathame navame tathā |
ekādaśe pañcame syur navaśrāddhāni ṣaṭ tathā ||
kecit pañcaiva navamaṃ bhaved antaritaṃ yadi |
ekādaśe ‘hni tat kuryād iti smṛtikṛto viduḥ || iti |
atra pakṣadvayasya vyavasthā śivasvāminā darśitā |
navaśrāddhāni pañcāhur āśvalāyanaśākhinaḥ |
āpastambāḥ ṣaḍ ity āhur vibhāṣā taittirīyiṇām || iti |
vaiśyādīnāṃ viśeṣo bhaviṣyatpurāṇe darśitaḥ |
nava sapta viśāṃ rājñāṃ navaśrāddhāny anukramāt |
ādyantayor varṇayos tu ṣaḍ ity āhur maharṣayaḥ ||
[449] iti | ṣaṇṇāṃ navaśrāddhānām upari kartavyaṃ māsikaṃ navamiśram | tathā ca āśvalāyanaḥ: “navamiśraṃ ṣaḍuttaram” iti | māsikānām upari kartavyaṃ pratyābdikādi purāṇam | ata eva hārītena prāyaścittakāṇḍe krameṇa navamāsikayoḥ prāyaścittam abhidhāyottarakālīnaṃ śrāddhaṃ purāṇaśabdena vyavahṛtya prāyaścittaṃ vihitam |
cāndrāyaṇaṃ navaśrāddhe prājāpatyaṃ tu māsike |
ekāhas tu purāṇeṣu prāyaścittaṃ vidhīyate || iti | (HārSm 21.17)
atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇam
itthaṃ pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭe abhihite | athobhayātmakaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam ucyate | tatra laugākṣiḥ |
śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśāpādya vidadhīta sapiṇḍanam | iti |
ṣoḍaśa śrāddhāni jātūkarṇyena darśitāni |
dvādaśa pratimāsyāni ādyaṣāṇmāsike tathā |
traipakṣikābdike ceti śrāddhāny etāni ṣoḍaśa || iti |
atrādyaṣāṇmāsikābdikaśabdāḥ ūnamāsikonaṣāṇmāsikonābdikaparāḥ | dvādaśamāsikānāṃ kālo yājñavalkyena darśitaḥ |
[450]        mṛte ‘hani tu kartavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivam ādyam ekādaśe ‘hani || iti | (YDh 1.256)
vatsaraṃ vatsaraparyantaṃ māsi māsi mṛte ‘hani śrāddhaṃ kartavyam | sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ mṛte ‘hani kartavyam | ādyaṃ tu māsikam ekādaśe ‘hani kartavyam | ūnaṣāṇmāsikādīnāṃ kālam āha gālavaḥ |
ūnaṣāṇmāsikaṃ ṣaṣṭhe māsārdhe hy ūnamāsikam |
taipakṣikaṃ tripakṣe syād ūnābdaṃ dvādaśe tathā || iti |
ślokagautamo ‘pi |
ekadvitridinair ūne tribhāgenaona eva vā |
śrāddhāny ūnābdinādīni kuryād ity āha gautamaḥ || iti | (ŚloGSm 3.27)
ūnamāsikasya kālavikalpam āha gobhilaḥ |
maraṇād dvādaśāhe syān māsy ūne vonamāsikam | iti |
ūnānāṃ varjyaṃ kālam āha gārgyaḥ |
nandāyāṃ bhārgavadine caturdaśyāṃ tripuṣkare |
ūnaśrāddhe na kurvīta gṛhī putradhanakṣayāt || iti |
marīcir api |
dvipuṣkare ca nandāsu sinīvālyāṃ bhṛgor dine |
caturdaśyāṃ ca nonāni kṛttikāsu tripuṣkare || iti |
tithivāranakṣatraviśeṣāṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ melanaṃ tripuṣkaram | [451] dvayor melanaṃ dvipuṣkaram | ke te viśeṣāḥ | dvitīyāsaptamīdvādaśyo bhadrātithayaḥ, bhānubhaumaśanaiścaravārāḥ, punarvasūttarāphalgunīviśākhottarāṣāḍhāpūrvābhādrapadānakṣatrāṇi |
evam etāni ṣoḍaśa śrāddhāni kṛtvā tataḥ sapiṇḍanaṃ vidadhīta | tasya kālam āha atriḥ: “atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | saṃvatsare pūrṇe dvādaśāhe vā” iti | kātyāyano ‘pi: “atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇam | saṃvatsare pūrṇe tripakṣe vā yad ahar vā vṛddhir āpadyate” iti | baudhāyano ‘pi: “atha saṃvatsare pūrṇe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tripakṣe vā tṛtīye māsi ṣaṣṭhe vaikādaśe vā dvādaśāhe vā” iti | ekādaśāhadvādaśāhatṛtīyapakṣatṛtīyamāsaṣaṣṭhamāsaikādaśamāsasaṃvatsarāntaśubhāgamāḥ ity aṣṭau kālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ | tatra vyavasthām āha hārītaḥ |
yā tu pūrvam amāvāsyā mṛtāhād daśamī bhahvet |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tasyāṃ kuryād eva suto ‘gnimān || iti | (HārSm 20.19)
mṛtāhād ūrdhvadinam ārabhyety arthaḥ | kārṣṇājinir api |
[452]        sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvavac cāgnimān sutaḥ |
parato daśarātrāc cet kuhūr abdoparītare || iti |
āhitāgnināmāvāsyāyāṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñasyāvaśyakartavyatvāt sapiṇḍīkaraṇam antareṇa tadasaṃbhavāc caikādaśe ‘hni darśāgame sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryam | piṇḍapitṛyajñakartavyatā tu śrūyate: “amāvāsyām aparāhṇe piṇḍapitṛyajñena caranti” iti | sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt pūrvaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñasyāsaṃbhavo gālavena darśitaḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt prete paitṛkaṃ padam āsthite |
āhitāgneḥ sinīvālyāṃ pitṛyajñaḥ pravartate || iti |
darśānāgame tu sāgnir dvādaśe ‘hni sapiṇḍanaṃ kuryāt | tad uktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
yajamāno ‘gnimān rājan pretaś cānagnimān bhavet |
dvādaśāhe bhavet kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ sutaiḥ || iti |
gobhilo ‘pi |
sāgnikas tu yadā kartā pretaś cānagnimān bhavet |
dvādaśāhe tadā kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ sutaiḥ || iti |
pretasya sāgnitve tṛtīyapakṣe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryam | tad āha sumantuḥ |
pretaś ced āhitāgniḥ syāt kartānagnir yadā bhavet |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tasya kuryāt pakṣe tṛtīyake ||
[453] iti | ubhayoḥ sāgnikatve dvādaśāhe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kāryam |
sāgnikas tu yadā kartā preto vāpy agnimān bhavet |
dvādaśāhe tadā kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ pituḥ || iti |
ubhayor anagnitve dvādaśāhādayaḥ sapta kālāḥ icchayā vikalpyante | tad uktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt yajamānas tva anagnimān |
anāhitāgneḥ pretasya pūrṇe ‘bde bharatarṣabha ||
dvādaśe ‘hani ṣaṣṭhe vā tripakṣe vā trimāsike |
ekādaśe ‘pi vā māsi maṅgalaṃ syād upasthitam || iti |
eteṣu saptasu kāleṣu dvādaśāhaḥ praśastaḥ | tad āha vyāghraḥ |
ānantyāt kuladharmāṇāṃ puṃsāṃ caivāyuṣaḥ kṣayāt |
asthiteś ca śarīrasya dvādaśāhaḥ praśasyate || iti |
etat sarvaṃ traivarṇikaviṣayam | śūdrasya tu dvādaśāha eva pratiniyataḥ | ata eva sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ |
mantravarjaṃ hi śūdrāṇāṃ dvādaśe ‘hani kīrtitam | iti |
yadā saṃvatsarapūrtteḥ prāg evaikādaśāhādiṣu ṣoḍaśaśrāddhāni kṛtvā sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kriyate tadā punar api svasvakāle māsikādīny āvartanīyāni | tad āha gobhilaḥ |
[454]        yasya saṃvatsarād arvāg vihitā tu sapiṇḍatā |
vidhivat tāni kurvīta punaḥ śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa || iti |
vidhivad iti yathāyogam ekoddiṣṭena pārvaṇena vā vidhinety arthaḥ | tad āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāk kuryāc chrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa |
ekoddiṣṭavidhānena kuryāt sarvāṇi tāni tu ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ yadā kuryāt tadā punaḥ |
pratyabdaṃ yo yathā kuryāt tathā kuryāt sadā punaḥ || iti |
āvartanaṃ cordhvabhāvinām eva nādhobhāvinām | tad āha kārṣṇājiniḥ |
arvāg abdād yatra yatra sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kṛtam |
tadūrdhvaṃ māsikānāṃ syād yathākālam anuṣṭhitiḥ || iti |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvam āvartanīyānām anumāsikādīnāṃ vṛddhiprāptau punar apakarṣaḥ kartavyaḥ | tad āha śāṭhyāyaniḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāg apakṛṣya kṛtāny api |
punar apy apakṛṣyante vṛddhyuttaraniṣedhanāt || iti |
niṣedhaś ca kātyāyanenoktaḥ |
nirvartya vṛddhitantraṃ tu māsikāni na tantrayet | iti |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇasya gauṇakālam āha ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaśrāddham uktakāle na cet kṛtam |
raudre haste ca rohiṇyāṃ maitrabhe vā samācaret || iti |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇetikartavyatām āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
[455]        gandhodakatilair yuktaṃ kuryāt pātracatuṣṭayam |
arghyārthaṃ pitṛpātreṣu pretapātraṃ prasecayet ||
ye samānā iti dvābhyāṃ śeṣaṃ pūrvavad ācaret || iti | (YDh 1.256, 254)
kātyāyano ‘pi: “tataḥ saṃvatsare pūrṇe catvāri pātrāṇi satilagandhodakaiḥ pūrayitvā trīṇi pitṝṇām | ekaṃ pretasya | pretapātraṃ pitṛpātreṣv āsiñcati | ye samānā iti dvābhyām | etena piṇḍo vyākhyātaḥ” iti | yadā pitā mriyate pitāmahas tiṣṭhati tadā prapitāmahādibhiḥ saha pituḥ sāpiṇḍyam | tad uktaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
mṛte pitari yasyātha vidyate ca pitāmahaḥ |
tena deyās trayaḥ piṇḍāḥ prapitāmahapūrvakāḥ || iti |
kecit tatra sāpiṇḍyam eva nābhyupagacchanti,
vyutkramāc ca pramītānāṃ naiva kāryā sapiṇḍatā |
iti vacanāt | apare punar asya vacanasya mātṛpitṛbhartṛvyatiriktaviṣayatvaṃ manyante | udāharanti ca skandapurāṇavacanam |
vyutkrameṇa mṛtānāṃ ca sapiṇḍīkṛtir iṣyate |
