yadi mātā yadi pitā bhartā caiṣa vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
[456] iti | atra vṛddhācārād vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | mātuḥ sāpiṇḍyaṃ pitāmahyādibhih saha kartavyam | tad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
mātuḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kathaṃ kāryaṃ bhavet sutaiḥ |
pitāmahyādibhiḥ sārdhaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
pramītapitṛkasya vikalpam āha yamaḥ |
jīvatpitā pitāmahyā mātuḥ kuryāt sapiṇḍatām |
pramītapitṛkaḥ pitrā pitāmahyātha vā sutaḥ || iti |
putrikāsuto mātṛsapiṇḍaṃ mātāmahādibhiḥ saha kuryāt | tathā ca baudhāyanaḥ |
ādiśet prathame piṇḍe mātaraṃ putrikāsutaḥ |
dvitīya pitaraṃ tasyās tṛtīye ca pitāmaham || iti |
anvārohaṇe tu bhartraiva sāpiṇḍyaṃ niyatam | tad āha śātātapaḥ |
mṛtā yānugatā nāthaṃ sā tena saha piṇḍatām |
arhati svargavāse ‘pi yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam || iti |
yamo ‘pi |
patyā caikena kartavyaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ striyāḥ |
sāmṛtāpi hi tenaikyaṃ gatā mantrāhutivrataiḥ || iti |
atraikyaśabdaḥ pitāmahyādivikalpavyāvṛttyarthaḥ | na tripuruṣavyāvṛttyarthaḥ | sapiṇḍīkaraṇasya pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭarūpatvāt | etat sarvaṃ brāhmādivivāheṣu draṣṭavyam | āsurādivivāhe tu caturdhā vikalpaḥ | tatra pakṣatrayam āha śātātapaḥ |
[457]        tanmātrā tatpitāmahyā tacchvaśrvā vā sapiṇḍanam |
āsurādivivāheṣu varṇānāṃ yoṣitāṃ bhavet || iti |
caturthaṃ pakṣam āha sumantuḥ |
pitā pitāmahe yojyaḥ pūrṇe saṃvatsare sutaiḥ |
mātā mātāmahe tadvad ity āha bhagavān śivaḥ || iti |
mātuḥ sāpiṇḍye gotraniyamam āha mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
brāhmādiṣu vivāheṣu yā tūḍhā kanyakā bhavet |
bhartṛgotreṇa kartavyā tasyāḥ piṇḍodakakriyā ||
āsurādivivāheṣu pitṛgotreṇa dharmavit || iti |
laugākṣir api |
mātāmahasya gotreṇa mātuḥ piṇḍodakakriyāḥ |
kurvīta putrikāputra evam āha prajāpatiḥ || iti |
patnyāḥ sāpiṇḍyaṃ darśayati paiṭhīnasiḥ |
aputrāyāṃ mṛtāyāṃ tu patiḥ kuryāt sapiṇḍanam |
śvaśrvādibhiḥ sahaivāsyāḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ bhavet || iti |
patyuḥ sāpiṇḍyam āha laugākṣiḥ |
[458]        sarvābhāve svayaṃ patnyaḥ svabhartṝṇām amantrakam |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryus tataḥ pārvaṇam eva ca || iti |
sumantur api |
aputre prasthite kartā nāsti cec chrāddhakarmaṇi |
tatra patny api kurvīta sāpiṇḍyaṃ pārvaṇaṃ tathā || iti |
yat tu smṛtyantaram,
aputrasya paretasya naiva kuryāt sapiṇḍatām |
āśaucam udakaṃ piṇḍam ekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam ||
aputrā ye mṛtāḥ kecit puruṣā vā tathā striyaḥ |
teṣāṃ sapiṇḍanābhāvad ekoddiṣṭaṃ na pārvaṇam ||
iti, tat putrotpādanavidhipraśaṃsāparatayā vyākhyeyam | yatīnāṃ sāpiṇḍyaṃ niṣedhaty uśanāḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ teṣāṃ na kartavyaṃ sutādibhiḥ |
tridaṇḍagrahaṇād eva pretatvaṃ naiva jāyate ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ na kurvīta yatīnāṃ caiva sarvadā |
ahany ekādaśe teṣāṃ pārvaṇaṃ tu vidhīyate || iti | (UśSm 7.19–20)
ity ubhayātmakaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ nirūpitam ||
atha pārvaṇavikṛtirūpaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ nirūpyate
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
evaṃ pradakṣiṇāvṛtko vṛddhau nāndīmukhān pitṝn |
yajeta dadhikarkandhumiśrāḥ piṇḍā yavaiḥ kriyā || (YDh 1.250)
[459] iti | evaṃ pārvaṇavad ity arthaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇudharmottare |
vṛddhau samarcayed vidvān nityaṃ nāndīmukhān pitṝn |
saṃpādito viśeṣas tu śeṣaṃ pārvaṇavad bhavet || iti | (ViDhPu 1.142.15)
pārvaṇavad ity anenāvāhanatripuruṣoddeśādayo ‘tidiśyante | ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ saṃpāditaḥ nāndīmukhasaṃjñakāḥ pitaraḥ iti | etac ca pradakṣiṇāvṛd badaradadhyādīnāṃ yājñavalkyoktānām itareṣāṃ ca viśeṣāṇām upalakṣaṇam | te ca viśeṣāḥ kātyāyanena darśitāḥ |
ābhyudayike śrāddhe pradakṣiṇam upacāraḥ | pitryamantravarjanam | ṛjavo darbhāḥ | yavais tilārthaḥ | susaṃpannam iti tṛptipraśnaḥ | susaṃpannam ity anujñā | dadhibadarākṣatamiśrāḥ piṇḍāḥ | nāndīmukhān pitṝn āvāhayiṣye – iti pṛcchati | nāndīmukhāḥ pitaraḥ prīyantām ity akṣayyasthāne | nāndīmukhān pitṝn arcayiṣye – iti pṛcchati | nāndīmukhāḥ pitaraḥ pitāmahāḥ prapitāmahāś ca prīyantām ity anena svadhāṃ kuryāt | yugmān āśayet | iti |
pracetā api |
mātṛśrāddhaṃ tu pūrvaṃ syāt pitṝṇāṃ tadanantaram |
tato mātāmahānāṃ ca vṛddhau śrāddhatrayaṃ smṛtam ||
[460]        na japet paitṛkaṃ japyaṃ na māṃsaṃ tatra dāpayet |
prāṅmukho devatīrthena kṣipraṃ deśavimārjanam || iti |
prāṅmukhaḥ piṇḍadānādikaṃ kuryād ity adhyāhāraḥ | ata eva pracetāḥ |
apasavyaṃ na kurvīta na kuryād apradakṣiṇam |
yathā copacared devān tathā vṛddhau pitṝn api ||
pradadyāt prāṅmukhaḥ piṇḍān vṛddhau savyena vāgyataḥ | iti |
piṇḍadāne viśeṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
prāṅmukho devatīrthena prākkūleṣu kuśeṣu ca |
datvā piṇḍān na kurvīta piṇḍapātram adhomukham || iti |
piṇḍadānaṃ na cocchiṣṭasaṃnidhau pradadyāt,
nāvāṅmukhaḥ piṇḍān vṛddhau nāmnā vāvāhya dadati |
iti śātātapasmaraṇāt | nāmoccāraṇaṃ ca prathamapiṇḍa eva, na dvitīye | tad uktaṃ caturviṃśatimate |
ekaṃ nāmnā paraṃ tūṣṇīṃ dadyāt piṇḍān pṛthak pṛthak | iti |
ekaikasmai dvau dvau piṇḍau | tatrādyaṃ nāmnā dvitīyaṃ tūṣṇīṃ dadyād ity arthaḥ | vṛddhiśrāddhe piṇḍadānaṃ vaikalpikam | tathā ca bhaviṣyatpurāṇam |
[461]        piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kuryāt na vā kuryān narādhipa |
vṛddhiśrāddhe mahābāho kuladharmān avekṣya tu || iti |
vṛddhiśrāddhanimittāny āha laugākṣiḥ |
navānnacaulagodāne somopāyanasaṃsave |
snānādhānavivāheṣu nāndīśrāddhaṃ vidhīyate || iti |
kārṣṇājinir api |
kanyāputravivāheṣu praveśe navaveśmanaḥ |
nāmakarmaṇi bālānāṃ cūḍākarmādike tathā ||
sīmantonnayane caiva putrādimukhadarśane |
nāndīmukhān pitṛgaṇān pūjayet prayato gṛhī || iti |
vṛddhagārgyo ‘pi |
agnyādhānābhiṣekādāv iṣṭāpūrte striyā ṛtau |
vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ prakurvīta āśramagrahaṇe tathā || iti |
atha śrāddhakartṛnirūpaṇam
itthaṃ śrāddhāni nirūpitāni | adhunā tatkartā nirūpyate | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
pramītasya pituḥ putraiḥ śrāddhaṃ deyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
jñātibandhusuhṛcchiṣyair ṛtvigbhṛtyapurohitaiḥ || iti |
viṣṇupurāṇe ‘pi |
putraḥ pautraḥ prapautro vā tadvad vā bhrātṛsantatiḥ |
[462]        sapiṇḍasantatir vāpi kriyārhā nṛpa jāyate || iti | (ViPu 3.13.30)
tatra mukhyānukalpau vivinakti śaṅkhaḥ |
pituḥ putreṇa kartavyā piṇḍadānodakakriyā |
putrābhāve tu patnī syāt patnyabhāve tu sodaraḥ || iti |
smṛtisaṃgrahe ‘pi |
putraḥ kuryāt pituḥ śrāddhaṃ patnī tu tadasaṃnidhau |
dhanahāry atha dauhitras tato bhrātā ca tatsutaḥ ||
bhrātuḥ sahodaro bhrātā kuryād dāhādi tatsutaḥ |
tatas ev asodaro bhrātā tadabhāve tu tatsutaḥ || iti |
putraśabdena mukhyā gauṇāś ca putrā gṛhyante | teṣāṃ sarveṣām abhāve pautraḥ kuryāt | tasyābhāve tu patnī | ata eva bṛhaspatinā pautrasya putrikāputrasāmyam uktam |
pautro ‘tha putrikāputraḥ svargaprāptikarāv ubhau |
rikthe ca piṇḍadāne ca samau tau parikīrtitau || iti |
na caitāvatā tayoḥ samavikalpaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ,
naiva pautreṇa kartavyaṃ putravāṃś cet pitāmahaḥ |
iti kātyāyanasmaraṇāt | “patnyabhāve tu sodaraḥ” ity ayaṃ kramaḥ patnyā dāyaharaṇe draṣṭavyaḥ | anyathā yo dāyaharaḥ sa eva kuryāt | ata eva viṣṇu-āpastambau: [463] “yaś cārthaharaḥ sa piṇḍadāyī” iti (ViSm 15.40) | putraḥ pitṛvittābhāve ‘pi piṇḍaṃ dadyāt tadabhāve sapiṇḍo dadyāt sapiṇḍābhāve samānodakādayaḥ kuryuḥ | tathā ca mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇam |
putrābhāve sapiṇḍās tu tadabhāve sahodakāḥ |
mātuḥ sapiṇḍā ye vā syur ye vā mātuḥ sahodakāḥ ||
kuryur enaṃ vidhiṃ samyak aputrasya sutāsutaḥ |
kuryān mātāmahāyaiva putrikātanayas tathā ||
sarvābhāve striyaḥ kuryuḥ svabhrtṝṇām amantrakam |
tadabhāve ca nṛpatiḥ kārayet tasya rikthataḥ ||
tajjātīyair naraiḥ samyak dāhādyāḥ sakalāḥ kriyāḥ |
sarveṣām eva varṇānāṃ bāndhavo nṛpatir yataḥ || iti | (MārPu 30.19–23)
sarvābhāve striyaḥ kuryur ity āsurādivivāhoḍhastrīviṣayam | ataḥ putrābhāve tu patnīty anena sahāvirodhaḥ, tasyāḥ patnītvābhāvāt | ata eva śātātapaḥ tasyāḥ patnītvaṃ niṣedhati |
krayakrītā tu yā nārī na sā patny abhidhīyate |
na sā daive na sā pitrye dāsīṃ tāṃ kavayo viduḥ ||
[464] iti | pūrvamadhamottarāsu putrādīnāṃ vyavasthitam adhikāraṃ darśayati parāśaro viṣṇupurāṇe |
pūrvāḥ kriyā madhyamāś ca tathaivottarasaṃjñitāḥ |
triprakārāḥ kriyā hy etās tāsāṃ bhedaṃ śṛṇuṣva me ||
ā dāhād ā daśāhāc ca madhye yāḥ syuḥ kriyā matāḥ |
tāḥ pūrvā madhyamā māsi māsy ekoddiṣṭasaṃjñitāḥ ||
prete pitṛtvam āpanne sapiṇḍīkaraṇād anu |
kriyante yāḥ kriyāḥ pitryāḥ procyante tā nṛpottarāḥ ||
pitṛmātṛsapiṇḍais tu samānasalilais tathā |
tatsaṅghātagataiś caiva rājñā vā dhanahāriṇā ||
pūrvāḥ kriyā madhyamāś ca putrādyair eva cottarāḥ |
dauhitrair vā naraśreṣṭha kāryās tattanayais tathā || iti | (ViPu 3.13.34–38)
sapiṇḍādyair avanipatyantaiḥ pūrvāḥ kriyā madhamāś ca kartavyāḥ | putrādyair eva bhrātṛsantatyantaiḥ dauhitrādyaiś cottarāḥ kriyāḥ kartavyāḥ | na sapiṇḍādyair avanipatyantair ity arthaḥ | aurasaḥ suto ‘nupanīto ‘pi dāhādikāḥ kriyāḥ kuryāt | tad āha sumantuḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kuryād avaśyaṃ tu pramītapitṛko hi yaḥ |
vratastho vāvratastho vā eka eva bhaved yadi || iti |
avratastho ‘nupanītaḥ | tad āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
[465]        kuryād anupanīto ‘pi śrāddham eko hi yaḥ sutaḥ |
pitṛyajñāhutiṃ pāṇau juhuyād brāhmaṇasya ca || iti |
atra viśeṣaḥ pracetasā darśitaḥ |
kṛtacūḍas tu kurvīta udakaṃ piṇḍam eva ca |
svadhākāraṃ prayuñjīta vedoccāraṃ na kārayet || iti |
mātāpitror ubhayor api kurvītety arthaḥ | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
kṛtacūḍo ‘nupetas tu pitroḥ śrāddhaṃ samācaret |
udāharet svadhākāraṃ na tu vedākṣarāṇy asau || iti |
yat tu manunoktam,
na hy asmin yujyate karma kiṃcid ā mauñjibandhanāt |
nābhivyāhared brahma svadhāninayanād ṛte || (MDh 2.171–72)
iti, tat varṣakṛtacūḍāviṣayam | tathā ca sumantuḥ |
anupeto ‘pi kurvīta mantravat paitṛmedhikam |
yady asau kṛtacūḍaḥ syād yadi syāc ca trivatsaraḥ || iti |
atha mātāmahādiśrāddhādhikāranirṇayaḥ
tatra vyāsaḥ |
pitṝn mātāmahāṃś caiva dvijaḥ śrāddhena tarpayet |
anṛṇaḥ syāt pitṝṇāṃ tu brahmalokaṃ ca gacchanti ||
[466] iti | mātāmahānām iti bahuvacanaṃ mātuḥ pitāmahaprapitāmahayor api saṃgrahārtham | tathā ca pulastyaḥ |
mātuḥ pitaram ārabhya trayo mātāmahāḥ smṛtāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu pitṛvac chrāddhaṃ kuryur duhitṛsūnavaḥ || iti |
pitṛśrāddhavan mātāmahaśrāddham api nityam, akaraṇe pratyavāyasmaraṇāt | tad uktaṃ skandapurāṇe |
pārvaṇaṃ kurute yas tu kevalaṃ pitṛhetutaḥ |
mātāmahyaṃ na kurute pitṛhā sa prajāyate || iti |
ṛṣyaśṛṅgo ‘pi |
pitaro yatra pūjyante tatra mātāmahā dhruvam |
aviśeṣeṇa kartavyaṃ viśeṣān narakaṃ vrajet || iti |
mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ kvacid apavadati kātyāyanaḥ |
karṣūsamanvitaṃ muktvā tathā śrāddhaṃ ca ṣoḍaśam |
pratyābdikaṃ ca śeṣeṣu piṇḍāḥ syuḥ ṣaḍ it sthitiḥ | iti | (KSm 3.24.14)
karṣūsamanvitaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaśrāddham | ṣoḍaśagrahaṇam ekoddiṣṭopalakṣaṇārtham | saṅghātamaraṇe śrāddhakramam āha ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
bhaved yadi sapiṇḍānāṃ yugapan maraṇaṃ tathā |
saṃbandhāsattim ālocya tatkramāc chrāddham ācaret || iti |
atra patnyādisapiṇḍeṣu saṃbandhāsattir evaṃ draṣṭavyā patipatnyoḥ saṃbandhaḥ pratyāsannaḥ | ekapratiyogikatvād avyavadhānāc ca | [467] putrasya tu mātāpitṛdvayanirūpyatvena vilambitapratipatter viprakṛṣṭaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | bhrātus tu pitṛjatvavyavadhānena tato ‘pi viprakṛṣṭaḥ | evam anyatrohanīyam | patnyādīnāṃ pitroś ca saṅghātamaraṇe ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
patnyāḥ putrasya tatputrabhrātros tattanayasya ca |
snuṣāśvaśrvoś ca pitroś ca saṅghātamaraṇam yadi ||
arvāg abdān mātṛpitṛpūrvaṃ sāpiṇḍyam ācaret || iti |
tatputraḥ putraputraḥ | tattanayo bhrātṛtanayaḥ | yat tu devalenoktam,
pramītau pitarau yasya dehas tasyāśucir bhavet |
na daivaṃ nāpi pitryaṃ ca yāvat pūrṇo na vatsaraḥ ||
iti, tat pūrvoktapatnyādivyatiriktasapiṇḍaviṣayam | ata eva laugākṣiḥ |
anyeṣāṃ pretakāryāṇi mahāgurunipātane |
kuryāt saṃvatsarād arvāk śrāddham ekaṃ tu varjayet || iti |
pretakāryāṇi dahanādīny ādyaśrāddhāntāni vivakṣitāni,
ādyaṃ śrāddham aśuddho ‘pi kuryād ekādaśe ‘hani |
iti viśeṣasmaraṇāt | ekam ity anyad ity arthaḥ | pitroḥ saṅghātamaraṇe devalaḥ |
[468]        pitror uparame putrāḥ kriyāḥ kuryur dvayor api |
anuvṛttau ca cānyeṣāṃ saṅghātamaraṇe ‘pi vā || iti |
sahagamanena pitroḥ saṅghātamaraṇe mātur anugamanena dināntaramaraṇe ‘pi tat tad dvādaśāhe kriyāṃ kuryuḥ | anyeṣāṃ pitroś ca saṅghātamaraṇe yathākālaṃ na kuryuḥ | kiṃ tu tripakṣa eva | tad āha laugākṣiḥ |
patnī putras tathā pautro mātā tatputrakā api |
pitarau ca yadaikasmin mriyeran vāsare tadā ||
ādyam ekādaśe kuryāt tripakṣe tu sapiṇḍanam || iti |
pitror anugamanaṃ vinā saṅghātamaraṇe mātur anugamanaṃ vinā dināntaramaraṇe ‘pi mātus tripakṣe sapiṇḍanaṃ kuryāt | tathā ca devalaḥ |
ekāhamaraṇe pitror anyasyānyadine mṛtau |
sapiṇḍanaṃ tripakṣe syād anuyānamṛtiṃ vinā || iti |
mātṛpitṛśrāddhadvayasya daivāt kālaikye ‘pi pitṛśrāddhaṃ pūrvaṃ kuryāt | tad āha kārṣṇājiniḥ |
pitroḥ śrāddhe samaṃ prāpte nave paryuṣite ‘pi vā |
pitṛpūrvaṃ sutaḥ kuryād anyatrāsattiyogyataḥ || iti |
anyatra mātṛpitṛvyatiriktaviṣaye saṃbandhāsattiyogyataḥ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
yat tu pracetasoktam,
[469]        naikaḥ śrāddhadvayaṃ kuryāt samāne ‘hani kutracit |
iti, tadaikasmin śrāddhe kriyamāṇe daivataikyāt anyasya prasaṅgāt yatra siddhis tad viṣayam | yathā nityaśrāddhāmāvāsyāśrāddhayor amāvāsyāśrāddhena nityaśrāddhasiddhiḥ prāsaṅgikī | yathā vā dārśikayugādiśrāddhayor yugādiśrāddhenaiva dārśikasiddhiḥ | pitror mṛtāhaikye saty anvāhohaṇaviṣaye laugākṣiṇā viśeṣa uktaḥ |
mṛte ‘hani samāsena piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ pṛthak |
navaśrāddhaṃ tu dampatyor anvārohaṇa eva tu || iti |
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ samāsena pākādyaikyena pṛthagasapatnīkaṃ kuryāt | navaśrāddham api tathā kuryāt | smṛtyantaram api |
ekacityāṃ samārūḍhau sampatī nidhanaṃ gatau |
pṛthak śrāddhaṃ tathā kuryād odanaṃ ha pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
anekamātṛbhir ekacityām anvārohaṇe kṛte pākādyaikyena prathamaṃ pitus tadanantaraṃ sākṣān mātus tato jyeṣṭhādikrameṇa kuryāt | tad āha bhṛguḥ |
ekakāle gatāsūnāṃ bahūnām atha vā dvayoḥ |
tantreṇa śrapaṇaṃ kuryāt śrāddhaṃ kuryāt pṛthak pṛthak ||
pūrvakasya mṛtasyādau dvitīyasya jaghanyataḥ |
tṛtīyasya tataḥ kuryāt saṃnipāteṣv ayaṃ kramaḥ || iti |
pūrvakasya mukhyasya pituḥ | dvitīyasya tato jaghanyāyā jananyāḥ | tṛtīyasya tato ‘pi jaghanyatayā paramātur ity arthaḥ | pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭayoḥ saṃnipāte jābāliḥ |
[470]        yady ekatra bhavetāṃ ced ekoddiṣṭaṃ ca pārvaṇam |
pārvaṇaṃ tv abhinirvartya ekoddiṣṭaṃ samācaret || iti |
anekanimittasaṃnipāte nimittānukrameṇa śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
dve bahūni nimittāni jāyerann ekavāsare |
naimittikāni kāryāṇi nimittotpattyanukramāt || iti |
yady apy ekadevatākaśrāddhadvayam ekasminn ahani na yuktam ekānuṣṭhānenetaraprayojanasyāpi prasaṅgāt siddheḥ tathāpi naimittikāni vacanabalād anekāny apy anuṣṭheyāni | tathā ca jābāliḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā tu tasyaiva punaḥ śrāddhaṃ na taddine |
naimittikaṃ tu kartavyaṃ nimittānukramodayam || iti |
nityakāmyayor ekadevatākayoḥ saṃnipāte kāmyenaiva nityasiddhiḥ | tad uktaṃ smṛtisaṃgrahe |
kāmyatantreṇa nityasya tantraṃ śrāddhasya sidhyati ||
iti ||
yatrāśaucavidhānajātam ajahat svārthaprayuktyā dṛśā
proktaṃ yatra ca saṃgṛhītavapuṣāṃ śrāddhaṃ samṛddhyai padam |
adhyāyaṃ tadabāhyanirṇayavidāṃ tāttīryam ārttichidraṃ
so ‘yaṃ vyākurute svatantramahimā mantrīśvaro mādhavaḥ ||
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārga-pravarttikaśrīvīrabukkabhūpālasāmrājyadhurandharasya mādhvāmātyasya kṛtau pārāśrasmṛtivyākhyāyāṃ mādhavīyāyāṃ tṛtīyo ‘dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ || 3 ||
